# Diagrammes v2 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Faire du constructeur de diagrammes un éditeur d'architecte complet : multi-sélection, zoom/pan, annuler/rétablir, alignement, export SVG/PNG (lot 1) ; notes, groupes imbriqués, coudes de liens et concurrence optimiste par `revision` (lot 2) ; profils et stéréotypes avec propriétés déclaratives (lot 3) ; vues générées depuis une requête ou un paquet, vivantes ou figées, rangées par couches (lot 4) ; modèles de diagrammes (lot 5).

**Architecture:** Un diagramme reste une *vue* (ADR 0031) : notes, groupes, coudes et stéréotypes dessinés sont de la présentation, les profils sont de la donnée de référence qui ne touche pas au métamodèle ArchiMate 3.2. Backend : `api → services → domain ← repositories`, `mcp/` à côté de `api/` ; modules de domaine purs `diagram_objects.py`, `profiles.py`, `generated_views.py`, `diagram_templates.py` ; tables `diagram_objects`, `diagram_edges`, colonnes de `diagram_nodes`/`diagrams`, `profiles*`, `element_stereotypes`, `diagram_templates` (une révision Alembic par lot qui touche le schéma). SPA : modules purs testés sans DOM dans `frontend/src/lib/` (`diagramGeometry.ts`, `diagramHistory.ts`, `diagramExport.ts`, `sceneObjects.ts`, `waypoints.ts`, `autoLayout.ts`), SVG écrit à la main, aucune bibliothèque de graphes ni de dessin.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, Pydantic v2, SQLAlchemy 2 async, Alembic, PostgreSQL + pgvector, `mcp` SDK 2.x, pytest ; Vue 3 `<script setup>` + TypeScript + Vite, `openapi-fetch`, Vitest + Testing Library, ESLint type-aware ; `uv`, `ruff`, `mypy --strict`. Aucune dépendance Python ou npm nouvelle.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-06-diagrammes-v2-design.md`

## Écarts constatés avec la spec

Le plan suit le **code réel** ; chaque écart ci-dessous a été vérifié en lisant le fichier cité.

1. `domain/diagrams.py` contient `check_layout(nodes)` (pas de `check_scene`) et `DiagramNode(element_id, x, y, width, height)`. Le plan **garde** `check_layout` et ajoute `check_scene(nodes, objects, edges)` dans `domain/diagram_objects.py`, qui l'appelle.
2. `Diagram` n'a pas de `revision`, `DiagramRepository` (`domain/ports.py:201`) n'a que `nodes_of`/`replace_layout(diagram_id, nodes, *, now) -> bool`, et `api/schemas.py:744` `DiagramLayout` ne porte que `nodes`. Le lot 2 **change la signature** `replace_layout(diagram_id, scene, *, revision, now) -> int | None` ; tous les appelants existants (service, doubles de `tests/conftest.py:513`, `tests/integration/test_diagram_store.py`, `tests/unit/test_diagram_service.py`, `tests/e2e/test_diagram_api.py`, `frontend/tests/useDiagram.spec.ts`, `frontend/tests/DiagramsSection.spec.ts`) sont migrés dans la même tâche.
3. L'ADR 0031 dit que `diagram_nodes.element_id` n'a **pas** de clé étrangère ; la migration `0006` (ADR 0033) l'a ajoutée. Le code fait foi : la suppression d'un élément emporte déjà ses boîtes, la spec a raison.
4. Les erreurs HTTP n'ont que l'enveloppe `{error, detail}` (`api/errors.py:_handle_domain_error`). Le 409 `diagram_revision_conflict` met la révision courante **dans `detail`** ; le SPA relit le diagramme (`GET`) pour la connaître au lieu de la lire dans un champ.
5. Un `ValueError` du domaine est déjà un 422 `invalid_input` (`_handle_value_error`) : `check_scene` lève `ValueError`, sans nouveau code. Seuls les conflits (`diagram_revision_conflict`, `diagram_is_live`, `has_annotations`, `generated_view_too_large`, `stereotype_in_use`) reçoivent une ligne dans `_STATUS`.
6. `PUT /diagrams/{id}/layout` répond `204` + en-tête `X-Diagram-Revision` (spec) : `main.py:520` n'expose que `X-Request-Id` aux navigateurs ; le plan ajoute l'en-tête à `expose_headers`. Le SPA le lit sur `result.response.headers` (`unwrap` reçoit `response`).
7. `mcp/server.py:294` `build_mcp_server` prend **quatre** fournisseurs positionnels (service, documents, ipam, files) et `main.py:402` + deux fixtures de `tests/unit/test_mcp_server.py:34-65` les construisent. Chaque lot qui ajoute un outil ajoute un fournisseur **requis** (diagrammes lot 2, profils lot 3, modèles lot 5) et met à jour ces trois sites. Décompte des outils : 34 → 36 (lot 2) → 39 (lot 3) → 40 (lot 4) → 42 (lot 5) ; la phrase « trente-quatre outils » de CLAUDE.md est mise à jour au lot 5.
8. `ElementRead` (`api/schemas.py:140`) et `Element` (`domain/model.py:55`) n'ont pas de stéréotypes. Le lot 3 ajoute `Element.stereotypes: tuple[StereotypeRef, ...] = ()` rempli par `ArchitectureService` (port `StereotypeSource` optionnel), **pas** par `repositories/architecture_store.py`. Les sites décorés sont `get_element`, `list_elements` et `view_of` (`services/architecture.py:91,100,279`) : si le sous-projet 1 a entre-temps réécrit `list_elements` (ElementQuery), appliquer la décoration à la nouvelle méthode de recherche aussi.
9. Le SPA n'a qu'**une** sélection (`?element=`, `DiagramsSection.vue:selectedId`), un zoom en boutons ±0,25 (`DiagramCanvas.vue:ZOOM_STEP`, plage 0,4–2) sans pan, et `shorten` local au canvas. Le plan ajoute une sélection locale multiple (`useSelection`) *à côté* de `?element=` (l'élément détaillé reste dans l'URL), déplace zoom/plage/`shorten` dans `lib/diagramGeometry.ts`, et passe un 4e paramètre optionnel `pan` à `toCanvasPoint` (appelants existants inchangés).
10. `ElementRead.layer` est déjà résolu par l'API : `layoutByLayers` (lot 4) lit `element.layer` ; `useMetamodel().layerOf` est inutile. Seul l'**ordre visuel** des couches est une table de présentation.
11. Le sous-projet 2 (audit) n'existe pas dans le dépôt : repli prévu par la spec, des lignes `logger.info(..., extra={"action": ...})` dans `services/`, sans texte de note ni valeur de propriété.
12. `frontend/tests/support/api.ts` : `Route` n'a pas de `headers` ; le lot 2 en ajoute (nécessaire pour `X-Diagram-Revision`). Les nœuds des fixtures SPA deviennent `{element_id, x, y, width, height, parent_object_id: null, z: 0}` (champs requis, ADR 0035).
13. Numérotation Alembic : dernière révision existante `0008`. Ce plan utilise `0009` (lot 2), `0010` (lot 3), `0011` (lot 4), `0012` (lot 5) **à renuméroter** (fichier, `revision`, `down_revision`, `tests/unit/test_migrations.py`) si une autre migration (sous-projets 1, 2 : `packages`, `saved_queries`, audit) est entrée avant. La migration du lot 4 référence des tables du sous-projet 1.
14. Playwright n'est pas monté (CLAUDE.md) : les parcours visibles sont couverts par des composants Vitest.
15. Sous-projet 1 : `domain/query.py` (`ElementQuery`), `domain/saved_queries.py`, `domain/packages.py` n'existent pas encore dans ce dépôt. Le lot 4 commence par une garde (Task 29) qui l'établit et fixe les noms réels à utiliser.
16. `POST /diagrams/generate` ne prend pas `generation` (spec §5) : le serveur ne connaît aucune coordonnée calculée (spec §1), donc « Figée d'emblée » est `generate` puis `freeze`, enchaînés par le SPA (Task 32, Task 35).
17. `profile_properties` gagne une colonne `position` (ordre de déclaration, absent de la table de la spec) et le port `ProfileRepository` porte aussi la lecture des étiquettes d'un élément ; `ElementRead.stereotypes` est un champ requis (client régénéré, Task 25).
18. `resolve_members` ne prend pas « un filtre et un catalogue » (spec §3.4) mais `SourceMembers(ids, total)`, ce que renvoie la recherche du sous-projet 1 : le refus à plus de 500 éléments cite ainsi le décompte réel, sans second appel. Le filtre n'est jamais réécrit ici (Task 30, adaptateur de la Task 31).
19. `check_scene` n'a pas d'équivalent « supprimer un groupe » côté service : la suppression d'un groupe qui remonte ses enfants est un calcul pur du SPA (`removeGroup`, Task 15) qui produit la scène à enregistrer ; côté serveur, `parent_id` en `SET NULL` n'est que le filet DDL (Task 11). Le test de service correspondant enregistre la scène déjà remontée (Task 10).

## Global Constraints

- Flèche des dépendances : `api → services → domain ← repositories` ; `mcp/` voisin d'`api/`, jamais de dépôt dans l'adaptateur MCP ; `domain/` n'importe ni FastAPI ni SQLAlchemy.
- Chaque méthode publique de `DiagramService`, `ProfileService`, `TemplateService` commence par `require_caller()` (lecture) ou `require_editor()` (écriture) ; `tests/unit/test_service_guards.py` (dictionnaire `WRITES`) est mis à jour pour chaque nouvelle méthode.
- Bornes déclarées une fois dans `api/schemas.py` et importées par `mcp/server.py` : objets de vue ≤ 500, arêtes ≤ 2000, boîtes ≤ 500 (`MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES`), coordonnées ±100 000, taille de boîte 20–5 000, texte d'objet ≤ 2000, coudes ≤ 20 (`MAX_WAYPOINTS`), profondeur de groupes ≤ 5 (`MAX_GROUP_DEPTH`).
- Entrées Pydantic `extra="forbid"` (`_Input`) ; sorties déclarées ; couleurs `#rrggbb` validées par motif côté API ; `icon_key` est un enum fermé exposé dans OpenAPI.
- Un champ d'entrée sans défaut reste requis pour ne pas dédoubler le schéma en `-Input`/`-Output` (ADR 0035) : `PUT /layout` reçoit `{revision, nodes, objects, edges}` tous requis.
- Un groupe n'est pas un élément du graphe et ne crée jamais de relation ; un modèle ou une instanciation ne contourne jamais `ArchitectureService`.
- Valeurs de propriétés de stéréotype : chaînes ≤ 500 caractères, motif ≤ 200 caractères, clés IPAM (`IPAM_PROPERTIES` de `domain/ipam.py`) refusées dans une déclaration ; `required` n'est jamais bloquant à l'écriture (`missing_required` sert le sous-projet 4).
- Aucune donnée sensible dans les journaux : jamais le texte d'une note ni une valeur de propriété ; une ligne `action=` par écriture dans `services/`.
- Notes et titres de groupes sont du texte, jamais du HTML ; l'export SVG échappe tout texte, valide chaque couleur et n'embarque ni `<script>` ni `href`.
- Concurrence : `PUT /layout` compare `revision` sous `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, 409 `diagram_revision_conflict` si elle diffère, incrémente dans la même transaction.
- Noms de contraintes : convention gelée de `db/base.py` (`pk_`, `fk_<table>_<col>_<ref>`, `uq_`, `ix_`, `ck_<table>_<nom>`).
- Contrat : `make openapi` après chaque lot qui bouge le schéma, `make openapi-check` vert ; jamais d'interface TypeScript écrite à la main qui reflète un modèle Pydantic.
- SPA : aucune règle ArchiMate en TypeScript (métamodèle demandé à `/metamodel`) ; tout chargement déclenchable par l'URL ou une frappe passe par `useLatestRequest()` ; `no-floating-promises` respecté ; un lecteur ne voit ni palette, ni poignée de lien, ni annulation, ni import, ni génération — l'API refuse (403) quand même.
- Historique d'édition : plafond `HISTORY_LIMIT = 100`, mémoire seulement, vidé à l'ouverture d'un diagramme ; ce qui touche le graphe (créer une relation) n'est pas annulable par la pile.
- Vues générées : vivante par défaut (aucune ligne `diagram_nodes`), plafond `MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES = 500` sinon 422 `generated_view_too_large` avec le décompte ; dégeler refusé (409) s'il existe notes, groupes ou tracés sans `discard_annotations=true`.
- Import de profil ou de modèle : ≤ 256 Ko, `profile_format: 1` / `version: 1` vérifiés, mêmes validateurs que l'écriture manuelle.
- Couverture backend ≥ 90 % (`fail_under = 90`), `ruff`, `mypy --strict`, `bandit` propres ; `make check` vert à la fin de chaque lot.
- Commits : Conventional Commits en anglais, terminés par `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Commande unitaire backend** : depuis `backend/`, `uv run pytest <chemin> -x` (sans `--cov`). **Commande unitaire frontend** : depuis `frontend/`, `npm test -- --run <chemin>`.
- **ITEST `<chemin>`** (après `make pg-up` à la racine), depuis `backend/` : `EA_DEBUG=true EA_POSTGRES_ENABLED=true EA_POSTGRES_HOST=127.0.0.1 EA_POSTGRES_PORT=5433 EA_POSTGRES_PASSWORD=developmentonly EA_EMBEDDINGS_ENABLED=false uv run pytest <chemin> -x`.
- Ordre des lots : 1 (SPA seul, aucune dépendance backend) ; 2 précède 5 ; 1 et 3 sont indépendants ; 4 attend le sous-projet 1 mais ni 3 ni 5.

## Review Focus

- **Groupe vide et suppression d'un groupe** : un groupe sans enfant est valide ; supprimer un groupe remonte ses enfants (boîtes, notes, sous-groupes) au parent du groupe sans toucher aucun élément. Testé en pur (`removeGroup`, Task 15) et par `check_scene` (Task 9).
- **Imbrication cyclique ou trop profonde** : `parent_id` qui boucle, groupe parent de lui-même, sixième niveau. Refusé par `check_scene` (Task 9, 422) et empêché avant l'envoi par `canNest` (Task 15).
- **Suppression d'un élément dont une boîte est dans un groupe** : la boîte disparaît avec l'élément (cascade), le groupe et ses autres enfants restent, une note ancrée à cet élément perd son ancre (`SET NULL`). Testé en intégration (Task 11) et à la lecture (le diagramme s'ouvre sans trou).
- **Annuler/rétablir après sauvegarde** : annuler après qu'un autosave est parti restaure l'état local *et* déclenche un nouvel autosave ; annuler après un conflit de révision n'est pas possible (historique vidé au rechargement). Testé Task 4 et Task 16.
- **Lecteur sans droits** : `replace_layout`, `generate`, `freeze`, `unfreeze`, profils, modèles refusés par le service (403) ; barre d'outils, annulation, palette, annotations et poignées absentes du DOM. Testé aux Tasks 10, 12, 7, 18, 23, 32.

# Lot 1 — Éditeur confort (SPA seul, aucune migration, aucun changement d'API)

### Task 1: Géométrie pure — zoom/pan, sélection au rectangle, alignement, répartition, pas clavier

**Files:**
- Modify: `frontend/src/lib/diagramGeometry.ts` (remplacer `toCanvasPoint`, lignes 31-34 ; ajouter à la fin du fichier, après la ligne 106)
- Modify: `frontend/tests/diagramGeometry.spec.ts` (ajouter à la fin ; étendre l'import, lignes 3-14)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Point`, `Size`, `Rect`, `MIN_CANVAS`, `MIN_BOX` (déjà exportés).
- Produces (TypeScript) :
  - `toCanvasPoint(client: Point, origin: Point, zoom: number, pan?: Point): Point`
  - `type View = { zoom: number; pan: Point }`, `ZOOM_RANGE: readonly [0.4, 2]`, `clampZoom(zoom: number): number`
  - `zoomAt(view: View, factor: number, anchor: Point): View` (`anchor` = pixels depuis le coin haut-gauche du canvas)
  - `fitView(boxes: Rect[], viewport: Size, margin?: number): View`
  - `type Placed = Rect & { id: string }`, `type Moved = Point & { id: string }`
  - `boundingBox(rects: Rect[]): Rect | null`, `rubberBand(from: Point, to: Point): Rect`, `boxesIn(rect: Rect, boxes: Placed[]): string[]`
  - `type AlignMode = 'left' | 'centre' | 'right' | 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom'`, `type DistributeAxis = 'horizontal' | 'vertical'`
  - `alignBoxes(boxes: Placed[], mode: AlignMode): Moved[]`, `distributeBoxes(boxes: Placed[], axis: DistributeAxis): Moved[]`
  - `nudgeDelta(key: string, coarse: boolean): Point | null`
  - `CHARACTER_WIDTH = 7.5`, `shortenToWidth(name: string, width: number): string`

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

Dans `frontend/tests/diagramGeometry.spec.ts`, étendre l'import existant avec `AlignMode, DistributeAxis, ZOOM_RANGE, alignBoxes, boundingBox, boxesIn, clampZoom, distributeBoxes, fitView, nudgeDelta, rubberBand, shortenToWidth, zoomAt` (garder les noms déjà importés), puis ajouter à la fin du fichier :

```ts
const placed = (id: string, x: number, y: number, width = 100, height = 50) => ({
  id,
  x,
  y,
  width,
  height,
})
const at2 = (moved: { id: string; x: number; y: number }[], id: string) =>
  moved.find((box) => box.id === id)!

describe('toCanvasPoint with a pan', () => {
  it('adds the pan to the scaled pointer', () => {
    expect(toCanvasPoint({ x: 300, y: 250 }, { x: 100, y: 50 }, 2, { x: 40, y: 10 })).toEqual({
      x: 140,
      y: 110,
    })
  })

  it('keeps the old three-argument behaviour', () => {
    expect(toCanvasPoint({ x: 300, y: 250 }, { x: 100, y: 50 }, 1)).toEqual({ x: 200, y: 200 })
  })
})

describe('zoomAt', () => {
  it('keeps the canvas point under the cursor where it was', () => {
    const view = zoomAt({ zoom: 1, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } }, 2, { x: 100, y: 50 })

    expect(view.zoom).toBe(2)
    expect(toCanvasPoint({ x: 100, y: 50 }, { x: 0, y: 0 }, view.zoom, view.pan)).toEqual({
      x: 100,
      y: 50,
    })
  })

  it('never leaves the allowed range', () => {
    expect(zoomAt({ zoom: 1.9, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } }, 10, { x: 0, y: 0 }).zoom).toBe(ZOOM_RANGE[1])
    expect(clampZoom(0.01)).toBe(ZOOM_RANGE[0])
  })
})

describe('fitView', () => {
  it('shows an empty diagram at 100 %', () => {
    expect(fitView([], { width: 800, height: 600 })).toEqual({ zoom: 1, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } })
  })

  it('shrinks to hold boxes wider than the viewport', () => {
    const view = fitView([placed('a', 0, 0, 1600, 100)], { width: 800, height: 600 }, 0)

    expect(view.zoom).toBe(0.5)
  })
})

describe('selection rectangle', () => {
  it('normalises a band dragged up and to the left', () => {
    expect(rubberBand({ x: 50, y: 40 }, { x: 10, y: 5 })).toEqual({
      x: 10,
      y: 5,
      width: 40,
      height: 35,
    })
  })

  it('selects every box the band touches, and no other', () => {
    const boxes = [placed('a', 0, 0), placed('b', 300, 0), placed('c', 90, 40)]

    expect(boxesIn({ x: 80, y: 30, width: 40, height: 40 }, boxes)).toEqual(['a', 'c'])
  })

  it('boundingBox is null for nothing and the union otherwise', () => {
    expect(boundingBox([])).toBeNull()
    expect(boundingBox([placed('a', 10, 10), placed('b', 200, 100, 50, 50)])).toEqual({
      x: 10,
      y: 10,
      width: 240,
      height: 140,
    })
  })
})

describe('alignBoxes', () => {
  const a = placed('a', 10, 0)
  const b = placed('b', 60, 40, 40, 50)

  it.each<[AlignMode, number, number]>([
    ['left', 10, 10],
    ['right', 10, 70],
    ['centre', 10, 40],
  ])('aligns on the %s edge of the selection', (mode, ax, bx) => {
    const moved = alignBoxes([a, b], mode)

    expect([at2(moved, 'a').x, at2(moved, 'b').x]).toEqual([ax, bx])
  })

  it('aligns tops, middles and bottoms on the vertical axis', () => {
    expect(at2(alignBoxes([a, b], 'top'), 'b').y).toBe(0)
    expect(at2(alignBoxes([a, b], 'bottom'), 'a').y).toBe(40)
    expect(at2(alignBoxes([a, b], 'middle'), 'a').y).toBe(20)
  })

  it('leaves a lone box where it is', () => {
    expect(alignBoxes([a], 'right')).toEqual([{ id: 'a', x: 10, y: 0 }])
  })
})

describe('distributeBoxes', () => {
  it('equalises the gaps between three boxes, keeping the outer two', () => {
    const boxes = [placed('c', 200, 0, 20), placed('a', 0, 0, 20), placed('b', 30, 0, 20)]

    const moved = distributeBoxes(boxes, 'horizontal')

    expect(at2(moved, 'a').x).toBe(0)
    expect(at2(moved, 'b').x).toBe(100)
    expect(at2(moved, 'c').x).toBe(200)
  })

  it.each<DistributeAxis>(['horizontal', 'vertical'])(
    'does nothing with fewer than three boxes (%s)',
    (axis) => {
      const two = [placed('a', 0, 0), placed('b', 500, 500)]

      expect(distributeBoxes(two, axis)).toEqual([
        { id: 'a', x: 0, y: 0 },
        { id: 'b', x: 500, y: 500 },
      ])
    },
  )
})

describe('nudgeDelta', () => {
  it('moves one pixel, ten with shift', () => {
    expect(nudgeDelta('ArrowRight', false)).toEqual({ x: 1, y: 0 })
    expect(nudgeDelta('ArrowUp', true)).toEqual({ x: 0, y: -10 })
  })

  it('ignores every other key', () => {
    expect(nudgeDelta('a', false)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('shortenToWidth', () => {
  it('keeps a name that fits and ellipsises one that does not', () => {
    expect(shortenToWidth('Facturation', 200)).toBe('Facturation')
    expect(shortenToWidth('Facturation clients', 60)).toBe('Factura…')
  })
})
```

Dans `frontend/src/lib/diagramGeometry.ts`, remplacer la fonction `toCanvasPoint` (lignes 31-34) par :

```ts
/**
 * A pointer in client pixels to canvas units; `origin` is the canvas's client
 * top-left and `pan` the canvas point shown at that corner.
 */
export function toCanvasPoint(
  client: Point,
  origin: Point,
  zoom: number,
  pan: Point = { x: 0, y: 0 },
): Point {
  return { x: (client.x - origin.x) / zoom + pan.x, y: (client.y - origin.y) / zoom + pan.y }
}
```

Ajouter à la fin du fichier :

```ts
/** How the canvas is looked at: a scale, and the canvas point at its top-left corner. */
export type View = { zoom: number; pan: Point }

export const ZOOM_RANGE = [0.4, 2] as const

export function clampZoom(zoom: number): number {
  return Math.min(ZOOM_RANGE[1], Math.max(ZOOM_RANGE[0], zoom))
}

/** Scale by `factor` keeping the canvas point under `anchor` (px from the canvas corner) fixed. */
export function zoomAt(view: View, factor: number, anchor: Point): View {
  const zoom = clampZoom(view.zoom * factor)
  const under = { x: anchor.x / view.zoom + view.pan.x, y: anchor.y / view.zoom + view.pan.y }
  return { zoom, pan: { x: under.x - anchor.x / zoom, y: under.y - anchor.y / zoom } }
}

/** A box together with the id it is selected by. */
export type Placed = Rect & { id: string }
/** Where a box ends up after an arrangement. */
export type Moved = Point & { id: string }

/** The smallest rectangle holding every box, or `null` for none. */
export function boundingBox(rects: Rect[]): Rect | null {
  if (rects.length === 0) {
    return null
  }
  const left = Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.x))
  const top = Math.min(...rects.map((rect) => rect.y))
  const right = Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.x + rect.width))
  const bottom = Math.max(...rects.map((rect) => rect.y + rect.height))
  return { x: left, y: top, width: right - left, height: bottom - top }
}

/** The view that shows every box in `viewport`, never past the zoom range. */
export function fitView(boxes: Rect[], viewport: Size, margin = 24): View {
  const bounds = boundingBox(boxes)
  if (!bounds) {
    return { zoom: 1, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } }
  }
  const zoom = clampZoom(
    Math.min(
      (viewport.width - 2 * margin) / bounds.width,
      (viewport.height - 2 * margin) / bounds.height,
    ),
  )
  return { zoom, pan: { x: bounds.x - margin / zoom, y: bounds.y - margin / zoom } }
}

/** The rectangle between two corners, whichever way it was dragged. */
export function rubberBand(from: Point, to: Point): Rect {
  return {
    x: Math.min(from.x, to.x),
    y: Math.min(from.y, to.y),
    width: Math.abs(to.x - from.x),
    height: Math.abs(to.y - from.y),
  }
}

/** The ids of the boxes a rectangle touches, in the order given. */
export function boxesIn(rect: Rect, boxes: Placed[]): string[] {
  return boxes
    .filter(
      (box) =>
        box.x < rect.x + rect.width &&
        rect.x < box.x + box.width &&
        box.y < rect.y + rect.height &&
        rect.y < box.y + box.height,
    )
    .map((box) => box.id)
}

export type AlignMode = 'left' | 'centre' | 'right' | 'top' | 'middle' | 'bottom'
export type DistributeAxis = 'horizontal' | 'vertical'

/** Line the boxes up on one edge or axis of their common bounds. One box stays put. */
export function alignBoxes(boxes: Placed[], mode: AlignMode): Moved[] {
  const bounds = boundingBox(boxes)
  if (!bounds || boxes.length < 2) {
    return boxes.map(({ id, x, y }) => ({ id, x, y }))
  }
  return boxes.map((box) => {
    switch (mode) {
      case 'left':
        return { id: box.id, x: bounds.x, y: box.y }
      case 'right':
        return { id: box.id, x: bounds.x + bounds.width - box.width, y: box.y }
      case 'centre':
        return { id: box.id, x: bounds.x + (bounds.width - box.width) / 2, y: box.y }
      case 'top':
        return { id: box.id, x: box.x, y: bounds.y }
      case 'bottom':
        return { id: box.id, x: box.x, y: bounds.y + bounds.height - box.height }
      case 'middle':
        return { id: box.id, x: box.x, y: bounds.y + (bounds.height - box.height) / 2 }
    }
  })
}

/**
 * Space three or more boxes so the gaps between them are equal, the two
 * outermost staying where they are. Fewer than three: nothing to distribute.
 */
export function distributeBoxes(boxes: Placed[], axis: DistributeAxis): Moved[] {
  if (boxes.length < 3) {
    return boxes.map(({ id, x, y }) => ({ id, x, y }))
  }
  const horizontal = axis === 'horizontal'
  const start = (box: Placed) => (horizontal ? box.x : box.y)
  const size = (box: Placed) => (horizontal ? box.width : box.height)
  const ordered = [...boxes].sort((a, b) => start(a) - start(b) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
  const first = start(ordered[0])
  const end = Math.max(...ordered.map((box) => start(box) + size(box)))
  const gap = (end - first - ordered.reduce((sum, box) => sum + size(box), 0)) / (ordered.length - 1)
  let cursor = first
  return ordered.map((box) => {
    const at = cursor
    cursor += size(box) + gap
    return horizontal ? { id: box.id, x: at, y: box.y } : { id: box.id, x: box.x, y: at }
  })
}

const ARROWS: Record<string, Point> = {
  ArrowLeft: { x: -1, y: 0 },
  ArrowRight: { x: 1, y: 0 },
  ArrowUp: { x: 0, y: -1 },
  ArrowDown: { x: 0, y: 1 },
}

/** The move an arrow key asks for — 1 px, 10 with shift — or `null` for any other key. */
export function nudgeDelta(key: string, coarse: boolean): Point | null {
  const unit = ARROWS[key]
  if (!unit) {
    return null
  }
  const step = coarse ? 10 : 1
  return { x: unit.x * step, y: unit.y * step }
}

/** Roughly how wide one character of a box's name is drawn. */
export const CHARACTER_WIDTH = 7.5

/** As much of `name` as a box `width` wide holds: a wider box shows more of it. */
export function shortenToWidth(name: string, width: number): string {
  const limit = Math.max(4, Math.floor(width / CHARACTER_WIDTH))
  return name.length > limit ? `${name.slice(0, limit - 1)}…` : name
}
```

Note : `nudgeDelta('ArrowUp', true)` doit rendre `{ x: 0, y: -10 }` — `0 * 10` vaut `0`, mais `-0` apparaît si `unit.x` vaut `-0` ; les constantes `ARROWS` n'en contiennent pas, `toEqual` accepte de toute façon `0` contre `0`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { HISTORY_LIMIT, createHistory, type Command } from '../src/lib/diagramHistory'

/** A counter whose every change is a command already applied. */
function counter() {
  let value = 0
  const history = createHistory()
  const set = (next: number, mergeKey?: string) => {
    const before = value
    value = next
    const command: Command = {
      label: `to ${next}`,
      mergeKey,
      do: () => {
        value = next
      },
      undo: () => {
        value = before
      },
    }
    history.record(command)
  }
  return { history, set, value: () => value }
}

describe('createHistory', () => {
  it('undoes then redoes, in order', () => {
    const { history, set, value } = counter()
    set(1)
    set(2)

    history.undo()
    expect(value()).toBe(1)
    history.undo()
    expect(value()).toBe(0)
    expect(history.canUndo()).toBe(false)

    history.redo()
    history.redo()
    expect(value()).toBe(2)
    expect(history.canRedo()).toBe(false)
  })

  it('forgets what could be redone once something new is recorded', () => {
    const { history, set } = counter()
    set(1)
    history.undo()

    set(5)

    expect(history.canRedo()).toBe(false)
  })

  it('merges consecutive commands sharing a key into one entry, keeping the first undo', () => {
    const { history, set, value } = counter()
    set(1, 'nudge')
    set(2, 'nudge')
    set(3, 'nudge')

    expect(history.size()).toBe(1)
    history.undo()
    expect(value()).toBe(0)
    history.redo()
    expect(value()).toBe(3)
  })

  it('does not merge across a seal, nor across different keys', () => {
    const { history, set } = counter()
    set(1, 'nudge')
    history.seal()
    set(2, 'nudge')
    set(3, 'other')

    expect(history.size()).toBe(3)
  })

  it('keeps at most the limit, dropping the oldest', () => {
    const { history, set, value } = counter()
    for (let i = 1; i <= HISTORY_LIMIT + 5; i += 1) {
      set(i)
    }

    expect(history.size()).toBe(HISTORY_LIMIT)
    for (let i = 0; i < HISTORY_LIMIT; i += 1) {
      history.undo()
    }
    expect(value()).toBe(5)
  })

  it('tells its owner when it changes, and undo on an empty stack is a no-op', () => {
    const onChange = vi.fn()
    const history = createHistory({ onChange })

    expect(history.undo()).toBe(false)
    history.record({ label: 'x', do: () => undefined, undo: () => undefined })

    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  })

  it('clear empties both stacks', () => {
    const { history, set } = counter()
    set(1)
    history.undo()

    history.clear()

    expect([history.canUndo(), history.canRedo()]).toEqual([false, false])
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/lib/diagramHistory.ts` :

```ts
// Undo and redo for one editing session of a diagram.
//
// A drag has already moved the box on screen by the time it ends, so what is
// recorded is a command *already applied*: `undo` puts the scene back, `do`
// puts it forward again (redo). Memory only — an undo stack that survived a
// reload would undo a layout somebody else may have saved since. Nothing here
// knows what a scene is; the caller's closures do.

export type Command = {
  label: string
  do: () => void
  undo: () => void
  /** Consecutive commands with the same key are one entry — arrow-key nudges. */
  mergeKey?: string
}

/** The most gestures that can be taken back. */
export const HISTORY_LIMIT = 100

export function createHistory(options: { limit?: number; onChange?: () => void } = {}) {
  const limit = options.limit ?? HISTORY_LIMIT
  const done: Command[] = []
  const undone: Command[] = []
  // Set by undo, redo and `seal`: the next record starts a fresh entry
  // instead of folding into the one on top.
  let sealed = false

  const changed = () => options.onChange?.()

  function record(command: Command): void {
    const top = done[done.length - 1]
    if (top && !sealed && command.mergeKey !== undefined && top.mergeKey === command.mergeKey) {
      done[done.length - 1] = { ...top, do: command.do, label: command.label }
    } else {
      done.push(command)
      if (done.length > limit) {
        done.shift()
      }
    }
    sealed = false
    undone.length = 0
    changed()
  }

  function undo(): boolean {
    const command = done.pop()
    if (!command) {
      return false
    }
    command.undo()
    undone.push(command)
    sealed = true
    changed()
    return true
  }

  function redo(): boolean {
    const command = undone.pop()
    if (!command) {
      return false
    }
    command.do()
    done.push(command)
    sealed = true
    changed()
    return true
  }

  function clear(): void {
    done.length = 0
    undone.length = 0
    sealed = false
    changed()
  }

  return {
    record,
    undo,
    redo,
    clear,
    seal: () => {
      sealed = true
    },
    canUndo: () => done.length > 0,
    canRedo: () => undone.length > 0,
    size: () => done.length,
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import {
  LIGHT_THEME,
  escapeXml,
  exportSize,
  safeColour,
  serializeSvg,
  type ExportScene,
} from '../src/lib/diagramExport'

const BOX = {
  id: 'a',
  name: 'Facturation',
  type: 'Composant applicatif',
  fill: '#b5ffff',
  x: 10,
  y: 20,
  width: 132,
  height: 46,
}

const SCENE: ExportScene = {
  boxes: [BOX, { ...BOX, id: 'b', name: 'Commandes', x: 300 }],
  edges: [{ id: 'r', points: [{ x: 142, y: 43 }, { x: 300, y: 43 }], label: 'sert' }],
}

describe('escapeXml', () => {
  it('escapes the five characters XML cares about', () => {
    expect(escapeXml(`<a href="x">&'</a>`)).toBe('&lt;a href=&quot;x&quot;&gt;&amp;&apos;&lt;/a&gt;')
  })
})

describe('safeColour', () => {
  it('keeps #rrggbb and replaces anything else, injection included', () => {
    expect(safeColour('#AaBb09', '#000000')).toBe('#AaBb09')
    expect(safeColour('red" onload="x', '#000000')).toBe('#000000')
    expect(safeColour('url(javascript:x)', '#000000')).toBe('#000000')
  })
})

describe('serializeSvg', () => {
  it('is a standalone document with the boxes, the link and its verb', () => {
    const svg = serializeSvg(SCENE)

    expect(svg.startsWith('<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>')).toBe(true)
    expect(svg).toContain('xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"')
    expect(svg).toContain('Facturation')
    expect(svg).toContain('Commandes')
    expect(svg).toContain('<polyline')
    expect(svg).toContain('sert')
    expect(svg).toContain('fill="#b5ffff"')
  })

  it('never lets a name become markup', () => {
    const svg = serializeSvg({
      ...SCENE,
      boxes: [{ ...BOX, name: '<script>alert(1)</script>', fill: '"><script>x</script>' }],
    })

    expect(svg).not.toContain('<script')
    expect(svg).toContain('&lt;script&gt;')
    expect(svg).not.toContain('"><script')
  })

  it('embeds no external reference', () => {
    const svg = serializeSvg(SCENE)

    expect(svg).not.toMatch(/href=|xlink|<image|<use|<foreignObject|https?:\/\/(?!www\.w3\.org)/)
  })

  it('is deterministic', () => {
    expect(serializeSvg(SCENE, LIGHT_THEME)).toBe(serializeSvg(SCENE, LIGHT_THEME))
  })

  it('sizes the image to the content plus a margin', () => {
    const size = exportSize(SCENE)

    expect(size.width).toBe(300 + 132 - 10 + 48)
    expect(size.height).toBe(46 + 48)
  })

  it('draws an empty diagram as a small blank page', () => {
    expect(exportSize({ boxes: [], edges: [] })).toEqual({ width: 48, height: 48 })
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('buildExportScene', () => {
```

`frontend/src/lib/diagramExport.ts` :

```ts
// A diagram as an image.
//
// The SVG is built here as a string rather than copied from the live DOM: the
// screen's SVG leans on CSS variables that invert in the dark theme, and an
// export that changed colour with the viewer's theme would not be a document.
// So the palette is fixed and light, every colour is inline, and every piece
// of text is escaped — names are typed by people, and the file ends up opened
// by other people's tools. No script, no external reference, ever.
// The PNG is that same SVG drawn on a <canvas>; the SVG is the reference.
import { shortenToWidth, type Point, type Size } from './diagramGeometry'

export type ExportBox = {
  id: string
  name: string
  type: string
  fill: string
  x: number
  y: number
  width: number
  height: number
}
export type ExportEdge = { id: string; points: Point[]; label: string }
export type ExportScene = { boxes: ExportBox[]; edges: ExportEdge[] }
export type ExportTheme = { background: string; ink: string; stroke: string; font: string }
export type ExportFormat = 'svg' | 'png' | 'png2'

export const LIGHT_THEME: ExportTheme = {
  background: '#ffffff',
  ink: '#24202b',
  stroke: '#5b5666',
  font: 'system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", Roboto, sans-serif',
}

/** White space around the content, in canvas units. */
export const EXPORT_MARGIN = 24

const HEX_COLOUR = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/

export function escapeXml(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
    .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
    .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
    .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
    .replaceAll("'", '&apos;')
}

/** A colour that is certainly `#rrggbb`, or `fallback`: a colour is an attribute value. */
export function safeColour(value: string, fallback: string): string {
  return HEX_COLOUR.test(value) ? value : fallback
}

const num = (value: number) => (Number.isFinite(value) ? String(Math.round(value * 100) / 100) : '0')

function bounds(scene: ExportScene): { x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number } {
  const xs = [
    ...scene.boxes.flatMap((box) => [box.x, box.x + box.width]),
    ...scene.edges.flatMap((edge) => edge.points.map((point) => point.x)),
  ]
  const ys = [
    ...scene.boxes.flatMap((box) => [box.y, box.y + box.height]),
    ...scene.edges.flatMap((edge) => edge.points.map((point) => point.y)),
  ]
  if (xs.length === 0) {
    return { x: 0, y: 0, width: 0, height: 0 }
  }
  const [left, top] = [Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys)]
  return { x: left, y: top, width: Math.max(...xs) - left, height: Math.max(...ys) - top }
}

/** The image size: the content plus a margin on every side. */
export function exportSize(scene: ExportScene): Size {
  const content = bounds(scene)
  return { width: content.width + 2 * EXPORT_MARGIN, height: content.height + 2 * EXPORT_MARGIN }
}

export function serializeSvg(scene: ExportScene, theme: ExportTheme = LIGHT_THEME): string {
  const content = bounds(scene)
  const size = exportSize(scene)
  const origin = { x: content.x - EXPORT_MARGIN, y: content.y - EXPORT_MARGIN }
  const ink = safeColour(theme.ink, '#000000')
  const stroke = safeColour(theme.stroke, '#555555')
  const out: string[] = [
    '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>',
    `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${num(size.width)}" height="${num(size.height)}" ` +
      `viewBox="${num(origin.x)} ${num(origin.y)} ${num(size.width)} ${num(size.height)}" ` +
      `font-family="${escapeXml(theme.font)}" font-size="12">`,
    `<rect x="${num(origin.x)}" y="${num(origin.y)}" width="${num(size.width)}" height="${num(size.height)}" fill="${safeColour(theme.background, '#ffffff')}"/>`,
    `<defs><marker id="arrow" viewBox="0 0 10 10" refX="9" refY="5" markerWidth="7" markerHeight="7" orient="auto-start-reverse"><path d="M 0 0 L 10 5 L 0 10 z" fill="${stroke}"/></marker></defs>`,
  ]
  for (const edge of scene.edges) {
    if (edge.points.length < 2) {
      continue
    }
    const points = edge.points.map((point) => `${num(point.x)},${num(point.y)}`).join(' ')
    const middle = edge.points[Math.floor((edge.points.length - 1) / 2)]
    const next = edge.points[Math.floor((edge.points.length - 1) / 2) + 1]
    out.push(
      `<polyline points="${points}" fill="none" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="1.5" marker-end="url(#arrow)"/>`,
      `<text x="${num((middle.x + next.x) / 2)}" y="${num((middle.y + next.y) / 2 - 4)}" text-anchor="middle" fill="${ink}">${escapeXml(edge.label)}</text>`,
    )
  }
  for (const box of scene.boxes) {
    const centre = box.x + box.width / 2
    out.push(
      `<rect x="${num(box.x)}" y="${num(box.y)}" width="${num(box.width)}" height="${num(box.height)}" rx="6" fill="${safeColour(box.fill, '#e8e6ec')}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`,
      `<text x="${num(centre)}" y="${num(box.y + box.height / 2 - 2)}" text-anchor="middle" font-weight="600" fill="${ink}">${escapeXml(shortenToWidth(box.name, box.width))}</text>`,
      `<text x="${num(centre)}" y="${num(box.y + box.height / 2 + 13)}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="${ink}">${escapeXml(shortenToWidth(box.type, box.width))}</text>`,
    )
  }
  out.push('</svg>')
  return out.join('\n')
}

// ponytail: the two functions below need a real browser (Image, <canvas>);
// jsdom has neither, so they are exercised by hand and through a mocked module
// in the section's spec. The SVG above is the tested artefact.
/** Draw an SVG string on a canvas `scale` times larger and return it as a PNG. */
export async function rasterize(svg: string, size: Size, scale: 1 | 2): Promise<Blob> {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8' }))
  try {
    const image = new Image()
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      image.onload = () => resolve()
      image.onerror = () => reject(new Error("L'image n'a pas pu être dessinée."))
      image.src = url
    })
    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
    canvas.width = Math.ceil(size.width * scale)
    canvas.height = Math.ceil(size.height * scale)
    const context = canvas.getContext('2d')
    if (!context) {
      throw new Error("Le navigateur ne sait pas dessiner l'image.")
    }
    context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
    return await new Promise<Blob>((resolve, reject) => {
      canvas.toBlob(
        (blob) => (blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error("L'image n'a pas pu être produite."))),
        'image/png',
      )
    })
  } finally {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
  }
}

/** Hand a blob to the browser as a download. */
export function download(blob: Blob, filename: string): void {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = filename
  link.click()
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}
```

```ts
// The diagram as the export sees it: boxes with their fill, links as strokes.
// Kept apart from `lib/diagramExport.ts` because it reads the metamodel's
// colours and the relationship verbs, which `lib/` does not import.
import { edgeSegment } from '../../lib/diagramGeometry'
import type { ExportScene } from '../../lib/diagramExport'
import { LAYER_COLOURS } from '../metamodel/useMetamodel'
import { verbOf } from '../relationships/labels'
import type { Box, RelationshipRead } from './useDiagram'

export function buildExportScene(
  boxes: Box[],
  relationships: RelationshipRead[],
  typeLabel: (value: string) => string,
): ExportScene {
  const byId = new Map(boxes.map((box) => [box.element.id, box]))
  return {
    boxes: boxes.map((box) => ({
      id: box.element.id,
      name: box.element.name,
      type: typeLabel(box.element.element_type),
      fill: LAYER_COLOURS[box.element.layer],
      x: box.x,
      y: box.y,
      width: box.width,
      height: box.height,
    })),
    edges: relationships.flatMap((relationship) => {
      const source = byId.get(relationship.source_id)
      const target = byId.get(relationship.target_id)
      const segment = source && target ? edgeSegment(source, target) : null
      return segment
        ? [
            {
              id: relationship.id,
              points: [segment.from, segment.to],
              label: verbOf(relationship.relationship_type, relationship.access_type),
            },
          ]
        : []
    }),
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import { useSelection } from '../src/features/diagrams/useSelection'

describe('useSelection', () => {
  it('toggles an id in and out, keeping the order of arrival', () => {
    const selection = useSelection()

    selection.toggle('a')
    selection.toggle('b')
    selection.toggle('a')

    expect(selection.ids.value).toEqual(['b'])
  })

  it('replaces the whole set without duplicates, and clears', () => {
    const selection = useSelection()

    selection.set(['a', 'b', 'a'])
    expect(selection.ids.value).toEqual(['a', 'b'])

    selection.clear()
    expect(selection.ids.value).toEqual([])
  })
})
```

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { SAVE_PAUSE_MS, useDiagram } from '../src/features/diagrams/useDiagram'
import {
  anElement,
  aRelationship,
  stubApi,
  type RecordedCall,
  type Route,
} from './support/api'

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

const DIAGRAM_ID = 'dddddddd-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
const A = anElement({ id: 'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', name: 'Facturation' })
const B = anElement({ id: 'bbbbbbbb-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', name: 'Commandes' })

const READ: Route = {
  path: `/diagrams/${DIAGRAM_ID}`,
  body: {
    id: DIAGRAM_ID,
    name: 'Vue',
    description: '',
    created_at: '2026-09-13T12:00:00Z',
    updated_at: '2026-09-13T12:00:00Z',
    nodes: [{ element_id: A.id, x: 10, y: 20, width: 132, height: 46 }],
    elements: [A],
    relationships: [],
  },
}
const SAVE: Route = { method: 'PUT', path: `/diagrams/${DIAGRAM_ID}/layout`, status: 204 }
const LINK: Route = {
  method: 'POST',
  path: '/relationships',
  status: 201,
  body: aRelationship({ source_id: A.id, target_id: B.id }),
}

const saves = (calls: RecordedCall[]) => calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'PUT')
const savedIds = (call: RecordedCall) =>
  (call.body as { nodes: { element_id: string }[] }).nodes.map((node) => node.element_id)
const ids = (editor: ReturnType<typeof useDiagram>) =>
  editor.boxes.value.map((box) => box.element.id)

async function pause(): Promise<void> {
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(SAVE_PAUSE_MS)
  await vi.waitFor(() => undefined)
}

async function opened() {
  const calls = stubApi([READ, SAVE, LINK])
  const editor = useDiagram()
  await editor.open(DIAGRAM_ID)
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })
  return { calls, editor }
}

describe('useDiagram — undo and redo', () => {
  it('takes back a placed box, then puts it back', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })

    editor.undo()
    expect(ids(editor)).toEqual([A.id])
    expect(editor.canRedo.value).toBe(true)

    editor.redo()
    expect(ids(editor)).toEqual([A.id, B.id])
  })

  it('saves again after an undo that follows a save', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })
    await pause()
    expect(saves(calls)).toHaveLength(1)

    editor.undo()
    await pause()

    expect(saves(calls)).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(savedIds(saves(calls)[1])).toEqual([A.id])
  })

  it('records one drag as one entry', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()

    editor.move(A.id, { x: 20, y: 30 })
    editor.move(A.id, { x: 40, y: 50 })
    editor.move(A.id, { x: 60, y: 70 })
    editor.endGesture()
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.boxes.value[0]).toMatchObject({ x: 10, y: 20 })
    expect(editor.canUndo.value).toBe(false)
  })

  it('folds consecutive keyboard nudges of the same box into one entry', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()

    editor.move(A.id, { x: 11, y: 20 })
    editor.endGesture('keyboard')
    editor.move(A.id, { x: 12, y: 20 })
    editor.endGesture('keyboard')
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.boxes.value[0]).toMatchObject({ x: 10 })
  })

  it('takes back a resize', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()

    editor.resize(A.id, { width: 300, height: 90 })
    editor.endGesture()
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.boxes.value[0]).toMatchObject({ width: 132, height: 46 })
  })

  it('does not put a created relationship on the stack: the graph is not a view', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })
    editor.undo()
    editor.redo()

    await editor.connect({ relationship_type: 'serving', source_id: A.id, target_id: B.id })
    editor.undo() // takes back the placement of B, never the relationship
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.relationships.value).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('forgets its history when another diagram is opened', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })
    expect(editor.canUndo.value).toBe(true)

    await editor.open(DIAGRAM_ID)

    expect(editor.canUndo.value).toBe(false)
  })

  it('takes back several removals at once', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })

    editor.removeMany([A.id, B.id])
    expect(ids(editor)).toEqual([])
    editor.undo()

    expect(ids(editor)).toEqual([A.id, B.id])
  })
})

describe('useDiagram — arranging a selection', () => {
  it('aligns boxes on their left edge and takes it back in one step', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    editor.place(B, { x: 300, y: 100 })

    editor.align([A.id, B.id], 'left')
    expect(editor.boxes.value.map((box) => box.x)).toEqual([10, 10])
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.boxes.value.map((box) => box.x)).toEqual([10, 300])
  })

  it('records nothing when the arrangement changes nothing', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()

    editor.align([A.id], 'left')

    expect(editor.canUndo.value).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/features/diagrams/useSelection.ts` :

```ts
// Which boxes are selected on the canvas.
//
// The *one* element whose detail is shown stays in the URL (`?element=`,
// docs/adr/0011); a set of boxes picked to be moved, aligned or removed
// together is a working state of this editing session and is not shared.
import { ref } from 'vue'

export function useSelection() {
  const ids = ref<string[]>([])

  function set(next: string[]): void {
    ids.value = [...new Set(next)]
  }

  function toggle(id: string): void {
    ids.value = ids.value.includes(id) ? ids.value.filter((other) => other !== id) : [...ids.value, id]
  }

  function clear(): void {
    ids.value = []
  }

  return { ids, set, toggle, clear }
}
```

1. Remplacer la ligne d'import `import { DEFAULT_BOX, type Point, type Size } from '../../lib/diagramGeometry'` par :

```ts
import {
  DEFAULT_BOX,
  alignBoxes,
  distributeBoxes,
  type AlignMode,
  type DistributeAxis,
  type Moved,
  type Placed,
  type Point,
  type Size,
} from '../../lib/diagramGeometry'
import { createHistory } from '../../lib/diagramHistory'
```

2. Après `const saveError = ref('')`, ajouter :

```ts
  // Undo and redo (docs/adr: historique côté SPA). Memory only, per editing
  // session: `open` and `close` empty it. A command restores the *boxes* and
  // asks for a save; the graph (a relationship created from a link) is not
  // part of it — taking one back is `DELETE /relationships`, offered by hand.
  const canUndo = ref(false)
  const canRedo = ref(false)
  const history = createHistory({
    onChange: () => {
      canUndo.value = history.canUndo()
      canRedo.value = history.canRedo()
    },
  })
  const copy = (list: DiagramNode[]): DiagramNode[] => list.map((node) => ({ ...node }))
  /** The boxes as they were when the gesture in progress began. */
  let gestureBefore: DiagramNode[] | null = null

  function record(label: string, before: DiagramNode[], mergeKey?: string): void {
    const after = copy(nodes.value)
    const restoring = (scene: DiagramNode[]) => () => {
      nodes.value = copy(scene)
      persist()
    }
    history.record({
      label,
      undo: restoring(before),
      do: restoring(after),
      ...(mergeKey ? { mergeKey } : {}),
    })
  }
```

3. Dans `open`, dans le callback `(answer) => { … }` après `relationships.value = answer.relationships`, ajouter `gestureBefore = null` puis `history.clear()`. Dans `close`, après `relationships.value = []`, ajouter les deux mêmes lignes.

4. Remplacer `place`, `move`, `resize` et `remove` (de `function place` à la fin de `function remove`) par :

```ts
  function place(element: ElementRead, at: Point): void {
    if (placed.value.has(element.id)) {
      return
    }
    const before = copy(nodes.value)
    nodes.value.push({ element_id: element.id, x: at.x, y: at.y, ...DEFAULT_BOX })
    if (!byId.value.has(element.id)) {
      elements.value.push(element)
    }
    record('Ajouter un élément', before)
    persist()
  }

  /** Move a box on screen only; `endGesture` once the gesture is over. */
  function move(elementId: string, to: Point): void {
    const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === elementId)
    if (node) {
      gestureBefore ??= copy(nodes.value)
      node.x = to.x
      node.y = to.y
    }
  }

  /** Resize a box on screen only; `endGesture` once the gesture is over. */
  function resize(elementId: string, size: Size): void {
    const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === elementId)
    if (node) {
      gestureBefore ??= copy(nodes.value)
      node.width = size.width
      node.height = size.height
    }
  }

  /**
   * The gesture that `move` and `resize` were part of is over: one history
   * entry for all of it, then the save. Arrow-key nudges of the same boxes
   * fold into one entry (`source: 'keyboard'`).
   */
  function endGesture(source?: 'keyboard'): void {
    const before = gestureBefore
    gestureBefore = null
    if (before) {
      const changed = nodes.value.filter((node) => {
        const old = before.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === node.element_id)
        return (
          !old ||
          old.x !== node.x ||
          old.y !== node.y ||
          old.width !== node.width ||
          old.height !== node.height
        )
      })
      if (changed.length > 0) {
        const key =
          source === 'keyboard'
            ? `keyboard:${changed.map((node) => node.element_id).sort().join(',')}`
            : undefined
        record('Déplacer ou redimensionner', before, key)
      }
    }
    persist()
  }

  /** Take boxes off the diagram. The elements themselves are never deleted. */
  function removeMany(elementIds: string[]): void {
    const before = copy(nodes.value)
    nodes.value = nodes.value.filter((node) => !elementIds.includes(node.element_id))
    record('Retirer du diagramme', before)
    persist()
  }

  function remove(elementId: string): void {
    removeMany([elementId])
  }

  /** Apply the new top-lefts an arrangement computed, as one undoable step. */
  function arrange(label: string, elementIds: string[], compute: (boxes: Placed[]) => Moved[]): void {
    const chosen: Placed[] = nodes.value
      .filter((node) => elementIds.includes(node.element_id))
      .map((node) => ({
        id: node.element_id,
        x: node.x,
        y: node.y,
        width: node.width,
        height: node.height,
      }))
    const before = copy(nodes.value)
    let changed = false
    for (const target of compute(chosen)) {
      const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === target.id)
      if (node && (node.x !== target.x || node.y !== target.y)) {
        node.x = target.x
        node.y = target.y
        changed = true
      }
    }
    if (changed) {
      record(label, before)
      persist()
    }
  }

  function align(elementIds: string[], mode: AlignMode): void {
    arrange('Aligner', elementIds, (boxes) => alignBoxes(boxes, mode))
  }

  function distribute(elementIds: string[], axis: DistributeAxis): void {
    arrange('Répartir', elementIds, (boxes) => distributeBoxes(boxes, axis))
  }

  function undo(): void {
    history.undo()
  }

  function redo(): void {
    history.redo()
  }
```

5. Dans l'objet retourné, ajouter après `remove,` : `removeMany, endGesture, align, distribute, undo, redo, canUndo, canRedo,`.

```ts
describe('DiagramCanvas — a selection of several boxes', () => {
  const both = [A.id, B.id]

  it('toggles a box with shift+click instead of selecting it alone', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Facturation/ }), { shiftKey: true })

    expect(rendered.emitted().toggle).toEqual([[A.id]])
    expect(rendered.emitted().select).toBeUndefined()
  })

    const rendered = draw()

    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointerdown', 250, 90))
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointermove', 50, -10))
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointerup', 50, -10))

    expect(rendered.emitted()['select-many']).toEqual([[[A.id]]])
  })

    await key('ArrowLeft', { altKey: true, shiftKey: true })

})
```

Dans `DiagramCanvas.vue`, partie `<script setup>` :

1. Imports : ajouter `boxesIn, nudgeDelta, rubberBand, shortenToWidth, MIN_BOX` à l'import de `'../../lib/diagramGeometry'` ; **supprimer** la constante locale `CHARACTER_WIDTH` et la fonction locale `shorten`, et dans le template remplacer chaque appel `shorten(` par `shortenToWidth(`.

2. Remplacer `defineProps`/`defineEmits` par :

```ts
const props = defineProps<{
  boxes: Box[]
  relationships: RelationshipRead[]
  /** The element `?element=` names, or ''. */
  selectedId: string
  /** Every box selected, `selectedId` included; omitted, the selection is `selectedId` alone. */
  selection?: string[]
  typeLabel: (value: string) => string
  /** A reader's canvas: boxes can be selected, never dropped, moved, linked or removed. */
  readonly?: boolean
}>()

const emit = defineEmits<{
  place: [elementId: string, at: Point]
  move: [elementId: string, to: Point]
  resize: [elementId: string, size: Size]
  /** A move or resize gesture ended: the moment to save. */
  moved: [source?: 'keyboard']
  select: [elementId: string]
  toggle: [elementId: string]
  'select-many': [elementIds: string[]]
  remove: [elementId: string]
  'remove-many': [elementIds: string[]]
  connect: [sourceId: string, targetId: string]
}>()

/** A band shorter than this is a click on the background, not a selection. */
const BAND_SLOP = 4
```

3. Remplacer le type `Gesture` et les computed `selected` par :

```ts
type Grab = { elementId: string; offset: Point }
type Gesture =
  | { kind: 'move'; grabs: Grab[]; moved: boolean }
  | { kind: 'resize'; elementId: string; offset: Point; moved: boolean }
  | { kind: 'link'; sourceId: string; to: Point }
  | { kind: 'band'; from: Point; to: Point }
const gesture = ref<Gesture | null>(null)

const chosen = computed(
  () => new Set([...(props.selection ?? []), ...(props.selectedId ? [props.selectedId] : [])]),
)
const several = computed(() => chosen.value.size > 1)
```
et, à la place de l'ancien `const selected = computed(() => byId.value.get(props.selectedId))` :

```ts
/** The link and resize handles belong to a lone selected box, never to a set. */
const selected = computed(() => (several.value ? undefined : byId.value.get(props.selectedId)))
const rectOf = (box: Box) => ({ id: box.element.id, x: box.x, y: box.y, width: box.width, height: box.height })
```

4. Remplacer `grab` :

```ts
function grab(event: PointerEvent, box: Box): void {
  if (event.button !== 0 || props.readonly || event.shiftKey) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  const pointer = at(event)
  const members = chosen.value.has(box.element.id)
    ? props.boxes.filter((candidate) => chosen.value.has(candidate.element.id))
    : [box]
  gesture.value = {
    kind: 'move',
    grabs: members.map((member) => ({
      elementId: member.element.id,
      offset: { x: pointer.x - member.x, y: pointer.y - member.y },
    })),
    moved: false,
  }
}

/** A drag that starts on the empty canvas draws a selection band. */
function startBackground(event: PointerEvent): void {
  if (event.button !== 0 || event.target !== event.currentTarget) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  const start = at(event)
  gesture.value = { kind: 'band', from: start, to: start }
}
```

5. Dans `drag`, après la branche `link` (avant `current.moved = true`), ajouter :

```ts
  if (current.kind === 'band') {
    current.to = pointer
    return
  }
```
et remplacer le `emit('move', current.elementId, {...})` final par :

```ts
  for (const held of current.grabs) {
    // ponytail: each box is clamped to the canvas on its own, so a group
    // dragged against the left edge squeezes together; clamp the group's
    // bounding box instead if that is ever noticed.
    emit('move', held.elementId, {
      x: Math.max(0, pointer.x - held.offset.x),
      y: Math.max(0, pointer.y - held.offset.y),
    })
  }
```

6. Dans `interrupt`, remplacer la condition par `if (current && current.kind !== 'link' && current.kind !== 'band' && current.moved)`. Dans `release`, **avant** le bloc `if (current && current.kind !== 'link')`, insérer :

```ts
  if (current?.kind === 'band') {
    const band = rubberBand(current.from, current.to)
    const hits = band.width + band.height < BAND_SLOP ? [] : boxesIn(band, props.boxes.map(rectOf))
    emit('select-many', hits)
    return
  }
```
et remplacer `if (current && current.kind !== 'link')` par `if (current && current.kind !== 'link')` (inchangé : `band` est déjà sorti).

7. Remplacer `removeIfSelected` et ajouter les gestionnaires clavier :

```ts
function removeIfSelected(box: Box): void {
  if (props.readonly || !chosen.value.has(box.element.id)) {
    return
  }
  if (several.value) {
    emit('remove-many', [...chosen.value])
  } else {
    emit('remove', box.element.id)
  }
}

function onClick(event: MouseEvent, box: Box): void {
  if (event.shiftKey) {
    emit('toggle', box.element.id)
  } else {
    emit('select', box.element.id)
  }
}

/** Arrows nudge (Shift = 10 px); Alt+arrows resize. Every gesture has a keyboard twin. */
function onNodeKey(event: KeyboardEvent, box: Box): void {
  const delta = nudgeDelta(event.key, event.shiftKey)
  if (!delta || props.readonly) {
    return
  }
  event.preventDefault()
  if (event.altKey) {
    emit('resize', box.element.id, {
      width: Math.max(MIN_BOX.width, box.width + delta.x),
      height: Math.max(MIN_BOX.height, box.height + delta.y),
    })
    emit('moved', 'keyboard')
    return
  }
  const targets = chosen.value.has(box.element.id)
    ? props.boxes.filter((candidate) => chosen.value.has(candidate.element.id))
    : [box]
  for (const target of targets) {
    emit('move', target.element.id, {
      x: Math.max(0, target.x + delta.x),
      y: Math.max(0, target.y + delta.y),
    })
  }
  emit('moved', 'keyboard')
}

function onCanvasKey(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
  if ((event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey) && event.key.toLowerCase() === 'a') {
    event.preventDefault()
    emit('select-many', props.boxes.map((box) => box.element.id))
  }
}
```

Template : sur `<svg>` ajouter `tabindex="0"`, `@pointerdown="startBackground"` et `@keydown="onCanvasKey"`. Sur `<g class="canvas__node">` : remplacer `:class="{ 'canvas__node--selected': box.element.id === selectedId }"` par `:class="{ 'canvas__node--selected': chosen.has(box.element.id) }"`, `:aria-pressed="chosen.has(box.element.id)"`, `@click="onClick($event, box)"`, ajouter `@keydown="onNodeKey($event, box)"` (garder les deux `@keydown.enter` et `@keydown.delete` existants). Avant la poignée de lien, ajouter :

```vue

          v-if="gesture?.kind === 'band'"
          class="canvas__band-rect"
          :x="Math.min(gesture.from.x, gesture.to.x)"
          :y="Math.min(gesture.from.y, gesture.to.y)"
          :width="Math.abs(gesture.to.x - gesture.from.x)"
          :height="Math.abs(gesture.to.y - gesture.from.y)"
        />
```

```css

  stroke: var(--accent, #6b4fbb);
  stroke-dasharray: 4 3;
  pointer-events: none;
}
```

```ts
describe('DiagramCanvas — zoom and pan', () => {
  const widthOf = () => Number(svg().getAttribute('width'))
  const originOf = () => svg().getAttribute('viewBox')!.split(' ').map(Number)

  it('zooms with the wheel, around the cursor', async () => {
    draw()

    await fireEvent.wheel(svg(), { deltaY: -100, clientX: 100, clientY: 50 })

    expect(widthOf()).toBeCloseTo(880, 1)
    const [panX, panY] = originOf()
    expect(panX).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(panY).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('zooms out with the wheel the other way, never below the floor', async () => {
    draw()

    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {
      await fireEvent.wheel(svg(), { deltaY: 100 })
    }

    expect(widthOf()).toBeCloseTo(800 * 0.4, 1)
  })

  it('fits every box in the view, then goes back to 100 %', async () => {
    draw({ boxes: [{ ...BOXES[0], width: 1600 }] })

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ajuster à la vue' }))
    const fitted = widthOf()
    expect(fitted).toBeLessThan(1600 + 160)

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: '100 %' }))
    expect(widthOf()).toBe(1760)
    expect(originOf().slice(0, 2)).toEqual([0, 0])
  })

  it('pans with the middle button', async () => {

    await fireEvent(svg(), new MouseEvent('pointerdown', { clientX: 100, clientY: 100, button: 1, bubbles: true }))
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointermove', 60, 80))
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointerup', 60, 80))

    expect(originOf().slice(0, 2)).toEqual([40, 20])
  })

  it('pans with Space held and a drag, and not with a plain drag (that draws a band)', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()

    await fireEvent.keyDown(svg(), { key: ' ' })
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointerdown', 100, 100))
    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointermove', 90, 100))

    const drop = pointer('drop', 330, 220)

})
```

Dans le `<script setup>` de `DiagramCanvas.vue` :

1. Imports : ajouter `MIN_CANVAS, clampZoom, fitView, zoomAt, type View` à l'import de `'../../lib/diagramGeometry'` ; supprimer les constantes locales `ZOOM_RANGE` et `ZOOM_STEP` remplacées par `const ZOOM_STEP = 0.25` et `const WHEEL_STEP = 1.1` (garder `ZOOM_STEP`).

2. Remplacer `const zoom = ref(1)` par :

```ts
const view = ref<View>({ zoom: 1, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } })
const zoom = computed(() => view.value.zoom)
const area = useTemplateRef<HTMLDivElement>('area')
/** True while the space bar is down: a drag then pans instead of drawing a band. */
const spaceHeld = ref(false)
```

3. Remplacer `step` et `at` :

```ts
function step(by: number): void {
  view.value = { ...view.value, zoom: clampZoom(view.value.zoom + by) }
}

/** Where the pointer is, in canvas units. */
function at(event: MouseEvent): Point {
  const rect = svg.value?.getBoundingClientRect()
  return toCanvasPoint(
    { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY },
    { x: rect?.left ?? 0, y: rect?.top ?? 0 },
    view.value.zoom,
    view.value.pan,
  )
}

function onWheel(event: WheelEvent): void {
  const rect = svg.value?.getBoundingClientRect()
  const anchor = { x: event.clientX - (rect?.left ?? 0), y: event.clientY - (rect?.top ?? 0) }
  view.value = zoomAt(view.value, event.deltaY < 0 ? WHEEL_STEP : 1 / WHEEL_STEP, anchor)
}

function fit(): void {
  const viewport = {
    width: area.value?.clientWidth || MIN_CANVAS.width,
    height: area.value?.clientHeight || MIN_CANVAS.height,
  }
  view.value = fitView(props.boxes.map(rectOf), viewport)
}

function actualSize(): void {
  view.value = { zoom: 1, pan: { x: 0, y: 0 } }
}

function onCanvasKeyUp(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
  if (event.key === ' ') {
    spaceHeld.value = false
  }
}
```

4. Dans le type `Gesture`, ajouter `| { kind: 'pan'; start: Point; origin: Point }`. Dans `onCanvasKey` (Task 5), ajouter au début : `if (event.key === ' ') { event.preventDefault(); spaceHeld.value = true; return }`. Dans `grab`, ajouter `|| spaceHeld.value` à la condition de sortie. Remplacer `startBackground` par :

```ts
function startBackground(event: PointerEvent): void {
  const panning = event.button === 1 || (event.button === 0 && spaceHeld.value)
  if (panning) {
    capture(event)
    gesture.value = {
      kind: 'pan',
      start: { x: event.clientX, y: event.clientY },
      origin: view.value.pan,
    }
    return
  }
  if (event.button !== 0 || event.target !== event.currentTarget) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  const start = at(event)
  gesture.value = { kind: 'band', from: start, to: start }
}
```

5. Dans `drag`, **avant** `const pointer = at(event)`, ajouter :

```ts
  if (current.kind === 'pan') {
    view.value = {
      ...view.value,
      pan: {
        x: current.origin.x - (event.clientX - current.start.x) / view.value.zoom,
        y: current.origin.y - (event.clientY - current.start.y) / view.value.zoom,
      },
    }
    return
  }
```
Dans `release`, ajouter en tête (après `gesture.value = null`) : `if (current?.kind === 'pan') { return }`. Dans `interrupt`, étendre la condition d'émission : ajouter `&& current.kind !== 'pan'`.

6. Template : sur `.canvas__zoom` ajouter après le bouton `Zoomer` :

```vue
        <button type="button" aria-label="Ajuster à la vue" @click="fit">Ajuster</button>
        <button type="button" aria-label="100 %" @click="actualSize">100 %</button>
```
Sur `<div class="canvas__area">` ajouter `ref="area"`. Sur `<svg>` : remplacer `:viewBox="`0 0 ${extent.width} ${extent.height}`"` par `:viewBox="`${view.pan.x} ${view.pan.y} ${extent.width} ${extent.height}`"`, remplacer `extent.width * zoom` / `extent.height * zoom` par `extent.width * view.zoom` / `extent.height * view.zoom` (la variable `zoom` reste valable), et ajouter `@wheel.prevent="onWheel"` et `@keyup="onCanvasKeyUp"`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

`frontend/tests/DiagramToolbar.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import DiagramToolbar from '../src/features/diagrams/DiagramToolbar.vue'

function draw(props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return render(DiagramToolbar, {
    props: { canWrite: true, canUndo: true, canRedo: false, selectionCount: 3, ...props },
  })
}

describe('DiagramToolbar', () => {
  it('offers undo and redo, enabled by what the history holds', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Annuler' })).toBeEnabled()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Rétablir' })).toBeDisabled()
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Annuler' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().undo).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('aligns with two boxes or more, distributes with three or more', () => {
    draw({ selectionCount: 2 })

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aligner à gauche' })).toBeEnabled()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Répartir horizontalement' })).toBeDisabled()
  })

  it('emits the alignment and the export format asked for', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Aligner à droite' }))
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Exporter en SVG' }))
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Exporter en PNG 2x' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().align).toEqual([['right']])
    expect(rendered.emitted().export).toEqual([['svg'], ['png2']])
  })

  it('shows a reader the export and nothing that changes the diagram', () => {
    draw({ canWrite: false })

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Exporter en SVG' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Annuler' })).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Aligner/ })).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// The editor's tool strip: take back, arrange a selection, save as an image.

// It decides nothing — it names the gesture the user chose. Everything that

// docs/adr/0032); exporting only reads, so a reader keeps it.

  canUndo: boolean

  align: [mode: AlignMode]
  distribute: [axis: DistributeAxis]
  export: [format: ExportFormat]

]
```

3. Après `onMounted(async () => { await metamodel.load() })`, remplacer ce bloc par :

```ts
onMounted(async () => {
  window.addEventListener('keydown', onShortcut)
  await metamodel.load()
})

onBeforeUnmount(() => {
  window.removeEventListener('keydown', onShortcut)
})

/** Ctrl/Cmd+Z undoes, +Shift or Y redoes — never while typing in a field, never for a reader. */
function onShortcut(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
  if (!canWrite.value || !(event.ctrlKey || event.metaKey)) {
    return
  }
  const target = event.target as HTMLElement | null
  if (target && ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(target.tagName)) {
    return
  }
  const key = event.key.toLowerCase()
  if (key === 'z' && event.shiftKey) {
    event.preventDefault()
    editor.redo()
  } else if (key === 'z') {
    event.preventDefault()
    editor.undo()
  } else if (key === 'y') {
    event.preventDefault()
    editor.redo()
  }
}
```

4. Dans le `watch(diagramId …)`, au début du callback ajouter `selection.clear()`. Après `function onConnect`, ajouter :

```ts
function onSelect(elementId: string): void {
  selection.set([elementId])
  select(elementId)
}

function onSelectMany(ids: string[]): void {
  selection.set(ids)
  if (ids.length === 1) {
    select(ids[0])
  } else {
    deselect()
  }
}

function removeSelected(ids: string[]): void {
  editor.removeMany(ids)
  selection.clear()
  deselect()
}

function arrange(mode: AlignMode): void {
  editor.align(selection.ids.value, mode)
}

function spread(axis: DistributeAxis): void {
  editor.distribute(selection.ids.value, axis)
}

async function exportAs(format: ExportFormat): Promise<void> {
  exportError.value = ''
  const scene = buildExportScene(editor.boxes.value, editor.relationships.value, metamodel.labelOf)
  const svg = serializeSvg(scene)
  const name = (editor.diagram.value?.name ?? 'diagramme').replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}_-]+/gu, '-')
  try {
    if (format === 'svg') {
      download(new Blob([svg], { type: 'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8' }), `${name}.svg`)
    } else {
      download(await rasterize(svg, exportSize(scene), format === 'png2' ? 2 : 1), `${name}.png`)
    }
  } catch (caught) {
    exportError.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}
```
(`select` et `deselect` sont les fonctions déjà présentes dans le fichier ; `messageOf` est déjà importé.) Si `removeBox` existe déjà et appelle `editor.remove`, le laisser tel quel.

`docs/adr/00NN-historique-export-et-auto-layout-cote-spa.md` (adapter `NN` partout) :

````markdown
---
titre: Historique, export et auto-layout du constructeur de diagrammes, côté SPA
date: 2026-10-10
statut: Accepté
affects: frontend/src/lib/diagramGeometry.ts, frontend/src/lib/diagramHistory.ts, frontend/src/lib/diagramExport.ts, frontend/src/lib/autoLayout.ts, frontend/src/features/diagrams/useDiagram.ts, frontend/src/features/diagrams/DiagramCanvas.vue
---

# NN. Historique, export et auto-layout côté SPA

Date : 2026-10-10
Statut : Accepté

## Contexte

[`0031`](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md) et [`0035`](0035-taille-des-boites-de-diagramme.md) donnent un
constructeur qui pose des boîtes, les déplace, les redimensionne et enregistre après chaque geste.
Un architecte attend davantage : sélectionner plusieurs boîtes, zoomer et se déplacer sur la toile,
revenir en arrière, aligner, obtenir une image à joindre à un document, et — pour les vues générées —
une mise en page automatique. Chacun de ces besoins tente une dépendance (une bibliothèque de diagrammes,
une bibliothèque d'annulation, `html2canvas`, `dagre`). [`0010`](0010-dessiner-le-voisinage-d-un-element.md)
et [`0013`](0013-ecran-d-analyse-d-impact.md) ont déjà posé la règle : le graphe se dessine à la main,
sur des modules purs testés sans monter de composant.

## Décision

**Tout cela s'écrit dans `frontend/src/lib/`, en fonctions pures testées sans DOM, et n'ajoute aucune
dépendance.**

| Sujet | Choix |
|---|---|
| Géométrie | `diagramGeometry.ts` : zoom centré sous le curseur (`zoomAt`), pan, rectangle de sélection, alignement et répartition. Le zoom reste dans [0,4 ; 2] |
| Historique | `diagramHistory.ts` : pile de commandes `{do, undo}` déjà appliquées, plafond 100, fusion des micro-gestes par clé (flèches du clavier), un glisser = une entrée |
| Portée de l'annulation | Les boîtes (et, plus tard, notes, groupes, tracés). **Pas le graphe** : une relation créée depuis un lien est un fait, `DELETE /relationships` la retire, explicitement |
| Durée de vie | Mémoire de la session d'édition ; vidée à l'ouverture d'un diagramme et après un conflit de révision. Un historique rechargé annulerait la disposition d'un collègue |
| Annuler et enregistrer | Annuler restaure l'état local puis demande l'enregistrement habituel (anti-rebond) : il n'existe pas de « défaire côté serveur » |
| Export | `serializeSvg` fabrique un SVG autonome (palette claire fixe, styles en ligne, texte échappé, couleurs `#rrggbb` validées, ni `<script>` ni lien externe). Le PNG est ce SVG dessiné sur un `<canvas>` (1x/2x) |
| Auto-layout | `autoLayout.ts` (lot des vues générées) : rangées par couche ArchiMate, tri par barycentre, déterministe ; il ne partage avec les anneaux du voisinage que `BOX_WIDTH`/`BOX_HEIGHT` |
| Clavier | Chaque geste a un équivalent : flèches ±1 px (Maj ±10), Alt+flèches redimensionnent, Ctrl/Cmd+A, Suppr, Ctrl+Z / Ctrl+Maj+Z |

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Une bibliothèque de diagrammes (JointJS, React Flow, Cytoscape) | Sélection, zoom, annulation et routage fournis | Dépendance lourde, modèle de données propre qui concurrence celui des vues ; contredit 0010/0013 | Écarté |
| `html2canvas` ou copie du DOM pour l'export | Aucun code de sérialisation | Rend la page telle que le thème sombre la colore, polices et variables CSS incluses : une image qui change avec le visiteur | Écarté |
| Annulation par instantané du serveur (versions) | Survit au rechargement | Un historique de versions est le sujet d'un autre sous-projet ; ici le besoin est le droit à l'erreur de la séance | Écarté |
| Auto-layout par force (type `d3-force`) | Beau sur un graphe quelconque | Non déterministe, dépendance ; ArchiMate a une lecture en couches que le dessin doit montrer | Écarté |
| Annuler aussi les relations créées | Un seul « Annuler » | Une annulation silencieuse d'un fait du graphe, vu par les autres éditeurs et par l'analyse d'impact | Écarté |

## Conséquences

- **Le PNG dépend du navigateur** (`canvas`, polices) ; le SVG est la référence. `rasterize` n'est pas
  testable sous jsdom : il est couvert par un module simulé dans les specs de la section.
- **L'historique est borné à 100 entrées** et perdu au rechargement : c'est voulu.
- **Un conflit de révision vide l'historique** : l'état local « recollé » repart d'une base neuve.
- **Pas de Playwright** : les parcours (annuler, exporter) sont couverts par des composants Vitest.
- Les modules de `lib/` n'importent rien de `features/` : la scène d'export est construite côté
  `features/diagrams/exportScene.ts`, qui connaît les couleurs de couche et les verbes de relation.

## Références

- ADR liés : [0010](0010-dessiner-le-voisinage-d-un-element.md), [0013](0013-ecran-d-analyse-d-impact.md),
  [0031](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md), [0035](0035-taille-des-boites-de-diagramme.md)
- Code concerné : `frontend/src/lib/diagramGeometry.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/diagramHistory.ts`,
  `frontend/src/lib/diagramExport.ts`, `frontend/src/features/diagrams/useDiagram.ts`
````
