### Task 9: Domaine — objets de vue (`diagram_objects.py`) et `check_scene`

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_objects.py`
- Create: `backend/tests/unit/test_diagram_objects.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DiagramNode`, `check_layout` de `ea.domain.diagrams` (inchangés dans cette tâche).
- Produces (Python) :
  - constantes `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH = 5`, `MAX_WAYPOINTS = 20`, `MAX_OBJECT_TEXT = 2000`
  - `class ObjectKind(StrEnum): NOTE = "note"; GROUP = "group"`
  - `@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True) Point(x: float, y: float)`
  - `DiagramObject(id: UUID, kind: ObjectKind, x: float, y: float, width: float, height: float, text: str = "", colour: str | None = None, z: int = 0, parent_id: UUID | None = None, anchor_element_id: UUID | None = None)`
  - `DiagramEdge(relationship_id: UUID, waypoints: tuple[Point, ...] = ())`
  - `DiagramScene(nodes: tuple[DiagramNode, ...] = (), objects: tuple[DiagramObject, ...] = (), edges: tuple[DiagramEdge, ...] = ())`
  - `check_scene(nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode], objects: Sequence[DiagramObject], edges: Sequence[DiagramEdge]) -> DiagramScene` (lève `ValueError` → 422 `invalid_input`)

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

`backend/tests/unit/test_diagram_objects.py` :

```python
"""The rules of a scene: notes, groups and bends — pure, no store."""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.diagram_objects import (
    MAX_GROUP_DEPTH,
    MAX_WAYPOINTS,
    DiagramEdge,
    DiagramObject,
    ObjectKind,
    Point,
    check_scene,
)
from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode


def a_group(parent: UUID | None = None, **overrides: object) -> DiagramObject:
    fields: dict[str, object] = {
        "id": uuid4(),
        "kind": ObjectKind.GROUP,
        "x": 0.0,
        "y": 0.0,
        "width": 300.0,
        "height": 200.0,
        "text": "Domaine Paiement",
        "parent_id": parent,
    }
    return DiagramObject(**{**fields, **overrides})  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def a_note(parent: UUID | None = None, **overrides: object) -> DiagramObject:
    return a_group(parent, **{"kind": ObjectKind.NOTE, "text": "À revoir", **overrides})


def a_chain(length: int) -> list[DiagramObject]:
    """`length` groups, each inside the previous one."""
    chain: list[DiagramObject] = []
    for _ in range(length):
        chain.append(a_group(chain[-1].id if chain else None))
    return chain


def test_an_empty_scene_is_valid() -> None:
    scene = check_scene([], [], [])

    assert (scene.nodes, scene.objects, scene.edges) == ((), (), ())


def test_an_empty_group_is_valid() -> None:
    group = a_group()

    assert check_scene([], [group], []).objects == (group,)


def test_a_box_can_sit_in_a_group_and_a_group_in_a_group() -> None:
    outer = a_group()
    inner = a_group(outer.id)
    element = uuid4()

    scene = check_scene([DiagramNode(element, 5, 5, parent_object_id=inner.id)], [outer, inner], [])

    assert scene.nodes[0].parent_object_id == inner.id


def test_an_element_is_still_placed_once() -> None:
    element = uuid4()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="placed twice"):
        check_scene([DiagramNode(element, 0, 0), DiagramNode(element, 9, 9)], [], [])


def test_two_objects_cannot_share_an_id() -> None:
    note = a_note()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="twice"):
        check_scene([], [note, note], [])


def test_a_group_cannot_be_anchored_to_an_element() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="group"):
        check_scene([], [a_group(anchor_element_id=uuid4())], [])


def test_a_note_is_anchored_to_an_element_that_is_on_the_diagram() -> None:
    element = uuid4()
    note = a_note(anchor_element_id=element)

    assert check_scene([DiagramNode(element, 0, 0)], [note], []).objects == (note,)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="anchored"):
        check_scene([], [note], [])


def test_a_parent_must_exist_and_be_a_group() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="no group"):
        check_scene([], [a_note(parent=uuid4())], [])
    note = a_note()
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="only be placed in a group"):
        check_scene([DiagramNode(uuid4(), 0, 0, parent_object_id=note.id)], [note], [])


def test_a_group_cannot_be_its_own_parent() -> None:
    group = a_group()
    looping = a_group(group.id, id=group.id)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="loop"):
        check_scene([], [looping], [])


def test_two_groups_cannot_contain_each_other() -> None:
    first, second = a_group(), a_group()
    first = a_group(second.id, id=first.id)
    second = a_group(first.id, id=second.id)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="loop"):
        check_scene([], [first, second], [])


def test_groups_nest_five_deep_and_no_deeper() -> None:
    chain = a_chain(MAX_GROUP_DEPTH)
    assert check_scene([], chain, []).objects == tuple(chain)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="deep"):
        check_scene([], [*chain, a_group(chain[-1].id)], [])


def test_a_note_may_sit_in_the_deepest_group() -> None:
    chain = a_chain(MAX_GROUP_DEPTH)

    assert len(check_scene([], [*chain, a_note(chain[-1].id)], []).objects) == MAX_GROUP_DEPTH + 1


def test_a_link_is_routed_once_with_a_bounded_number_of_bends() -> None:
    relationship = uuid4()
    bends = tuple(Point(float(i), 0.0) for i in range(MAX_WAYPOINTS))

    assert check_scene([], [], [DiagramEdge(relationship, bends)]).edges[0].waypoints == bends
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="bends"):
        check_scene([], [], [DiagramEdge(relationship, (*bends, Point(0, 0)))])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="routed twice"):
        check_scene([], [], [DiagramEdge(relationship), DiagramEdge(relationship)])


def test_a_note_longer_than_the_bound_is_refused() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="characters"):
        check_scene([], [a_note(text="x" * 2001)], [])
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DiagramNode:
    """One box: an element, the top-left corner it is drawn at, and its size.

    `parent_object_id` names the group (docs/adr: objets de vue) the box is
    drawn inside, `z` its stacking order. Both are presentation: a group is not
    an element and creates no relationship.
    """

    x: float
    y: float

    height: float = DEFAULT_BOX_HEIGHT
```

`backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_objects.py` :

```python
"""What a diagram draws that is not an element: notes, groups, and the bends of a link.

All of it is presentation (docs/adr: objets de vue). A **group is not an
element of the graph and never creates a relationship**: putting three boxes in
"Domaine Paiement" says "visually together" and nothing else. A note anchored
to an element is a dotted line, not a relationship. A link keeps being the
relationship of the graph; only its stroke — the bends — is the view's.

Nothing here imports a framework or a driver, per `CLAUDE.md`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Final
from uuid import UUID

from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode, check_layout

#: How many groups may contain one another — a note may sit in the deepest.
MAX_GROUP_DEPTH: Final = 5
#: Bends a single link may carry.
MAX_WAYPOINTS: Final = 20
MAX_OBJECT_TEXT: Final = 2000


class ObjectKind(StrEnum):
    NOTE = "note"
    GROUP = "group"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Point:
    x: float
    y: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DiagramObject:
    """A note (its text is the body) or a group (its text is the title)."""

    id: UUID
    kind: ObjectKind
    x: float
    y: float
    width: float
    height: float
    text: str = ""
    colour: str | None = None
    z: int = 0
    parent_id: UUID | None = None
    anchor_element_id: UUID | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DiagramEdge:
    """The stroke of a link whose route was changed. No row = a straight segment."""

    relationship_id: UUID
    waypoints: tuple[Point, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DiagramScene:
    nodes: tuple[DiagramNode, ...] = ()
    objects: tuple[DiagramObject, ...] = ()
    edges: tuple[DiagramEdge, ...] = ()


def _groups_above(
    start: UUID | None, by_id: Mapping[UUID, DiagramObject], *, owner: UUID | None
) -> int:
    """How many groups lie between `start` and the top; refuses a loop or a non-group."""
    depth = 0
    seen: set[UUID] = set()
    current = start
    while current is not None:
        if current in seen or current == owner:
            msg = "groups cannot contain one another in a loop"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        seen.add(current)
        parent = by_id.get(current)
        if parent is None:
            msg = f"no group {current} on the diagram"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if parent.kind is not ObjectKind.GROUP:
            msg = "an object can only be placed in a group"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        depth += 1
        current = parent.parent_id
    return depth


def check_scene(
    nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode],
    objects: Sequence[DiagramObject],
    edges: Sequence[DiagramEdge],
) -> DiagramScene:
    """The rules of a scene that need no store: each concept once, groups that nest sanely.

    Whether the elements and the relationships exist is the service's question.
    """
    layout = check_layout(nodes)
    by_id: dict[UUID, DiagramObject] = {}
    for item in objects:
        if item.id in by_id:
            msg = f"object {item.id} appears twice on the diagram"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if len(item.text) > MAX_OBJECT_TEXT:
            msg = f"the text of an object is at most {MAX_OBJECT_TEXT} characters"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if item.kind is ObjectKind.GROUP and item.anchor_element_id is not None:
            msg = "a group cannot be anchored to an element"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        by_id[item.id] = item
    placed = {node.element_id for node in layout}
    for item in objects:
        if item.anchor_element_id is not None and item.anchor_element_id not in placed:
            msg = f"a note is anchored to {item.anchor_element_id}, which is not on the diagram"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        # A group nested five deep is the limit, so a group itself may have
        # only four groups above it; a note or a box may have five.
        limit = MAX_GROUP_DEPTH - (1 if item.kind is ObjectKind.GROUP else 0)
        if _groups_above(item.parent_id, by_id, owner=item.id) > limit:
            msg = f"groups nest at most {MAX_GROUP_DEPTH} deep"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    for node in layout:
        if _groups_above(node.parent_object_id, by_id, owner=None) > MAX_GROUP_DEPTH:
            msg = f"groups nest at most {MAX_GROUP_DEPTH} deep"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    routed: set[UUID] = set()
    for edge in edges:
        if edge.relationship_id in routed:
            msg = f"relationship {edge.relationship_id} is routed twice"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        routed.add(edge.relationship_id)
        if len(edge.waypoints) > MAX_WAYPOINTS:
            msg = f"a link has at most {MAX_WAYPOINTS} bends"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    return DiagramScene(nodes=layout, objects=tuple(objects), edges=tuple(edges))
```

Les messages d'erreur contiennent les mots recherchés par les tests : `"twice"`, `"group"`, `"anchored"`, `"no group"`, `"only be placed in a group"`, `"loop"`, `"deep"`, `"bends"`, `"routed twice"`, `"characters"`, `"placed twice"` (ce dernier vient de `check_layout`).

```python
async def lay_out(
    service: DiagramService,
    diagram_id: UUID,
    nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode],
    objects: Sequence[DiagramObject] = (),
    edges: Sequence[DiagramEdge] = (),
) -> int:
    """Save a scene on the diagram's *current* revision — the single-writer case."""
    current = await service.get(diagram_id)
    return await service.replace_layout(
        diagram_id, revision=current.revision, nodes=nodes, objects=objects, edges=edges
    )
```

```python
"""The scene use cases: revision, groups, bends — against in-memory stores."""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramEdge, DiagramObject, ObjectKind, Point
from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode
from ea.domain.errors import DiagramRevisionConflictError, NotAuthorisedError
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.diagrams import DiagramService
from tests.conftest import a_reader, lay_out


async def an_element(service: ArchitectureService, name: str) -> object:
    return await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name=name)


def a_group(**overrides: object) -> DiagramObject:
    fields: dict[str, object] = {
        "id": uuid4(),
        "kind": ObjectKind.GROUP,
        "x": 0.0,
        "y": 0.0,
        "width": 300.0,
        "height": 200.0,
        "text": "Domaine",
    }
    return DiagramObject(**{**fields, **overrides})  # type: ignore[arg-type]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestRevision:
    async def test_every_save_moves_the_revision_on_by_one(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")
        assert diagram.revision == 0

        first = await diagram_service.replace_layout(diagram.id, revision=0, nodes=[])
        second = await diagram_service.replace_layout(diagram.id, revision=1, nodes=[])

        assert (first, second) == (1, 2)
        assert (await diagram_service.get(diagram.id)).revision == 2

    async def test_a_stale_revision_is_refused_and_the_winner_is_kept(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        winner = await an_element(service, "Gagnant")
        loser = await an_element(service, "Perdant")
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")
        await diagram_service.replace_layout(
            diagram.id, revision=0, nodes=[DiagramNode(winner.id, 0, 0)]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        )

        with pytest.raises(DiagramRevisionConflictError, match="revision 1"):
            await diagram_service.replace_layout(
                diagram.id, revision=0, nodes=[DiagramNode(loser.id, 0, 0)]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            )

        opened = await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)
        assert [node.element_id for node in opened.nodes] == [winner.id]  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        assert opened.diagram.revision == 1

    async def test_a_reader_cannot_save_a_scene(self, diagram_service: DiagramService) -> None:
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")

        with acting_as(a_reader()), pytest.raises(NotAuthorisedError):
            await diagram_service.replace_layout(diagram.id, revision=0, nodes=[])


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestTheScene:
    async def test_groups_notes_and_bends_come_back_as_saved(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        source = await an_element(service, "A")
        target = await an_element(service, "B")
        link = await service.connect(
            relationship_type=RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION,
            source_id=source.id,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            target_id=target.id,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        )
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")
        group = a_group()
        note = a_group(id=uuid4(), kind=ObjectKind.NOTE, text="Voir ADR", anchor_element_id=source.id)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        edge = DiagramEdge(link.id, (Point(50, 80), Point(90, 40)))

        await lay_out(
            diagram_service,
            diagram.id,
            [
                DiagramNode(source.id, 10, 10, parent_object_id=group.id),  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
                DiagramNode(target.id, 400, 10),  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            ],
            [group, note],
            [edge],
        )
        opened = await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)

        assert {obj.id for obj in opened.objects} == {group.id, note.id}
        assert opened.edges == (edge,)
        assert opened.nodes[0].parent_object_id == group.id or opened.nodes[1].parent_object_id == group.id

    async def test_removing_a_group_with_its_children_lifted_keeps_them_where_they_are_put(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        element = await an_element(service, "A")
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")
        outer = a_group()
        inner = a_group(parent_id=outer.id)
        await lay_out(
            diagram_service,
            diagram.id,
            [DiagramNode(element.id, 5, 5, parent_object_id=inner.id)],  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            [outer, inner],
        )

        # The editor lifts the box to the grandparent when it removes `inner`.
        await lay_out(
            diagram_service,
            diagram.id,
            [DiagramNode(element.id, 5, 5, parent_object_id=outer.id)],  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            [outer],
        )
        opened = await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)

        assert [obj.id for obj in opened.objects] == [outer.id]
        assert opened.nodes[0].parent_object_id == outer.id
        assert len(opened.graph.elements) == 1

    async def test_a_route_for_a_link_that_is_not_between_two_placed_elements_is_refused(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        source = await an_element(service, "A")
        target = await an_element(service, "B")
        link = await service.connect(
            relationship_type=RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION,
            source_id=source.id,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
            target_id=target.id,  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
        )
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="between two elements"):
            await lay_out(
                diagram_service,
                diagram.id,
                [DiagramNode(source.id, 0, 0)],  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
                edges=[DiagramEdge(link.id, ())],
            )

    async def test_a_group_loop_is_refused_before_anything_is_stored(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Vente")
        first, second = a_group(), a_group()
        first = a_group(id=first.id, parent_id=second.id)
        second = a_group(id=second.id, parent_id=first.id)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="loop"):
            await lay_out(diagram_service, diagram.id, [], [first, second])

        assert (await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)).objects == ()
```

```python
    async def open(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> DiagramDetail:
        """A diagram with the part of the graph it shows, stale boxes left out."""
        require_caller()
        diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)
        nodes = await self._repository.nodes_of(diagram_id)
        objects = await self._repository.objects_of(diagram_id)
        edges = await self._repository.edges_of(diagram_id)
        graph = await self._architecture.view_of([node.element_id for node in nodes])
        present = {element.id for element in graph.elements}
        linked = {link.id for link in graph.relationships}
        return DiagramDetail(
            diagram=diagram,
            nodes=tuple(node for node in nodes if node.element_id in present),
            graph=graph,
            objects=objects,
            edges=tuple(edge for edge in edges if edge.relationship_id in linked),
        )
```
et `replace_layout` :

```python
    async def replace_layout(
        self,
        diagram_id: UUID,
        *,
        revision: int,
        nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode],
        objects: Sequence[DiagramObject] = (),
        edges: Sequence[DiagramEdge] = (),
    ) -> int:
        """Replace the whole scene of a diagram, against the revision the caller saw.

        A scene that breaks `check_scene` is refused before anything is read;
        an unknown diagram is a 404; an element the graph lacks, or a route for
        a link that does not join two placed elements, is refused; a stale
        `revision` is a conflict. The elements and links are checked in one
        graph query, not one per box. Returns the new revision.
        """
        require_editor()
        scene = check_scene(nodes, objects, edges)
        diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)
        graph = await self._architecture.view_of([node.element_id for node in scene.nodes])
        present = {element.id for element in graph.elements}
        missing = [str(node.element_id) for node in scene.nodes if node.element_id not in present]
        if missing:
            msg = f"no element with id {', '.join(missing)}"
            raise UnknownLayoutElementError(msg)
        joined = {link.id for link in graph.relationships}
        stray = [str(edge.relationship_id) for edge in scene.edges if edge.relationship_id not in joined]
        if stray:
            msg = f"no relationship between two elements of the diagram: {', '.join(stray)}"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        stored = await self._repository.replace_layout(
            diagram_id, scene, revision=revision, now=self._now()
        )
        if stored is None:
            raise _not_found(diagram_id)  # deleted between the read and the write
        # Counts only: the text of a note is the author's, and may be sensitive.
        logger.info(
            "diagram %r saved at revision %d",
            diagram.name,
            stored,
            extra={
                "action": "diagram_scene_replaced",
                "diagram_id": str(diagram_id),
                "revision": stored,
                "nodes": len(scene.nodes),
                "objects": len(scene.objects),
                "edges": len(scene.edges),
            },
        )
        return stored
```

```python
    async def replace_layout(
        self, diagram_id: UUID, scene: DiagramScene, *, revision: int, now: datetime
    ) -> int | None:
        stored = self.diagrams.get(diagram_id)
        if stored is None:
            return None
        current = self.revisions.get(diagram_id, 0)
        if revision != current:
            msg = f"the diagram is at revision {current}, not {revision}"
            raise DiagramRevisionConflictError(msg)
        self.nodes[diagram_id] = scene.nodes
        self.objects[diagram_id] = scene.objects
        self.edges[diagram_id] = scene.edges
        self.revisions[diagram_id] = current + 1
        self.diagrams[diagram_id] = stored.touched(now)
        return current + 1
```

```python
def test_a_diagram_object_is_a_note_or_a_group_and_only_a_note_is_anchored() -> None:
    table = Base.metadata.tables["diagram_objects"]
    names = {c.name for c in table.constraints}

    assert {"ck_diagram_objects_kind", "ck_diagram_objects_group_has_no_anchor"} <= names


def test_the_parent_and_the_anchor_let_go_instead_of_deleting() -> None:
    table = Base.metadata.tables["diagram_objects"]

    for column in ("parent_id", "anchor_element_id"):
        key = next(iter(table.c[column].foreign_keys))
        assert key.ondelete == "SET NULL"
    assert next(iter(Base.metadata.tables["diagram_nodes"].c.parent_object_id.foreign_keys)).ondelete == "SET NULL"


def test_a_route_follows_its_relationship_and_its_diagram() -> None:
    table = Base.metadata.tables["diagram_edges"]

    assert [c.name for c in table.primary_key.columns] == ["diagram_id", "relationship_id"]
    for column in ("diagram_id", "relationship_id"):
        assert next(iter(table.c[column].foreign_keys)).ondelete == "CASCADE"


def test_a_diagram_carries_a_revision_that_starts_at_zero() -> None:
    column = Base.metadata.tables["diagrams"].c.revision

    assert column.nullable is False
    assert str(column.server_default.arg) == "0"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
```

`backend/tests/integration/test_diagram_scene_store.py` :

```python
"""The scene tables against a real PostgreSQL: cascades, SET NULL, one transaction, a revision."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest
from alembic import command
from alembic.config import Config
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import (
    DiagramEdge,
    DiagramObject,
    DiagramScene,
    ObjectKind,
    Point,
)
from ea.domain.diagrams import Diagram, DiagramNode
from ea.domain.errors import DiagramRevisionConflictError
from ea.domain.model import Element, Relationship
from ea.repositories.architecture_store import PostgresArchitectureRepository
from ea.repositories.diagram_store import PostgresDiagramRepository

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.postgres, pytest.mark.asyncio]

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def an_element(name: str) -> Element:
    return Element.create(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name=name, now=NOW)


def a_group(**overrides: object) -> DiagramObject:
    fields: dict[str, object] = {
        "id": uuid4(),
        "kind": ObjectKind.GROUP,
        "x": 0.0,
        "y": 0.0,
        "width": 300.0,
        "height": 200.0,
        "text": "Domaine",
    }
    return DiagramObject(**{**fields, **overrides})  # type: ignore[arg-type]


@pytest.fixture
def graph(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresArchitectureRepository:
    return PostgresArchitectureRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


@pytest.fixture
def diagrams(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresDiagramRepository:
    return PostgresDiagramRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


async def a_stored_diagram(diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository) -> Diagram:
    return await diagrams.add(Diagram.create(name=f"Vue {uuid4()}", now=NOW))


async def test_a_scene_round_trips_with_nesting_notes_and_bends(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    source = await graph.add_element(an_element("A"))
    target = await graph.add_element(an_element("B"))
    link = await graph.add_relationship(
        Relationship.between(RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION, source, target, now=NOW)
    )
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    outer = a_group()
    inner = a_group(parent_id=outer.id)
    note = a_group(kind=ObjectKind.NOTE, text="À revoir", anchor_element_id=source.id, colour="#ffe0e0", z=3)
    scene = DiagramScene(
        nodes=(DiagramNode(source.id, 10, 10, parent_object_id=inner.id, z=2), DiagramNode(target.id, 400, 10)),
        objects=(outer, inner, note),
        edges=(DiagramEdge(link.id, (Point(100, 80), Point(250, 60))),),
    )

    revision = await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, scene, revision=0, now=NOW)

    assert revision == 1
    assert {o.id: o for o in await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id)} == {o.id: o for o in scene.objects}
    assert await diagrams.edges_of(diagram.id) == scene.edges
    assert {n.element_id: n for n in await diagrams.nodes_of(diagram.id)} == {n.element_id: n for n in scene.nodes}
    stored = await diagrams.get(diagram.id)
    assert stored is not None and stored.revision == 1


async def test_a_stale_revision_writes_nothing(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    element = await graph.add_element(an_element("A"))
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(nodes=(DiagramNode(element.id, 1, 1),)), revision=0, now=NOW)

    with pytest.raises(DiagramRevisionConflictError, match="revision 1"):
        await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(objects=(a_group(),)), revision=0, now=NOW)

    assert await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id) == ()
    assert len(await diagrams.nodes_of(diagram.id)) == 1


async def test_two_savers_on_one_revision_leave_exactly_one_winner(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository,
) -> None:
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)

    results = await asyncio.gather(
        *(
            diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(objects=(a_group(text=str(i)),)), revision=0, now=NOW)
            for i in range(4)
        ),
        return_exceptions=True,
    )

    assert sum(1 for r in results if r == 1) == 1
    assert sum(1 for r in results if isinstance(r, DiagramRevisionConflictError)) == 3


async def test_a_refused_scene_leaves_the_previous_one_in_place(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository,
) -> None:
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    kept = a_group()
    await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(objects=(kept,)), revision=0, now=NOW)

    with pytest.raises(Exception):  # noqa: B017, PT011 — a foreign key violation, whichever driver type
        await diagrams.replace_layout(
            diagram.id,
            DiagramScene(edges=(DiagramEdge(uuid4(), ()),)),  # a relationship that does not exist
            revision=1,
            now=NOW,
        )

    assert [o.id for o in await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id)] == [kept.id]


async def test_deleting_an_element_takes_its_box_and_frees_its_note_but_not_the_group_or_its_siblings(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    doomed = await graph.add_element(an_element("Doomed"))
    sibling = await graph.add_element(an_element("Sibling"))
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    group = a_group()
    note = a_group(kind=ObjectKind.NOTE, anchor_element_id=doomed.id)
    await diagrams.replace_layout(
        diagram.id,
        DiagramScene(
            nodes=(
                DiagramNode(doomed.id, 0, 0, parent_object_id=group.id),
                DiagramNode(sibling.id, 50, 0, parent_object_id=group.id),
            ),
            objects=(group, note),
        ),
        revision=0,
        now=NOW,
    )

    assert await graph.delete_element(doomed.id) is True

    nodes = await diagrams.nodes_of(diagram.id)
    assert [(n.element_id, n.parent_object_id) for n in nodes] == [(sibling.id, group.id)]
    objects = {o.id: o for o in await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id)}
    assert set(objects) == {group.id, note.id}
    assert objects[note.id].anchor_element_id is None


async def test_deleting_a_relationship_takes_its_route(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    source = await graph.add_element(an_element("A"))
    target = await graph.add_element(an_element("B"))
    link = await graph.add_relationship(
        Relationship.between(RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION, source, target, now=NOW)
    )
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    await diagrams.replace_layout(
        diagram.id,
        DiagramScene(
            nodes=(DiagramNode(source.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(target.id, 300, 0)),
            edges=(DiagramEdge(link.id, (Point(150, 50),)),),
        ),
        revision=0,
        now=NOW,
    )

    await graph.delete_relationship(link.id)

    assert await diagrams.edges_of(diagram.id) == ()


async def test_deleting_a_diagram_takes_its_objects_and_routes(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository,
) -> None:
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(objects=(a_group(),)), revision=0, now=NOW)

    assert await diagrams.delete(diagram.id) is True

    assert await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id) == ()


async def test_a_parent_group_that_goes_away_lets_its_children_go_free(
    engine_at_head: AsyncEngine, diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository
) -> None:
    diagram = await a_stored_diagram(diagrams)
    group = a_group()
    note = a_group(kind=ObjectKind.NOTE, parent_id=group.id)
    await diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, DiagramScene(objects=(group, note)), revision=0, now=NOW)

    # The DDL net, not a use case: a bare delete of the group row.
    async with engine_at_head.begin() as connection:
        await connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM diagram_objects WHERE id = :id"), {"id": group.id})  # bound parameter

    remaining = await diagrams.objects_of(diagram.id)
    assert [(o.id, o.parent_id) for o in remaining] == [(note.id, None)]


async def test_revision_0009_applies_over_a_database_that_already_has_diagrams(
    postgres_engine: AsyncEngine, alembic_config: Config
) -> None:
    await asyncio.to_thread(command.upgrade, alembic_config, "0008")
    try:
        diagram_id: UUID = uuid4()
        element_id: UUID = uuid4()
        async with postgres_engine.begin() as connection:
            # Test-only raw SQL, every value bound: the schema before 0009 has no ORM model.
            await connection.execute(
                text(
                    "INSERT INTO elements (id, element_type, layer, aspect, name, description, documentation, properties, created_at, updated_at) "
                    "VALUES (:id, 'node', 'technology', 'active_structure', 'n', '', '', '{}'::jsonb, :now, :now)"
                ),
                {"id": element_id, "now": NOW},
            )
            await connection.execute(
                text("INSERT INTO diagrams (id, name, description, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (:id, 'Vieille vue', '', :now, :now)"),
                {"id": diagram_id, "now": NOW},
            )
            await connection.execute(
                text("INSERT INTO diagram_nodes (diagram_id, element_id, x, y, width, height) VALUES (:d, :e, 1, 2, 132, 46)"),
                {"d": diagram_id, "e": element_id},
            )

        await asyncio.to_thread(command.upgrade, alembic_config, "head")

        async with postgres_engine.connect() as connection:
            revision = (await connection.execute(text("SELECT revision FROM diagrams WHERE id = :id"), {"id": diagram_id})).scalar_one()
            z = (await connection.execute(text("SELECT z FROM diagram_nodes WHERE diagram_id = :id"), {"id": diagram_id})).scalar_one()
        assert (revision, z) == (0, 0)
    finally:
        await asyncio.to_thread(command.downgrade, alembic_config, "base")
```

```python
class DiagramsOnStoredElements(PostgresDiagramRepository):
    """Each element a layout names gets a row first — foreign key since 0006."""

    def __init__(self, engine: AsyncEngine) -> None:
        super().__init__(create_session_factory(engine))
        self._engine = engine

    async def replace_layout(
        self, diagram_id: UUID, scene: DiagramScene, *, revision: int, now: datetime
    ) -> int | None:
        await ensure_elements(self._engine, *(node.element_id for node in scene.nodes))
        return await super().replace_layout(diagram_id, scene, revision=revision, now=now)
```

```python
class DiagramObjectRecord(Base):
    """A note or a group: drawn on one diagram, owning no fact.

    One table for both: same geometry, same stacking, same nesting; only the
    meaning of `text` differs (a group's title, a note's body).
    """

    __tablename__ = "diagram_objects"
    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("kind in ('note', 'group')", name="kind"),
        CheckConstraint("kind = 'note' or anchor_element_id is null", name="group_has_no_anchor"),
    )

    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, primary_key=True)
    diagram_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("diagrams.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False, index=True
    )
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    parent_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("diagram_objects.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True
    )
    x: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Double, nullable=False)
    y: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Double, nullable=False)
    width: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Double, nullable=False)
    height: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(Double, nullable=False)
    text: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False, server_default="")
    colour: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=True)
    z: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, server_default="0")
    # Indexed on its own: deleting an element sets this to NULL, and must not scan.
    anchor_element_id: Mapped[UUID | None] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("elements.id", ondelete="SET NULL"), nullable=True, index=True
    )


class DiagramEdgeRecord(Base):
    """The route of one link on one diagram. No row = a straight segment."""

    __tablename__ = "diagram_edges"

    diagram_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("diagrams.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True
    )
    relationship_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("relationships.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True, index=True
    )
    waypoints: Mapped[list[list[float]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=False, server_default="[]"
    )
```

`backend/migrations/versions/0009_diagram_scene.py` :

```python
"""les objets de vue, les tracés de liens et la révision d'un diagramme

Revision ID: 0009
Revises: 0008
Date: 2026-10-10

Notes et groupes (`diagram_objects`, une seule table : même géométrie, même
empilement, même imbrication), tracés de liens (`diagram_edges`), parent et
ordre d'empilement d'une boîte, et `diagrams.revision` — le compteur qui permet
de refuser un enregistrement fait sur une copie périmée (ADR sur les objets de
vue). Rien à convertir : tout est ajout nullable ou défaut serveur, les
diagrammes existants gardent leurs boîtes à l'identique.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision: str = "0009"
down_revision: str | None = "0008"
branch_labels: str | Sequence[str] | None = None
depends_on: str | Sequence[str] | None = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.add_column(
        "diagrams", sa.Column("revision", sa.Integer(), server_default="0", nullable=False)
    )
    op.create_table(
        "diagram_objects",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("diagram_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("kind", sa.String(length=8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("parent_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("x", sa.Double(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("y", sa.Double(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("width", sa.Double(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("height", sa.Double(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("text", sa.Text(), server_default="", nullable=False),
        sa.Column("colour", sa.String(length=7), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("z", sa.Integer(), server_default="0", nullable=False),
        sa.Column("anchor_element_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=True),
        sa.CheckConstraint("kind in ('note', 'group')", name=op.f("ck_diagram_objects_kind")),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "kind = 'note' or anchor_element_id is null",
            name=op.f("ck_diagram_objects_group_has_no_anchor"),
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["diagram_id"],
            ["diagrams.id"],
            name=op.f("fk_diagram_objects_diagram_id_diagrams"),
            ondelete="CASCADE",
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["parent_id"],
            ["diagram_objects.id"],
            name=op.f("fk_diagram_objects_parent_id_diagram_objects"),
            ondelete="SET NULL",
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["anchor_element_id"],
            ["elements.id"],
            name=op.f("fk_diagram_objects_anchor_element_id_elements"),
            ondelete="SET NULL",
        ),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint("id", name=op.f("pk_diagram_objects")),
    )
    op.create_index(
        op.f("ix_diagram_objects_diagram_id"), "diagram_objects", ["diagram_id"], unique=False
    )
    op.create_index(
        op.f("ix_diagram_objects_anchor_element_id"),
        "diagram_objects",
        ["anchor_element_id"],
        unique=False,
    )
    op.create_table(
        "diagram_edges",
        sa.Column("diagram_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("relationship_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column(
            "waypoints", postgresql.JSONB(astext_type=sa.Text()), server_default="[]", nullable=False
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["diagram_id"],
            ["diagrams.id"],
            name=op.f("fk_diagram_edges_diagram_id_diagrams"),
            ondelete="CASCADE",
        ),
        sa.ForeignKeyConstraint(
            ["relationship_id"],
            ["relationships.id"],
            name=op.f("fk_diagram_edges_relationship_id_relationships"),
            ondelete="CASCADE",
        ),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint("diagram_id", "relationship_id", name=op.f("pk_diagram_edges")),
    )
    op.create_index(
        op.f("ix_diagram_edges_relationship_id"),
        "diagram_edges",
        ["relationship_id"],
        unique=False,
    )
    op.add_column("diagram_nodes", sa.Column("parent_object_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=True))
    op.add_column("diagram_nodes", sa.Column("z", sa.Integer(), server_default="0", nullable=False))
    op.create_foreign_key(
        op.f("fk_diagram_nodes_parent_object_id_diagram_objects"),
        "diagram_nodes",
        "diagram_objects",
        ["parent_object_id"],
        ["id"],
        ondelete="SET NULL",
    )
    op.create_index(
        op.f("ix_diagram_nodes_parent_object_id"),
        "diagram_nodes",
        ["parent_object_id"],
        unique=False,
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagram_nodes_parent_object_id"), table_name="diagram_nodes")
    op.drop_constraint(
        op.f("fk_diagram_nodes_parent_object_id_diagram_objects"),
        "diagram_nodes",
        type_="foreignkey",
    )
    op.drop_column("diagram_nodes", "z")
    op.drop_column("diagram_nodes", "parent_object_id")
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagram_edges_relationship_id"), table_name="diagram_edges")
    op.drop_table("diagram_edges")
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagram_objects_anchor_element_id"), table_name="diagram_objects")
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagram_objects_diagram_id"), table_name="diagram_objects")
    op.drop_table("diagram_objects")
    op.drop_column("diagrams", "revision")
```

```python
    async def nodes_of(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> tuple[DiagramNode, ...]:
        statement = (
            select(DiagramNodeRecord)
            .where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            .order_by(DiagramNodeRecord.element_id)
        )
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return tuple(
                DiagramNode(
                    element_id=row.element_id,
                    x=row.x,
                    y=row.y,
                    width=row.width,
                    height=row.height,
                    parent_object_id=row.parent_object_id,
                    z=row.z,
                )
                for row in (await session.scalars(statement))
            )

    async def objects_of(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> tuple[DiagramObject, ...]:
        statement = (
            select(DiagramObjectRecord)
            .where(DiagramObjectRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            .order_by(DiagramObjectRecord.z, DiagramObjectRecord.id)
        )
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return tuple(
                DiagramObject(
                    id=row.id,
                    kind=ObjectKind(row.kind),
                    x=row.x,
                    y=row.y,
                    width=row.width,
                    height=row.height,
                    text=row.text,
                    colour=row.colour,
                    z=row.z,
                    parent_id=row.parent_id,
                    anchor_element_id=row.anchor_element_id,
                )
                for row in (await session.scalars(statement))
            )

    async def edges_of(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> tuple[DiagramEdge, ...]:
        statement = (
            select(DiagramEdgeRecord)
            .where(DiagramEdgeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            .order_by(DiagramEdgeRecord.relationship_id)
        )
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return tuple(
                DiagramEdge(
                    relationship_id=row.relationship_id,
                    waypoints=tuple(Point(x=p[0], y=p[1]) for p in row.waypoints),
                )
                for row in (await session.scalars(statement))
            )
```

et remplacer `replace_layout` :

```python
    async def replace_layout(
        self, diagram_id: UUID, scene: DiagramScene, *, revision: int, now: datetime
    ) -> int | None:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            # `FOR UPDATE` queues concurrent saves of one diagram (autosave fires
            # on every drag) *and* makes the revision comparison race-free: two
            # savers on revision N cannot both read N. It also makes a
            # concurrent DELETE of the diagram wait.
            row = await session.get(DiagramRecord, diagram_id, with_for_update=True)
            if row is None:
                return None
            if row.revision != revision:
                msg = f"the diagram is at revision {row.revision}, not {revision}"
                raise DiagramRevisionConflictError(msg)
            await session.execute(
                delete(DiagramNodeRecord).where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            )
            await session.execute(
                delete(DiagramEdgeRecord).where(DiagramEdgeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            )
            await session.execute(
                delete(DiagramObjectRecord).where(DiagramObjectRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            )
            # Parents first: the mappers are related by a foreign key and no
            # `relationship()`, so the unit of work would insert in any order.
            for level in _by_depth(scene.objects):
                session.add_all(
                    DiagramObjectRecord(
                        id=o.id,
                        diagram_id=diagram_id,
                        kind=o.kind.value,
                        parent_id=o.parent_id,
                        x=o.x,
                        y=o.y,
                        width=o.width,
                        height=o.height,
                        text=o.text,
                        colour=o.colour,
                        z=o.z,
                        anchor_element_id=o.anchor_element_id,
                    )
                    for o in level
                )
                await session.flush()
            session.add_all(
                DiagramNodeRecord(
                    diagram_id=diagram_id,
                    element_id=n.element_id,
                    x=n.x,
                    y=n.y,
                    width=n.width,
                    height=n.height,
                    parent_object_id=n.parent_object_id,
                    z=n.z,
                )
                for n in scene.nodes
            )
            session.add_all(
                DiagramEdgeRecord(
                    diagram_id=diagram_id,
                    relationship_id=e.relationship_id,
                    waypoints=[[p.x, p.y] for p in e.waypoints],
                )
                for e in scene.edges
            )
            row.revision = revision + 1
            row.updated_at = now
            return row.revision
```
et la fonction de module :

```python
def _by_depth(objects: Sequence[DiagramObject]) -> list[list[DiagramObject]]:
    """The objects grouped by how many groups lie above them, outermost first.

    `check_scene` has already refused a loop, so every chain ends.
    """
    by_id = {o.id: o for o in objects}
    levels: dict[int, list[DiagramObject]] = {}
    for item in objects:
        depth, current = 0, item
        while current.parent_id is not None and current.parent_id in by_id:
            depth += 1
            current = by_id[current.parent_id]
        levels.setdefault(depth, []).append(item)
    return [levels[depth] for depth in sorted(levels)]
```
(`Sequence` est déjà importé sous `TYPE_CHECKING` ; la fonction ne l'utilise qu'en annotation.)

```python
"""The scene contract of `PUT /diagrams/{id}/layout`, end to end: revisions, groups, routes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

import httpx
import pytest
import pytest_asyncio

from ea.core.config import Settings
from ea.main import create_app
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from tests.conftest import InMemoryDiagrams, StaticVerifier, a_reader, an_editor

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

EDITOR, READER = "editor-token", "reader-token"


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def client(
    service: ArchitectureService, diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams
) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=False),
        architecture_service=service,
        diagrams=diagrams,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(
        transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test"
    ) as client:
        yield client


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def secured(
    service: ArchitectureService, diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams, nobody_calling: None
) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=True),
        architecture_service=service,
        diagrams=diagrams,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        verifier=StaticVerifier({EDITOR: an_editor(), READER: a_reader()}),
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(
        transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test"
    ) as client:

async def an_element(client: httpx.AsyncClient, name: str = "Facturation") -> str:
    response = await client.post(
        "/elements", json={"element_type": "application_component", "name": name}
    )
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return str(response.json()["id"])


async def a_diagram(client: httpx.AsyncClient, name: str = "Vente") -> str:
    response = await client.post("/diagrams", json={"name": name})
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return str(response.json()["id"])


def box(element: str, **overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "element_id": element,
        "x": 10.0,
        "y": 20.0,
        "width": 200.0,
        "height": 80.0,
        "parent_object_id": None,
        "z": 0,
        **overrides,
    }


def group(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": str(uuid4()),
        "kind": "group",
        "x": 0.0,
        "y": 0.0,
        "width": 300.0,
        "height": 200.0,
        "text": "Domaine Paiement",
        "colour": None,
        "z": 0,
        "parent_id": None,
        "anchor_element_id": None,
        **overrides,
    }


def scene(
    revision: int = 0, nodes: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None, **rest: Any
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"revision": revision, "nodes": nodes or [], "objects": [], "edges": [], **rest}


class TestTheRevision:
    async def test_a_save_returns_the_new_revision_in_a_header(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)

        first = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0))
        second = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(1))

        assert (first.status_code, first.headers["x-diagram-revision"]) == (204, "1")
        assert (second.status_code, second.headers["x-diagram-revision"]) == (204, "2")
        assert (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{diagram}")).json()["revision"] == 2

    async def test_two_editors_on_one_revision_the_second_gets_a_409_and_the_first_survives(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        winner = await an_element(client, "Gagnant")
        loser = await an_element(client, "Perdant")
        assert (
            await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0, [box(winner)]))
        ).status_code == 204

        stale = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0, [box(loser)]))

        assert stale.status_code == 409
        assert stale.json()["error"] == "diagram_revision_conflict"
        assert "revision 1" in stale.json()["detail"]
        read = (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{diagram}")).json()
        assert [n["element_id"] for n in read["nodes"]] == [winner]
        assert read["revision"] == 1
        # The loser reloads, sees revision 1, and can save again.
        retry = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(1, [box(loser)]))
        assert retry.status_code == 204

    async def test_a_body_without_a_revision_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        body = scene(0)
        del body["revision"]

        response = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=body)

        assert response.status_code == 422

    async def test_the_header_is_exposed_to_a_browser(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)

        response = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout",
            json=scene(0),
            headers={"Origin": "http://localhost:5173"},
        )

        assert "x-diagram-revision" in response.headers["access-control-expose-headers"].lower()


class TestTheScene:
    async def test_notes_groups_and_bends_round_trip(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        source = await an_element(client, "A")
        target = await an_element(client, "B")
        link = (
            await client.post(
                "/relationships",
                json={"relationship_type": "association", "source_id": source, "target_id": target},
            )
        ).json()["id"]
        outer = group()
        note = group(kind="note", text="Voir ADR", anchor_element_id=source, colour="#ffe0e0")
        body = scene(
            0,
            [box(source, parent_object_id=outer["id"]), box(target, x=400.0)],
            objects=[outer, note],
            edges=[{"relationship_id": link, "waypoints": [{"x": 100.0, "y": 80.0}]}],
        )

        assert (await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=body)).status_code == 204

        read = (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{diagram}")).json()
        assert {o["id"] for o in read["objects"]} == {outer["id"], note["id"]}
        assert read["edges"] == [
            {"relationship_id": link, "waypoints": [{"x": 100.0, "y": 80.0}]}
        ]
        assert {n["element_id"]: n["parent_object_id"] for n in read["nodes"]}[source] == outer["id"]

    async def test_an_empty_group_is_accepted(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)

        response = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0, objects=[group()])
        )

        assert response.status_code == 204

    async def test_groups_that_contain_each_other_are_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        first, second = group(), group()
        first["parent_id"], second["parent_id"] = second["id"], first["id"]

        response = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0, objects=[first, second])
        )

        assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]) == (422, "invalid_input")

    async def test_a_sixth_level_of_groups_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        chain: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for _ in range(6):
            chain.append(group(parent_id=chain[-1]["id"] if chain else None))

        response = await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json=scene(0, objects=chain))

        assert response.status_code == 422

    async def test_a_route_for_a_link_not_between_two_placed_elements_is_a_422(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        source = await an_element(client, "A")
        target = await an_element(client, "B")
        link = (
            await client.post(
                "/relationships",
                json={"relationship_type": "association", "source_id": source, "target_id": target},
            )
        ).json()["id"]

        response = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout",
            json=scene(0, [box(source)], edges=[{"relationship_id": link, "waypoints": []}]),
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422

    async def test_too_many_bends_or_a_bad_colour_never_reach_the_domain(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)

        bends = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout",
            json=scene(0, edges=[{"relationship_id": str(uuid4()), "waypoints": [{"x": 0, "y": 0}] * 21}]),
        )
        colour = await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout",
            json=scene(0, objects=[group(colour='red" onload="x')]),
        )

        assert (bends.status_code, colour.status_code) == (422, 422)

    async def test_deleting_an_element_leaves_the_group_and_its_other_children(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        diagram = await a_diagram(client)
        doomed = await an_element(client, "Doomed")
        sibling = await an_element(client, "Sibling")
        outer = group()
        await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout",
            json=scene(
                0,
                [box(doomed, parent_object_id=outer["id"]), box(sibling, parent_object_id=outer["id"], x=300.0)],
                objects=[outer],
            ),
        )

        await client.delete(f"/elements/{doomed}")

        read = (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{diagram}")).json()
        assert [n["element_id"] for n in read["nodes"]] == [sibling]
        assert [o["id"] for o in read["objects"]] == [outer["id"]]


class TestWhoMaySave:
    async def test_a_reader_reads_the_scene_but_cannot_save_it(
        self, secured: httpx.AsyncClient
    ) -> None:
        editor = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {EDITOR}"}
        reader = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {READER}"}
        created = await secured.post("/diagrams", json={"name": "Vente"}, headers=editor)

    async def test_nobody_saves_without_a_token(self, secured: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        response = await secured.put(f"/diagrams/{uuid4()}/layout", json=scene(0))

        assert response.status_code == 401
```

```python
#: Notes and groups on one diagram, and the links whose route was changed.
MAX_DIAGRAM_OBJECTS: Final = 500
MAX_DIAGRAM_EDGES: Final = 2_000
#: Stacking order, either way. Only the order matters, not the number.
ZIndex = Annotated[int, Field(ge=-10_000, le=10_000)]
Revision = Annotated[int, Field(ge=0)]
#: `#rrggbb` only: a colour ends up in an SVG attribute, so anything else is an injection.
Colour = Annotated[str, Field(pattern=r"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$")]
ObjectText = Annotated[str, Field(max_length=MAX_OBJECT_TEXT)]
```
Remplacer `DiagramNode`, `DiagramLayout` et `DiagramRead` par :

```python
class DiagramNode(_Input):
    """One box: an element, the top-left corner of the box and its size, in canvas units."""

    element_id: UUID
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate
    # Required, not defaulted: a default would split this model into an input
    # and an output schema, and rename the type the SPA imports.
    width: BoxSize
    height: BoxSize
    parent_object_id: UUID | None
    z: ZIndex

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, node: PlacedNode) -> DiagramNode:
        return cls(
            element_id=node.element_id,
            x=node.x,
            y=node.y,
            width=node.width,
            height=node.height,
            parent_object_id=node.parent_object_id,
            z=node.z,
        )

    def placed(self) -> PlacedNode:
        return PlacedNode(
            element_id=self.element_id,
            x=self.x,
            y=self.y,
            width=self.width,
            height=self.height,
            parent_object_id=self.parent_object_id,
            z=self.z,
        )


class DiagramPoint(_Input):
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate


class DiagramEdge(_Input):
    """The route of one link on this diagram. A link with no row is drawn straight."""

    relationship_id: UUID
    waypoints: Annotated[list[DiagramPoint], Field(max_length=MAX_WAYPOINTS)]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, edge: PlacedEdge) -> DiagramEdge:
        return cls(
            relationship_id=edge.relationship_id,
            waypoints=[DiagramPoint(x=p.x, y=p.y) for p in edge.waypoints],
        )

    def placed(self) -> PlacedEdge:
        return PlacedEdge(
            relationship_id=self.relationship_id,
            waypoints=tuple(PlacedPoint(x=p.x, y=p.y) for p in self.waypoints),
        )


class DiagramObject(_Input):
    """A note (its text is the body) or a group (its text is the title). Never a fact."""

    id: UUID
    kind: ObjectKind
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate
    width: BoxSize
    height: BoxSize
    text: ObjectText
    colour: Colour | None
    z: ZIndex
    parent_id: UUID | None
    anchor_element_id: UUID | None

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, item: PlacedObject) -> DiagramObject:
        return cls(
            id=item.id,
            kind=item.kind,
            x=item.x,
            y=item.y,
            width=item.width,
            height=item.height,
            text=item.text,
            colour=item.colour,
            z=item.z,
            parent_id=item.parent_id,
            anchor_element_id=item.anchor_element_id,
        )

    def placed(self) -> PlacedObject:
        return PlacedObject(
            id=self.id,
            kind=self.kind,
            x=self.x,
            y=self.y,
            width=self.width,
            height=self.height,
            text=self.text,
            colour=self.colour,
            z=self.z,
            parent_id=self.parent_id,
            anchor_element_id=self.anchor_element_id,
        )


class DiagramLayout(_Input):
    """The whole scene of a diagram, replacing the stored one — against the revision the client saw.

    Every field is required, empty lists included: an optional one would split
    this model into `-Input` and `-Output` schemas (docs/adr/0035).
    """

    revision: Revision
    nodes: Annotated[list[DiagramNode], Field(max_length=MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES)]
    objects: Annotated[list[DiagramObject], Field(max_length=MAX_DIAGRAM_OBJECTS)]
    edges: Annotated[list[DiagramEdge], Field(max_length=MAX_DIAGRAM_EDGES)]
```
Et dans `DiagramRead`, ajouter après `nodes: list[DiagramNode]` : `objects: list[DiagramObject]`, `edges: list[DiagramEdge]`, `revision: int`, et dans `of(...)` : `revision=diagram.revision`, `objects=[DiagramObject.of(o) for o in detail.objects]`, `edges=[DiagramEdge.of(e) for e in detail.edges]`.

`backend/src/ea/api/diagrams.py` — ajouter `REVISION_HEADER: Final = "X-Diagram-Revision"` (import `Final` de `typing`), étendre `LAYOUT_REJECTED` avec `status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT: _ERROR`, remplacer l'endpoint :

```python
@router.put(
    "/diagrams/{diagram_id}/layout",
    status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT,
    responses=LAYOUT_REJECTED,
)
async def replace_diagram_layout(
    diagram_id: UUID, body: DiagramLayout, diagrams: Diagrams
) -> Response:
    """Replace the whole scene of a diagram at once.

    `revision` is the one the client read; if somebody saved since, nothing is
    written and the answer is a 409 `diagram_revision_conflict`. Otherwise the
    new revision comes back in `X-Diagram-Revision`. Each element must exist
    and appear once, and a route may only name a link joining two placed
    elements; otherwise nothing is written.
    """
    revision = await diagrams.replace_layout(
        diagram_id,
        revision=body.revision,
        nodes=[node.placed() for node in body.nodes],
        objects=[item.placed() for item in body.objects],
        edges=[edge.placed() for edge in body.edges],
    )
    return Response(
        status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT, headers={REVISION_HEADER: str(revision)}
    )
```


```python
    # --- Diagrams (read only: geometry is not an agent's work) ------------

    @server.tool(annotations=READS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def list_diagrams() -> list[DiagramSummaryRead]:
        """The saved diagrams: id, name, description and how many boxes each holds.

        A diagram is a *view* of the catalogue — which elements are drawn and
        where — and owns no fact. Start here to find the id `get_diagram` wants.

        return [DiagramSummaryRead.of(d) for d in await get_diagrams().list_diagrams()]

    @server.tool(annotations=READS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def get_diagram(diagram_id: DiagramId) -> DiagramRead:
        """One diagram opened: its boxes with the elements they show, its notes, groups and
        routed links, and the relationships between the elements drawn.

        Read it to answer "what does this schema show". There is no tool that
        moves a box: a layout is a person's gesture, not data to compute.
        """
        return DiagramRead.of(await get_diagrams().open(diagram_id))
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `components['schemas']['DiagramObject' | 'DiagramNode']` (client régénéré, Task 14), `Point`, `Rect`, `clipToBox`, `centreOf`, `edgeSegment` (Task 1 / existants).
- Produces (TypeScript) :
  - `SceneParts = { nodes: DiagramNode[]; objects: DiagramObject[] }`, `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH = 5` (garde d'ergonomie : l'API décide)
  - `descendantsOf(objects, groupId): Set<string>`, `groupsAbove(objects, id): number`, `groupHeight(objects, id): number`
  - `canNest(objects, childId: string | null, childIsGroup: boolean, parentId: string): boolean`
  - `groupAt(objects, point: Point, child: { id: string | null; isGroup: boolean }): string | null` (groupe le plus profond qui contient `point` et qui peut accueillir l'enfant)
  - `moveWithChildren(parts: SceneParts, groupId: string, dx: number, dy: number): SceneParts`
  - `removeGroup(parts: SceneParts, groupId: string): SceneParts` (remonte les enfants au parent du groupe)
  - `MAX_WAYPOINTS = 20`, `routeOf(source: Rect, target: Rect, waypoints: Point[]): Point[] | null`, `midpoints(route: Point[]): Point[]`, `insertWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], segment: number, at: Point): Point[]`, `moveWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], index: number, to: Point): Point[]`, `removeWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], index: number): Point[]`
  - `newId(): string`

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

`frontend/tests/sceneObjects.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import {
  MAX_GROUP_DEPTH,
  canNest,
  descendantsOf,
  groupAt,
  groupHeight,
  groupsAbove,
  moveWithChildren,
  removeGroup,
  type SceneParts,
} from '../src/lib/sceneObjects'

type Obj = SceneParts['objects'][number]

const group = (id: string, parent: string | null = null, extra: Partial<Obj> = {}): Obj => ({
  id,
  kind: 'group',
  x: 0,
  y: 0,
  width: 400,
  height: 300,
  text: id,
  colour: null,
  z: 0,
  parent_id: parent,
  anchor_element_id: null,
  ...extra,
})
const note = (id: string, parent: string | null = null): Obj =>
  group(id, parent, { kind: 'note', width: 100, height: 50 })
const box = (element_id: string, parent: string | null, x = 0, y = 0) => ({
  element_id,
  x,
  y,
  width: 100,
  height: 40,
  parent_object_id: parent,
  z: 0,
})

/** g1 > g2 > g3 > g4 > g5, the deepest the API allows. */
const chain = (length: number) =>
  Array.from({ length }, (_, i) => group(`g${i + 1}`, i === 0 ? null : `g${i}`))

describe('nesting', () => {
  it('counts the groups above an object and below a group', () => {
    const objects = chain(3)

    expect(groupsAbove(objects, 'g3')).toBe(2)
    expect(groupHeight(objects, 'g1')).toBe(2)
    expect(groupHeight(objects, 'g3')).toBe(0)
  })

  it('collects every descendant, however deep', () => {
    const objects = [...chain(3), note('n', 'g2')]

    expect(descendantsOf(objects, 'g1')).toEqual(new Set(['g2', 'g3', 'n']))
  })

  it('refuses to put a group into itself or into one of its own descendants', () => {
    const objects = chain(3)

    expect(canNest(objects, 'g1', true, 'g1')).toBe(false)
    expect(canNest(objects, 'g1', true, 'g3')).toBe(false)
    expect(canNest(objects, 'g3', true, 'g1')).toBe(true)
  })

  it('refuses a sixth level of groups but welcomes a note or a box in the fifth', () => {
    const objects = chain(MAX_GROUP_DEPTH)

    expect(canNest(objects, null, true, 'g5')).toBe(false)
    expect(canNest(objects, null, false, 'g5')).toBe(true)
  })

  it('counts the depth of the whole subtree being moved, not just its root', () => {
    const objects = [...chain(2), group('a'), group('b', 'a'), group('c', 'b')]

    // g2 sits at level 2; moving the 3-deep tree `a` under it would make 5, then 6.
    expect(canNest(objects, 'a', true, 'g2')).toBe(true)
    expect(canNest([...objects, group('d', 'c')], 'a', true, 'g2')).toBe(false)
  })

  it('refuses a parent that is a note or does not exist', () => {
    expect(canNest([note('n')], null, false, 'n')).toBe(false)
    expect(canNest([], null, false, 'ghost')).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('groupAt', () => {
  const objects = [
    group('outer', null, { x: 0, y: 0, width: 500, height: 400 }),
    group('inner', 'outer', { x: 50, y: 50, width: 200, height: 150 }),
  ]

  it('picks the deepest group holding the point', () => {
    expect(groupAt(objects, { x: 100, y: 100 }, { id: null, isGroup: false })).toBe('inner')
    expect(groupAt(objects, { x: 400, y: 350 }, { id: null, isGroup: false })).toBe('outer')
  })

  it('is null outside every group', () => {
    expect(groupAt(objects, { x: 900, y: 900 }, { id: null, isGroup: false })).toBeNull()
  })

  it('never picks the group being moved, nor one of its descendants', () => {
    expect(groupAt(objects, { x: 100, y: 100 }, { id: 'outer', isGroup: true })).toBeNull()
    expect(groupAt(objects, { x: 100, y: 100 }, { id: 'inner', isGroup: true })).toBe('outer')
  })
})

describe('moveWithChildren', () => {
  it('moves the group, its sub-groups, notes and boxes — and nothing else', () => {
    const parts: SceneParts = {
      objects: [
        group('g', null, { x: 10, y: 10 }),
        group('sub', 'g', { x: 20, y: 20 }),
        note('n', 'sub'),
        group('other', null, { x: 500, y: 500 }),
      ],
      nodes: [box('in', 'sub', 30, 30), box('out', null, 600, 600)],
    }

    const moved = moveWithChildren(parts, 'g', 5, -5)

    const at = (id: string) => moved.objects.find((o) => o.id === id)!
    expect([at('g').x, at('g').y]).toEqual([15, 5])
    expect([at('sub').x, at('sub').y]).toEqual([25, 15])
    expect([at('other').x, at('other').y]).toEqual([500, 500])
    expect(moved.nodes.find((n) => n.element_id === 'in')).toMatchObject({ x: 35, y: 25 })
    expect(moved.nodes.find((n) => n.element_id === 'out')).toMatchObject({ x: 600, y: 600 })
  })
})

describe('removeGroup', () => {
  it('lifts boxes, notes and sub-groups to the parent of the group', () => {
    const parts: SceneParts = {
      objects: [group('outer'), group('inner', 'outer'), note('n', 'inner'), group('deep', 'inner')],
      nodes: [box('e', 'inner')],
    }

    const result = removeGroup(parts, 'inner')

    expect(result.objects.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual(['outer', 'n', 'deep'])
    expect(result.objects.filter((o) => o.id !== 'outer').map((o) => o.parent_id)).toEqual([
      'outer',
      'outer',
    ])
    expect(result.nodes[0].parent_object_id).toBe('outer')
  })

  it('frees the children of a top-level group', () => {
    const parts: SceneParts = { objects: [group('g'), note('n', 'g')], nodes: [box('e', 'g')] }

    const result = removeGroup(parts, 'g')

    expect(result.objects[0].parent_id).toBeNull()
    expect(result.nodes[0].parent_object_id).toBeNull()
  })

  it('removes an empty group and leaves a missing id alone', () => {
    const parts: SceneParts = { objects: [group('g')], nodes: [] }

    expect(removeGroup(parts, 'g').objects).toEqual([])
    expect(removeGroup(parts, 'ghost')).toEqual(parts)
  })
})
```

`frontend/tests/waypoints.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import {
  MAX_WAYPOINTS,
  insertWaypoint,
  midpoints,
  moveWaypoint,
  removeWaypoint,
  routeOf,
} from '../src/lib/waypoints'

const left = { x: 0, y: 0, width: 100, height: 40 }
const right = { x: 400, y: 0, width: 100, height: 40 }

describe('routeOf', () => {
  it('is the trimmed straight segment when there is no bend', () => {
    expect(routeOf(left, right, [])).toEqual([
      { x: 100, y: 20 },
      { x: 400, y: 20 },
    ])
  })

  it('leaves each box towards the nearest bend and ends on the last bend', () => {
    const route = routeOf(left, right, [{ x: 250, y: 200 }])!

    expect(route).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(route[1]).toEqual({ x: 250, y: 200 })
    expect(route[0].y).toBeGreaterThan(20) // leaves downwards, not straight across
    expect(route[2].y).toBeGreaterThan(20)
  })

  it('is null when the boxes overlap and there is no bend to route through', () => {
    expect(routeOf(left, { ...right, x: 50 }, [])).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('editing the bends', () => {
  const route = [
    { x: 0, y: 0 },
    { x: 100, y: 0 },
    { x: 100, y: 100 },
  ]

  it('finds the middle of each segment', () => {
    expect(midpoints(route)).toEqual([
      { x: 50, y: 0 },
      { x: 100, y: 50 },
    ])
  })

  it('inserts a bend before the one the segment ends on', () => {
    const bends = [{ x: 1, y: 1 }, { x: 3, y: 3 }]

    expect(insertWaypoint(bends, 1, { x: 2, y: 2 })).toEqual([
      { x: 1, y: 1 },
      { x: 2, y: 2 },
      { x: 3, y: 3 },
    ])
    expect(insertWaypoint(bends, 0, { x: 0, y: 0 })[0]).toEqual({ x: 0, y: 0 })
    expect(insertWaypoint(bends, 2, { x: 4, y: 4 })[2]).toEqual({ x: 4, y: 4 })
  })

  it('never exceeds the bound the API enforces', () => {
    const full = Array.from({ length: MAX_WAYPOINTS }, (_, i) => ({ x: i, y: 0 }))

    expect(insertWaypoint(full, 0, { x: 0, y: 9 })).toEqual(full)
  })

  it('moves and removes a bend without touching the others', () => {
    const bends = [{ x: 1, y: 1 }, { x: 2, y: 2 }]

    expect(moveWaypoint(bends, 1, { x: 9, y: 9 })).toEqual([{ x: 1, y: 1 }, { x: 9, y: 9 }])
    expect(removeWaypoint(bends, 0)).toEqual([{ x: 2, y: 2 }])
    expect(bends).toHaveLength(2) // the input is never mutated
  })
})
```
