`frontend/src/lib/newId.ts` :

```ts
// A fresh id for a note or a group, made in the browser: the object exists
// before the server has heard of it, and the server accepts the id it is given.

export function newId(): string {
  const crypto = globalThis.crypto as Crypto | undefined
  if (typeof crypto?.randomUUID === 'function') {
    return crypto.randomUUID()
  }
  // ponytail: an environment without `crypto.randomUUID` (an old WebView) gets
  // Math.random — fine for a view object's id, which protects nothing.
  return 'xxxxxxxx-xxxx-4xxx-8xxx-xxxxxxxxxxxx'.replace(/x/g, () =>
    Math.floor(Math.random() * 16).toString(16),
  )
}
```

`frontend/src/lib/sceneObjects.ts` :

```ts
// Where things sit inside a group, and what happens to them when it moves or goes.
//
// A group is "drawn together" and nothing more (docs/adr: objets de vue): it
// is not an element and creates no relationship, so removing one never touches
// the graph. These functions only edit the *scene* — boxes and objects — and
// never mutate their input. The depth limit below mirrors the API's
// `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH`; it spares the user a refusal, it does not replace it.
import type { components } from '../api/schema'
import type { Point } from './diagramGeometry'

type SceneObject = components['schemas']['DiagramObject']
type SceneNode = components['schemas']['DiagramNode']
export type SceneParts = { nodes: SceneNode[]; objects: SceneObject[] }

/** Groups that may contain one another; a note or a box may sit in the deepest. */
export const MAX_GROUP_DEPTH = 5

const indexed = (objects: SceneObject[]) => new Map(objects.map((item) => [item.id, item]))

/** Every object, however deep, under `groupId`. */
export function descendantsOf(objects: SceneObject[], groupId: string): Set<string> {
  const found = new Set<string>()
  const queue = [groupId]
  while (queue.length > 0) {
    const parent = queue.pop()
    for (const item of objects) {
      if (item.parent_id === parent && !found.has(item.id)) {
        found.add(item.id)
        queue.push(item.id)
      }
    }
  }
  return found
}

/** How many groups lie above `id`. A loop, which the API refuses anyway, stops the count. */
export function groupsAbove(objects: SceneObject[], id: string): number {
  const byId = indexed(objects)
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  let depth = 0
  let current = byId.get(id)?.parent_id ?? null
  while (current !== null && !seen.has(current)) {
    seen.add(current)
    depth += 1
    current = byId.get(current)?.parent_id ?? null
  }
  return depth
}

/** How many levels of groups lie strictly below group `id`. */
export function groupHeight(objects: SceneObject[], id: string): number {
  const children = objects.filter((item) => item.parent_id === id && item.kind === 'group')
  return children.length === 0 ? 0 : 1 + Math.max(...children.map((c) => groupHeight(objects, c.id)))
}

/**
 * Whether an object (`childId`, null for a box, which has no object id) may be
 * put inside `parentId` without a loop and within the depth limit.
 */
export function canNest(
  objects: SceneObject[],
  childId: string | null,
  childIsGroup: boolean,
  parentId: string,
): boolean {
  const parent = objects.find((item) => item.id === parentId)
  if (!parent || parent.kind !== 'group') {
    return false
  }
  if (childId !== null && (parentId === childId || descendantsOf(objects, childId).has(parentId))) {
    return false
  }
  const parentLevel = groupsAbove(objects, parentId) + 1
  if (!childIsGroup) {
    return parentLevel <= MAX_GROUP_DEPTH
  }
  const below = childId === null ? 0 : groupHeight(objects, childId)
  return parentLevel + 1 + below <= MAX_GROUP_DEPTH
}

/** The deepest group that holds `point` and may take the child, or null. */
export function groupAt(
  objects: SceneObject[],
  point: Point,
  child: { id: string | null; isGroup: boolean },
): string | null {
  const candidates = objects
    .filter(
      (item) =>
        item.kind === 'group' &&
        point.x >= item.x &&
        point.x <= item.x + item.width &&
        point.y >= item.y &&
        point.y <= item.y + item.height &&
        canNest(objects, child.id, child.isGroup, item.id),
    )
    .map((item) => ({ item, depth: groupsAbove(objects, item.id), area: item.width * item.height }))
    .sort((a, b) => b.depth - a.depth || a.area - b.area || a.item.id.localeCompare(b.item.id))
  return candidates[0]?.item.id ?? null
}

/** Translate a group, everything inside it at any depth, and nothing else. */
export function moveWithChildren(
  parts: SceneParts,
  groupId: string,
  dx: number,
  dy: number,
): SceneParts {
  const moving = descendantsOf(parts.objects, groupId)
  moving.add(groupId)
  return {
    objects: parts.objects.map((item) =>
      moving.has(item.id) ? { ...item, x: item.x + dx, y: item.y + dy } : item,
    ),
    nodes: parts.nodes.map((node) =>
      node.parent_object_id !== null && moving.has(node.parent_object_id)
        ? { ...node, x: node.x + dx, y: node.y + dy }
        : node,
    ),
  }
}

/**
 * Take a group away, lifting what it held to the group's own parent. No
 * element is deleted — a box leaves the group, not the diagram.
 */
export function removeGroup(parts: SceneParts, groupId: string): SceneParts {
  const group = parts.objects.find((item) => item.id === groupId)
  if (!group) {
    return parts
  }
  return {
    objects: parts.objects
      .filter((item) => item.id !== groupId)
      .map((item) => (item.parent_id === groupId ? { ...item, parent_id: group.parent_id } : item)),
    nodes: parts.nodes.map((node) =>
      node.parent_object_id === groupId ? { ...node, parent_object_id: group.parent_id } : node,
    ),
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/lib/waypoints.ts` :

```ts
// The route of a link: the bends a person put in it.
//
// A link stays the relationship of the graph (docs/adr: objets de vue); only
// its stroke belongs to the view. With no bend it is the straight segment
// trimmed to the two borders, exactly as before; with bends it leaves each box
// towards the nearest bend. Pure functions, nothing is mutated.
import { clipToBox, edgeSegment, type Point, type Rect } from './diagramGeometry'

/** Mirrors the API's bound on bends per link; the API still decides. */
export const MAX_WAYPOINTS = 20

/** The points of the stroke, source border first, or null when there is nothing sensible to draw. */
export function routeOf(source: Rect, target: Rect, waypoints: Point[]): Point[] | null {
  if (waypoints.length === 0) {
    const segment = edgeSegment(source, target)
    return segment ? [segment.from, segment.to] : null
  }
  const first = waypoints[0]
  const last = waypoints[waypoints.length - 1]
  return [clipToBox(first, source), ...waypoints, clipToBox(last, target)]
}

/** The middle of each segment: where a handle to add a bend sits. */
export function midpoints(route: Point[]): Point[] {
  return route.slice(1).map((point, index) => ({
    x: (route[index].x + point.x) / 2,
    y: (route[index].y + point.y) / 2,
  }))
}

/** A bend added on `segment` (0 = the one leaving the source) sits at that index. */
export function insertWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], segment: number, at: Point): Point[] {
  if (waypoints.length >= MAX_WAYPOINTS) {
    return waypoints
  }
  return [...waypoints.slice(0, segment), at, ...waypoints.slice(segment)]
}

export function moveWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], index: number, to: Point): Point[] {
  return waypoints.map((point, position) => (position === index ? to : point))
}

export function removeWaypoint(waypoints: Point[], index: number): Point[] {
  return waypoints.filter((_, position) => position !== index)
}
```

**Files:**

- Create: `frontend/tests/useDiagramScene.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `removeGroup`, `moveWithChildren`, `groupAt`, `newId`, `insertWaypoint`/`moveWaypoint`/`removeWaypoint` (Task 15) ; `ApiError` de `lib/api`.
- Produces (TypeScript), ajouts au retour de `useDiagram()` :
  - `conflict: Ref<boolean>` ; `reloadAfterConflict(): Promise<void>` ; `pasteMine(): void` ; `discardMine(): void`
  - `addObject(kind: 'note' | 'group', at: Point): string`
  - `updateObject(id: string, patch: Partial<Pick<DiagramObject, 'text' | 'colour' | 'anchor_element_id'>>): void`
  - `moveObject(id: string, to: Point): void`, `resizeObject(id: string, size: Size): void` (gestes, clos par `endGesture`)
  - `removeObject(id: string): void` (une note disparaît ; un groupe remonte ses enfants)
  - `addWaypoint(relationshipId: string, segment: number, at: Point): void`, `moveWaypoint(relationshipId: string, index: number, to: Point): void` (gestes), `removeWaypoint(relationshipId: string, index: number): void`, `straighten(relationshipId: string): void`
  - `routes: ComputedRef<DiagramEdge[]>` n'est pas ajouté : `edges` suffit.
  - Invariants : retirer une boîte élague les routes dont une extrémité a quitté le diagramme et détache les notes ancrées sur son élément (sinon le serveur répond 422) ; déposer une boîte ou un objet dans un groupe fixe son parent à la fin du geste ; un conflit ne relance **jamais** seul.

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { SAVE_PAUSE_MS, useDiagram } from '../src/features/diagrams/useDiagram'
import { anElement, aRelationship, stubApi, type RecordedCall, type Route } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

const DIAGRAM_ID = 'dddddddd-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
const LINK_ID = 'rrrrrrrr-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
const A = anElement({ id: 'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', name: 'Facturation' })
const B = anElement({ id: 'bbbbbbbb-2222-4222-8222-222222222222', name: 'Commandes' })
const LINK = aRelationship({ id: LINK_ID, source_id: A.id, target_id: B.id })

const node = (element_id: string, x: number, y: number) => ({
  element_id,
  x,
  y,
  width: 132,
  height: 46,
  parent_object_id: null,
  z: 0,
})

function aDiagram(overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    id: DIAGRAM_ID,
    name: 'Vue',
    description: '',
    created_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
    updated_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
    revision: 0,
    nodes: [node(A.id, 10, 20), node(B.id, 400, 20)],
    objects: [],
    edges: [],
    elements: [A, B],
    relationships: [LINK],
    ...overrides,
  }
}

const READ = (overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Route => ({
  path: `/diagrams/${DIAGRAM_ID}`,
  body: aDiagram(overrides),
})
const SAVE: Route = {
  method: 'PUT',
  path: `/diagrams/${DIAGRAM_ID}/layout`,
  status: 204,
  headers: { 'X-Diagram-Revision': '1' },
}
const CONFLICT: Route = {
  method: 'PUT',
  path: `/diagrams/${DIAGRAM_ID}/layout`,
  status: 409,
  body: { error: 'diagram_revision_conflict', detail: 'the diagram is at revision 5, not 0' },
}

const saves = (calls: RecordedCall[]) => calls.filter((call) => call.method === 'PUT')
type Saved = {
  revision: number
  nodes: { element_id: string; parent_object_id: string | null; x: number }[]
  objects: { id: string; kind: string; parent_id: string | null; x: number; text: string; anchor_element_id: string | null }[]
  edges: { relationship_id: string; waypoints: { x: number; y: number }[] }[]
}
const body = (call: RecordedCall) => call.body as Saved

async function pause(): Promise<void> {
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(SAVE_PAUSE_MS)
  await vi.waitFor(() => undefined)
}

async function opened(routes: Route[] = [READ(), SAVE]) {
  const calls = stubApi(routes)
  const editor = useDiagram()
  await editor.open(DIAGRAM_ID)
  vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })
  return { calls, editor }
}

describe('useDiagram — notes and groups', () => {
  it('adds a group and a note and saves them with the revision it read', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened()

    const group = editor.addObject('group', { x: 0, y: 100 })
    const note = editor.addObject('note', { x: 50, y: 300 })
    await pause()

    const saved = body(saves(calls)[0])
    expect(saved.revision).toBe(0)
    expect(saved.objects.map((o) => o.id).sort()).toEqual([group, note].sort())
    expect(saved.objects.find((o) => o.id === note)?.kind).toBe('note')
  })

  it('drops a box into a group, and out of it again, at the end of the gesture', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    const group = editor.addObject('group', { x: 0, y: 100 }) // 320 x 200 at (0, 100)

    editor.move(A.id, { x: 50, y: 150 })
    editor.endGesture()
    expect(editor.nodes.value.find((n) => n.element_id === A.id)?.parent_object_id).toBe(group)

    editor.move(A.id, { x: 900, y: 900 })
    editor.endGesture()
    expect(editor.nodes.value.find((n) => n.element_id === A.id)?.parent_object_id).toBeNull()
  })

  it('moves a group with everything inside it', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    const group = editor.addObject('group', { x: 0, y: 100 })
    editor.move(A.id, { x: 50, y: 150 })
    editor.endGesture()

    editor.moveObject(group, { x: 100, y: 200 })

    expect(editor.nodes.value.find((n) => n.element_id === A.id)).toMatchObject({ x: 150, y: 250 })
  })

  it('removes a group by lifting its children, and takes it back', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    const outer = editor.addObject('group', { x: 0, y: 100 })
    const inner = editor.addObject('group', { x: 20, y: 120 })
    editor.updateObject(inner, { text: 'Interne' })
    // inner sits in outer, A in inner (each adopted where it is dropped)
    editor.moveObject(inner, { x: 20, y: 120 })
    editor.endGesture()
    editor.move(A.id, { x: 40, y: 140 })
    editor.endGesture()
    const parentOfA = () => editor.nodes.value.find((n) => n.element_id === A.id)?.parent_object_id
    expect(parentOfA()).toBe(inner)

    editor.removeObject(inner)

    expect(editor.objects.value.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual([outer])
    expect(parentOfA()).toBe(outer)
    editor.undo()
    expect(editor.objects.value.map((o) => o.id)).toContain(inner)
    expect(parentOfA()).toBe(inner)
  })

  it('removes an empty group and a note without touching a box', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    const empty = editor.addObject('group', { x: 600, y: 600 })
    const note = editor.addObject('note', { x: 700, y: 700 })

    editor.removeObject(empty)
    editor.removeObject(note)

    expect(editor.objects.value).toEqual([])
    expect(editor.boxes.value).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('folds the typing of a note into one undo step', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened()
    const note = editor.addObject('note', { x: 0, y: 0 })

    editor.updateObject(note, { text: 'A' })
    editor.updateObject(note, { text: 'AB' })
    editor.updateObject(note, { text: 'ABC' })
    editor.undo()

    expect(editor.objects.value[0].text).toBe('')
  })
})

describe('useDiagram — routed links', () => {
  it('adds a bend and drags it as one gesture, then straightens the link', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened()

    editor.addWaypoint(LINK_ID, 0, { x: 250, y: 150 })
    editor.moveWaypoint(LINK_ID, 0, { x: 260, y: 160 })
    editor.endGesture()
    await pause()
    expect(body(saves(calls)[0]).edges).toEqual([
      { relationship_id: LINK_ID, waypoints: [{ x: 260, y: 160 }] },
    ])

    editor.undo()
    expect(editor.edges.value).toEqual([])
    editor.redo()
    editor.straighten(LINK_ID)
    expect(editor.edges.value).toEqual([])
  })

  it('removes one bend and keeps the others', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened([
      READ({ edges: [{ relationship_id: LINK_ID, waypoints: [{ x: 1, y: 1 }, { x: 2, y: 2 }] }] }),
      SAVE,
    ])

    editor.removeWaypoint(LINK_ID, 0)

    expect(editor.edges.value[0].waypoints).toEqual([{ x: 2, y: 2 }])
  })

  it('drops a route and a note anchor when the box they hang on leaves the diagram', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened([
      READ({
        edges: [{ relationship_id: LINK_ID, waypoints: [{ x: 250, y: 150 }] }],
        objects: [
          {
            id: 'nnnnnnnn-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
            kind: 'note',
            x: 0,
            y: 0,
            width: 100,
            height: 50,
            text: 'à voir',
            colour: null,
            z: 0,
            parent_id: null,
            anchor_element_id: B.id,
          },
        ],
      }),
      SAVE,
    ])

    editor.removeMany([B.id])
    await pause()

    const saved = body(saves(calls)[0])
    expect(saved.edges).toEqual([])
    expect(saved.objects[0].anchor_element_id).toBeNull()
    editor.undo()
    expect(editor.edges.value).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})

describe('useDiagram — undo after a save', () => {
  it('restores the groups too, and saves the restored scene', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened()
    editor.addObject('group', { x: 0, y: 100 })
    await pause()
    expect(saves(calls)).toHaveLength(1)

    editor.undo()
    await pause()

    expect(saves(calls)).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(body(saves(calls)[1]).objects).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe('useDiagram — a revision conflict', () => {
  it('says so, keeps the local work, and never retries by itself', async () => {
    const { calls, editor } = await opened([READ(), CONFLICT])

    editor.addObject('note', { x: 0, y: 0 })
    await pause()
    await pause()

    expect(editor.conflict.value).toBe(true)
    expect(editor.saveError.value).toContain('revision 5')
    expect(saves(calls)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(editor.objects.value).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('reloads the colleague\'s version, then pastes mine back on the new revision', async () => {
    stubApi([READ(), CONFLICT])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(DIAGRAM_ID)
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })
    editor.addObject('note', { x: 0, y: 0 })
    await pause()
    expect(editor.conflict.value).toBe(true)

    // Now the server answers with the colleague's diagram, at revision 5.
    stubApi([READ({ revision: 5, nodes: [node(A.id, 99, 99)], elements: [A], relationships: [] }), SAVE])
    await editor.reloadAfterConflict()
    expect(editor.reloaded.value).toBe(true)
    expect(editor.revision.value).toBe(5)
    expect(editor.boxes.value).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(editor.objects.value).toEqual([])
    expect(editor.canUndo.value).toBe(false) // a rebuilt history cannot undo across a conflict

    editor.pasteMine()
    await pause()

    expect(editor.conflict.value).toBe(false)
    expect(editor.reloaded.value).toBe(false)
    expect(editor.objects.value).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(editor.boxes.value).toHaveLength(2) // my scene, with the elements it needs
  })

  it('lets the user give up their changes instead', async () => {
    const { editor } = await opened([READ(), CONFLICT])
    editor.addObject('note', { x: 0, y: 0 })
    await pause()
    stubApi([READ({ revision: 5 }), SAVE])

    await editor.reloadAfterConflict()
    editor.discardMine()

    expect(editor.conflict.value).toBe(false)
    expect(editor.objects.value).toEqual([])
  })
})
```

2. Remplacer, dans le bloc d'historique de la Task 4, `copy`, `gestureBefore` et `record` par la **scène complète** :

```ts
  type Scene = { nodes: DiagramNode[]; objects: DiagramObject[]; edges: DiagramEdge[] }
  /** A plain-data copy of the whole scene: what a history entry remembers. */
  function cloneScene(): Scene {
    return {
      nodes: nodes.value.map((node) => ({ ...node })),
      objects: objects.value.map((item) => ({ ...item })),
      edges: edges.value.map((edge) => ({
        ...edge,
        waypoints: edge.waypoints.map((point) => ({ ...point })),
      })),
    }
  }
  function restore(scene: Scene): void {
    // A JSON round trip rather than `structuredClone`: the scene is plain data,
    // and this works wherever the SPA or its tests run.
    const fresh = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(scene)) as Scene
    nodes.value = fresh.nodes
    objects.value = fresh.objects
    edges.value = fresh.edges
  }
  /** The scene as it was when the gesture in progress began. */
  let gestureBefore: Scene | null = null
  /** Boxes and objects the pointer itself moved: the only ones that change group. */
  const grabbed = new Set<string>()

  function record(label: string, before: Scene, mergeKey?: string): void {
    const after = cloneScene()
    const restoring = (scene: Scene) => () => {
      restore(scene)
      persist()
    }
    history.record({
      label,
      undo: restoring(before),
      do: restoring(after),
      ...(mergeKey ? { mergeKey } : {}),
    })
  }

  function differing<T>(before: T[], after: T[], key: (item: T) => string): string[] {
    const old = new Map(before.map((item) => [key(item), JSON.stringify(item)]))
    return after.filter((item) => old.get(key(item)) !== JSON.stringify(item)).map(key)
  }
```

3. Remplacer `place`, `move`, `resize`, `endGesture`, `removeMany`, `remove`, `arrange` par leur version « scène » (les noms et signatures sont ceux de la Task 4) :

```ts
  function place(element: ElementRead, at: Point): void {
    if (placed.value.has(element.id)) {
      return
    }
    const before = cloneScene()
    nodes.value.push({
      element_id: element.id,
      x: at.x,
      y: at.y,
      ...DEFAULT_BOX,
      parent_object_id: null,
      z: 0,
    })
    if (!byId.value.has(element.id)) {
      elements.value.push(element)
    }
    record('Ajouter un élément', before)
    persist()
  }

  /** Move a box on screen only; `endGesture` once the gesture is over. */
  function move(elementId: string, to: Point): void {
    const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === elementId)
    if (node) {
      gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
      grabbed.add(elementId)
      node.x = to.x
      node.y = to.y
    }
  }

  /** Resize a box on screen only; `endGesture` once the gesture is over. */
  function resize(elementId: string, size: Size): void {
    const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === elementId)
    if (node) {
      gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
      node.width = size.width
      node.height = size.height
    }
  }

  /** The group (if any) a thing just dropped should now belong to. */
  function adopt(): void {
    for (const id of grabbed) {
      const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === id)
      if (node) {
        node.parent_object_id = groupAt(objects.value, centreOf(node), { id: null, isGroup: false })
        continue
      }
      const item = objects.value.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id)
      if (item) {
        item.parent_id = groupAt(objects.value, centreOf(item), {
          id: item.id,
          isGroup: item.kind === 'group',
        })
      }
    }
    grabbed.clear()
  }

  /**
   * The gesture that `move`, `resize`, `moveObject` and the bend setters were
   * part of is over: boxes dropped into a group join it, then one history
   * entry for all of it, then the save. Arrow-key nudges of the same things
   * fold into one entry (`source: 'keyboard'`).
   */
  function endGesture(source?: 'keyboard'): void {
    const before = gestureBefore
    gestureBefore = null
    if (before) {
      adopt()
      const after = cloneScene()
      const changed = [
        ...differing(before.nodes, after.nodes, (n) => n.element_id),
        ...differing(before.objects, after.objects, (o) => o.id),
        ...differing(before.edges, after.edges, (e) => e.relationship_id),
      ]
      if (changed.length > 0) {
        const key = source === 'keyboard' ? `keyboard:${changed.sort().join(',')}` : undefined
        record('Déplacer ou redimensionner', before, key)
      }
    }
    grabbed.clear()
    persist()
  }

  /**
   * Keep the scene one the server accepts after boxes left it: a route whose
   * link no longer joins two placed elements is dropped (the API answers 422
   * to a stray one), and a note anchored on a removed element is let go.
   */
  function prune(): void {
    const present = new Set(nodes.value.map((node) => node.element_id))
    const joined = new Set(
      relationships.value
        .filter((link) => present.has(link.source_id) && present.has(link.target_id))
        .map((link) => link.id),
    )
    edges.value = edges.value.filter((edge) => joined.has(edge.relationship_id))
    objects.value = objects.value.map((item) =>
      item.anchor_element_id && !present.has(item.anchor_element_id)
        ? { ...item, anchor_element_id: null }
        : item,
    )
  }

  /** Take boxes off the diagram. The elements themselves are never deleted. */
  function removeMany(elementIds: string[]): void {
    const before = cloneScene()
    nodes.value = nodes.value.filter((node) => !elementIds.includes(node.element_id))
    prune()
    record('Retirer du diagramme', before)
    persist()
  }

  function remove(elementId: string): void {
    removeMany([elementId])
  }

  /** Apply the new top-lefts an arrangement computed, as one undoable step. */
  function arrange(label: string, elementIds: string[], compute: (boxes: Placed[]) => Moved[]): void {
    const chosen: Placed[] = nodes.value
      .filter((node) => elementIds.includes(node.element_id))
      .map((node) => ({
        id: node.element_id,
        x: node.x,
        y: node.y,
        width: node.width,
        height: node.height,
      }))
    const before = cloneScene()
    let changed = false
    for (const target of compute(chosen)) {
      const node = nodes.value.find((candidate) => candidate.element_id === target.id)
      if (node && (node.x !== target.x || node.y !== target.y)) {
        node.x = target.x
        node.y = target.y
        changed = true
      }
    }
    if (changed) {
      record(label, before)
      persist()
    }
  }
```

4. Ajouter les opérations sur les objets et les coudes, juste après `distribute` :

```ts
  // --- Notes and groups (docs/adr: objets de vue) -------------------------

  const DEFAULT_OBJECT: Record<'note' | 'group', Size & { text: string; z: number }> = {
    note: { width: 180, height: 90, text: '', z: 1 },
    group: { width: 320, height: 200, text: 'Groupe', z: -1 },
  }

  /** A new note or group at `at`; returns its id. A group sits behind, a note in front. */
  function addObject(kind: 'note' | 'group', at: Point): string {
    const before = cloneScene()
    const id = newId()
    const base = DEFAULT_OBJECT[kind]
    objects.value.push({
      id,
      kind,
      x: at.x,
      y: at.y,
      width: base.width,
      height: base.height,
      text: base.text,
      colour: null,
      z: base.z,
      parent_id: null,
      anchor_element_id: null,
    })
    record(kind === 'group' ? 'Ajouter un groupe' : 'Ajouter une note', before)
    persist()
    return id
  }

  function updateObject(
    id: string,
    patch: Partial<Pick<DiagramObject, 'text' | 'colour' | 'anchor_element_id'>>,
  ): void {
    const item = objects.value.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id)
    if (!item) {
      return
    }
    const before = cloneScene()
    Object.assign(item, patch)
    // Typing is one step, not one per key.
    record('Modifier', before, `edit:${id}:${Object.keys(patch).sort().join(',')}`)
    persist()
  }

  /** Move a note or a group on screen; a group takes everything inside it along. */
  function moveObject(id: string, to: Point): void {
    const item = objects.value.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id)
    if (!item) {
      return
    }
    gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
    grabbed.add(id)
    const moved = moveWithChildren(
      { nodes: nodes.value, objects: objects.value },
      id,
      to.x - item.x,
      to.y - item.y,
    )
    nodes.value = moved.nodes
    objects.value = moved.objects
  }

  function resizeObject(id: string, size: Size): void {
    const item = objects.value.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id)
    if (item) {
      gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
      item.width = size.width
      item.height = size.height
    }
  }

  /** A note disappears; a group disappears and hands what it held to its own parent. */
  function removeObject(id: string): void {
    const item = objects.value.find((candidate) => candidate.id === id)
    if (!item) {
      return
    }
    const before = cloneScene()
    if (item.kind === 'group') {
      const next = removeGroup({ nodes: nodes.value, objects: objects.value }, id)
      nodes.value = next.nodes
      objects.value = next.objects
    } else {
      objects.value = objects.value.filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== id)
    }
    record(item.kind === 'group' ? 'Retirer le groupe' : 'Supprimer la note', before)
    persist()
  }

  // --- The route of a link ---------------------------------------------

  function waypointsOf(relationshipId: string) {
    return edges.value.find((edge) => edge.relationship_id === relationshipId)?.waypoints ?? []
  }

  function setWaypoints(relationshipId: string, waypoints: { x: number; y: number }[]): void {
    const others = edges.value.filter((edge) => edge.relationship_id !== relationshipId)
    // No bend left = no row: the link goes back to its straight segment.
    edges.value = waypoints.length > 0 ? [...others, { relationship_id: relationshipId, waypoints }] : others
  }

  /** Start a bend on a segment; the drag that follows is `moveWaypoint`, ended by `endGesture`. */
  function addWaypoint(relationshipId: string, segment: number, at: Point): void {
    gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
    setWaypoints(relationshipId, insertWaypoint(waypointsOf(relationshipId), segment, at))
  }

  function moveWaypoint(relationshipId: string, index: number, to: Point): void {
    gestureBefore ??= cloneScene()
    setWaypoints(relationshipId, movedWaypoint(waypointsOf(relationshipId), index, to))
  }

  function removeWaypoint(relationshipId: string, index: number): void {
    const before = cloneScene()
    setWaypoints(relationshipId, removedWaypoint(waypointsOf(relationshipId), index))
    record('Supprimer un coude', before)
    persist()
  }

  /** Back to the straight segment. */
  function straighten(relationshipId: string): void {
    const before = cloneScene()
    setWaypoints(relationshipId, [])
    record('Redresser le lien', before)
    persist()
  }
```

5. Conflit de révision. Ajouter l'état après `saveError` : `const conflict = ref(false)`, `const reloaded = ref(false)` (vrai une fois la version de l'autre chargée, tant que mes modifications ne sont ni recollées ni abandonnées) et `let mine: (Scene & { elements: ElementRead[]; relationships: RelationshipRead[] }) | null = null`. Dans le `catch (caught)` de `saveNow`, **avant** le bloc existant, ajouter :

```ts
        if (caught instanceof ApiError && caught.code === 'diagram_revision_conflict') {
          // Somebody saved first. Nothing is retried: the next step is the
          // user's — reload their version, paste mine over it, or give up.
          conflict.value = true
          saveError.value = caught.message
          return
        }
```
(le `return` dans le `catch` laisse `pending = false`, posé plus haut dans `saveNow`, et le `finally` remet `saving` à faux). Ajouter :

```ts
  /** Keep my scene aside, then load the version the colleague saved. */
  async function reloadAfterConflict(): Promise<void> {
    const current = diagram.value
    if (!current) {
      return
    }
    autosave.cancel()
    mine = {
      ...cloneScene(),
      elements: elements.value.map((element) => ({ ...element })),
      relationships: relationships.value.map((link) => ({ ...link })),
    }
    await open(current.id) // empties the history: nothing can be undone across a conflict
    saveError.value = ''
    reloaded.value = true
  }

  /** Put my scene back over the colleague's, on the revision just loaded, and save it. */
  function pasteMine(): void {
    if (!mine) {
      return
    }
    restore(mine)
    for (const element of mine.elements) {
      if (!byId.value.has(element.id)) {
        elements.value.push(element)
      }
    }
    for (const link of mine.relationships) {
      if (!relationships.value.some((known) => known.id === link.id)) {
        relationships.value.push(link)
      }
    }
    mine = null
    conflict.value = false
    reloaded.value = false
    saveError.value = ''
    persist()
  }

  function discardMine(): void {
    mine = null
    conflict.value = false
    reloaded.value = false
    saveError.value = ''
  }
```
Dans `open`'s callback ne rien changer sinon `conflict.value` reste vrai tant que `pasteMine`/`discardMine` n'est pas appelé (voulu). Dans `close()`, ajouter `conflict.value = false`, `reloaded.value = false` et `mine = null`.

```ts
  const LINK = 'rrrrrrrr-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
  const link = aRelationship({ id: LINK, source_id: A.id, target_id: B.id })

    x: 0,
    y: 100,

    height: 200,

    colour: null,
    z: -1,
    parent_id: null,
    anchor_element_id: null,
  }

    ...aGroup,
    id: 'nnnnnnnn-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
    kind: 'note' as const,
    x: 500,
    y: 100,
    width: 180,
    height: 90,

    z: 1,
    anchor_element_id: B.id,
  }
  const drawn = (props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>

    drawn()

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Groupe « Domaine Paiement »/ })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Note/ })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(/À revoir <b>vite<\/b>/)).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(document.querySelector('b')).toBeNull()
  })

  })

    await fireEvent(
      screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Groupe « Domaine Paiement »/ }),

    await fireEvent(svg(), pointer('pointerup', 60, 160))

    expect(rendered.emitted().moved).toHaveLength(1)

    const note = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Note/ })

    await fireEvent.keyDown(note, { key: 'Delete' })

    expect(rendered.emitted()['bend-move']).toEqual([[LINK, 0, { x: 260, y: 170 }]])
    expect(rendered.emitted().moved).toHaveLength(1)

    })

    expect(document.querySelector('.canvas__bend')).toBeNull()
    expect(document.querySelector('.canvas__resize')).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

4. Remplacer le computed `edges` et ajouter les calculs d'objets :

```ts
const routes = computed(
  () => new Map((props.routed ?? []).map((edge) => [edge.relationship_id, edge.waypoints])),
)

/** Links whose two ends are drawn, with their stroke, their bends and the handles to add one. */
const edges = computed(() =>
  props.relationships.flatMap((relationship) => {
    const source = byId.value.get(relationship.source_id)
    const target = byId.value.get(relationship.target_id)
    const waypoints = routes.value.get(relationship.id) ?? []
    const route = source && target ? routeOf(source, target, waypoints) : null
    if (!route) {
      return []
    }
    const middle = Math.floor((route.length - 1) / 2)
    return [
      {
        relationship,
        route,
        waypoints,
        points: route.map((point) => `${point.x},${point.y}`).join(' '),
        label: {
          x: (route[middle].x + route[middle + 1].x) / 2,
          y: (route[middle].y + route[middle + 1].y) / 2 - 4,
        },
        handles: waypoints.length < MAX_WAYPOINTS ? midpoints(route) : [],
      },
    ]
  }),
)

const objectsOf = (kind: 'group' | 'note') =>
  [...(props.objects ?? [])].filter((item) => item.kind === kind).sort((a, b) => a.z - b.z)
const groups = computed(() => objectsOf('group'))
const notes = computed(() => objectsOf('note'))
const objectById = computed(() => new Map((props.objects ?? []).map((item) => [item.id, item])))
const selectedObject = computed(() => objectById.value.get(props.selectedObjectId ?? ''))

/** The dotted line from a note to the element it is anchored on — drawing, not a relationship. */
const anchors = computed(() =>
  notes.value.flatMap((note) => {
    const box = note.anchor_element_id ? byId.value.get(note.anchor_element_id) : undefined
    const segment = box ? edgeSegment(note, box) : null
    return segment ? [{ id: note.id, ...segment }] : []
  }),
)

const fillOf = (item: DiagramObject, fallback: string) => safeColour(item.colour ?? '', fallback)

/** The lines of a note that fit its height, each cut to its width. */
function noteLines(item: DiagramObject): string[] {
  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.floor((item.height - 12) / 14))
  return item.text.split('\n').slice(0, rows).map((line) => shortenToWidth(line, item.width - 16))
}
```

5. Ajouter les gestionnaires d'objets et de coudes :

```ts
function grabObject(event: PointerEvent, item: DiagramObject): void {
  if (event.button !== 0 || props.readonly || spaceHeld.value) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  const pointer = at(event)
  gesture.value = {
    kind: 'object-move',
    id: item.id,
    offset: { x: pointer.x - item.x, y: pointer.y - item.y },
    moved: false,
  }
}

function startObjectResize(event: PointerEvent): void {
  const item = selectedObject.value
  if (event.button !== 0 || !item || props.readonly) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  const pointer = at(event)
  gesture.value = {
    kind: 'object-resize',
    id: item.id,
    offset: { x: pointer.x - (item.x + item.width), y: pointer.y - (item.y + item.height) },
    moved: false,
  }
}

/** Start a bend on a segment: it exists the moment it is grabbed, and follows the pointer. */
function startBend(event: PointerEvent, relationshipId: string, segment: number): void {
  if (event.button !== 0 || props.readonly) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  emit('bend-add', relationshipId, segment, at(event))
  gesture.value = { kind: 'bend', relationshipId, index: segment, moved: true }
}

function grabBend(event: PointerEvent, relationshipId: string, index: number): void {
  if (event.button !== 0 || props.readonly) {
    return
  }
  capture(event)
  gesture.value = { kind: 'bend', relationshipId, index, moved: false }
}

function removeObjectIfSelected(item: DiagramObject): void {
  if (item.id === props.selectedObjectId && !props.readonly) {
    emit('remove-object', item.id)
  }
}

function onObjectKey(event: KeyboardEvent, item: DiagramObject): void {
  const delta = nudgeDelta(event.key, event.shiftKey)
  if (!delta || props.readonly) {
    return
  }
  event.preventDefault()
  if (event.altKey) {
    emit('resize-object', item.id, {
      width: Math.max(MIN_BOX.width, item.width + delta.x),
      height: Math.max(MIN_BOX.height, item.height + delta.y),
    })
  } else {
    emit('move-object', item.id, {
      x: Math.max(0, item.x + delta.x),
      y: Math.max(0, item.y + delta.y),
    })
  }
  emit('moved', 'keyboard')
}
```

6. **Remplacer** `drag`, `interrupt` et `release` (versions des Tasks 5 et 6) par leur forme définitive :

```ts
function drag(event: PointerEvent): void {
  const current = gesture.value
  if (!current) {
    return
  }
  if (current.kind === 'pan') {
    view.value = {
      ...view.value,
      pan: {
        x: current.origin.x - (event.clientX - current.start.x) / view.value.zoom,
        y: current.origin.y - (event.clientY - current.start.y) / view.value.zoom,
      },
    }
    return
  }
  const pointer = at(event)
  switch (current.kind) {
    case 'link':
    case 'band':
      current.to = pointer
      return
    case 'bend':
      current.moved = true
      emit('bend-move', current.relationshipId, current.index, pointer)
      return
    case 'object-move':
      current.moved = true
      emit('move-object', current.id, {
        x: Math.max(0, pointer.x - current.offset.x),
        y: Math.max(0, pointer.y - current.offset.y),
      })
      return
    case 'object-resize': {
      const item = objectById.value.get(current.id)
      current.moved = true
      if (item) {
        emit(
          'resize-object',
          current.id,
          resizedTo(item, { x: pointer.x - current.offset.x, y: pointer.y - current.offset.y }),
        )
      }
      return
    }
    case 'resize': {
      const box = byId.value.get(current.elementId)
      current.moved = true
      if (box) {
        emit(
          'resize',
          current.elementId,
          resizedTo(box, { x: pointer.x - current.offset.x, y: pointer.y - current.offset.y }),
        )
      }
      return
    }
    case 'move':
      current.moved = true
      for (const held of current.grabs) {
        // ponytail: each box is clamped to the canvas on its own, so a group
        // dragged against the left edge squeezes together; clamp the group's
        // bounding box instead if that is ever noticed.
        emit('move', held.elementId, {
          x: Math.max(0, pointer.x - held.offset.x),
          y: Math.max(0, pointer.y - held.offset.y),
        })
      }
  }
}

/**
 * The system took the pointer away mid-gesture — a touch cancelled, a window
 * losing focus. A move already on screen is still reported, so it is saved; a
 * link or a band is never asked for, since nobody chose where it ends.
 */
function interrupt(): void {
  const current = gesture.value
  gesture.value = null
  if (current && 'moved' in current && current.moved) {
    emit('moved')
  }
}

function release(event: PointerEvent): void {
  const current = gesture.value
  gesture.value = null
  if (!current || current.kind === 'pan') {
    return
  }
  if (current.kind === 'band') {
    const band = rubberBand(current.from, current.to)
    const hits = band.width + band.height < BAND_SLOP ? [] : boxesIn(band, props.boxes.map(rectOf))
    emit('select-many', hits)
    return
  }
  if (current.kind === 'link') {
    const pointer = at(event)
    // The topmost box under the pointer: the last one drawn.
    const target = [...props.boxes].reverse().find(
      (box) =>
        pointer.x >= box.x &&
        pointer.x <= box.x + box.width &&
        pointer.y >= box.y &&
        pointer.y <= box.y + box.height,
    )
    if (target && target.element.id !== current.sourceId) {
      emit('connect', current.sourceId, target.element.id)
    }
    return
  }
  if (current.moved) {
    emit('moved')
  }
}
```

7. Template. Dans `<svg>`, **avant** `<g v-for="edge in edges" …>` ajouter les groupes ; remplacer le bloc des arêtes ; **après** le `<g v-for="box in boxes">` ajouter notes et ancres ; en fin de `<svg>`, la poignée de redimensionnement d'objet :

```vue
        <g
          v-for="item in groups"
          :key="item.id"
          class="canvas__object canvas__object--group"
          :class="{ 'canvas__object--selected': item.id === selectedObjectId }"
          role="button"
          tabindex="0"
          :aria-pressed="item.id === selectedObjectId"
          :aria-label="`Groupe « ${item.text} »`"
          @pointerdown="grabObject($event, item)"
          @click="emit('select-object', item.id)"
          @keydown="onObjectKey($event, item)"
          @keydown.delete.prevent="removeObjectIfSelected(item)"
        >
          <title>{{ item.text }}</title>
          <rect
            :x="item.x"
            :y="item.y"
            :width="item.width"
            :height="item.height"
            rx="8"
            :fill="fillOf(item, '#efeaf8')"
            fill-opacity="0.55"
          />
          <text :x="item.x + 10" :y="item.y + 18" class="canvas__group-title">
            {{ shortenToWidth(item.text, item.width - 20) }}
          </text>
        </g>

        <g v-for="edge in edges" :key="edge.relationship.id" class="canvas__edge"
           :class="{ 'canvas__edge--selected': edge.relationship.id === selectedEdgeId }">
          <polyline
            :points="edge.points"
            fill="none"
            marker-end="url(#ea-diagram-arrow)"
            @click="emit('select-edge', edge.relationship.id)"
          />
          <text :x="edge.label.x" :y="edge.label.y" text-anchor="middle">
            {{ verbOf(edge.relationship.relationship_type, edge.relationship.access_type) }}
          </text>
          <template v-if="!readonly">
            <circle
              v-for="(handle, segment) in edge.handles"
              :key="`add-${segment}`"
              class="canvas__bend canvas__bend--add"
              :cx="handle.x"
              :cy="handle.y"
              r="4"
              @pointerdown.stop="startBend($event, edge.relationship.id, segment)"
            >
              <title>Glisse pour ajouter un coude au lien</title>
            </circle>
            <circle
              v-for="(bend, index) in edge.waypoints"
              :key="`bend-${index}`"
              class="canvas__bend"
              :cx="bend.x"
              :cy="bend.y"
              r="5"
              @pointerdown.stop="grabBend($event, edge.relationship.id, index)"
              @dblclick.stop="emit('bend-remove', edge.relationship.id, index)"
            >
              <title>Glisse pour déplacer le coude ; double-clic pour le supprimer</title>
            </circle>
          </template>
        </g>
```

  - `ExportScene` gagne `groups?: ExportObject[]`, `notes?: ExportObject[]` avec `type ExportObject = { id: string; text: string; fill: string; x: number; y: number; width: number; height: number }` ; `ExportEdge.points` porte désormais les coudes

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import ObjectInspector from '../src/features/diagrams/ObjectInspector.vue'

const GROUP = {
  id: 'gggggggg-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
  kind: 'group' as const,
  x: 0,
  y: 0,
  width: 300,
  height: 200,
  text: 'Domaine',
  colour: null,
  z: 0,
  parent_id: null,
  anchor_element_id: null,
}
const NOTE = { ...GROUP, id: 'nnnnnnnn-0000-4000-8000-000000000000', kind: 'note' as const, text: 'À revoir' }
const CHILDREN = [
  { id: 'a', name: 'Facturation' },
  { id: 'b', name: 'Commandes' },
]

function draw(props: Record<string, unknown>) {
  return render(ObjectInspector, {
    props: { anchors: CHILDREN, children: [], canWrite: true, ...props },
  })
}

describe('ObjectInspector', () => {
  it('edits the text of a note as the user types', async () => {
    const rendered = draw({ item: NOTE })

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Texte de la note'), 'Voir ADR 0031')

    expect(rendered.emitted().update).toEqual([[{ text: 'Voir ADR 0031' }]])
  })

  it('anchors a note on an element, or lets it go', async () => {
    const rendered = draw({ item: NOTE })

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Ancrer sur un élément'), 'a')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Ancrer sur un élément'), '')

    expect(rendered.emitted().update).toEqual([
      [{ anchor_element_id: 'a' }],
      [{ anchor_element_id: null }],
    ])
  })

  it('offers no anchor for a group, and says removing it keeps its content', () => {
    draw({ item: GROUP })

    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Ancrer sur un élément')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Retirer le groupe' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByText(/les éléments qu'il contient restent sur le diagramme/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('links two boxes of a group only after an explicit choice of the pair', async () => {
    const rendered = draw({ item: GROUP, children: CHILDREN })

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Relier le contenu…' })).toBeDisabled()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Source'), 'a')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Cible'), 'b')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Relier le contenu…' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().link).toEqual([['a', 'b']])
  })

  it('straightens a routed link', async () => {
    const rendered = draw({ edge: { relationshipId: 'r', label: 'sert', bends: 2 } })

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Redresser le lien' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().straighten).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('is read-only for a reader', () => {
    draw({ item: NOTE, canWrite: false })

    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Texte de la note')).toBeDisabled()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Supprimer|Retirer/ })).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

```ts
describe('serializeSvg — groups and notes', () => {
  const object = { id: 'g', text: 'Domaine <Paiement>', fill: '#efeaf8', x: 0, y: 0, width: 300, height: 200 }

  it('draws a group behind and a note in front, their text escaped', () => {
    const svg = serializeSvg({
      ...SCENE,
      groups: [object],
      notes: [{ ...object, id: 'n', text: 'ligne 1\nligne 2 & <b>', fill: '"><script>' }],
    })

    expect(svg.indexOf('Domaine &lt;Paiement&gt;')).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(svg.indexOf('Domaine')).toBeLessThan(svg.indexOf('Facturation'))
    expect(svg.indexOf('ligne 1')).toBeGreaterThan(svg.indexOf('Facturation'))
    expect(svg).toContain('ligne 2 &amp; &lt;b&gt;')
    expect(svg).not.toContain('<script')
  })

  it('counts them in the size of the image', () => {
    const size = exportSize({ ...SCENE, groups: [{ ...object, x: -100, width: 50 }] })

    expect(size.width).toBe(300 + 132 - -100 + 48)
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/features/diagrams/AnnotationPalette.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// The two things a diagram can draw that are not elements: a note and a group.
// Neither is a fact — a group says "drawn together", never "composed of".
const emit = defineEmits<{ 'add-note': []; 'add-group': [] }>()
</script>

<template>
  <section class="annotations" aria-label="Annotations">
    <h3>Annotations</h3>
    <button type="button" @click="emit('add-note')">Ajouter une note</button>
```

```ts
  for (const note of scene.notes ?? []) {
    out.push(
      `<rect x="${num(note.x)}" y="${num(note.y)}" width="${num(note.width)}" height="${num(note.height)}" rx="3" fill="${safeColour(note.fill, '#fff4b0')}" stroke="${stroke}"/>`,
```

`frontend/src/features/diagrams/exportScene.ts` : remplacer la fonction par

```ts
import { routeOf } from '../../lib/waypoints'
import type { ExportObject, ExportScene } from '../../lib/diagramExport'
import type { Box, DiagramEdge, DiagramObject, RelationshipRead } from './useDiagram'

const FILLS = { group: '#efeaf8', note: '#fff4b0' } as const

const exported = (item: DiagramObject): ExportObject => ({
  id: item.id,
  text: item.text,
  fill: item.colour ?? FILLS[item.kind],
  x: item.x,
  y: item.y,
  width: item.width,
  height: item.height,
})

export function buildExportScene(
  boxes: Box[],
  relationships: RelationshipRead[],
  typeLabel: (value: string) => string,
  routed: DiagramEdge[] = [],
  objects: DiagramObject[] = [],
): ExportScene {
  const byId = new Map(boxes.map((box) => [box.element.id, box]))
  const bends = new Map(routed.map((edge) => [edge.relationship_id, edge.waypoints]))
  const ordered = [...objects].sort((a, b) => a.z - b.z)
  return {
    boxes: boxes.map((box) => ({
      id: box.element.id,
      name: box.element.name,
      type: typeLabel(box.element.element_type),
      fill: LAYER_COLOURS[box.element.layer],
      x: box.x,
      y: box.y,
      width: box.width,
      height: box.height,
    })),
    edges: relationships.flatMap((relationship) => {
      const source = byId.get(relationship.source_id)
      const target = byId.get(relationship.target_id)
      const points =
        source && target ? routeOf(source, target, bends.get(relationship.id) ?? []) : null
      return points
        ? [
            {
              id: relationship.id,
              points,
              label: verbOf(relationship.relationship_type, relationship.access_type),
            },
          ]
        : []
    }),
    groups: ordered.filter((item) => item.kind === 'group').map(exported),
    notes: ordered.filter((item) => item.kind === 'note').map(exported),
  }
}
```
(garder l'import de `LAYER_COLOURS` et `verbOf` ; retirer l'import de `edgeSegment`.) Dans `DiagramsSection.vue` `exportAs` : appeler `buildExportScene(editor.boxes.value, editor.relationships.value, metamodel.labelOf, editor.edges.value, editor.objects.value)`.

```ts
const selectedObjectId = ref('')

const selectedObject = computed(() => editor.objects.value.find((item) => item.id === selectedObjectId.value))
```

```ts
function addAnnotation(kind: 'note' | 'group'): void {

  selectedObjectId.value = editor.addObject(kind, { x: 40 + offset, y: 40 + offset })
  selectedEdgeId.value = ''

  selectedEdgeId.value = ''

  selectedObjectId.value = ''

  selectedObjectId.value = ''
}
```

````markdown
---
titre: Objets de vue — notes, groupes, tracés de liens — et concurrence optimiste des diagrammes
date: 2026-10-10
statut: Accepté
affects: backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_objects.py, backend/src/ea/domain/diagrams.py, backend/src/ea/db/models/diagram.py, backend/migrations/versions/0009_diagram_scene.py, backend/src/ea/repositories/diagram_store.py, backend/src/ea/services/diagrams.py, backend/src/ea/api/diagrams.py, backend/src/ea/api/schemas.py, backend/src/ea/mcp/server.py, frontend/src/features/diagrams/
---

# NN. Objets de vue : notes, groupes, tracés — et révision

Date : 2026-10-10
Statut : Accepté

## Contexte

[`0031`](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md) a posé qu'un diagramme est une *vue* : il retient quels
éléments sont dessinés et où, et ne possède aucun fait. Un schéma d'architecte dit plus que des boîtes :
une note qui explique un choix, un cadre « Domaine Paiement » autour de trois applications, un lien qui
contourne un obstacle. Rien de cela n'est un élément ni une relation. En parallèle, `0031` laissait
une dette en « À surveiller » : l'enregistrement complet de la disposition à chaque geste est sans
conflit pour un seul auteur, mais deux personnes se remplacent l'une l'autre en silence.

## Décision

**Notes et groupes sont des objets de vue dans une table polymorphe ; un groupe n'est pas un élément du
graphe et ne crée jamais de relation ; un lien dont le tracé change garde sa relation et ne retient que
ses coudes ; chaque enregistrement est comparé à une révision.**

| Point | Choix |
|---|---|
| Objets de vue | Une table `diagram_objects` (migration `0009`), `kind` ∈ `note`/`group` par contrainte `CHECK` ; même géométrie, même empilement `z`, même imbrication `parent_id` (clé étrangère `SET NULL`) |
| Une table, pas deux | Les deux genres ont la même lecture, le même remplacement transactionnel et la même détection de cycle ; seul le sens de `text` change (titre d'un groupe, corps d'une note) |
| Groupe ≠ élément | Aucune ligne dans `elements`, aucune relation. Supprimer un groupe remonte ses enfants au parent du groupe et ne touche aucun élément. La composition se tire à la main (`POST /relationships`, métamodèle consulté) ; « Relier le contenu… » ouvre le formulaire de lien existant pour une paire choisie, une relation à la fois |
| Boîte dans un groupe | `diagram_nodes.parent_object_id` (`SET NULL`) et `z` ; profondeur ≤ `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH = 5`, refusée par `check_scene` (cycle, parent qui n'est pas un groupe, sixième niveau) |
| Ancre d'une note | `anchor_element_id` vers `elements` (`SET NULL`), trait pointillé, jamais une relation ; contrainte `CHECK` : seul une note est ancrée |
| Tracé d'un lien | `diagram_edges(diagram_id, relationship_id, waypoints jsonb)`, au plus 20 coudes ; pas de ligne = segment droit rogné aux bords. Une relation supprimée emporte son tracé (cascade) |
| Concurrence | `diagrams.revision` ; `PUT /diagrams/{id}/layout` reçoit la révision lue, compare sous `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, répond `409 diagram_revision_conflict` si elle diffère, sinon l'incrémente dans la même transaction et la renvoie dans `X-Diagram-Revision` |
| Corps du `PUT` | `{revision, nodes, objects, edges}`, tous requis, listes éventuellement vides : un champ optionnel dédoublerait le schéma en `-Input`/`-Output` (`0035`) |
| Conflit côté SPA | Rien n'est rejoué seul. Le SPA propose de recharger la version de l'autre, puis de recoller sa propre scène sur la nouvelle révision, ou d'abandonner. L'historique d'annulation est vidé |
| Lecture MCP | `list_diagrams` et `get_diagram`, lecture seule. Aucun outil n'écrit une coordonnée : la géométrie n'est pas le travail d'un agent |

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Deux tables, `diagram_notes` et `diagram_groups` | Schéma « pur » | Duplique la lecture, le remplacement transactionnel et la détection de cycles | Écarté |
| Un groupe est un élément ArchiMate `grouping` | Une seule notion | Un `grouping` est un fait du graphe : il porte des relations, apparaît dans le voisinage et l'impact ; un cadre de dessin ne doit pas | Écarté |
| Créer une composition automatique pour chaque boîte glissée dans un groupe | « Ça se fait tout seul » | Écrit des faits que personne n'a décidés, et pour une paire que le métamodèle peut refuser | Écarté |
| Un verrou d'édition par diagramme | Aucun conflit | Verrou oublié, session morte ; l'autosave rend le verrou invisible | Écarté |
| Fusion automatique des deux scènes | Pas de perte | Une fusion de géométrie est illisible à relire ; le cas est rare (un rédacteur à la fois) | Écarté |
| Un lien routé stocké sur la relation | Pas de table de plus | Le tracé est de la vue : deux diagrammes montrent le même lien différemment | Écarté |

## Conséquences

- **Retirer une boîte du diagramme élague** les tracés et détache les notes ancrées sur son élément
  (`useDiagram.prune`) ; sinon le serveur répondrait 422 pour un tracé de lien dont une extrémité a quitté la vue.
- **Supprimer un élément du catalogue** emporte sa boîte (clé étrangère), laisse le groupe et ses autres
  enfants, et détache une note ancrée sur lui.
- **Le `PUT` est un changement de contrat** : un onglet resté ouvert sur l'ancien SPA pendant un
  déploiement voit son enregistrement refusé (422) jusqu'au rechargement.
- **Le 409 ne transporte pas la révision courante dans un champ** : l'enveloppe d'erreur n'a que `error`
  et `detail`, qui la nomme ; le SPA relit le diagramme.
- **Journal** : une ligne `action=diagram_scene_replaced` par enregistrement, avec des comptes ; jamais le texte
  d'une note. Le sous-projet d'audit, quand il existera, remplace cette ligne par son port.
- Le texte d'une note est du texte : le SPA ne l'insère jamais comme HTML, l'export SVG l'échappe.
- Complète [`0031`](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md) sans le supplanter ; solde sa dette « deux auteurs ».

## Références

- ADR liés : [0005](0005-metamodele-archimate-3-2.md), [0031](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md),
  [0033](0033-postgresql-seul-pour-le-graphe.md), [0035](0035-taille-des-boites-de-diagramme.md)
- Code concerné : `backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_objects.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/diagrams.py`,
  `backend/src/ea/repositories/diagram_store.py`, `backend/migrations/versions/0009_diagram_scene.py`,
  `frontend/src/lib/sceneObjects.ts`, `frontend/src/lib/waypoints.ts`
````

  - `missing_required(properties: Mapping[str, str], stereotypes: Sequence[Stereotype]) -> tuple[str, ...]`
  - `profile_to_document(profile: Profile) -> dict[str, Any]`, `profile_from_document(document: Mapping[str, Any], *, now: datetime) -> Profile`, `PROFILE_FORMAT = 1`
  - constantes `MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH = 500`, `MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH = 200`, `MAX_PROPERTIES_PER_STEREOTYPE = 30`, `MAX_ALLOWED_VALUES = 50`, `MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 100`

```python
"""Profiles and stereotypes: pure rules, no store."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.profiles import (
    MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH,
    IconKey,
    Profile,
    PropertyKind,
    Stereotype,
    StereotypeProperty,
    check_pattern,
    missing_required,
    profile_from_document,
    profile_to_document,
    validate_stereotype_properties,
)

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def a_property(**overrides: object) -> StereotypeProperty:
    fields: dict[str, object] = {"key": "owner", "label": "Responsable"}
    return StereotypeProperty(**{**fields, **overrides})  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def a_stereotype(*properties: StereotypeProperty, **overrides: object) -> Stereotype:
    fields: dict[str, object] = {
        "id": uuid4(),
        "profile_id": uuid4(),
        "name": "database",
        "base_type": ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT,
        "colour": "#ffe0e0",
        "icon_key": IconKey.DATABASE,
        "properties": properties,
    }
    return Stereotype(**{**fields, **overrides})  # type: ignore[arg-type]


class TestDeclaring:
    def test_a_key_is_a_plain_identifier_and_never_an_ip_addressing_key(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="identifier"):
            a_property(key="1 owner")
        for reserved in ("ip_address", "cidr", "vrf", "ip_reserved"):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="reserved"):
                a_property(key=reserved)

    def test_an_enumeration_needs_values_and_a_pattern_needs_a_pattern(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="allowed"):
            a_property(kind=PropertyKind.ENUM)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pattern"):
            a_property(kind=PropertyKind.PATTERN)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="free"):
            a_property(allowed_values=("a",))

    def test_a_stereotype_extends_a_real_type_and_never_a_junction(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="connector"):
            a_stereotype(base_type=ElementType.AND_JUNCTION)

    def test_a_colour_is_rrggbb_or_nothing(self) -> None:
        assert a_stereotype(colour=None).colour is None
        for bad in ("red", "#fff", '#ffee00" onload="x'):
            with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="colour"):
                a_stereotype(colour=bad)

    def test_two_properties_cannot_share_a_key_within_a_stereotype(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="twice"):
            a_stereotype(a_property(), a_property(label="Autre"))

    def test_a_profile_cannot_hold_two_stereotypes_of_one_name(self) -> None:
        profile_id = uuid4()
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="twice"):
            Profile(
                id=profile_id,
                name="SI",
                description="",
                created_at=NOW,
                updated_at=NOW,
                stereotypes=(
                    a_stereotype(profile_id=profile_id),
                    a_stereotype(profile_id=profile_id),
                ),
            )


class TestThePattern:
    @pytest.mark.parametrize("pattern", [r"^[A-Z]{3}-\d{4}$", r"(ab)+", r"[a-z]+\.[a-z]+", r"(?:oui|non)"])
    def test_a_linear_pattern_is_accepted(self, pattern: str) -> None:
        check_pattern(pattern)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("pattern", [r"(a+)+$", r"(a|b)*c", r"(.*a){10}", r"(a)\1", r"((a+)b)+"])
    def test_a_pattern_that_can_backtrack_catastrophically_is_refused(self, pattern: str) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pattern"):
            check_pattern(pattern)

    def test_a_pattern_is_bounded_and_must_compile(self) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at most"):
            check_pattern("a" * 201)
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pattern"):
            check_pattern("[unclosed")


class TestValidatingValues:
    def test_an_enumeration_accepts_its_values_and_refuses_others(self) -> None:
        tier = a_property(key="tier", label="Niveau", kind=PropertyKind.ENUM, allowed_values=("1", "2", "3"))
        stereotype = a_stereotype(tier)

        assert validate_stereotype_properties({"tier": "2"}, [stereotype]) == {"tier": "2"}
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tier"):
            validate_stereotype_properties({"tier": "9"}, [stereotype])

    def test_a_pattern_must_match_the_whole_value(self) -> None:
        code = a_property(key="code", label="Code", kind=PropertyKind.PATTERN, pattern=r"[A-Z]{3}-\d{4}")
        stereotype = a_stereotype(code)

        validate_stereotype_properties({"code": "ABC-1234"}, [stereotype])
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="code"):
            validate_stereotype_properties({"code": "ABC-1234 et plus"}, [stereotype])

    def test_a_value_longer_than_the_bound_is_refused(self) -> None:
        stereotype = a_stereotype(a_property())

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="characters"):
            validate_stereotype_properties({"owner": "x" * (MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH + 1)}, [stereotype])

    def test_a_key_nobody_declared_stays_free_and_a_blank_value_is_not_validated(self) -> None:
        tier = a_property(key="tier", label="Niveau", kind=PropertyKind.ENUM, allowed_values=("1",))
        stereotype = a_stereotype(tier)

        result = validate_stereotype_properties({"tier": "  ", "note libre": "x"[:0] or "ok"}, [stereotype])

        assert result["tier"] == "  "
        assert result["note libre"] == "ok"

    def test_two_stereotypes_may_not_declare_the_same_key(self) -> None:
        first = a_stereotype(a_property(), name="un")
        second = a_stereotype(a_property(), name="deux")

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="declared by both"):
            validate_stereotype_properties({}, [first, second])

    def test_required_is_reported_not_enforced(self) -> None:
        stereotype = a_stereotype(a_property(required=True), a_property(key="env", label="Env", required=True))

        assert validate_stereotype_properties({}, [stereotype]) == {}
        assert missing_required({"env": "prod"}, [stereotype]) == ("owner",)
        assert missing_required({"owner": " ", "env": "prod"}, [stereotype]) == ("owner",)
        assert missing_required({"owner": "ops", "env": "prod"}, [stereotype]) == ()


class TestTheDocument:
    def test_a_profile_survives_export_and_import(self) -> None:
        profile_id = uuid4()
        tier = a_property(key="tier", label="Niveau", kind=PropertyKind.ENUM, allowed_values=("1", "2"), help="1 = critique")
        profile = Profile(
            id=profile_id,
            name="SI",
            description="Profil du SI",
            created_at=NOW,
            updated_at=NOW,
            stereotypes=(a_stereotype(a_property(required=True), tier, profile_id=profile_id),),
        )

        document = profile_to_document(profile)
        imported = profile_from_document(document, now=NOW)

        assert document["profile_format"] == 1
        assert imported.name == "SI"
        assert imported.id != profile.id
        again = profile_to_document(imported)
        assert again == document

    def test_a_document_of_another_format_or_with_a_dangerous_pattern_is_refused(self) -> None:
        profile = Profile(id=uuid4(), name="SI", description="", created_at=NOW, updated_at=NOW)
        document = profile_to_document(profile)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="profile_format"):
            profile_from_document({**document, "profile_format": 2}, now=NOW)
        dangerous = {
            **document,
            "stereotypes": [
                {
                    "name": "x",
                    "base_type": "node",
                    "colour": None,
                    "icon_key": "none",
                    "properties": [
                        {"key": "k", "label": "K", "required": False, "kind": "pattern",
                         "allowed_values": [], "pattern": "(a+)+", "help": ""}
                    ],
                }
            ],
        }
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="pattern"):
            profile_from_document(dangerous, now=NOW)

    def test_an_unknown_base_type_is_refused(self) -> None:
        document = {
            "profile_format": 1,
            "name": "SI",
            "description": "",
            "stereotypes": [{"name": "x", "base_type": "spaceship", "colour": None, "icon_key": "none", "properties": []}],
        }

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="spaceship"):
            profile_from_document(document, now=NOW)
```
