`backend/src/ea/domain/profiles.py` :

```python
"""Profiles and stereotypes: reference data that extends nothing in ArchiMate.

A stereotype is a **label laid on an element of one base type**, never a new
type: the relationship rules stay those of the base type (docs/adr/0005) and
the metamodel does not move. What a stereotype adds is the properties people
expect on such an element, declared here and stored where every other
user-defined attribute lives — in `elements.properties`, as strings, without a
prefix, like the IP addressing (docs/adr/0020).

`required` never blocks a write (an element is created, then completed);
`missing_required` is what a quality rule reads. A value that is present *is*
checked, as `validate_ipam_properties` checks an address.

Nothing here imports a framework or a driver, per `CLAUDE.md`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Final
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.ipam import IPAM_PROPERTIES
from ea.domain.model import PROPERTY_KEY, StereotypeRef

PROFILE_FORMAT: Final = 1
MAX_NAME_LENGTH: Final = 100
MAX_LABEL_LENGTH: Final = 100
MAX_HELP_LENGTH: Final = 300
MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH: Final = 500
MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH: Final = 200
MAX_ALLOWED_VALUES: Final = 50
MAX_PROPERTIES_PER_STEREOTYPE: Final = 30
MAX_STEREOTYPES_PER_PROFILE: Final = 100

_HEX_COLOUR = re.compile(r"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$")


class IconKey(StrEnum):
    """The closed set of icons the SPA ships — an icon is picked, never uploaded."""

    NONE = "none"
    DATABASE = "database"
    SERVER = "server"
    CLOUD = "cloud"
    USER = "user"
    LOCK = "lock"
    DOCUMENT = "document"
    GEAR = "gear"
    QUEUE = "queue"
    API = "api"


class PropertyKind(StrEnum):
    FREE = "free"
    ENUM = "enum"
    PATTERN = "pattern"


def _named(value: str, what: str, limit: int) -> str:
    cleaned = value.strip()
    if not cleaned or len(cleaned) > limit:
        msg = f"{what} is between 1 and {limit} characters"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    return cleaned


def _refuse_catastrophic(pattern: str) -> None:
    """Refuse the shapes whose backtracking can explode: a quantified group that holds
    a quantifier or an alternation, and back-references.

    Deliberately stricter than the regex language: `(jpg|png)+` is refused too.
    A subset that is certainly linear beats a dependency (`re2`) for a field
    whose values are short.
    """
    holds: list[bool] = []  # per open group: a quantifier or a `|` is inside it
    in_class = False
    index = 0
    while index < len(pattern):
        char = pattern[index]
        if char == "\\":
            following = pattern[index + 1 : index + 2]
            if following.isdigit() and following != "0":
                msg = "a pattern cannot use back-references"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            index += 2
            continue
        if in_class:
            in_class = char != "]"
        elif char == "[":
            in_class = True
        elif char == "(":
            holds.append(False)
        elif char == ")":
            risky = holds.pop() if holds else False
            if pattern[index + 1 : index + 2] in {"+", "*", "{"}:
                if risky:
                    msg = "a pattern cannot repeat a group that itself repeats or alternates"
                    raise ValueError(msg)
                if holds:
                    holds[-1] = True
        elif char in {"+", "*", "{", "|"} and holds:
            holds[-1] = True
        index += 1


def check_pattern(pattern: str) -> None:
    """Accept only a pattern that is short, compiles, and cannot backtrack catastrophically."""
    if not pattern or len(pattern) > MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH:
        msg = f"a pattern is at most {MAX_PATTERN_LENGTH} characters"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    _refuse_catastrophic(pattern)
    try:
        re.compile(pattern)
    except re.error as error:
        msg = f"not a valid pattern: {error}"
        raise ValueError(msg) from error


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StereotypeProperty:
    """One property a stereotype expects: a key in `elements.properties`, and how to read it."""

    key: str
    label: str
    required: bool = False
    kind: PropertyKind = PropertyKind.FREE
    allowed_values: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    pattern: str | None = None
    help: str = ""

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not PROPERTY_KEY.match(self.key):
            msg = f"property name {self.key!r} is not a plain identifier"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if self.key in IPAM_PROPERTIES:
            msg = f"property name {self.key!r} is reserved by the IP addressing convention"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        _named(self.label, "a property label", MAX_LABEL_LENGTH)
        if len(self.help) > MAX_HELP_LENGTH:
            msg = f"a property help is at most {MAX_HELP_LENGTH} characters"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if self.kind is PropertyKind.ENUM:
            values = self.allowed_values
            if not values or len(values) > MAX_ALLOWED_VALUES or len(set(values)) != len(values):
                msg = f"an enumeration has 1 to {MAX_ALLOWED_VALUES} distinct allowed values"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            if any(not v or len(v) > MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH for v in values):
                msg = "an allowed value is between 1 and 500 characters"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            if self.pattern is not None:
                msg = "an enumeration has no pattern"
                raise ValueError(msg)
        elif self.kind is PropertyKind.PATTERN:
            if self.pattern is None:
                msg = "a pattern property needs its pattern"
                raise ValueError(msg)
            check_pattern(self.pattern)
            if self.allowed_values:
                msg = "a pattern property has no allowed values"
                raise ValueError(msg)
        elif self.allowed_values or self.pattern is not None:
            msg = "a free property has neither allowed values nor a pattern"
            raise ValueError(msg)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Stereotype:
    id: UUID
    profile_id: UUID
    name: str
    base_type: ElementType
    colour: str | None
    icon_key: IconKey
    properties: tuple[StereotypeProperty, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _named(self.name, "a stereotype name", MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
        if self.base_type.is_connector:
            msg = "a stereotype cannot extend a connector (a junction is not a modelled element)"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if self.colour is not None and not _HEX_COLOUR.match(self.colour):
            msg = "a stereotype colour is #rrggbb"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if len(self.properties) > MAX_PROPERTIES_PER_STEREOTYPE:
            msg = f"a stereotype has at most {MAX_PROPERTIES_PER_STEREOTYPE} properties"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        keys = [p.key for p in self.properties]
        if len(set(keys)) != len(keys):
            msg = "a property key is declared twice in one stereotype"
            raise ValueError(msg)

    @property
    def ref(self) -> StereotypeRef:
        return StereotypeRef(
            id=self.id, name=self.name, colour=self.colour, icon_key=self.icon_key.value
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Profile:
    id: UUID
    name: str
    description: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    stereotypes: tuple[Stereotype, ...] = ()

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        _named(self.name, "a profile name", MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
        names = [s.name for s in self.stereotypes]
        if len(set(names)) != len(names):
            msg = "a stereotype name is used twice in one profile"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if len(names) > MAX_STEREOTYPES_PER_PROFILE:
            msg = f"a profile has at most {MAX_STEREOTYPES_PER_PROFILE} stereotypes"
            raise ValueError(msg)


def check_value(prop: StereotypeProperty, value: str) -> None:
    """The format of a value that is there. Blank means "not set" and is never checked."""
    if len(value) > MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH:
        msg = f"{prop.key}: a value is at most {MAX_PROPERTY_VALUE_LENGTH} characters"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    if prop.kind is PropertyKind.ENUM and value not in prop.allowed_values:
        msg = f"{prop.key}: {value!r} is not one of {', '.join(prop.allowed_values)}"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    if prop.kind is PropertyKind.PATTERN and not re.fullmatch(prop.pattern or "", value):
        msg = f"{prop.key}: {value!r} does not match the expected form ({prop.pattern})"
        raise ValueError(msg)


def _declared(stereotypes: Sequence[Stereotype]) -> dict[str, StereotypeProperty]:
    declared: dict[str, StereotypeProperty] = {}
    owner: dict[str, str] = {}
    for stereotype in stereotypes:
        for prop in stereotype.properties:
            if prop.key in declared:
                msg = (
                    f"property {prop.key!r} is declared by both {owner[prop.key]!r} "
                    f"and {stereotype.name!r}: an element cannot carry both"
                )
                raise ValueError(msg)
            declared[prop.key] = prop
            owner[prop.key] = stereotype.name
    return declared


def validate_stereotype_properties(
    properties: Mapping[str, str], stereotypes: Sequence[Stereotype]
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """The properties to store, once the format of each declared value is checked.

    A key nobody declared stays free: an element keeps its own attributes.
    `required` is not enforced here — see `missing_required`.
    """
    declared = _declared(stereotypes)
    for key, prop in declared.items():
        value = properties.get(key)
        if value is not None and value.strip():
            check_value(prop, value)
    return dict(properties)


def missing_required(
    properties: Mapping[str, str], stereotypes: Sequence[Stereotype]
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """The required keys that are absent or blank — what a quality rule reports."""
    declared = _declared(stereotypes)
    return tuple(
        sorted(
            key
            for key, prop in declared.items()
            if prop.required and not properties.get(key, "").strip()
        )
    )


def profile_to_document(profile: Profile) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """A profile as a portable, versioned document — ids and dates are not part of it."""
    return {
        "profile_format": PROFILE_FORMAT,
        "name": profile.name,
        "description": profile.description,
        "stereotypes": [
            {
                "name": s.name,
                "base_type": s.base_type.value,
                "colour": s.colour,
                "icon_key": s.icon_key.value,
                "properties": [
                    {
                        "key": p.key,
                        "label": p.label,
                        "required": p.required,
                        "kind": p.kind.value,
                        "allowed_values": list(p.allowed_values),
                        "pattern": p.pattern,
                        "help": p.help,
                    }
                    for p in s.properties
                ],
            }
            for s in profile.stereotypes
        ],
    }


def profile_from_document(document: Mapping[str, Any], *, now: datetime) -> Profile:
    """A new profile from a document, through the very constructors a manual write uses."""
    if document.get("profile_format") != PROFILE_FORMAT:
        msg = f"profile_format must be {PROFILE_FORMAT}"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    profile_id = uuid4()
    try:
        stereotypes = tuple(
            Stereotype(
                id=uuid4(),
                profile_id=profile_id,
                name=raw["name"],
                base_type=ElementType(raw["base_type"]),
                colour=raw["colour"],
                icon_key=IconKey(raw["icon_key"]),
                properties=tuple(
                    StereotypeProperty(
                        key=p["key"],
                        label=p["label"],
                        required=p["required"],
                        kind=PropertyKind(p["kind"]),
                        allowed_values=tuple(p["allowed_values"]),
                        pattern=p["pattern"],
                        help=p["help"],
                    )
                    for p in raw["properties"]
                ),
            )
            for raw in document["stereotypes"]
        )
        return Profile(
            id=profile_id,
            name=document["name"],
            description=document["description"].strip(),
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
            stereotypes=stereotypes,
        )
    except KeyError as error:
        msg = f"the profile document lacks {error}"
        raise ValueError(msg) from error
```
Les motifs de test : `(a|b)*c` → `|` dans le groupe suivi de `*` → refusé ; `(.*a){10}` → `*` dans le groupe suivi de `{` → refusé ; `((a+)b)+` → `(a+)` refusé... `(a+)` est suivi de `b` donc pas quantifié ; le groupe externe contient un quantificateur (`+` interne) et est suivi de `+` → refusé ✓ ; `(a)\1` → back-reference ✓ ; `(ab)+` accepté ✓ ; `(?:oui|non)` : `|` dans le groupe, suivi de rien ✓ accepté ; `^[A-Z]{3}-\d{4}$` : `{` au niveau haut (pas de groupe ouvert) accepté ✓ ; `[a-z]+\.[a-z]+` ✓. Le message du test « pattern » : « a pattern cannot … » contient `pattern` ✓ ; `[unclosed` : « not a valid pattern » ✓ ; 201 caractères : « a pattern is at most 200 characters » contient « at most » ✓.

  - `class StereotypeSource(Protocol)` : `async refs_of(element_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, tuple[StereotypeRef, ...]]`, `async stereotypes_of(element_id: UUID) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]`
  - `class ProfileRepository(StereotypeSource, Protocol)` : `list_profiles() -> tuple[Profile, ...]`, `get_profile(id) -> Profile | None`, `add_profile(profile) -> Profile`, `save_profile(profile) -> Profile` (nom, description, date seulement), `delete_profile(id) -> bool`, `list_stereotypes() -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]`, `get_stereotype(id) -> Stereotype | None`, `add_stereotype(stereotype) -> Stereotype`, `replace_stereotype(stereotype) -> Stereotype`, `delete_stereotype(id) -> bool`, `applied_counts(stereotype_ids) -> dict[UUID, int]`, `set_for_element(element_id, stereotype_ids) -> None`
  - tables `profiles`, `profile_stereotypes`, `profile_properties` (colonne `position` en plus de la spec, pour garder l'ordre déclaré), `element_stereotypes`
  - contraintes : `uq_profiles_name`, `uq_profile_stereotypes_profile_id_name`, `fk_profile_stereotypes_profile_id_profiles` (CASCADE), `fk_profile_properties_stereotype_id_profile_stereotypes` (CASCADE), `fk_element_stereotypes_element_id_elements` (CASCADE), `fk_element_stereotypes_stereotype_id_profile_stereotypes` (RESTRICT), `ix_element_stereotypes_stereotype_id`

```python
def test_removing_a_stereotype_that_is_applied_is_restricted_while_an_element_takes_its_tags_with_it() -> None:
    table = Base.metadata.tables["element_stereotypes"]

    assert next(iter(table.c.stereotype_id.foreign_keys)).ondelete == "RESTRICT"
    assert next(iter(table.c.element_id.foreign_keys)).ondelete == "CASCADE"
    assert table.c.stereotype_id.index is True


def test_a_stereotype_name_is_unique_within_its_profile_and_a_profile_name_everywhere() -> None:
    names = {c.name for t in ("profiles", "profile_stereotypes") for c in Base.metadata.tables[t].constraints}

    assert {"uq_profiles_name", "uq_profile_stereotypes_profile_id_name"} <= names
```

```python
"""Profiles against a real PostgreSQL: the whole tree in one transaction, RESTRICT, cascades."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.errors import DuplicateProfileError
from ea.domain.model import Element
from ea.domain.profiles import IconKey, Profile, PropertyKind, Stereotype, StereotypeProperty
from ea.repositories.architecture_store import PostgresArchitectureRepository
from ea.repositories.profile_store import PostgresProfileRepository

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.postgres, pytest.mark.asyncio]

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def a_profile(name: str = "SI") -> Profile:
    profile_id = uuid4()
    stereotype = Stereotype(
        id=uuid4(),
        profile_id=profile_id,
        name="database",
        base_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT,
        colour="#ffe0e0",
        icon_key=IconKey.DATABASE,
        properties=(
            StereotypeProperty(key="owner", label="Responsable", required=True),
            StereotypeProperty(key="tier", label="Niveau", kind=PropertyKind.ENUM, allowed_values=("1", "2")),
        ),
    )
    return Profile(id=profile_id, name=name, description="", created_at=NOW, updated_at=NOW, stereotypes=(stereotype,))


@pytest.fixture
def profiles(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresProfileRepository:
    return PostgresProfileRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


@pytest.fixture
def graph(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresArchitectureRepository:
    return PostgresArchitectureRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


async def test_a_profile_round_trips_with_its_stereotypes_and_their_properties_in_order(
    profiles: PostgresProfileRepository,
) -> None:
    stored = await profiles.add_profile(a_profile())

    read = await profiles.get_profile(stored.id)

    assert read == stored
    assert [p.key for p in read.stereotypes[0].properties] == ["owner", "tier"]  # type: ignore[union-attr]


async def test_a_profile_name_is_unique_and_the_store_stays_usable(profiles: PostgresProfileRepository) -> None:
    await profiles.add_profile(a_profile("SI"))

    with pytest.raises(DuplicateProfileError):
        await profiles.add_profile(a_profile("SI"))
    await profiles.add_profile(a_profile("Autre"))

    assert [p.name for p in await profiles.list_profiles()] == ["Autre", "SI"]


async def test_replacing_a_stereotype_replaces_its_properties_atomically(
    profiles: PostgresProfileRepository,
) -> None:
    profile = await profiles.add_profile(a_profile())
    original = profile.stereotypes[0]
    changed = Stereotype(
        id=original.id, profile_id=original.profile_id, name="base de données",
        base_type=original.base_type, colour=None, icon_key=IconKey.NONE,
        properties=(StereotypeProperty(key="env", label="Environnement"),),
    )

    await profiles.replace_stereotype(changed)

    read = await profiles.get_stereotype(original.id)
    assert read == changed


async def test_tags_are_listed_for_many_elements_in_one_call_and_replaced_as_a_set(
    profiles: PostgresProfileRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    profile = await profiles.add_profile(a_profile())
    stereotype = profile.stereotypes[0]
    one = await graph.add_element(Element.create(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="A", now=NOW))
    two = await graph.add_element(Element.create(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="B", now=NOW))

    await profiles.set_for_element(one.id, [stereotype.id])
    refs = await profiles.refs_of([one.id, two.id])

    assert refs[one.id] == (stereotype.ref,)
    assert two.id not in refs or refs[two.id] == ()
    assert (await profiles.stereotypes_of(one.id))[0].properties == stereotype.properties
    assert await profiles.applied_counts([stereotype.id]) == {stereotype.id: 1}

    await profiles.set_for_element(one.id, [])
    assert await profiles.refs_of([one.id]) == {}


async def test_a_stereotype_in_use_cannot_be_deleted_but_an_element_takes_its_tags_with_it(
    profiles: PostgresProfileRepository, graph: PostgresArchitectureRepository
) -> None:
    profile = await profiles.add_profile(a_profile())
    stereotype = profile.stereotypes[0]
    element = await graph.add_element(Element.create(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="A", now=NOW))
    await profiles.set_for_element(element.id, [stereotype.id])

    with pytest.raises(Exception):  # noqa: B017, PT011 — RESTRICT surfaces as the driver's integrity error
        await profiles.delete_stereotype(stereotype.id)
    assert await profiles.get_stereotype(stereotype.id) is not None

    await graph.delete_element(element.id)

    assert await profiles.applied_counts([stereotype.id]) == {}
    assert await profiles.delete_stereotype(stereotype.id) is True


async def test_deleting_an_unused_profile_takes_its_stereotypes(profiles: PostgresProfileRepository) -> None:
    profile = await profiles.add_profile(a_profile())

    assert await profiles.delete_profile(profile.id) is True

    assert await profiles.list_stereotypes() == ()
```

```python
class ProfileNotFoundError(DomainError):
    """A profile referenced by id is absent from the store."""


class StereotypeNotFoundError(DomainError):
    """A stereotype referenced by id is absent from the store."""


class DuplicateProfileError(DomainError):
    """A profile name, or a stereotype name within its profile, is already taken."""


class StereotypeInUseError(DomainError):
    """A stereotype is applied to elements and cannot be removed or re-typed."""
```

`backend/src/ea/domain/ports.py` : dans le bloc `TYPE_CHECKING` importer `from ea.domain.model import StereotypeRef` (si `Element` y est déjà importé, l'étendre) et `from ea.domain.profiles import Profile, Stereotype`. Ajouter après `DiagramRepository` :

```python
class StereotypeSource(Protocol):
    """What the architecture service needs of the profiles: tags to show, rules to check."""

    async def refs_of(
        self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID]
    ) -> dict[UUID, tuple[StereotypeRef, ...]]:
        """The stereotypes each element carries; an element with none may be absent."""
        ...

    async def stereotypes_of(self, element_id: UUID) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        """The full stereotypes (with their property rules) one element carries."""
        ...


class ProfileRepository(StereotypeSource, Protocol):
    """Persistence for profiles, their stereotypes and where they are applied."""

    async def list_profiles(self) -> tuple[Profile, ...]: ...

    async def get_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> Profile | None: ...

    async def add_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        """Store a profile and every stereotype it holds in one transaction."""
        ...

    async def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        """Overwrite the name, description and update time only."""
        ...

    async def delete_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> bool: ...

    async def list_stereotypes(self) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]: ...

    async def get_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Stereotype | None: ...

    async def add_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype: ...

    async def replace_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype:
        """Replace a stereotype and, in the same transaction, all its properties."""
        ...

    async def delete_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> bool: ...

    async def applied_counts(self, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, int]:
        """How many elements carry each stereotype; those carried by none are absent."""
        ...

    async def set_for_element(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> None:
        """Make the set of stereotypes an element carries exactly this one."""
        ...
```

`backend/src/ea/db/models/profile.py` :

```python
"""Profiles, stereotypes and where they are applied (docs/adr: profils et stéréotypes).

Reference data: a stereotype never changes the metamodel, so `base_type` is a
plain string checked by the domain — a PostgreSQL enum would freeze ArchiMate
into the schema. What an element carries of a stereotype is one row of
`element_stereotypes`; the *values* live in `elements.properties`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, Uuid
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from ea.db.base import Base


class ProfileRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "profiles"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("name"),)

    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False, server_default="")
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)


class StereotypeRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "profile_stereotypes"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("profile_id", "name"),)

    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, primary_key=True)
    profile_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("profiles.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), nullable=False
    )
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)
    base_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=False)
    colour: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=True)
    icon_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=False)


class PropertyRecord(Base):
    __tablename__ = "profile_properties"

    stereotype_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("profile_stereotypes.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True
    )
    key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(63), primary_key=True)
    # The declared order, so a form shows the properties as the author wrote them.
    position: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, server_default="0")
    label: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=False)
    required: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, nullable=False, server_default="false")
    kind: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=False)
    allowed_values: Mapped[list[Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False, server_default="[]")
    pattern: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    help: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False, server_default="")


class ElementStereotypeRecord(Base):
    """One stereotype laid on one element. RESTRICT on the definition, CASCADE on the element."""

    __tablename__ = "element_stereotypes"

    element_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid, ForeignKey("elements.id", ondelete="CASCADE"), primary_key=True
    )
    stereotype_id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(
        Uuid,
        ForeignKey("profile_stereotypes.id", ondelete="RESTRICT"),
        primary_key=True,
        index=True,
    )
```

```python
type de base, jamais un type nouveau (ADR sur les profils). `base_type` est une
chaîne contrôlée par le domaine — un enum PostgreSQL figerait le métamodèle dans
le schéma. `profile_properties.position` garde l'ordre de déclaration. Rien à

from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql
```

`backend/src/ea/repositories/profile_store.py` :

```python
"""The PostgreSQL implementation of `ProfileRepository` — see docs/adr: profils et stéréotypes.

Like the diagram store it takes the session factory and opens one unit of
work per call. A profile and its stereotypes are written in one transaction;
replacing a stereotype replaces its properties in the same one. A name already
taken is recognised by the **name of the constraint**, never by the message.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, cast

from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.engine import CursorResult
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from ea.db.models.profile import (
    ElementStereotypeRecord,
    ProfileRecord,
    PropertyRecord,
    StereotypeRecord,
)
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.errors import DuplicateProfileError, ProfileNotFoundError
from ea.domain.model import StereotypeRef
from ea.domain.profiles import (
    IconKey,
    Profile,
    PropertyKind,
    Stereotype,
    StereotypeProperty,
)

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Sequence
    from uuid import UUID

    from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

_TAKEN = {"uq_profiles_name", "uq_profile_stereotypes_profile_id_name"}


def _constraint_of(error: IntegrityError) -> str:
    diag = getattr(error.orig, "diag", None)
    return str(getattr(diag, "constraint_name", "") or "")


def _taken(error: IntegrityError, what: str) -> Exception:
    if _constraint_of(error) in _TAKEN:
        return DuplicateProfileError(f"{what} is already taken")
    return error


def _property(row: PropertyRecord) -> StereotypeProperty:
    return StereotypeProperty(
        key=row.key,
        label=row.label,
        required=row.required,
        kind=PropertyKind(row.kind),
        allowed_values=tuple(row.allowed_values),
        pattern=row.pattern,
        help=row.help,
    )


def _stereotype(row: StereotypeRecord, props: Sequence[PropertyRecord]) -> Stereotype:
    return Stereotype(
        id=row.id,
        profile_id=row.profile_id,
        name=row.name,
        base_type=ElementType(row.base_type),
        colour=row.colour,
        icon_key=IconKey(row.icon_key),
        properties=tuple(_property(p) for p in sorted(props, key=lambda p: p.position)),
    )


def _rows(stereotype: Stereotype) -> tuple[StereotypeRecord, list[PropertyRecord]]:
    return (
        StereotypeRecord(
            id=stereotype.id,
            profile_id=stereotype.profile_id,
            name=stereotype.name,
            base_type=stereotype.base_type.value,
            colour=stereotype.colour,
            icon_key=stereotype.icon_key.value,
        ),
        [
            PropertyRecord(
                stereotype_id=stereotype.id,
                key=p.key,
                position=index,
                label=p.label,
                required=p.required,
                kind=p.kind.value,
                allowed_values=list(p.allowed_values),
                pattern=p.pattern,
                help=p.help,
            )
            for index, p in enumerate(stereotype.properties)
        ],
    )


class PostgresProfileRepository:
    def __init__(self, sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        self._sessions = sessions

    async def _stereotypes(
        self, session: AsyncSession, *, profile_id: UUID | None = None, ids: Sequence[UUID] | None = None
    ) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        statement = select(StereotypeRecord).order_by(StereotypeRecord.name, StereotypeRecord.id)
        if profile_id is not None:
            statement = statement.where(StereotypeRecord.profile_id == profile_id)
        if ids is not None:
            statement = statement.where(StereotypeRecord.id.in_(ids))
        rows = list(await session.scalars(statement))
        props = list(
            await session.scalars(
                select(PropertyRecord).where(PropertyRecord.stereotype_id.in_([r.id for r in rows]))
            )
        )
        return tuple(
            _stereotype(row, [p for p in props if p.stereotype_id == row.id]) for row in rows
        )

    def _profile(self, row: ProfileRecord, stereotypes: Sequence[Stereotype]) -> Profile:
        return Profile(
            id=row.id,
            name=row.name,
            description=row.description,
            created_at=row.created_at,
            updated_at=row.updated_at,
            stereotypes=tuple(s for s in stereotypes if s.profile_id == row.id),
        )

    async def list_profiles(self) -> tuple[Profile, ...]:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            rows = list(await session.scalars(select(ProfileRecord).order_by(ProfileRecord.name)))
            stereotypes = await self._stereotypes(session)
            return tuple(self._profile(row, stereotypes) for row in rows)

    async def get_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> Profile | None:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            row = await session.get(ProfileRecord, profile_id)
            if row is None:
                return None
            return self._profile(row, await self._stereotypes(session, profile_id=profile_id))

    async def add_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            session.add(
                ProfileRecord(
                    id=profile.id,
                    name=profile.name,
                    description=profile.description,
                    created_at=profile.created_at,
                    updated_at=profile.updated_at,
                )
            )
            try:
                await session.flush()
                for stereotype in profile.stereotypes:
                    record, props = _rows(stereotype)
                    session.add(record)
                    await session.flush()
                    session.add_all(props)
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(error, f"the profile name {profile.name!r}") from error
        return profile

    async def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            row = await session.get(ProfileRecord, profile.id)
            if row is None:
                msg = f"no profile with id {profile.id}"
                raise ProfileNotFoundError(msg)
            row.name = profile.name
            row.description = profile.description
            row.updated_at = profile.updated_at
            try:
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(error, f"the profile name {profile.name!r}") from error
        return profile

    async def delete_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> bool:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            deleted = await session.execute(delete(ProfileRecord).where(ProfileRecord.id == profile_id))
        return bool(cast("CursorResult[Any]", deleted).rowcount)

    async def list_stereotypes(self) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return await self._stereotypes(session)

    async def get_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Stereotype | None:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            found = await self._stereotypes(session, ids=[stereotype_id])
            return found[0] if found else None

    async def add_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            record, props = _rows(stereotype)
            session.add(record)
            try:
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(error, f"the stereotype name {stereotype.name!r}") from error
            session.add_all(props)
        return stereotype

    async def replace_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            row = await session.get(StereotypeRecord, stereotype.id, with_for_update=True)
            if row is None:
                msg = f"no stereotype with id {stereotype.id}"
                raise ProfileNotFoundError(msg)
            row.name = stereotype.name
            row.base_type = stereotype.base_type.value
            row.colour = stereotype.colour
            row.icon_key = stereotype.icon_key.value
            await session.execute(
                delete(PropertyRecord).where(PropertyRecord.stereotype_id == stereotype.id)
            )
            try:
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(error, f"the stereotype name {stereotype.name!r}") from error
            session.add_all(_rows(stereotype)[1])
        return stereotype

    async def delete_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> bool:
        """RESTRICT: a stereotype still applied is refused by the database, not by a lookup."""
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            deleted = await session.execute(
                delete(StereotypeRecord).where(StereotypeRecord.id == stereotype_id)
            )
        return bool(cast("CursorResult[Any]", deleted).rowcount)

    async def applied_counts(self, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, int]:
        statement = (
            select(ElementStereotypeRecord.stereotype_id, func.count())
            .where(ElementStereotypeRecord.stereotype_id.in_(stereotype_ids))
            .group_by(ElementStereotypeRecord.stereotype_id)
        )
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return {row[0]: row[1] for row in await session.execute(statement)}

    async def set_for_element(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> None:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            await session.execute(
                delete(ElementStereotypeRecord).where(ElementStereotypeRecord.element_id == element_id)
            )
            session.add_all(
                ElementStereotypeRecord(element_id=element_id, stereotype_id=sid)
                for sid in dict.fromkeys(stereotype_ids)
            )

    async def refs_of(self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, tuple[StereotypeRef, ...]]:
        statement = (
            select(ElementStereotypeRecord.element_id, StereotypeRecord)
            .join(StereotypeRecord, StereotypeRecord.id == ElementStereotypeRecord.stereotype_id)
            .where(ElementStereotypeRecord.element_id.in_(element_ids))
            .order_by(StereotypeRecord.name)
        )
        found: dict[UUID, list[StereotypeRef]] = {}
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            for element_id, row in await session.execute(statement):
                found.setdefault(element_id, []).append(
                    StereotypeRef(id=row.id, name=row.name, colour=row.colour, icon_key=row.icon_key)
                )
        return {key: tuple(value) for key, value in found.items()}

    async def stereotypes_of(self, element_id: UUID) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            ids = list(
                await session.scalars(
                    select(ElementStereotypeRecord.stereotype_id).where(
                        ElementStereotypeRecord.element_id == element_id
                    )
                )
            )
            return await self._stereotypes(session, ids=ids) if ids else ()
```
(Si `session.add` d'une `ProfileRecord` suivie d'un `flush()` avant les enfants est nécessaire — c'est fait dans `add_profile` — les clés étrangères n'étant reliées par aucun `relationship()`.) La levée de `IntegrityError` du RESTRICT sur `delete_stereotype` est volontairement laissée remonter : le service la prévient par `applied_counts` ; le test d'intégration prouve que la base refuse quand même.

```python
class InMemoryProfiles:
    """A dictionary pretending to be the profile tables and the application of stereotypes."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.profiles: dict[UUID, Profile] = {}
        self.applied: dict[UUID, tuple[UUID, ...]] = {}  # element id -> stereotype ids

    def _all(self) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        return tuple(s for p in self.profiles.values() for s in p.stereotypes)

    def _refuse_a_taken_name(self, profile: Profile) -> None:
        if any(p.name == profile.name and p.id != profile.id for p in self.profiles.values()):
            msg = f"the profile name {profile.name!r} is already taken"
            raise DuplicateProfileError(msg)

    async def list_profiles(self) -> tuple[Profile, ...]:
        return tuple(sorted(self.profiles.values(), key=lambda p: p.name))

    async def get_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> Profile | None:
        return self.profiles.get(profile_id)

    async def add_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        self._refuse_a_taken_name(profile)
        self.profiles[profile.id] = profile
        return profile

    async def save_profile(self, profile: Profile) -> Profile:
        stored = self.profiles.get(profile.id)
        if stored is None:
            raise ProfileNotFoundError(str(profile.id))
        self._refuse_a_taken_name(profile)
        self.profiles[profile.id] = replace(profile, stereotypes=stored.stereotypes)
        return profile

    async def delete_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> bool:
        return self.profiles.pop(profile_id, None) is not None

    async def list_stereotypes(self) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        return tuple(sorted(self._all(), key=lambda s: s.name))

    async def get_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Stereotype | None:
        return next((s for s in self._all() if s.id == stereotype_id), None)

    async def add_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype:
        profile = self.profiles[stereotype.profile_id]
        if any(s.name == stereotype.name for s in profile.stereotypes):
            msg = f"the stereotype name {stereotype.name!r} is already taken"
            raise DuplicateProfileError(msg)
        self.profiles[profile.id] = replace(profile, stereotypes=(*profile.stereotypes, stereotype))
        return stereotype

    async def replace_stereotype(self, stereotype: Stereotype) -> Stereotype:
        profile = self.profiles[stereotype.profile_id]
        if any(s.name == stereotype.name and s.id != stereotype.id for s in profile.stereotypes):
            msg = f"the stereotype name {stereotype.name!r} is already taken"
            raise DuplicateProfileError(msg)
        self.profiles[profile.id] = replace(
            profile,
            stereotypes=tuple(stereotype if s.id == stereotype.id else s for s in profile.stereotypes),
        )
        return stereotype

    async def delete_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> bool:
        for profile in self.profiles.values():
            if any(s.id == stereotype_id for s in profile.stereotypes):
                self.profiles[profile.id] = replace(
                    profile, stereotypes=tuple(s for s in profile.stereotypes if s.id != stereotype_id)
                )
                return True
        return False

    async def applied_counts(self, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, int]:
        counts: dict[UUID, int] = {}
        for ids in self.applied.values():
            for sid in ids:
                if sid in stereotype_ids:
                    counts[sid] = counts.get(sid, 0) + 1
        return counts

    async def set_for_element(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> None:
        self.applied[element_id] = tuple(dict.fromkeys(stereotype_ids))

    async def refs_of(self, element_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> dict[UUID, tuple[StereotypeRef, ...]]:
        found: dict[UUID, tuple[StereotypeRef, ...]] = {}
        for element_id in element_ids:
            stereotypes = [s for sid in self.applied.get(element_id, ()) if (s := next((x for x in self._all() if x.id == sid), None))]
            if stereotypes:
                found[element_id] = tuple(s.ref for s in sorted(stereotypes, key=lambda s: s.name))
        return found

    async def stereotypes_of(self, element_id: UUID) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        return tuple(s for sid in self.applied.get(element_id, ()) if (s := next((x for x in self._all() if x.id == sid), None)))
```

```python
"""Profiles through the service: rules behind every door, and what a reader may do."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.errors import (
    DuplicateProfileError,
    NotAuthorisedError,
    StereotypeInUseError,
    StereotypeNotFoundError,
)
from ea.domain.profiles import IconKey, PropertyKind, StereotypeProperty
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.profiles import ProfileService
from tests.conftest import a_reader

TIER = StereotypeProperty(key="tier", label="Niveau", kind=PropertyKind.ENUM, allowed_values=("1", "2"))
OWNER = StereotypeProperty(key="owner", label="Responsable", required=True)


async def a_stereotype(
    profiles: ProfileService,
    *props: StereotypeProperty,
    name: str = "database",
    base: ElementType = ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT,
    profile_name: str = "SI",
):
    profile = await profiles.create_profile(name=profile_name)
    return await profiles.add_stereotype(
        profile.id, name=name, base_type=base, colour="#ffe0e0", icon_key=IconKey.DATABASE, properties=props
    )


async def an_element(service: ArchitectureService, **kw: object):
    return await service.create_element(
        element_type=kw.pop("element_type", ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT), name="Facturation", **kw  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestProfiles:
    async def test_a_profile_name_is_unique(self, profile_service: ProfileService) -> None:
        await profile_service.create_profile(name="SI")

        with pytest.raises(DuplicateProfileError):
            await profile_service.create_profile(name="SI")

    async def test_an_exported_profile_can_be_imported_back_under_another_name(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService
    ) -> None:
        await a_stereotype(profile_service, TIER, OWNER)
        (profile,) = await profile_service.list_profiles()
        document = await profile_service.export_profile(profile.id)

        with pytest.raises(DuplicateProfileError):
            await profile_service.import_profile(document)
        imported = await profile_service.import_profile({**document, "name": "SI (copie)"})

        assert [s.name for s in imported.stereotypes] == ["database"]

    async def test_a_reader_lists_but_never_writes(self, profile_service: ProfileService) -> None:
        with acting_as(a_reader()):
            assert await profile_service.list_stereotypes() == ()
            with pytest.raises(NotAuthorisedError):
                await profile_service.import_profile(
                    {"profile_format": 1, "name": "X", "description": "", "stereotypes": []}
                )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestApplying:
    async def test_a_stereotype_is_applied_to_an_element_of_its_base_type_and_shown_on_it(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service, TIER)
        element = await an_element(service)

        applied = await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        assert [ref.name for ref in applied.stereotypes] == ["database"]
        assert [r.name for r in (await service.get_element(element.id)).stereotypes] == ["database"]
        listed = await service.list_elements(ElementFilter())
        assert [r.name for r in listed[0].stereotypes] == ["database"]

    async def test_a_stereotype_of_another_type_is_refused_with_both_types_named(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        node = await an_element(service, element_type=ElementType.NODE)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="application_component.*node"):
            await profile_service.apply_stereotype(node.id, stereotype.id)

    async def test_the_values_already_there_are_revalidated(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service, TIER)
        element = await an_element(service, properties={"tier": "9"})

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tier"):
            await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)
        assert (await service.get_element(element.id)).stereotypes == ()

    async def test_two_stereotypes_declaring_one_key_cannot_both_be_applied(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        first = await a_stereotype(profile_service, OWNER, name="un", profile_name="P1")
        second = await a_stereotype(profile_service, OWNER, name="deux", profile_name="P2")
        element = await an_element(service)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="declared by both"):
            await profile_service.apply_stereotypes(element.id, [first.id, second.id])

    async def test_an_unknown_stereotype_is_a_not_found(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        from uuid import uuid4

        element = await an_element(service)

        with pytest.raises(StereotypeNotFoundError):
            await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, uuid4())

    async def test_removing_a_stereotype_leaves_the_properties_alone(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service, TIER)
        element = await an_element(service, properties={"tier": "1"})
        await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        removed = await profile_service.remove_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        assert removed.stereotypes == ()
        assert dict(removed.properties) == {"tier": "1"}

    async def test_a_reader_cannot_apply(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        element = await an_element(service)

        with acting_as(a_reader()), pytest.raises(NotAuthorisedError):
            await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestTheRuleBehindEveryDoor:
    async def test_updating_an_element_checks_the_stereotypes_it_already_carries(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service, TIER, OWNER)
        element = await an_element(service)
        await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="tier"):
            await service.update_element(element.id, properties={"tier": "9"})
        updated = await service.update_element(element.id, properties={"tier": "2", "libre": "ok"})

        assert dict(updated.properties) == {"tier": "2", "libre": "ok"}
        assert [r.name for r in updated.stereotypes] == ["database"]

    async def test_a_missing_required_property_never_blocks_a_write(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service, OWNER)
        element = await an_element(service)
        await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        updated = await service.update_element(element.id, properties={})

        assert dict(updated.properties) == {}


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestChangingDefinitions:
    async def test_a_stereotype_in_use_cannot_be_deleted_and_the_count_is_named(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        for _ in range(2):

            await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        with pytest.raises(StereotypeInUseError, match="2 element"):
            await profile_service.delete_stereotype(stereotype.id)

    async def test_it_can_be_deleted_once_nothing_carries_it(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        element = await an_element(service)
        await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)
        await profile_service.remove_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        await profile_service.delete_stereotype(stereotype.id)

        assert await profile_service.list_stereotypes() == ()

    async def test_a_profile_holding_a_stereotype_in_use_cannot_be_deleted(
        self, profile_service: ProfileService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        element = await an_element(service)
        await profile_service.apply_stereotype(element.id, stereotype.id)

        with pytest.raises(StereotypeInUseError):

    async def test_the_base_type_of_a_stereotype_in_use_cannot_change(

    ) -> None:
        stereotype = await a_stereotype(profile_service)
        element = await an_element(service)

                name=stereotype.name,
                base_type=ElementType.NODE,
                colour=None,
                icon_key=IconKey.NONE,
                properties=(),
            )
```

```python
    async def _decorated(self, elements: Sequence[Element]) -> tuple[Element, ...]:
        """The elements with the stereotypes they carry — a label, shown wherever they are read."""
        if self._stereotypes is None or not elements:
            return tuple(elements)
        refs = await self._stereotypes.refs_of([element.id for element in elements])
        return tuple(replace(element, stereotypes=refs.get(element.id, ())) for element in elements)
```

`backend/src/ea/services/profiles.py` :

```python
"""Use cases over profiles and the stereotypes applied to elements.

A stereotype is a label on an element of one base type; it adds no type, so the
relationship rules stay those of the base type (docs/adr/0005). The rule this
layer owns: **what an element carries must agree with what it is** — a
stereotype of another base type is refused, two stereotypes declaring one key
cannot be carried together, and the values already there are checked against
the formats declared. `required` is reported by `domain.profiles.missing_required`
and never enforced here.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from ea.domain.errors import (
    ProfileNotFoundError,
    StereotypeInUseError,
    StereotypeNotFoundError,
)
from ea.domain.profiles import (
    IconKey,
    Profile,
    Stereotype,
    StereotypeProperty,
    profile_from_document,
    profile_to_document,
    validate_stereotype_properties,
)
from ea.services.caller import require_caller, require_editor

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
    from ea.domain.model import Element
    from ea.domain.ports import ProfileRepository
    from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService

Clock = Callable[[], datetime]

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


class ProfileService:
    """The single entry point `api/` and `mcp/` use for profiles and stereotypes."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: ProfileRepository,
        architecture: ArchitectureService,
        *,
        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
    ) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._architecture = architecture
        self._now = clock

    # --- Reading ------------------------------------------------------------

    async def list_profiles(self) -> tuple[Profile, ...]:
        require_caller()
        return await self._repository.list_profiles()

    async def get_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> Profile:
        require_caller()
        profile = await self._repository.get_profile(profile_id)
        if profile is None:
            msg = f"no profile with id {profile_id}"
            raise ProfileNotFoundError(msg)
        return profile

    async def list_stereotypes(self) -> tuple[Stereotype, ...]:
        require_caller()
        return await self._repository.list_stereotypes()

    async def export_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:
        require_caller()
        return profile_to_document(await self.get_profile(profile_id))

    # --- Profiles -------------------------------------------------------------

    async def create_profile(self, *, name: str, description: str = "") -> Profile:
        require_editor()
        now = self._now()
        stored = await self._repository.add_profile(
            Profile(id=uuid4(), name=name, description=description.strip(), created_at=now, updated_at=now)
        )
        self._audit("profile created", "profile_changed", stored.id, "created")
        return stored

    async def update_profile(
        self, profile_id: UUID, *, name: str | None = None, description: str | None = None
    ) -> Profile:
        require_editor()
        current = await self.get_profile(profile_id)
        revised = replace(
            current,
            name=current.name if name is None else name,
            description=current.description if description is None else description.strip(),
            updated_at=self._now(),
        )
        stored = await self._repository.save_profile(revised)
        self._audit("profile updated", "profile_changed", stored.id, "updated")
        return stored

    async def delete_profile(self, profile_id: UUID) -> None:
        require_editor()
        profile = await self.get_profile(profile_id)
        counts = await self._repository.applied_counts([s.id for s in profile.stereotypes])
        if counts:
            msg = (
                f"the profile {profile.name!r} has stereotypes applied to "
                f"{sum(counts.values())} element(s)"
            )
            raise StereotypeInUseError(msg)
        await self._repository.delete_profile(profile_id)
        self._audit("profile deleted", "profile_changed", profile_id, "deleted")

    async def import_profile(self, document: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Profile:
        """A profile from a document, through the validators a manual write uses."""
        require_editor()
        stored = await self._repository.add_profile(profile_from_document(document, now=self._now()))
        self._audit("profile imported", "profile_changed", stored.id, "imported")
        return stored

    # --- Stereotypes ------------------------------------------------------------

    async def add_stereotype(
        self,
        profile_id: UUID,
        *,
        name: str,
        base_type: ElementType,
        colour: str | None,
        icon_key: IconKey,
        properties: Sequence[StereotypeProperty],
    ) -> Stereotype:
        require_editor()
        await self.get_profile(profile_id)
        stored = await self._repository.add_stereotype(
            Stereotype(
                id=uuid4(),
                profile_id=profile_id,
                name=name,
                base_type=base_type,
                colour=colour,
                icon_key=icon_key,
                properties=tuple(properties),
            )
        )
        self._audit("stereotype added", "profile_changed", profile_id, "stereotype_added")
        return stored

    async def replace_stereotype(
        self,
        stereotype_id: UUID,
        *,
        name: str,
        base_type: ElementType,
        colour: str | None,
        icon_key: IconKey,
        properties: Sequence[StereotypeProperty],
    ) -> Stereotype:
        require_editor()
        current = await self._stereotype(stereotype_id)
        applied = (await self._repository.applied_counts([stereotype_id])).get(stereotype_id, 0)
        if applied and base_type is not current.base_type:
            msg = (
                f"the stereotype {current.name!r} is applied to {applied} element(s): "
                "its base type cannot change"
            )
            raise StereotypeInUseError(msg)
        stored = await self._repository.replace_stereotype(
            Stereotype(
                id=stereotype_id,
                profile_id=current.profile_id,
                name=name,
                base_type=base_type,
                colour=colour,
                icon_key=icon_key,
                properties=tuple(properties),
            )
        )
        self._audit("stereotype replaced", "profile_changed", current.profile_id, "stereotype_replaced")
        return stored

    async def delete_stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> None:
        require_editor()
        current = await self._stereotype(stereotype_id)
        applied = (await self._repository.applied_counts([stereotype_id])).get(stereotype_id, 0)
        if applied:
            msg = f"the stereotype {current.name!r} is applied to {applied} element(s)"
            raise StereotypeInUseError(msg)
        await self._repository.delete_stereotype(stereotype_id)
        self._audit("stereotype deleted", "profile_changed", current.profile_id, "stereotype_deleted")

    # --- On elements ------------------------------------------------------------

    async def apply_stereotypes(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_ids: Sequence[UUID]) -> Element:
        """Make the set of stereotypes an element carries exactly `stereotype_ids`."""
        require_editor()
        element = await self._architecture.get_element(element_id)
        chosen = [await self._stereotype(sid) for sid in dict.fromkeys(stereotype_ids)]
        for stereotype in chosen:
            if stereotype.base_type is not element.element_type:
                msg = (
                    f"stereotype {stereotype.name!r} applies to {stereotype.base_type.value}, "
                    f"not to {element.element_type.value}"
                )
                raise ValueError(msg)
        validate_stereotype_properties(element.properties, chosen)
        await self._repository.set_for_element(element_id, [s.id for s in chosen])
        # Counts and ids only: a property value may be sensitive.
        logger.info(
            "stereotypes of an element set",
            extra={
                "action": "element_stereotypes_changed",
                "element_id": str(element_id),
                "stereotypes": len(chosen),
            },
        )
        return await self._architecture.get_element(element_id)

    async def apply_stereotype(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Element:
        require_editor()
        element = await self._architecture.get_element(element_id)
        carried = [ref.id for ref in element.stereotypes]
        return await self.apply_stereotypes(element_id, [*carried, stereotype_id])

    async def remove_stereotype(self, element_id: UUID, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Element:
        """Take the label off. The properties stay: they are the element's own."""
        require_editor()
        element = await self._architecture.get_element(element_id)
        kept = [ref.id for ref in element.stereotypes if ref.id != stereotype_id]
        return await self.apply_stereotypes(element_id, kept)

    async def _stereotype(self, stereotype_id: UUID) -> Stereotype:
        found = await self._repository.get_stereotype(stereotype_id)
        if found is None:
            msg = f"no stereotype with id {stereotype_id}"
            raise StereotypeNotFoundError(msg)
        return found

    @staticmethod
    def _audit(message: str, action: str, profile_id: UUID, change: str) -> None:
        logger.info(
            message, extra={"action": action, "profile_id": str(profile_id), "change": change}
        )
```
Remarque : `_stereotype` est un helper non public (préfixe `_`) donc hors du test de gardes ; `test_service_guards` exige que **chaque coroutine publique** commence par `require_caller()`/`require_editor()` : `apply_stereotype`/`remove_stereotype` commencent par `require_editor()` ✓.

```python
"""Profile endpoints end to end: statuses, typed errors, the round trip of a document."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any

import httpx
import pytest
import pytest_asyncio

from ea.core.config import Settings
from ea.main import create_app
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from tests.conftest import InMemoryProfiles, StaticVerifier, a_reader, an_editor

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

EDITOR, READER = "editor-token", "reader-token"


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def client(service: ArchitectureService, profiles: InMemoryProfiles) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=False),
        architecture_service=service,
        profiles=profiles,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:
        yield client


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def secured(
    service: ArchitectureService, profiles: InMemoryProfiles, nobody_calling: None
) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=True),
        architecture_service=service,
        profiles=profiles,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        verifier=StaticVerifier({EDITOR: an_editor(), READER: a_reader()}),
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:
        yield client


def stereotype(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "name": "database",
        "base_type": "application_component",
        "colour": "#ffe0e0",
        "icon_key": "database",
        "properties": [
            {"key": "tier", "label": "Niveau", "required": True, "kind": "enum",
             "allowed_values": ["1", "2"], "pattern": None, "help": ""},
        ],
        **overrides,
    }


async def a_profile(client: httpx.AsyncClient, name: str = "SI") -> dict[str, Any]:
    response = await client.post("/profiles", json={"name": name, "description": ""})
    assert response.status_code == 201, response.text
    return dict(response.json())


async def an_element(client: httpx.AsyncClient, element_type: str = "application_component") -> str:
    response = await client.post("/elements", json={"element_type": element_type, "name": "Facturation"})
    return str(response.json()["id"])


class TestProfilesAndStereotypes:
    async def test_a_profile_is_created_read_and_listed_with_its_stereotypes(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)

        created = await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())
        read = (await client.get(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}")).json()
        flat = (await client.get("/stereotypes")).json()

        assert created.status_code == 201
        assert [s["name"] for s in read["stereotypes"]] == ["database"]
        assert [s["id"] for s in flat] == [created.json()["id"]]

    async def test_a_taken_profile_name_is_a_409_duplicate(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        await a_profile(client)

        response = await client.post("/profiles", json={"name": "SI", "description": ""})

        assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]) == (409, "duplicate")

    async def test_a_dangerous_pattern_or_a_reserved_key_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        url = f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes"
        prop = {"label": "X", "required": False, "allowed_values": [], "help": ""}

        redos = await client.post(url, json=stereotype(properties=[{**prop, "key": "k", "kind": "pattern", "pattern": "(a+)+"}]))
        reserved = await client.post(url, json=stereotype(properties=[{**prop, "key": "ip_address", "kind": "free", "pattern": None}]))
        colour = await client.post(url, json=stereotype(colour='red" onload="x'))

        assert (redos.status_code, reserved.status_code, colour.status_code) == (422, 422, 422)

    async def test_an_icon_outside_the_closed_set_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)

        response = await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype(icon_key="skull"))

        assert response.status_code == 422


class TestOnElements:
    async def test_applying_shows_the_stereotype_on_the_element_everywhere(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        sid = (await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())).json()["id"]
        element = await an_element(client)

        applied = await client.put(f"/elements/{element}/stereotypes", json={"stereotype_ids": [sid]})

        assert applied.status_code == 200
        assert [s["name"] for s in applied.json()["stereotypes"]] == ["database"]
        assert [s["name"] for s in (await client.get(f"/elements/{element}")).json()["stereotypes"]] == ["database"]
        page = (await client.get("/elements")).json()
        assert [s["name"] for s in page["items"][0]["stereotypes"]] == ["database"]

    async def test_a_stereotype_of_another_type_is_a_422_naming_both(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        sid = (await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())).json()["id"]
        node = await an_element(client, "node")

        response = await client.put(f"/elements/{node}/stereotypes", json={"stereotype_ids": [sid]})

        assert response.status_code == 422
        assert "application_component" in response.json()["detail"] and "node" in response.json()["detail"]

    async def test_patching_a_property_against_a_carried_stereotype_is_checked(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        sid = (await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())).json()["id"]
        element = await an_element(client)
        await client.put(f"/elements/{element}/stereotypes", json={"stereotype_ids": [sid]})

        bad = await client.patch(f"/elements/{element}", json={"properties": {"tier": "9"}})
        good = await client.patch(f"/elements/{element}", json={"properties": {"tier": "2"}})

        assert (bad.status_code, good.status_code) == (422, 200)
        assert [s["name"] for s in good.json()["stereotypes"]] == ["database"]

    async def test_deleting_a_stereotype_in_use_is_a_409_with_the_count(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        sid = (await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())).json()["id"]
        element = await an_element(client)
        await client.put(f"/elements/{element}/stereotypes", json={"stereotype_ids": [sid]})

        response = await client.delete(f"/stereotypes/{sid}")

        assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]) == (409, "stereotype_in_use")
        assert "1 element" in response.json()["detail"]


class TestExportAndImport:
    async def test_a_profile_goes_out_and_comes_back_in_under_another_name(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        profile = await a_profile(client)
        await client.post(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/stereotypes", json=stereotype())

        document = (await client.get(f"/profiles/{profile['id']}/export")).json()
        imported = await client.post(
            "/profiles/import", content=json.dumps({**document, "name": "SI (copie)"}),
            headers={"content-type": "application/json"},
        )

        assert document["profile_format"] == 1
        assert imported.status_code == 201
        assert imported.json()["stereotypes"][0]["properties"][0]["key"] == "tier"

    async def test_a_document_of_another_format_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        response = await client.post(
            "/profiles/import",
            content=json.dumps({"profile_format": 2, "name": "X", "description": "", "stereotypes": []}),
            headers={"content-type": "application/json"},
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422

    async def test_a_document_over_256_kb_is_refused_before_it_is_read(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        big = json.dumps({"profile_format": 1, "name": "X", "description": "x" * 300_000, "stereotypes": []})

        response = await client.post("/profiles/import", content=big, headers={"content-type": "application/json"})

        assert response.status_code == 422
        assert "256" in response.json()["detail"]

    async def test_malformed_json_is_a_422_that_leaks_nothing(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        response = await client.post("/profiles/import", content="{not json", headers={"content-type": "application/json"})

        assert response.status_code == 422
        assert "pydantic" not in response.text.lower()


class TestWhoMayWrite:
    async def test_a_reader_reads_profiles_and_cannot_write_or_import(self, secured: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        editor = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {EDITOR}"}
        reader = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {READER}"}
        created = await secured.post("/profiles", json={"name": "SI", "description": ""}, headers=editor)
        pid = created.json()["id"]

        listed = await secured.get("/profiles", headers=reader)
        created_by_reader = await secured.post("/profiles", json={"name": "X", "description": ""}, headers=reader)
        imported = await secured.post(
            "/profiles/import",
            content=json.dumps({"profile_format": 1, "name": "Y", "description": "", "stereotypes": []}),
            headers={**reader, "content-type": "application/json"},
        )
        deleted = await secured.delete(f"/profiles/{pid}", headers=reader)

        assert listed.status_code == 200
        assert (created_by_reader.status_code, imported.status_code, deleted.status_code) == (403, 403, 403)
```

```python
class StereotypeRef(BaseModel):
    """The stereotype an element carries — enough to draw it, none of its rules."""

    id: UUID
    name: str
    colour: Colour | None
    icon_key: IconKey

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, ref: StereotypeRefModel) -> StereotypeRef:
        return cls(id=ref.id, name=ref.name, colour=ref.colour, icon_key=IconKey(ref.icon_key))
```
et ajouter à `ElementRead` : `stereotypes: list[StereotypeRef]` après `properties`, et dans `of` : `stereotypes=[StereotypeRef.of(ref) for ref in element.stereotypes]`. Rechercher les constructions directes (`grep -rn "ElementRead(" backend/src backend/tests`) et y ajouter `stereotypes=[]`.

```python
# --- Profiles and stereotypes (docs/adr: profils et stéréotypes) --------------

#: The size of a profile document an import accepts.
MAX_PROFILE_DOCUMENT_BYTES: Final = 256 * 1024
_ProfileName = Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=MAX_NAME_LENGTH)]


class StereotypePropertySchema(_Input):
    """One declared property. Every field is required: no default, no `-Input` twin."""

    key: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=63)]
    label: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)]
    required: bool
    kind: PropertyKind
    allowed_values: Annotated[list[Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=500)]], Field(max_length=50)]
    pattern: Annotated[str, Field(max_length=200)] | None
    help: Annotated[str, Field(max_length=300)]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, prop: StereotypeProperty) -> StereotypePropertySchema:
        return cls(
            key=prop.key,
            label=prop.label,
            required=prop.required,
            kind=prop.kind,
            allowed_values=list(prop.allowed_values),
            pattern=prop.pattern,
            help=prop.help,
        )

    def declared(self) -> StereotypeProperty:
        return StereotypeProperty(
            key=self.key,
            label=self.label,
            required=self.required,
            kind=self.kind,
            allowed_values=tuple(self.allowed_values),
            pattern=self.pattern,
            help=self.help,
        )


class StereotypeWrite(_Input):
    name: _ProfileName
    base_type: ElementType
    colour: Colour | None
    icon_key: IconKey
    properties: Annotated[list[StereotypePropertySchema], Field(max_length=MAX_PROPERTIES_PER_STEREOTYPE)]


class StereotypeRead(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    profile_id: UUID
    name: str
    base_type: ElementType
    colour: str | None
    icon_key: IconKey
    properties: list[StereotypePropertySchema]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, stereotype: Stereotype) -> StereotypeRead:
        return cls(
            id=stereotype.id,
            profile_id=stereotype.profile_id,
            name=stereotype.name,
            base_type=stereotype.base_type,
            colour=stereotype.colour,
            icon_key=stereotype.icon_key,
            properties=[StereotypePropertySchema.of(p) for p in stereotype.properties],
        )


class ProfileCreate(_Input):
    name: _ProfileName
    description: Description = ""


class ProfileUpdate(_Input):
    name: _ProfileName | None = None
    description: Description | None = None


class ProfileRead(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    name: str
    description: str
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    stereotypes: list[StereotypeRead]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, profile: Profile) -> ProfileRead:
        return cls(
            id=profile.id,
            name=profile.name,
            description=profile.description,
            created_at=profile.created_at,
            updated_at=profile.updated_at,
            stereotypes=[StereotypeRead.of(s) for s in profile.stereotypes],
        )


class ProfileDocument(_Input):
    """A profile as a portable document: no ids, no dates, a version."""

    profile_format: Literal[1]
    name: _ProfileName
    description: Description
    stereotypes: Annotated[list[StereotypeWrite], Field(max_length=MAX_STEREOTYPES_PER_PROFILE)]


class ElementStereotypesSet(_Input):
    """The whole set of stereotypes an element carries: it replaces the previous one."""

    stereotype_ids: Annotated[list[UUID], Field(max_length=20)]
```
