`backend/src/ea/api/profiles.py` :

```python
"""The endpoints of profiles and stereotypes — reference data, extending no ArchiMate type.

A stereotype is a label on an element of one base type (docs/adr: profils et
stéréotypes). The values stay in `elements.properties`; these routes only
define the labels and lay them on elements. Import and export are versioned
documents: the import is validated by the very constructors a manual write uses.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Request, Response, status
from pydantic import ValidationError

from ea.api.dependencies import Profiles
from ea.api.schemas import (
    MAX_PROFILE_DOCUMENT_BYTES,
    ElementRead,
    ElementStereotypesSet,
    ErrorResponse,
    ProfileCreate,
    ProfileDocument,
    ProfileRead,
    ProfileUpdate,
    StereotypeRead,
    StereotypeWrite,
)

router = APIRouter(tags=["profiles"])

_ERROR = {"model": ErrorResponse}
NOT_FOUND: dict[int | str, dict[str, type[ErrorResponse]]] = {status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND: _ERROR}
REJECTED: dict[int | str, dict[str, type[ErrorResponse]]] = {
    status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND: _ERROR,
    status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT: _ERROR,
    status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT: _ERROR,
}


@router.get("/profiles", response_model=list[ProfileRead])
async def list_profiles(profiles: Profiles) -> list[ProfileRead]:
    """Every profile with its stereotypes, sorted by name."""
    return [ProfileRead.of(p) for p in await profiles.list_profiles()]


@router.post(
    "/profiles", response_model=ProfileRead, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED, responses=REJECTED
)
async def create_profile(body: ProfileCreate, profiles: Profiles) -> ProfileRead:
    return ProfileRead.of(await profiles.create_profile(name=body.name, description=body.description))


@router.post(
    "/profiles/import",
    response_model=ProfileRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=REJECTED,
    openapi_extra={
        "requestBody": {
            "required": True,
            "content": {
                "application/json": {"schema": {"$ref": "#/components/schemas/ProfileDocument"}}
            },
        }
    },
)
async def import_profile(request: Request, profiles: Profiles) -> ProfileRead:
    """Create a profile from an exported document (at most 256 KB, `profile_format: 1`).

    The body is read and measured here rather than parsed by FastAPI, so the
    size ceiling is checked on the bytes and a malformed document is a typed
    422 rather than a validation report naming the server's models.
    """
    declared = request.headers.get("content-length")
    if declared and declared.isdigit() and int(declared) > MAX_PROFILE_DOCUMENT_BYTES:
        msg = "a profile document is at most 256 KB"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    raw = await request.body()
    if len(raw) > MAX_PROFILE_DOCUMENT_BYTES:
        msg = "a profile document is at most 256 KB"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    try:
        document = ProfileDocument.model_validate_json(raw)
    except ValidationError as error:
        first = error.errors()[0]
        place = ".".join(str(part) for part in first["loc"]) or "document"
        msg = f"invalid profile document: {place}: {first['msg']}"
        raise ValueError(msg) from None
    return ProfileRead.of(await profiles.import_profile(document.model_dump(mode="python")))


@router.get("/profiles/{profile_id}", response_model=ProfileRead, responses=NOT_FOUND)
async def read_profile(profile_id: UUID, profiles: Profiles) -> ProfileRead:
    return ProfileRead.of(await profiles.get_profile(profile_id))


@router.put("/profiles/{profile_id}", response_model=ProfileRead, responses=REJECTED)
async def update_profile(profile_id: UUID, body: ProfileUpdate, profiles: Profiles) -> ProfileRead:
    return ProfileRead.of(
        await profiles.update_profile(profile_id, name=body.name, description=body.description)
    )


@router.delete(
    "/profiles/{profile_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT, responses=REJECTED
)
async def delete_profile(profile_id: UUID, profiles: Profiles) -> Response:
    """Delete a profile — refused while one of its stereotypes is applied to an element."""
    await profiles.delete_profile(profile_id)
    return Response(status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)


@router.get(
    "/profiles/{profile_id}/export", response_model=ProfileDocument, responses=NOT_FOUND
)
async def export_profile(profile_id: UUID, profiles: Profiles) -> ProfileDocument:
    """The profile as a versioned document that `POST /profiles/import` accepts."""
    return ProfileDocument.model_validate(await profiles.export_profile(profile_id))


@router.post(
    "/profiles/{profile_id}/stereotypes",
    response_model=StereotypeRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=REJECTED,
)
async def add_stereotype(
    profile_id: UUID, body: StereotypeWrite, profiles: Profiles
) -> StereotypeRead:
    return StereotypeRead.of(
        await profiles.add_stereotype(
            profile_id,
            name=body.name,
            base_type=body.base_type,
            colour=body.colour,
            icon_key=body.icon_key,
            properties=[p.declared() for p in body.properties],
        )
    )


@router.get("/stereotypes", response_model=list[StereotypeRead])
async def list_stereotypes(profiles: Profiles) -> list[StereotypeRead]:
    """Every stereotype, flat — what the SPA needs to draw and to offer them."""
    return [StereotypeRead.of(s) for s in await profiles.list_stereotypes()]


@router.put("/stereotypes/{stereotype_id}", response_model=StereotypeRead, responses=REJECTED)
async def replace_stereotype(
    stereotype_id: UUID, body: StereotypeWrite, profiles: Profiles
) -> StereotypeRead:
    return StereotypeRead.of(
        await profiles.replace_stereotype(
            stereotype_id,
            name=body.name,
            base_type=body.base_type,
            colour=body.colour,
            icon_key=body.icon_key,
            properties=[p.declared() for p in body.properties],
        )
    )


@router.delete(
    "/stereotypes/{stereotype_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT, responses=REJECTED
)
async def delete_stereotype(stereotype_id: UUID, profiles: Profiles) -> Response:
    """Delete a stereotype — refused, with the count, while elements carry it."""
    await profiles.delete_stereotype(stereotype_id)
    return Response(status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)


@router.put("/elements/{element_id}/stereotypes", response_model=ElementRead, responses=REJECTED)
async def set_element_stereotypes(
    element_id: UUID, body: ElementStereotypesSet, profiles: Profiles
) -> ElementRead:
    """Replace the set of stereotypes an element carries.

    A stereotype of another base type is a 422, and so is a set of stereotypes
    declaring one key twice or a stored value that breaks a declared format.
    """
    return ElementRead.of(await profiles.apply_stereotypes(element_id, body.stereotype_ids))
```

```python
    # --- Profiles and stereotypes -------------------------------------------

    @server.tool(annotations=READS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def list_profiles() -> list[ProfileRead]:
        """The profiles, each with its stereotypes and the properties they expect.

        A stereotype is a label for one ArchiMate type ("database" on an
        application component) with the properties people expect on such an
        element. Read this before tagging an element: `apply_stereotype` only
        accepts a stereotype made for the element's own type.
        """
        return [ProfileRead.of(p) for p in await get_profiles().list_profiles()]

    @server.tool(annotations=EDITS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def apply_stereotype(element_id: ElementId, stereotype_id: StereotypeId) -> ElementRead:
        """Tag an element with a stereotype made for its type.

        Idempotent. A stereotype of another type is refused, saying which, and
        so is one whose declared property would conflict with another one the
        element already carries. The property *values* stay the element's own:
        write them with `update_element`.
        """
        return ElementRead.of(await get_profiles().apply_stereotype(element_id, stereotype_id))

    @server.tool(annotations=EDITS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def remove_stereotype(element_id: ElementId, stereotype_id: StereotypeId) -> ElementRead:
        """Take a stereotype's label off an element. Its properties are left untouched."""
        return ElementRead.of(await get_profiles().remove_stereotype(element_id, stereotype_id))
```

`frontend/tests/useProfiles.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { useProfiles } from '../src/features/profiles/useProfiles'
import { stubApi } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals())

const PROFILE = {
  id: 'pppppppp-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
  name: 'SI',
  description: '',
  created_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
  updated_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
  stereotypes: [],
}

describe('useProfiles', () => {
  it('loads the profiles', async () => {
    stubApi([{ path: '/profiles', body: [PROFILE] }])
    const profiles = useProfiles()

    await profiles.load()

    expect(profiles.profiles.value.map((p) => p.name)).toEqual(['SI'])
    expect(profiles.status.value).toBe('ready')
  })

  it('creates a profile then reloads the list', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: '/profiles', status: 201, body: PROFILE },
      { path: '/profiles', body: [PROFILE] },
    ])
    const profiles = useProfiles()

    await profiles.createProfile('SI')

    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ name: 'SI', description: '' })
    expect(profiles.profiles.value).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('throws the refusal of a stereotype in use, with its count, for the screen to show', async () => {
    stubApi([
      {
        method: 'DELETE',
        path: '/stereotypes/s1',
        status: 409,
        body: { error: 'stereotype_in_use', detail: 'applied to 3 element(s)' },
      },
    ])
    const profiles = useProfiles()

    await expect(profiles.removeStereotype('s1')).rejects.toThrow('applied to 3 element(s)')
  })

  it('exports as text and imports from text, refusing invalid JSON before any request', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      { path: `/profiles/${PROFILE.id}/export`, body: { profile_format: 1, name: 'SI', description: '', stereotypes: [] } },
      { method: 'POST', path: '/profiles/import', status: 201, body: PROFILE },
      { path: '/profiles', body: [PROFILE] },
    ])
    const profiles = useProfiles()

    expect(JSON.parse(await profiles.exportProfile(PROFILE.id)).profile_format).toBe(1)
    await expect(profiles.importProfile('{not json')).rejects.toThrow(/JSON/)
    expect(calls.some((c) => c.method === 'POST')).toBe(false)
    await profiles.importProfile(JSON.stringify({ profile_format: 1, name: 'SI', description: '', stereotypes: [] }))
    expect(calls.some((c) => c.method === 'POST')).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

```ts
const STEREOTYPE = {

const ROUTES: Route[] = [
  { path: '/profiles', body: [PROFILE] },
  { path: '/metamodel', body: METAMODEL },
]

function asAReader(): void {
  vi.mocked(useMe).mockReturnValueOnce({
    me: ref({ username: 'reader', can_write: false }),
    canWrite: computed(() => false),
    error: ref(null),
    load: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  })
}

describe('ProfilesSection', () => {
  it('lists the profiles with their stereotypes and the properties they expect', async () => {
    stubApi(ROUTES)
    render(ProfilesSection)

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'SI' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    const stereotype = screen.getByRole('listitem', { name: /database/ })
    expect(within(stereotype).getByText(/Niveau/)).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(within(stereotype).getByText(/obligatoire/i)).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('creates a profile by name', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([{ method: 'POST', path: '/profiles', status: 201, body: PROFILE }, ...ROUTES])
    render(ProfilesSection)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'SI' })

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom du profil'), 'Sécurité')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer le profil' }))

    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ name: 'Sécurité', description: '' }))
  })

  it('shows why a stereotype in use cannot be deleted', async () => {
    stubApi([
      { method: 'DELETE', path: `/stereotypes/${STEREOTYPE.id}`, status: 409,
        body: { error: 'stereotype_in_use', detail: 'The stereotype is applied to 3 element(s)' } },
      ...ROUTES,
    ])
    render(ProfilesSection)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'SI' })

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Supprimer le stéréotype database' }))

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('3 element(s)')
  })

  it('saves a new stereotype with a closed list of icons and the base types of the metamodel', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: `/profiles/${PROFILE.id}/stereotypes`, status: 201, body: STEREOTYPE },
      ...ROUTES,
    ])
    render(ProfilesSection)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'SI' })

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Ajouter un stéréotype à SI' }))
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom du stéréotype'), 'queue')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Type de base'), 'application_component')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Icône'), 'queue')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enregistrer le stéréotype' }))

    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST' && c.url.pathname.endsWith('/stereotypes'))?.body).toMatchObject({
        name: 'queue',
        base_type: 'application_component',
        icon_key: 'queue',
        properties: [],
      }),
    )
  })

  it('offers a reader the export and nothing that writes', async () => {
    stubApi(ROUTES)
    asAReader()
    render(ProfilesSection)
    await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'SI' })

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Exporter SI' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Nom du profil')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Importer un profil')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Supprimer/ })).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: /Ajouter un stéréotype/ })).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

```ts
// Profiles and their stereotypes: reference data that extends no ArchiMate type.
//
// A stereotype is a label on an element of one base type, with the properties
// people expect on it. The rules (a dangerous pattern, a reserved key, a
// stereotype in use) are the API's: a refusal is thrown for the screen to show.
import { ref } from 'vue'

import type { components } from '../../api/schema'
import { api, unwrap } from '../../lib/api'
import { useLatestRequest } from '../../lib/latest'

export type ProfileRead = components['schemas']['ProfileRead']
export type StereotypeRead = components['schemas']['StereotypeRead']
export type StereotypeWrite = components['schemas']['StereotypeWrite']
export type IconKey = components['schemas']['IconKey']

export function useProfiles() {
  const profiles = ref<ProfileRead[]>([])
  const listing = useLatestRequest()
  const { status, error } = listing

  async function load(): Promise<void> {
    await listing.run(
      async (signal) => unwrap(await api.GET('/profiles', { signal })),
      (answer) => {
        profiles.value = answer
      },
    )
  }

  async function createProfile(name: string): Promise<void> {
    unwrap(await api.POST('/profiles', { body: { name, description: '' } }))
    await load()
  }

  async function removeProfile(id: string): Promise<void> {
    unwrap(await api.DELETE('/profiles/{profile_id}', { params: { path: { profile_id: id } } }))
    await load()
  }

  /** Create (`stereotypeId` null) or replace a stereotype, properties included. */
  async function saveStereotype(
    profileId: string,
    stereotypeId: string | null,
    body: StereotypeWrite,
  ): Promise<void> {
    if (stereotypeId === null) {
      unwrap(
        await api.POST('/profiles/{profile_id}/stereotypes', {
          params: { path: { profile_id: profileId } },
          body,
        }),
      )
    } else {
      unwrap(
        await api.PUT('/stereotypes/{stereotype_id}', {
          params: { path: { stereotype_id: stereotypeId } },
          body,
        }),
      )
    }
    await load()
  }

  async function removeStereotype(id: string): Promise<void> {
    unwrap(
      await api.DELETE('/stereotypes/{stereotype_id}', { params: { path: { stereotype_id: id } } }),
    )
    await load()
  }

  /** The profile as the versioned document `importProfile` accepts. */
  async function exportProfile(id: string): Promise<string> {
    const document = unwrap(
      await api.GET('/profiles/{profile_id}/export', { params: { path: { profile_id: id } } }),
    )
    return JSON.stringify(document, null, 2)
  }

  /** Text that is not JSON never reaches the API; everything else is the API's to judge. */
  async function importProfile(text: string): Promise<void> {
    let document: components['schemas']['ProfileDocument']
    try {
      document = JSON.parse(text) as components['schemas']['ProfileDocument']
    } catch {
      throw new Error("Ce fichier n'est pas du JSON valide.")
    }
    unwrap(await api.POST('/profiles/import', { body: document }))
    await load()
  }

  return {
    profiles,
    status,
    error,
    load,
    createProfile,
    removeProfile,
    saveStereotype,
    removeStereotype,
    exportProfile,
    importProfile,
  }
}
```

`frontend/src/features/profiles/StereotypeEditor.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// The form of one stereotype: its name, the type it extends, its colour and
// icon, and the properties it expects. It checks nothing the API checks — a
// dangerous pattern or a reserved key comes back as the API's own refusal.
import { ref } from 'vue'

import type { IconKey, StereotypeRead, StereotypeWrite } from './useProfiles'

const props = defineProps<{
  stereotype?: StereotypeRead
  /** The base types, as `/metamodel` offers them. */
  types: { value: string; label: string }[]
  failure: string
}>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ save: [body: StereotypeWrite]; cancel: [] }>()

/** The closed set of icons the SPA ships (the API validates the same set). */
const ICONS: IconKey[] = [
  'none', 'database', 'server', 'cloud', 'user', 'lock', 'document', 'gear', 'queue', 'api',
]
type Draft = StereotypeWrite['properties'][number]

const name = ref(props.stereotype?.name ?? '')
const baseType = ref<string>(props.stereotype?.base_type ?? '')
const colour = ref(props.stereotype?.colour ?? '')
const icon = ref<IconKey>(props.stereotype?.icon_key ?? 'none')
const properties = ref<Draft[]>(props.stereotype?.properties.map((p) => ({ ...p })) ?? [])

function addProperty(): void {
  properties.value.push({
    key: '',
    label: '',
    required: false,
    kind: 'free',
    allowed_values: [],
    pattern: null,
    help: '',
  })
}

function save(): void {
  emit('save', {
    name: name.value.trim(),
    base_type: baseType.value as StereotypeWrite['base_type'],
    colour: colour.value || null,
    icon_key: icon.value,
    properties: properties.value.map((p) => ({
      ...p,
      allowed_values: p.kind === 'enum' ? p.allowed_values : [],
      pattern: p.kind === 'pattern' ? p.pattern : null,
    })),
  })
}

function valuesOf(p: Draft): string {
  return p.allowed_values.join('\n')
}
function setValues(p: Draft, text: string): void {
  p.allowed_values = text.split('\n').map((v) => v.trim()).filter(Boolean)
}
</script>

<template>
  <form class="stereotype-form" aria-label="Stéréotype" @submit.prevent="save">
    <label for="st-name">Nom du stéréotype</label>
    <input id="st-name" v-model="name" maxlength="100" autocomplete="off" />
    <label for="st-type">Type de base</label>
    <select id="st-type" v-model="baseType">
      <option value="" disabled>Choisir…</option>
      <option v-for="type in types" :key="type.value" :value="type.value">{{ type.label }}</option>
    </select>
    <label for="st-colour">Couleur</label>
    <input id="st-colour" v-model="colour" type="color" />
    <label for="st-icon">Icône</label>
    <select id="st-icon" v-model="icon">
      <option v-for="key in ICONS" :key="key" :value="key">{{ key }}</option>
    </select>

    <fieldset v-for="(p, index) in properties" :key="index" class="property">
      <legend>Propriété {{ index + 1 }}</legend>
      <label :for="`p-key-${index}`">Clé</label>
      <input :id="`p-key-${index}`" v-model="p.key" maxlength="63" autocomplete="off" />
      <label :for="`p-label-${index}`">Libellé</label>
      <input :id="`p-label-${index}`" v-model="p.label" maxlength="100" />
      <label><input v-model="p.required" type="checkbox" /> Obligatoire</label>
      <label :for="`p-kind-${index}`">Forme</label>
      <select :id="`p-kind-${index}`" v-model="p.kind">
        <option value="free">Libre</option>
        <option value="enum">Liste de valeurs</option>
        <option value="pattern">Motif</option>
      </select>
      <template v-if="p.kind === 'enum'">
        <label :for="`p-values-${index}`">Valeurs permises (une par ligne)</label>
        <textarea :id="`p-values-${index}`" :value="valuesOf(p)" rows="3" @input="setValues(p, ($event.target as HTMLTextAreaElement).value)" />
      </template>
      <template v-if="p.kind === 'pattern'">
        <label :for="`p-pattern-${index}`">Motif</label>
        <input :id="`p-pattern-${index}`" v-model="p.pattern" maxlength="200" />
      </template>
      <label :for="`p-help-${index}`">Aide</label>
      <input :id="`p-help-${index}`" v-model="p.help" maxlength="300" />
      <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="properties.splice(index, 1)">Retirer la propriété</button>
    </fieldset>

    <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="addProperty">Ajouter une propriété</button>
    <p v-if="failure" class="banner banner--error" role="alert">{{ failure }}</p>
    <div class="actions">
      <button type="submit">Enregistrer le stéréotype</button>
      <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="emit('cancel')">Renoncer</button>
    </div>
  </form>
</template>

.stereotype-form,
.property {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}
.property {
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 0.6rem;
}
.actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}
</style>
```

`frontend/src/features/profiles/ProfilesSection.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Profiles: the stereotypes people lay on elements, and the properties each expects.
//
// Reference data, rarely changed, read by people: the screen lists it, edits
// it for an editor, and moves it between environments as a versioned JSON file.
// It restates no rule — a refusal is the API's own sentence.
import { onMounted, ref } from 'vue'

import { messageOf } from '../../lib/api'
import { download } from '../../lib/diagramExport'
import { useMe } from '../../lib/me'
import { useMetamodel } from '../metamodel/useMetamodel'
import StereotypeEditor from './StereotypeEditor.vue'
import { useProfiles, type ProfileRead, type StereotypeRead, type StereotypeWrite } from './useProfiles'

const { canWrite } = useMe()
const profiles = useProfiles()
const metamodel = useMetamodel()

const newName = ref('')
const failure = ref('')
const editing = ref<{ profile: ProfileRead; stereotype: StereotypeRead | null } | null>(null)

onMounted(async () => {
  await Promise.all([profiles.load(), metamodel.load()])
})

/** Every base type the metamodel offers, labelled — asked for, never listed here. */
function types(): { value: string; label: string }[] {
  return metamodel.elementTypes.value.map((type) => ({ value: type.value, label: type.label }))
}

async function attempt(action: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
  failure.value = ''
  try {
    await action()
  } catch (caught) {
    failure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}

async function create(): Promise<void> {
  await attempt(async () => {
    await profiles.createProfile(newName.value.trim())
    newName.value = ''
  })
}

async function save(body: StereotypeWrite): Promise<void> {
  const target = editing.value
  if (!target) {
    return
  }
  await attempt(async () => {
    await profiles.saveStereotype(target.profile.id, target.stereotype?.id ?? null, body)
    editing.value = null
  })
}

async function exportFile(profile: ProfileRead): Promise<void> {
  await attempt(async () => {
    const text = await profiles.exportProfile(profile.id)
    download(new Blob([text], { type: 'application/json' }), `${profile.name}.profil.json`)
  })
}

async function importFile(event: Event): Promise<void> {
  const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement
  const file = input.files?.[0]
  if (!file) {
    return
  }
  await attempt(async () => profiles.importProfile(await file.text()))
  input.value = ''
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="profiles">
    <header>
      <h2>Profils</h2>
      <p class="hint">
        Un stéréotype est une étiquette posée sur un élément d'un type donné, avec les propriétés
        attendues. Il ne change ni le type de l'élément, ni ce qu'il peut relier.
      </p>
    </header>

    <form v-if="canWrite" class="create" aria-label="Créer un profil" @submit.prevent="create">
      <label for="profile-name">Nom du profil</label>
      <input id="profile-name" v-model="newName" maxlength="100" autocomplete="off" />
      <button type="submit" :disabled="!newName.trim()">Créer le profil</button>
      <label for="profile-import">Importer un profil</label>
      <input id="profile-import" type="file" accept="application/json,.json" @change="importFile" />
    </form>

    <p v-if="failure" class="banner banner--error" role="alert">{{ failure }}</p>
    <p v-if="profiles.status.value === 'error'" class="banner banner--error" role="alert">
      {{ profiles.error.value }}
    </p>

    <article v-for="profile in profiles.profiles.value" :key="profile.id" class="profile">
      <header>
        <h3>{{ profile.name }}</h3>
        <button type="button" class="secondary" :aria-label="`Exporter ${profile.name}`" @click="exportFile(profile)">
          Exporter
        </button>
        <button
          v-if="canWrite"
          type="button"
          class="secondary"
          :aria-label="`Supprimer le profil ${profile.name}`"
          @click="attempt(() => profiles.removeProfile(profile.id))"
        >
          Supprimer
        </button>
      </header>
      <p v-if="profile.description" class="hint">{{ profile.description }}</p>

      <ul class="stereotypes">
        <li
          v-for="stereotype in profile.stereotypes"
          :key="stereotype.id"
          :aria-label="stereotype.name"
        >
          <strong>«{{ stereotype.name }}»</strong> sur {{ metamodel.labelOf(stereotype.base_type) }}
          <ul>
            <li v-for="prop in stereotype.properties" :key="prop.key">
              {{ prop.label }} (<code>{{ prop.key }}</code>)<span v-if="prop.required"> — obligatoire</span>
              <span v-if="prop.kind === 'enum'"> — {{ prop.allowed_values.join(', ') }}</span>
              <span v-if="prop.kind === 'pattern'"> — motif <code>{{ prop.pattern }}</code></span>
            </li>
          </ul>
          <template v-if="canWrite">
            <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="editing = { profile, stereotype }">Modifier</button>
            <button
              type="button"
              class="secondary"
              :aria-label="`Supprimer le stéréotype ${stereotype.name}`"
              @click="attempt(() => profiles.removeStereotype(stereotype.id))"
            >
              Supprimer
            </button>
          </template>
        </li>
      </ul>
      <button
        v-if="canWrite"
        type="button"
        :aria-label="`Ajouter un stéréotype à ${profile.name}`"
        @click="editing = { profile, stereotype: null }"
      >
        Ajouter un stéréotype
      </button>
      <StereotypeEditor
        v-if="editing && editing.profile.id === profile.id"
        :key="editing.stereotype?.id ?? 'new'"
        :stereotype="editing.stereotype ?? undefined"
        :types="types()"
        :failure="failure"
        @save="save"
        @cancel="editing = null"
      />
    </article>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
.profiles,
.profile {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0.8rem;
}
.profile {
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 8px;
  padding: 1rem;
}
.profile header {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  gap: 0.6rem;
}
.profile h3 {
  margin: 0;
  flex: 1;
}
.create {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  max-width: 28rem;
}
</style>
```

### Task 27: SPA — stéréotypes sur l'élément (sélecteur, formulaire des propriétés) et rendu stéréotypé

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/lib/stereotypeRender.ts`, `frontend/src/features/profiles/useStereotypes.ts`, `frontend/src/features/elements/StereotypePanel.vue`
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/elements/ElementDetail.vue` (props/emits ; panneau), `frontend/src/features/elements/ElementCatalogue.vue:234`, `frontend/src/features/diagrams/DiagramsSection.vue:277` (réagir à `changed`)
- Modify: `frontend/src/features/diagrams/DiagramCanvas.vue` (rendu : `«nom»`, liseré, icône), `frontend/src/lib/diagramExport.ts` + `exportScene.ts` (stéréotype dans l'export)
- Create: `frontend/tests/stereotypeRender.spec.ts`, `frontend/tests/StereotypePanel.spec.ts`
- Modify: `frontend/tests/DiagramCanvas.spec.ts`, `frontend/tests/diagramExport.spec.ts`, `frontend/tests/ElementDetail.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ElementRead.stereotypes`, `GET /stereotypes`, `PUT /elements/{id}/stereotypes`, `PATCH /elements/{id}`.
- Produces (TypeScript) :
  - `STEREOTYPE_ICONS: Record<IconKey, string>` (chemins SVG 16×16 de présentation, jeu fermé), `stereotypeLabel(refs): string` (`«a» «b»`), `stereotypeStroke(refs): string | null` (la couleur du premier stéréotype colorié, validée `#rrggbb`, sinon `null`)
  - `useStereotypes()` → `{ all: Ref<StereotypeRead[]>, load(): Promise<void>, forType(elementType: string): StereotypeRead[] }`
  - `StereotypePanel` : props `element: ElementRead` ; émission `changed: []` ; un lecteur voit les stéréotypes appliqués et les valeurs, sans contrôle ; un propriétaire obligatoire est marqué `*` (la règle affichée est celle reçue, jamais recalculée) ; l'enregistrement d'une valeur envoie `PATCH /elements/{id}` avec **toute** la table de propriétés (le `PATCH` la remplace)
  - `ElementDetail` : nouvelle émission `changed: []` ; les deux parents rouvrent le détail sur `changed`
  - Rendu de la boîte : `«nom»` au-dessus du libellé, liseré de la couleur du stéréotype (la couleur de couche reste le fond), icône du jeu fermé en haut à droite

`frontend/tests/stereotypeRender.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import { STEREOTYPE_ICONS, stereotypeLabel, stereotypeStroke } from '../src/lib/stereotypeRender'

const ref = (name: string, colour: string | null, icon_key = 'none') => ({
  id: name,
  name,
  colour,
  icon_key,
}) as never

describe('stereotypeRender', () => {
  it('writes the guillemets of every stereotype in order', () => {
    expect(stereotypeLabel([ref('database', null), ref('critique', null)])).toBe('«database» «critique»')
    expect(stereotypeLabel([])).toBe('')
  })

  it('takes the stroke from the first stereotype that has a valid colour', () => {
    expect(stereotypeStroke([ref('a', null), ref('b', '#ff0000'), ref('c', '#00ff00')])).toBe('#ff0000')
    expect(stereotypeStroke([ref('a', 'red" onload="x')])).toBeNull()
    expect(stereotypeStroke([])).toBeNull()
  })

  it('ships an icon for every key of the closed set', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(STEREOTYPE_ICONS).sort()).toEqual(
      ['api', 'cloud', 'database', 'document', 'gear', 'lock', 'none', 'queue', 'server', 'user'],
    )
    expect(STEREOTYPE_ICONS.none).toBe('')
  })
})
```

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'

import StereotypePanel from '../src/features/elements/StereotypePanel.vue'
import { useMe } from '../src/lib/me'
import { anElement, stubApi, type Route } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals())

const DB = {
  id: 'ssssssss-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
  profile_id: 'pppppppp-0000-4000-8000-000000000000',
  name: 'database',
  base_type: 'application_component',
  colour: '#ffe0e0',
  icon_key: 'database',
  properties: [
    { key: 'tier', label: 'Niveau', required: true, kind: 'enum', allowed_values: ['1', '2'], pattern: null, help: '' },
    { key: 'code', label: 'Code', required: false, kind: 'pattern', allowed_values: [], pattern: '[A-Z]{3}', help: 'Trois lettres' },
  ],
}
const OTHER = { ...DB, id: 'oooooooo-0000-4000-8000-000000000000', name: 'router', base_type: 'node', properties: [] }
const ALL: Route = { path: '/stereotypes', body: [DB, OTHER] }

const element = anElement({
  id: 'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-111111111111',
  properties: { tier: '1' },
  stereotypes: [{ id: DB.id, name: 'database', colour: '#ffe0e0', icon_key: 'database' }],
})

function asAReader(): void {
  vi.mocked(useMe).mockReturnValueOnce({
    me: ref({ username: 'reader', can_write: false }),
    canWrite: computed(() => false),
    error: ref(null),
    load: vi.fn(() => Promise.resolve()),
  })
}

describe('StereotypePanel', () => {
  it('offers only the stereotypes of the element type that it does not carry yet', async () => {
    stubApi([ALL])
    render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element: anElement({ id: 'x', stereotypes: [] }) } })

    const select = await screen.findByLabelText('Ajouter un stéréotype')
    const options = [...select.querySelectorAll('option')].map((o) => o.textContent?.trim())
    expect(options).toContain('database')
    expect(options).not.toContain('router')
  })

  it('applies a stereotype by replacing the whole set', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([ALL, { method: 'PUT', path: `/elements/${element.id}/stereotypes`, body: element }])
    const rendered = render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element: { ...element, stereotypes: [] } } })
    await fireEvent.update(await screen.findByLabelText('Ajouter un stéréotype'), DB.id)

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Appliquer' }))

    await waitFor(() => expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PUT')?.body).toEqual({ stereotype_ids: [DB.id] }))
    expect(rendered.emitted().changed).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('shows the declared properties: a list for an enumeration, the hint of a pattern, a star for a required one', async () => {
    stubApi([ALL])
    render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element } })

    const tier = await screen.findByLabelText(/Niveau/)
    expect(tier.tagName).toBe('SELECT')
    expect(screen.getByText('*', { selector: '.required' })).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Code/)).toHaveAttribute('placeholder', expect.stringContaining('[A-Z]{3}'))
    expect(screen.getByText('Trois lettres')).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('saves a value with the whole property map, since the PATCH replaces it', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([ALL, { method: 'PATCH', path: `/elements/${element.id}`, body: element }])
    const rendered = render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element } })
    await fireEvent.update(await screen.findByLabelText(/Code/), 'ABC')

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enregistrer les propriétés' }))

    await waitFor(() =>
      expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'PATCH')?.body).toEqual({ properties: { tier: '1', code: 'ABC' } }),
    )
    expect(rendered.emitted().changed).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it("shows the API's refusal of a value in the panel", async () => {
    stubApi([
      ALL,
      { method: 'PATCH', path: `/elements/${element.id}`, status: 422,
        body: { error: 'invalid_input', detail: "tier: '9' is not one of 1, 2" } },
    ])
    render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element } })
    await fireEvent.update(await screen.findByLabelText(/Code/), 'ABC')

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Enregistrer les propriétés' }))

    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent("tier: '9' is not one of 1, 2")
  })

  it('lets a reader see the stereotypes and the values, and change nothing', async () => {
    stubApi([ALL])
    asAReader()
    render(StereotypePanel, { props: { element } })

    expect(await screen.findByText('«database»')).toBeInTheDocument()
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText('Ajouter un stéréotype')).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Enregistrer les propriétés' })).toBeNull()
    expect(screen.getByLabelText(/Niveau/)).toBeDisabled()
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/lib/stereotypeRender.ts` :

```ts
// How a stereotype looks on a box: guillemets, an outline, an icon.
//
// Presentation tables only, like `LAYER_LABELS` — not one rule of ArchiMate.
// The icons are a closed set the API validates by name (`IconKey`): a user
// picks one, never uploads one, so no arbitrary SVG ever reaches a page.
import type { components } from '../api/schema'

type StereotypeRef = components['schemas']['StereotypeRef']
type IconKey = components['schemas']['IconKey']

const HEX_COLOUR = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/

/** SVG path data on a 16 x 16 grid, drawn with the box's ink. `none` draws nothing. */
export const STEREOTYPE_ICONS: Record<IconKey, string> = {
  none: '',
  database: 'M2 3c0-1.2 2.7-2 6-2s6 .8 6 2v10c0 1.2-2.7 2-6 2s-6-.8-6-2zM2 3c0 1.2 2.7 2 6 2s6-.8 6-2M2 8c0 1.2 2.7 2 6 2s6-.8 6-2',
  server: 'M2 2h12v5H2zM2 9h12v5H2zM4.5 4.5h1M4.5 11.5h1',
  cloud: 'M4.5 13a3 3 0 0 1-.3-6A4 4 0 0 1 12 6.5 3.2 3.2 0 0 1 11.5 13z',
  user: 'M8 8a3 3 0 1 0 0-6 3 3 0 0 0 0 6zM2.5 14a5.5 5.5 0 0 1 11 0z',
  lock: 'M3.5 7h9v7h-9zM5.5 7V5a2.5 2.5 0 0 1 5 0v2',
  document: 'M3.5 1.5h6l3 3v10h-9zM9.5 1.5v3h3M5.5 8h5M5.5 11h5',
  gear: 'M8 5.5a2.5 2.5 0 1 0 0 5 2.5 2.5 0 0 0 0-5zM8 1v2M8 13v2M1 8h2M13 8h2M3 3l1.4 1.4M11.6 11.6 13 13M13 3l-1.4 1.4M4.4 11.6 3 13',
  queue: 'M1.5 4h13v8h-13zM5 4v8M8.5 4v8M12 4v8',
  api: 'M5 4 1.5 8 5 12M11 4l3.5 4L11 12M9.2 3 6.8 13',
}

/** `«a» «b»` for the stereotypes an element carries, or the empty string. */
export function stereotypeLabel(refs: StereotypeRef[]): string {
  return refs.map((ref) => `«${ref.name}»`).join(' ')
}

/** The outline colour: the first stereotype with a valid `#rrggbb`, else nothing. */
export function stereotypeStroke(refs: StereotypeRef[]): string | null {
  return refs.find((ref) => ref.colour !== null && HEX_COLOUR.test(ref.colour))?.colour ?? null
}
```
`frontend/src/features/profiles/useStereotypes.ts` :

```ts
// Every stereotype, flat: what the element panel offers and the canvas draws from.
import { ref } from 'vue'

import { api, unwrap } from '../../lib/api'
import { useLatestRequest } from '../../lib/latest'
import type { StereotypeRead } from './useProfiles'

export function useStereotypes() {
  const all = ref<StereotypeRead[]>([])
  const listing = useLatestRequest()

  async function load(): Promise<void> {
    await listing.run(
      async (signal) => unwrap(await api.GET('/stereotypes', { signal })),
      (answer) => {
        all.value = answer
      },
    )
  }

  /** The stereotypes made for this element type — the only ones the API will accept. */
  function forType(elementType: string): StereotypeRead[] {
    return all.value.filter((stereotype) => stereotype.base_type === elementType)
  }

  return { all, load, forType }
}
```
`frontend/src/features/elements/StereotypePanel.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// The stereotypes an element carries, and the properties they expect.
//
// It shows the rules it is given and applies none: an enumeration is a list,
// a pattern a hint, a required property a star. A value that breaks the
// declared format comes back as the API's refusal. The values live in the
// element's own `properties` (docs/adr: profils et stéréotypes), and the API's
// PATCH replaces that whole map — so a save sends all of it.
import { computed, onMounted, reactive, ref, watch } from 'vue'

import { api, messageOf, unwrap } from '../../lib/api'
import { useMe } from '../../lib/me'
import { stereotypeLabel } from '../../lib/stereotypeRender'
import { useStereotypes } from '../profiles/useStereotypes'
import type { ElementRead } from './useElementCatalogue'

const props = defineProps<{ element: ElementRead }>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ changed: [] }>()

const { canWrite } = useMe()
const catalogue = useStereotypes()
const chosen = ref('')
const failure = ref('')
const values = reactive<Record<string, string>>({})

onMounted(() => catalogue.load())

const carried = computed(() =>
  catalogue.all.value.filter((stereotype) => props.element.stereotypes.some((ref) => ref.id === stereotype.id)),
)
const offered = computed(() =>
  catalogue
    .forType(props.element.element_type)
    .filter((stereotype) => !props.element.stereotypes.some((ref) => ref.id === stereotype.id)),
)
const declared = computed(() => carried.value.flatMap((stereotype) => stereotype.properties))

watch(
  () => [props.element.id, props.element.properties] as const,
  () => {
    for (const key of Object.keys(values)) {
      delete values[key]
    }
    for (const prop of declared.value) {
      values[prop.key] = props.element.properties[prop.key] ?? ''
    }
  },
  { immediate: true },
)
watch(declared, () => {
  for (const prop of declared.value) {
    values[prop.key] ??= props.element.properties[prop.key] ?? ''
  }
})

async function apply(ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  failure.value = ''
  try {
    unwrap(
      await api.PUT('/elements/{element_id}/stereotypes', {
        params: { path: { element_id: props.element.id } },
        body: { stereotype_ids: ids },
      }),
    )
    chosen.value = ''
    emit('changed')
  } catch (caught) {
    failure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}

async function saveValues(): Promise<void> {
  failure.value = ''
  // The element's own attributes stay; the declared ones are laid over them,
  // and a blank declared value is a value not set.
  const next: Record<string, string> = { ...props.element.properties }
  for (const prop of declared.value) {
    const value = values[prop.key]?.trim() ?? ''
    if (value) {
      next[prop.key] = value
    } else {
      delete next[prop.key]
    }
  }
  try {
    unwrap(
      await api.PATCH('/elements/{element_id}', {
        params: { path: { element_id: props.element.id } },
        body: { properties: next },
      }),
    )
    emit('changed')
  } catch (caught) {
    failure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}
</script>

<template>
  <section class="stereotypes" aria-label="Stéréotypes">
    <h4>Stéréotypes</h4>
    <p v-if="element.stereotypes.length === 0" class="empty">Aucun stéréotype.</p>
    <ul v-else class="chips">
      <li v-for="ref in element.stereotypes" :key="ref.id">
        <span>{{ stereotypeLabel([ref]) }}</span>
        <button
          v-if="canWrite"
          type="button"
          class="secondary"
          :aria-label="`Retirer ${ref.name}`"
          @click="apply(element.stereotypes.filter((other) => other.id !== ref.id).map((other) => other.id))"
        >
          ×
        </button>
      </li>
    </ul>

    <div v-if="canWrite && offered.length > 0" class="add">
      <label for="stereotype-choice">Ajouter un stéréotype</label>
      <select id="stereotype-choice" v-model="chosen">
        <option value="" disabled>Choisir…</option>
        <option v-for="stereotype in offered" :key="stereotype.id" :value="stereotype.id">
          {{ stereotype.name }}
        </option>
      </select>
      <button
        type="button"
        :disabled="!chosen"
        @click="apply([...element.stereotypes.map((ref) => ref.id), chosen])"
      >
        Appliquer
      </button>
    </div>

    <form v-if="declared.length > 0" class="properties" @submit.prevent="saveValues">
      <div v-for="prop in declared" :key="prop.key" class="property">
        <label :for="`prop-${prop.key}`">
          {{ prop.label }}<span v-if="prop.required" class="required" title="Obligatoire">*</span>
        </label>
        <select v-if="prop.kind === 'enum'" :id="`prop-${prop.key}`" v-model="values[prop.key]" :disabled="!canWrite">
          <option value="">—</option>
          <option v-for="allowed in prop.allowed_values" :key="allowed" :value="allowed">{{ allowed }}</option>
        </select>
        <input
          v-else
          :id="`prop-${prop.key}`"
          v-model="values[prop.key]"
          :disabled="!canWrite"
          maxlength="500"
          :placeholder="prop.kind === 'pattern' ? `Forme attendue : ${prop.pattern}` : ''"
        />
        <small v-if="prop.help" class="hint">{{ prop.help }}</small>
      </div>
      <button v-if="canWrite" type="submit">Enregistrer les propriétés</button>
    </form>

    <p v-if="failure" class="banner banner--error" role="alert">{{ failure }}</p>
  </section>
</template>

<style scoped>
.stereotypes,
.properties,
.property {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}
.chips {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 0.4rem;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
}
.required {
  color: #b3261e;
  margin-left: 0.2rem;
}
</style>
```

````markdown
# NN. Profils et stéréotypes

Date : 2026-10-10
Statut : Accepté

## Contexte

Un architecte type finement ses éléments : « base de données » sur un composant applicatif, « API
externe » sur une interface, avec les propriétés attendues sur chacun (niveau de criticité, code
d'application, responsable). Sparx EA et UML appellent cela un profil et des stéréotypes. Le
métamodèle ([`0005`](0005-metamodele-archimate-3-2.md)) est complet et figé : 61 types, 11 relations,
des règles validées. Ajouter des types pour chaque besoin le rendrait faux ; laisser les propriétés
libres le rendrait inutilisable (la même clé s'écrit de trois manières).

## Décision

**Un stéréotype est une étiquette supplémentaire posée sur un élément d'un type de base, jamais un type
nouveau. Les valeurs restent dans `elements.properties`. La déclaration de ce qui est attendu est de la
donnée de référence.**

| Point | Choix |
|---|---|
| Modèle | `profiles` → `profile_stereotypes` → `profile_properties` (migration `0010`) ; `element_stereotypes(element_id, stereotype_id)`. Un stéréotype a **un** type de base (`base_type`, chaîne contrôlée par le domaine : un enum PostgreSQL figerait le métamodèle dans le schéma) |
| Règles de relation | Celles du type de base. Un stéréotype n'ouvre ni ne ferme aucune relation |
| Valeurs | Dans `elements.properties`, chaînes ≤ 500 caractères, sans préfixe, sous la clé déclarée — comme l'IPAM (`0020`). Clés réservées refusées à la déclaration : celles de l'IPAM (`IPAM_PROPERTIES`) |
| Forme d'une propriété | `free`, `enum` (1 à 50 valeurs) ou `pattern`. Le format d'une valeur **présente** est vérifié partout où des propriétés s'écrivent (`update_element`, `apply_stereotypes`) — la leçon de l'IPAM : une règle derrière une seule porte n'est pas une règle |
| `required` | **Jamais bloquant à l'écriture** : on crée l'élément, puis on le complète. `missing_required` est exposé pour que le sous-projet « contrôle qualité » en fasse une règle |
| Motif | Sous-ensemble sûr de `re` : ≤ 200 caractères, compilé à l'écriture du profil, refus des groupes quantifiés qui contiennent eux-mêmes un quantificateur ou une alternance, et des références arrière ; appliqué à des valeurs ≤ 500 caractères. Pas de `re2` |
| Collision de clé | Deux stéréotypes d'un même élément ne peuvent pas déclarer la même clé (422 à l'application) |
| Suppression | `element_stereotypes.stereotype_id` est `RESTRICT` : supprimer une définition appliquée est un 409 `stereotype_in_use` qui donne le décompte. Retirer l'application sur un élément ne touche pas ses propriétés. Changer le type de base d'un stéréotype appliqué est refusé |
| Rendu | `«nom»` au-dessus du libellé, liseré de la couleur `#rrggbb` du stéréotype, icône d'un jeu fermé embarqué par le SPA (`IconKey`, un enum de l'API). La couleur de couche reste le fond |
| Portage | Export et import JSON versionnés (`profile_format: 1`, ≤ 256 Ko), validés par les constructeurs qui servent l'écriture manuelle ; pas de synchronisation entre environnements |
| Agents | `list_profiles`, `apply_stereotype`, `remove_stereotype`. La définition d'un profil reste dans le SPA/HTTP : donnée rare, relue par des humains |

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Ajouter des types d'éléments au métamodèle | Natif | Le métamodèle est ArchiMate 3.2, complet ; un type de plus est une règle de plus à tenir | Écarté |
| Propriétés libres + convention orale | Rien à construire | La même clé s'écrit de trois manières ; aucune aide à la saisie | Écarté |
| Une table `element_properties` typée | Requêtes typées | Casse la règle « un jsonb, sans préfixe » et l'unicité par index partiel de l'IPAM | Écarté |
| Plusieurs types de base par stéréotype | Moins de doublons | Les règles de relation deviennent ambiguës ; on duplique le stéréotype si besoin | Écarté |
| Héritage entre stéréotypes | Réutilisation | Complexité de résolution des clés et des collisions ; hors périmètre | Écarté |
| `required` bloquant | Données complètes d'emblée | On ne peut plus créer un élément avant de connaître tout de lui ; contredit le geste du catalogue | Écarté |
| Dépendre de `re2` pour le motif | Garantie de linéarité | Une dépendance native pour un champ court | Écarté |
| Icônes SVG fournies par l'utilisateur | Liberté | Un SVG arbitraire est un vecteur d'injection ; jeu fermé | Écarté |

## Conséquences

- **Un changement de règle n'est pas rétroactif** : durcir l'énumération d'un stéréotype ne revalide pas
  les valeurs déjà stockées ; elles seront refusées à la prochaine écriture de l'élément.
- **Le sous-ensemble de motifs est volontairement pauvre** : `(jpg|png)+` est refusé. Le message le dit.
- **`ElementRead` gagne `stereotypes`** : le client est régénéré ; la décoration est faite par
  `ArchitectureService` (un lot de requêtes pour une page), pas par le dépôt du graphe.
- **L'import n'est pas atomique avec l'application** : il crée des définitions, il n'en applique aucune.
- **Journal** : `action=profile_changed` et `action=element_stereotypes_changed`, avec des identifiants et
  des comptes ; jamais une valeur de propriété.
- Nouvelle table de profils, donc `make pg-backup` la contient : la sauvegarde reste « tout le modèle »
  ([`0025`](0025-sauvegardes-postgresql.md)).

## Références

- ADR liés : [0005](0005-metamodele-archimate-3-2.md), [0020](0020-adressage-ip-dans-le-catalogue.md),
  [0033](0033-postgresql-seul-pour-le-graphe.md)
- Code concerné : `backend/src/ea/domain/profiles.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/profiles.py`,
  `backend/migrations/versions/0010_profiles.py`, `frontend/src/features/profiles/`
````

```python
"""Generated views: pure — what the source yields, and what changed since a view was frozen."""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.api.schemas import MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES
from ea.domain.errors import GeneratedViewTooLargeError
from ea.domain.generated_views import (
    MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS,
    Generation,
    PackageSource,
    QuerySource,
    SourceMembers,
    diff_members,
    resolve_members,
)


def test_the_ceiling_is_the_one_the_api_declares() -> None:
    assert MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS == MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES == 500


def test_a_view_is_live_or_frozen() -> None:
    assert [g.value for g in Generation] == ["live", "frozen"]


def test_sources_are_values() -> None:
    query = uuid4()

    assert QuerySource(query) == QuerySource(query)
    assert PackageSource(query).recursive is False
    assert PackageSource(query, recursive=True) != PackageSource(query)


def test_members_keep_their_order_and_lose_their_duplicates() -> None:
    a, b = uuid4(), uuid4()

    assert resolve_members(SourceMembers((a, b, a), total=3)) == (a, b)


def test_a_source_over_the_ceiling_is_refused_with_its_real_count() -> None:
    found = SourceMembers(tuple(uuid4() for _ in range(MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS)), total=1234)

    with pytest.raises(GeneratedViewTooLargeError, match="1234"):
        resolve_members(found)


def test_a_source_exactly_at_the_ceiling_is_accepted() -> None:
    ids = tuple(uuid4() for _ in range(MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS))

    assert resolve_members(SourceMembers(ids, total=MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS)) == ids


def test_diff_names_what_arrived_and_what_left() -> None:
    kept, gone, new = uuid4(), uuid4(), uuid4()

    added, removed = diff_members([kept, gone], [kept, new])

    assert (added, removed) == ((new,), (gone,))


def test_diff_is_empty_when_nothing_changed_and_ignores_order() -> None:
    a, b = uuid4(), uuid4()

    assert diff_members([a, b], [b, a]) == ((), ())
    assert diff_members([], []) == ((), ())


def test_diff_is_sorted_so_the_same_change_always_reads_the_same() -> None:
    ids = sorted(uuid4() for _ in range(4))

    added, _ = diff_members([], reversed(ids))

    assert list(added) == ids
```

`backend/src/ea/domain/errors.py`, à la suite des erreurs de profils :

```python
class GeneratedViewTooLargeError(DomainError):
    """A view's source yields more elements than a diagram can hold: narrow the filter."""


class GeneratedViewsUnavailableError(DomainError):
    """Generated views were asked for on a deployment without the queries and packages they read."""


class DiagramLiveError(DomainError):
    """A live view has no boxes of its own: it is recomputed at every opening and cannot be edited."""


class DiagramNotLiveError(DomainError):
    """The diagram is not a live view: there is nothing to freeze."""


class DiagramNotGeneratedError(DomainError):
    """The diagram was drawn by hand: it has no source to freeze, refresh or thaw."""


class DiagramHasAnnotationsError(DomainError):
    """Thawing a view would delete the notes, groups or routes drawn on it."""
```
`backend/src/ea/domain/generated_views.py` :

```python
"""Generated views: a diagram that is "everything that answers this query".

A view stays a view (docs/adr: objets de vue) — it owns no fact. *Live*, it
stores no box at all: who is on it is recomputed at every opening, and its
layout is the SPA's deterministic one. *Frozen*, the layout the SPA computed
is stored and the diagram is edited like a hand-drawn one; the reference to
its source is kept so a refresh can show what changed. The filter itself is
sub-project 1's (`ElementQuery`): nothing here restates it.

Nothing here imports a framework or a driver, per `CLAUDE.md`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Final
from uuid import UUID

from ea.domain.errors import GeneratedViewTooLargeError

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from ea.domain.model import Element

#: Mirrors `api.schemas.MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES` (a test holds the two together): a diagram
#: a person can still read. Past this it is an export, not a view.
MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS: Final = 500


class Generation(StrEnum):
    LIVE = "live"
    FROZEN = "frozen"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class QuerySource:
    query_id: UUID


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PackageSource:
    package_id: UUID
    recursive: bool = False


type ViewSource = QuerySource | PackageSource


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceMembers:
    """What a source answers: the first elements, and how many there are in all."""

    ids: tuple[UUID, ...]
    total: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SourceDiff:
    """What a frozen view would gain and lose if it followed its source again."""

    added: tuple[Element, ...]
    removed: tuple[UUID, ...]


def resolve_members(found: SourceMembers, *, limit: int = MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS) -> tuple[UUID, ...]:
    """The elements of a view, once the source is known to fit on one."""
    if found.total > limit:
        msg = (
            f"the source yields {found.total} elements; a diagram holds at most {limit} — "
            "narrow the filter"
        )
        raise GeneratedViewTooLargeError(msg)
    return tuple(dict.fromkeys(found.ids))


def diff_members(
    stored: Iterable[UUID], resolved: Iterable[UUID]
) -> tuple[tuple[UUID, ...], tuple[UUID, ...]]:
    """`(added, removed)`: what the source now holds that the view lacks, and the reverse."""
    before, after = set(stored), set(resolved)
    return tuple(sorted(after - before, key=str)), tuple(sorted(before - after, key=str))
```

Dans `backend/tests/conftest.py`, ajouter après `InMemoryDiagrams` :

```python
class InMemoryViewSources:
    """The queries and packages a generated view reads, as a dictionary of answers."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.answers: dict[ViewSource, list[UUID]] = {}

    async def members(self, source: ViewSource, *, limit: int) -> SourceMembers:
        if source not in self.answers:
            msg = f"no such source: {source}"
            raise ElementNotFoundError(msg)
        ids = self.answers[source]
        return SourceMembers(tuple(ids[:limit]), total=len(ids))
```
(imports : `from ea.domain.generated_views import SourceMembers, ViewSource`, `ElementNotFoundError`), compléter `InMemoryDiagrams` :

```python
    async def freeze(self, diagram_id: UUID, nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode], *, now: datetime) -> int | None:
        stored = self.diagrams.get(diagram_id)
        if stored is None:
            return None
        self.nodes[diagram_id] = tuple(nodes)
        self.revisions[diagram_id] = self.revisions.get(diagram_id, 0) + 1
        self.diagrams[diagram_id] = replace(stored.touched(now), generation=Generation.FROZEN)
        return self.revisions[diagram_id]

    async def unfreeze(self, diagram_id: UUID, *, now: datetime) -> int | None:
        stored = self.diagrams.get(diagram_id)
        if stored is None:
            return None
        for table in (self.nodes, self.objects, self.edges):
            table.pop(diagram_id, None)
        self.revisions[diagram_id] = self.revisions.get(diagram_id, 0) + 1
        self.diagrams[diagram_id] = replace(stored.touched(now), generation=Generation.LIVE)
        return self.revisions[diagram_id]

    async def names_using_source(
        self, *, query_id: UUID | None = None, package_id: UUID | None = None
    ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        def uses(diagram: Diagram) -> bool:
            source = diagram.source
            return (isinstance(source, QuerySource) and source.query_id == query_id) or (
                isinstance(source, PackageSource) and source.package_id == package_id
            )

        return tuple(sorted(d.name for d in self.diagrams.values() if uses(d)))
```

```python
"""Generated views through the service: live by default, frozen on request, never silently edited."""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramEdge, DiagramObject, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode
from ea.domain.errors import (
    DiagramHasAnnotationsError,
    DiagramLiveError,
    DiagramNotGeneratedError,
    DiagramNotLiveError,
    GeneratedViewsUnavailableError,
    GeneratedViewTooLargeError,
    NotAuthorisedError,
)
from ea.domain.generated_views import Generation, PackageSource, QuerySource
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.diagrams import DiagramService
from tests.conftest import InMemoryViewSources, a_reader, lay_out


async def element(service: ArchitectureService, name: str):
    return await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name=name)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestLive:
    async def test_a_live_view_stores_no_box_and_shows_what_the_source_yields_now(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        a, b = await element(service, "A"), await element(service, "B")
        link = await service.connect(relationship_type=RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION, source_id=a.id, target_id=b.id)
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id, b.id]

        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Périmètre", source=source)

        assert opened.diagram.generation is Generation.LIVE
        assert opened.nodes == () and opened.objects == ()
        assert {e.id for e in opened.graph.elements} == {a.id, b.id}
        assert [r.id for r in opened.graph.relationships] == [link.id]
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id]
        reopened = await diagram_service.open(opened.diagram.id)
        assert {e.id for e in reopened.graph.elements} == {a.id}

    async def test_a_source_that_does_not_fit_is_refused_with_the_count(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        source = PackageSource(uuid4(), recursive=True)
        view_sources.answers[source] = [uuid4() for _ in range(501)]

        with pytest.raises(GeneratedViewTooLargeError, match="501"):
            await diagram_service.generate(name="Trop", source=source)

        assert await diagram_service.list_diagrams() == ()

    async def test_a_live_view_cannot_be_laid_out(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = []
        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=source)

        with pytest.raises(DiagramLiveError):
            await lay_out(diagram_service, opened.diagram.id, [])

    async def test_a_reader_cannot_generate(self, diagram_service: DiagramService) -> None:
        with acting_as(a_reader()), pytest.raises(NotAuthorisedError):
            await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=QuerySource(uuid4()))

    async def test_without_sub_project_one_a_generation_says_it_is_unavailable(
        self, diagrams, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        bare = DiagramService(diagrams, service)

        with pytest.raises(GeneratedViewsUnavailableError):
            await bare.generate(name="Vue", source=QuerySource(uuid4()))


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestFreezing:
    async def _live(self, diagram_service, service, view_sources):
        a, b = await element(service, "A"), await element(service, "B")
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id, b.id]
        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=source)
        return opened.diagram, a, b, source

    async def test_freezing_stores_the_layout_the_spa_computed_and_keeps_the_source(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a, b, source = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)

        frozen = await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 10, 10), DiagramNode(b.id, 200, 10)])
        opened = await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)

        assert frozen.generation is Generation.FROZEN and frozen.source == source
        assert {n.element_id for n in opened.nodes} == {a.id, b.id}

    async def test_a_box_that_is_not_in_the_source_is_refused(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, _, _, _ = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        stranger = await element(service, "Étranger")

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="source"):
            await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(stranger.id, 0, 0)])

    async def test_a_frozen_view_is_edited_like_a_hand_drawn_one(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a, b, _ = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(b.id, 200, 0)])

        revision = await lay_out(diagram_service, diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 5, 5)])

        assert revision == 2  # the freeze was revision 1

    async def test_freezing_twice_or_freezing_a_hand_drawn_diagram_is_refused(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a, _, _ = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0)])
        by_hand = await diagram_service.create(name="À la main")

        with pytest.raises(DiagramNotLiveError):
            await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [])
        with pytest.raises(DiagramNotGeneratedError):
            await diagram_service.freeze(by_hand.id, [])

    async def test_refreshing_shows_the_gap_and_writes_nothing(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a, b, source = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(b.id, 200, 0)])
        c = await element(service, "C")
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id, c.id]

        diff = await diagram_service.source_diff(diagram.id)

        assert [e.id for e in diff.added] == [c.id]
        assert diff.removed == (b.id,)
        assert {n.element_id for n in (await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)).nodes} == {a.id, b.id}

    async def test_a_source_that_vanished_is_a_not_found_not_a_crash(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a, _, source = await self._live(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        await diagram_service.freeze(diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0)])
        del view_sources.answers[source]

        with pytest.raises(Exception, match="no such source"):
            await diagram_service.source_diff(diagram.id)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestThawing:
    async def _frozen(self, diagram_service, service, view_sources):
        a = await element(service, "A")
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id]
        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=source)
        await diagram_service.freeze(opened.diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0)])
        return opened.diagram, a

    async def test_thawing_a_view_without_annotations_goes_back_to_live(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, _ = await self._frozen(diagram_service, service, view_sources)

        thawed = await diagram_service.unfreeze(diagram.id)
        opened = await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)

        assert thawed.generation is Generation.LIVE
        assert opened.nodes == ()

    async def test_thawing_refuses_to_lose_a_note_unless_told_to(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        diagram, a = await self._frozen(diagram_service, service, view_sources)
        note = DiagramObject(uuid4(), ObjectKind.NOTE, 0, 0, 100, 50, text="à voir")
        await lay_out(diagram_service, diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0)], [note])

        with pytest.raises(DiagramHasAnnotationsError):
            await diagram_service.unfreeze(diagram.id)
        thawed = await diagram_service.unfreeze(diagram.id, discard_annotations=True)

        assert thawed.generation is Generation.LIVE
        assert (await diagram_service.open(diagram.id)).objects == ()

    async def test_a_custom_route_counts_as_an_annotation(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, service: ArchitectureService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        a, b = await element(service, "A"), await element(service, "B")
        link = await service.connect(relationship_type=RelationshipType.ASSOCIATION, source_id=a.id, target_id=b.id)
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = [a.id, b.id]
        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=source)
        await diagram_service.freeze(opened.diagram.id, [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(b.id, 300, 0)])
        from ea.domain.diagram_objects import Point

        await lay_out(
            diagram_service,
            opened.diagram.id,
            [DiagramNode(a.id, 0, 0), DiagramNode(b.id, 300, 0)],
            edges=[DiagramEdge(link.id, (Point(150, 100),))],
        )

        with pytest.raises(DiagramHasAnnotationsError):
            await diagram_service.unfreeze(opened.diagram.id)

    async def test_a_live_view_cannot_be_thawed(
        self, diagram_service: DiagramService, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        source = QuerySource(uuid4())
        view_sources.answers[source] = []
        opened = await diagram_service.generate(name="Vue", source=source)

        with pytest.raises(DiagramLiveError):
            await diagram_service.unfreeze(opened.diagram.id)
```

```python
"""Generated views against a real PostgreSQL: the source columns, freeze, thaw, RESTRICT."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramObject, DiagramScene, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.diagrams import Diagram, DiagramNode
from ea.domain.generated_views import Generation, PackageSource, QuerySource
from ea.domain.model import Element
from ea.repositories.architecture_store import PostgresArchitectureRepository
from ea.repositories.diagram_store import PostgresDiagramRepository

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.postgres, pytest.mark.asyncio]

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


async def a_saved_query(engine: AsyncEngine) -> UUID:
    """A row of sub-project 1's `saved_queries` — columns as its migration declares them."""
    query_id = uuid4()
    async with engine.begin() as connection:
        # Test-only raw SQL, every value bound.
        await connection.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO saved_queries (id, name, description, query, created_by, created_at, updated_at) "
                "VALUES (:id, :name, '', CAST('{\"version\": 1}' AS jsonb), 'test', :now, :now)"
            ),
            {"id": query_id, "name": f"q-{query_id}", "now": NOW},
        )
    return query_id


async def a_package(engine: AsyncEngine) -> UUID:
    package_id = uuid4()
    async with engine.begin() as connection:
        await connection.execute(
            text("INSERT INTO packages (id, name, description, created_at, updated_at) VALUES (:id, :name, '', :now, :now)"),
            {"id": package_id, "name": f"p-{package_id}", "now": NOW},
        )
    return package_id


@pytest.fixture
def diagrams(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresDiagramRepository:
    return PostgresDiagramRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


async def test_a_generated_view_round_trips_with_its_source(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, engine_at_head: AsyncEngine
) -> None:
    query = QuerySource(await a_saved_query(engine_at_head))
    package = PackageSource(await a_package(engine_at_head), recursive=True)

    by_query = await diagrams.add(Diagram.create(name="Q", now=NOW, generation=Generation.LIVE, source=query))
    by_package = await diagrams.add(Diagram.create(name="P", now=NOW, generation=Generation.LIVE, source=package))

    assert (await diagrams.get(by_query.id)).source == query  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert (await diagrams.get(by_package.id)).source == package  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert (await diagrams.get(by_query.id)).generation is Generation.LIVE  # type: ignore[union-attr]


async def test_a_source_in_use_cannot_be_deleted_and_the_diagrams_are_named(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, engine_at_head: AsyncEngine
) -> None:
    query_id = await a_saved_query(engine_at_head)
    await diagrams.add(Diagram.create(name="Vue A", now=NOW, generation=Generation.LIVE, source=QuerySource(query_id)))

    assert await diagrams.names_using_source(query_id=query_id) == ("Vue A",)
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
        async with engine_at_head.begin() as connection:
            await connection.execute(text("DELETE FROM saved_queries WHERE id = :id"), {"id": query_id})


async def test_a_diagram_cannot_have_two_sources_nor_a_generation_without_one(
    engine_at_head: AsyncEngine,
) -> None:
    query_id = await a_saved_query(engine_at_head)
    package_id = await a_package(engine_at_head)
    insert = text(
        "INSERT INTO diagrams (id, name, description, created_at, updated_at, generation, source_query_id, source_package_id) "
        "VALUES (:id, :name, '', :now, :now, :generation, :q, :p)"
    )

    for generation, q, p in (("live", query_id, package_id), ("live", None, None), (None, query_id, None)):
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            async with engine_at_head.begin() as connection:
                await connection.execute(insert, {"id": uuid4(), "name": f"d-{uuid4()}", "now": NOW, "generation": generation, "q": q, "p": p})


async def test_freezing_stores_the_boxes_and_bumps_the_revision_and_thawing_clears_everything(
    diagrams: PostgresDiagramRepository, engine_at_head: AsyncEngine
) -> None:
    graph = PostgresArchitectureRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))
    element = await graph.add_element(Element.create(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="A", now=NOW))
    query = QuerySource(await a_saved_query(engine_at_head))
    live = await diagrams.add(Diagram.create(name="Vue", now=NOW, generation=Generation.LIVE, source=query))

    revision = await diagrams.freeze(live.id, [DiagramNode(element.id, 1, 2)], now=NOW)
    frozen = await diagrams.get(live.id)

    assert (revision, frozen.generation, frozen.revision) == (1, Generation.FROZEN, 1)  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert [n.element_id for n in await diagrams.nodes_of(live.id)] == [element.id]

    note = DiagramObject(uuid4(), ObjectKind.NOTE, 0, 0, 100, 50, text="x")
    await diagrams.replace_layout(live.id, DiagramScene(nodes=(DiagramNode(element.id, 1, 2),), objects=(note,)), revision=1, now=NOW)
    thawed_revision = await diagrams.unfreeze(live.id, now=NOW)

    assert thawed_revision == 3
    assert (await diagrams.nodes_of(live.id), await diagrams.objects_of(live.id)) == ((), ())
    assert (await diagrams.get(live.id)).generation is Generation.LIVE  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert (await diagrams.get(live.id)).source == query  # type: ignore[union-attr]
```

`backend/migrations/versions/0011_generated_views.py` (`down_revision` = la dernière révision relevée à la Task 29, `"0010"` si aucune autre) :

```python
"""les vues générées : source, vivante ou figée

Revision ID: 0011
Revises: 0010
Date: 2026-10-10

Un diagramme peut être « tout ce qui répond à cette requête » ou « tout ce que
ce paquet contient » (ADR sur les vues générées). Il référence sa source par
clé étrangère `RESTRICT` : supprimer une requête ou un paquet lu par une vue
est un 409 qui nomme les diagrammes, jamais une vue qui change de sens en
silence. Rien à convertir : colonnes nullables, les diagrammes existants sont
des diagrammes dessinés à la main (`generation` NULL).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op

revision: str = "0011"
down_revision: str | None = "0010"
branch_labels: str | Sequence[str] | None = None
depends_on: str | Sequence[str] | None = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.add_column("diagrams", sa.Column("generation", sa.String(length=6), nullable=True))
    op.add_column("diagrams", sa.Column("source_query_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=True))
    op.add_column("diagrams", sa.Column("source_package_id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=True))
    op.add_column(
        "diagrams",
        sa.Column("source_recursive", sa.Boolean(), server_default="false", nullable=False),
    )
    op.create_foreign_key(
        op.f("fk_diagrams_source_query_id_saved_queries"),
        "diagrams",
        "saved_queries",
        ["source_query_id"],
        ["id"],
        ondelete="RESTRICT",
    )
    op.create_foreign_key(
        op.f("fk_diagrams_source_package_id_packages"),
        "diagrams",
        "packages",
        ["source_package_id"],
        ["id"],
        ondelete="RESTRICT",
    )
    op.create_index(op.f("ix_diagrams_source_query_id"), "diagrams", ["source_query_id"])
    op.create_index(op.f("ix_diagrams_source_package_id"), "diagrams", ["source_package_id"])
    op.create_check_constraint(
        op.f("ck_diagrams_generation"), "diagrams", "generation in ('live', 'frozen')"
    )
    op.create_check_constraint(
        op.f("ck_diagrams_one_source"),
        "diagrams",
        "num_nonnulls(source_query_id, source_package_id) <= 1",
    )
    op.create_check_constraint(
        op.f("ck_diagrams_generation_iff_source"),
        "diagrams",
        "(generation is null) = (source_query_id is null and source_package_id is null)",
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_constraint(op.f("ck_diagrams_generation_iff_source"), "diagrams", type_="check")
    op.drop_constraint(op.f("ck_diagrams_one_source"), "diagrams", type_="check")
    op.drop_constraint(op.f("ck_diagrams_generation"), "diagrams", type_="check")
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagrams_source_package_id"), table_name="diagrams")
    op.drop_index(op.f("ix_diagrams_source_query_id"), table_name="diagrams")
    op.drop_constraint(op.f("fk_diagrams_source_package_id_packages"), "diagrams", type_="foreignkey")
    op.drop_constraint(op.f("fk_diagrams_source_query_id_saved_queries"), "diagrams", type_="foreignkey")
    op.drop_column("diagrams", "source_recursive")
    op.drop_column("diagrams", "source_package_id")
    op.drop_column("diagrams", "source_query_id")
    op.drop_column("diagrams", "generation")
```

`backend/src/ea/repositories/diagram_store.py` : `_to_diagram` ajoute
