avec

```python
def _source_of(row: DiagramRecord) -> ViewSource | None:
    if row.source_query_id is not None:
        return QuerySource(row.source_query_id)
    if row.source_package_id is not None:
        return PackageSource(row.source_package_id, row.source_recursive)
    return None
```
`add` écrit `generation=diagram.generation.value if diagram.generation else None`, `source_query_id`, `source_package_id`, `source_recursive` à partir de `diagram.source`. Ajouter :

```python
    async def freeze(
        self, diagram_id: UUID, nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode], *, now: datetime
    ) -> int | None:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            row = await session.get(DiagramRecord, diagram_id, with_for_update=True)
            if row is None:
                return None
            await session.execute(
                delete(DiagramNodeRecord).where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id)
            )
            session.add_all(
                DiagramNodeRecord(
                    diagram_id=diagram_id,
                    element_id=n.element_id,
                    x=n.x,
                    y=n.y,
                    width=n.width,
                    height=n.height,
                    parent_object_id=None,
                    z=n.z,
                )
                for n in nodes
            )
            row.generation = Generation.FROZEN.value
            row.revision += 1
            row.updated_at = now
            return row.revision

    async def unfreeze(self, diagram_id: UUID, *, now: datetime) -> int | None:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            row = await session.get(DiagramRecord, diagram_id, with_for_update=True)
            if row is None:
                return None
            await session.execute(delete(DiagramNodeRecord).where(DiagramNodeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id))
            await session.execute(delete(DiagramEdgeRecord).where(DiagramEdgeRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id))
            await session.execute(delete(DiagramObjectRecord).where(DiagramObjectRecord.diagram_id == diagram_id))
            row.generation = Generation.LIVE.value
            row.revision += 1
            row.updated_at = now
            return row.revision

    async def names_using_source(
        self, *, query_id: UUID | None = None, package_id: UUID | None = None
    ) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        statement = select(DiagramRecord.name).order_by(DiagramRecord.name)
        if query_id is not None:
            statement = statement.where(DiagramRecord.source_query_id == query_id)
        elif package_id is not None:
            statement = statement.where(DiagramRecord.source_package_id == package_id)
        else:
            return ()
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return tuple(await session.scalars(statement))
```
(importer `Generation`, `PackageSource`, `QuerySource`, `ViewSource`.)

`backend/src/ea/services/view_sources.py` — **le seul fichier qui connaît les noms du sous-projet 1** (relevés à la Task 29) :

```python
"""The adapter between generated views and sub-project 1's queries and packages.

A generated view reads its members from the filter sub-project 1 owns
(`ElementQuery`, docs/adr: référentiel) — never from a copy of it. This is the
one module that knows how to ask: change it, and only it, if those names move.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from ea.domain.generated_views import PackageSource, QuerySource, SourceMembers, ViewSource
from ea.domain.query import ElementQuery

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
    from ea.services.packages import PackageService
    from ea.services.saved_queries import SavedQueryService


class QueriesAndPackages:
    """`ViewSourceResolver` over the saved queries and the package tree."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        architecture: ArchitectureService,
        saved_queries: SavedQueryService,
        packages: PackageService,
    ) -> None:
        self._architecture = architecture
        self._saved_queries = saved_queries
        self._packages = packages

    async def _query_of(self, source: ViewSource) -> ElementQuery:
        if isinstance(source, QuerySource):
            return (await self._saved_queries.get(source.query_id)).query
        await self._packages.get(source.package_id)  # a missing package is a not-found
        return ElementQuery(package_id=source.package_id, include_subpackages=source.recursive)

    async def members(self, source: ViewSource, *, limit: int) -> SourceMembers:
        query = replace(await self._query_of(source), limit=limit, offset=0)
        page = await self._architecture.search_elements(query)
        return SourceMembers(tuple(element.id for element in page.items), total=page.total)
```
Son test (`tests/unit/test_view_sources.py`) injecte trois doubles minimaux (un `ArchitectureService` factice dont `search_elements` renvoie un objet `items`/`total`, un `SavedQueryService` factice dont `get` renvoie un objet portant `.query`, un `PackageService` factice) et vérifie : un paquet récursif construit `ElementQuery(package_id=…, include_subpackages=True)`, une requête enregistrée garde son filtre et reçoit `limit` et `offset=0`, un paquet absent laisse remonter l'erreur du sous-projet 1.

`backend/src/ea/services/diagrams.py` — imports : `from ea.domain.generated_views import Generation, MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS, SourceDiff, ViewSource, diff_members, resolve_members` et les erreurs du lot ; `TYPE_CHECKING` : `ViewSourceResolver`. `__init__` : paramètre `sources: ViewSourceResolver | None = None` et `self._sources = sources`. Ajouter :

```python
    def _resolver(self) -> ViewSourceResolver:
        if self._sources is None:
            msg = "generated views need the queries and packages, which this deployment lacks"
            raise GeneratedViewsUnavailableError(msg)
        return self._sources

    async def _members_of(self, diagram: Diagram) -> tuple[UUID, ...]:
        assert diagram.source is not None  # noqa: S101 — callers checked `generation`
        found = await self._resolver().members(diagram.source, limit=MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS)
        return resolve_members(found)
```
`open` : après `diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)` ajouter

```python

            return DiagramDetail(diagram=diagram, nodes=(), graph=await self._architecture.view_of(members))
```
`replace_layout` : après `diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)` ajouter `if diagram.generation is Generation.LIVE: raise DiagramLiveError("a live view is recomputed at every opening and cannot be edited: freeze it first")`. Nouvelles méthodes :

```python
    async def generate(self, *, name: str, source: ViewSource) -> DiagramDetail:
        """A new live view of everything a query or a package yields.

        The source is read once now, to refuse a missing one and one too big
        for a diagram; nothing of it is stored but the reference.
        """
        require_editor()
        found = await self._resolver().members(source, limit=MAX_GENERATED_MEMBERS)
        resolve_members(found)
        stored = await self._repository.add(
            Diagram.create(name=name, now=self._now(), generation=Generation.LIVE, source=source)
        )
        self._audit("generated view created: %r", stored, "diagram_generated")
        return await self.open(stored.id)

    async def source_diff(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> SourceDiff:
        """What a frozen view would gain and lose if it followed its source. Writes nothing."""
        require_caller()
        diagram = await self._frozen(diagram_id)
        stored = [node.element_id for node in await self._repository.nodes_of(diagram_id)]
        added, removed = diff_members(stored, await self._members_of(diagram))
        return SourceDiff(added=(await self._architecture.view_of(added)).elements, removed=removed)

    async def freeze(self, diagram_id: UUID, nodes: Sequence[DiagramNode]) -> Diagram:
        """Store the layout the SPA computed and make the view an ordinary editable diagram."""
        require_editor()
        layout = check_layout(nodes)
        diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)
        if diagram.generation is None:
            raise DiagramNotGeneratedError("this diagram was drawn by hand: it has no source")
        if diagram.generation is Generation.FROZEN:
            raise DiagramNotLiveError("this view is already frozen")
        members = set(await self._members_of(diagram))
        strangers = [str(n.element_id) for n in layout if n.element_id not in members]
        if strangers:
            msg = f"not in the source of this view: {', '.join(strangers)}"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if await self._repository.freeze(diagram_id, layout, now=self._now()) is None:
            raise _not_found(diagram_id)
        self._audit("view frozen: %r", diagram, "diagram_frozen")
        return await self.get(diagram_id)

    async def unfreeze(self, diagram_id: UUID, *, discard_annotations: bool = False) -> Diagram:
        """Back to a live view. Notes, groups and custom routes are not silently lost."""
        require_editor()
        diagram = await self._frozen(diagram_id)
        annotated = bool(
            await self._repository.objects_of(diagram_id) or await self._repository.edges_of(diagram_id)
        )
        if annotated and not discard_annotations:
            raise DiagramHasAnnotationsError(
                "this view carries notes, groups or routed links: thawing would delete them"
            )
        if await self._repository.unfreeze(diagram_id, now=self._now()) is None:
            raise _not_found(diagram_id)
        self._audit("view thawed: %r", diagram, "diagram_unfrozen")
        return await self.get(diagram_id)

    async def _frozen(self, diagram_id: UUID) -> Diagram:
        diagram = await self.get(diagram_id)
        if diagram.generation is None:
            raise DiagramNotGeneratedError("this diagram was drawn by hand: it has no source")
        if diagram.generation is Generation.LIVE:
            raise DiagramLiveError("this view is live: it has no stored boxes to refresh or thaw")
        return diagram
```
(`check_layout` se réimporte de `ea.domain.diagrams`.) Les méthodes publiques `generate`, `freeze`, `unfreeze` commencent par `require_editor()`, `source_diff` par `require_caller()` — le test de gardes les exige.

```python
def by_query(query_id: UUID) -> dict[str, Any]:

    return {"element_id": element, "x": x, "y": 20.0, "width": 132.0, "height": 46.0, "parent_object_id": None, "z": 0}

        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:

class TestFreezingAndRefreshing:
    async def _view(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], str, UUID]:
        a = await an_element(client, "A")
        query = uuid4()
        view_sources.answers[QuerySource(query)] = [UUID(a)]
        view = (await client.post("/diagrams/generate", json={"name": "V", "source": by_query(query)})).json()
        return view, a, query

    async def test_freezing_stores_the_layout_and_the_view_becomes_editable(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        view, a, _ = await self._view(client, view_sources)

        frozen = await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/freeze", json={"nodes": [box(a)]})

        assert frozen.status_code == 200
        assert (frozen.json()["generation"], [n["element_id"] for n in frozen.json()["nodes"]]) == ("frozen", [a])
        twice = await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/freeze", json={"nodes": [box(a)]})
        assert (twice.status_code, twice.json()["error"]) == (409, "diagram_not_live")

    async def test_the_source_diff_shows_the_gap_without_writing_it(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        view, a, query = await self._view(client, view_sources)
        await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/freeze", json={"nodes": [box(a)]})
        b = await an_element(client, "B")
        view_sources.answers[QuerySource(query)] = [UUID(b)]

        diff = await client.get(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/source-diff")

        assert diff.status_code == 200
        assert [e["id"] for e in diff.json()["added"]] == [b]
        assert diff.json()["removed"] == [a]
        assert [n["element_id"] for n in (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}")).json()["nodes"]] == [a]

    async def test_thawing_refuses_to_lose_a_note_unless_discard_is_explicit(
        self, client: httpx.AsyncClient, view_sources: InMemoryViewSources
    ) -> None:
        view, a, _ = await self._view(client, view_sources)
        await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/freeze", json={"nodes": [box(a)]})
        note = {"id": str(uuid4()), "kind": "note", "x": 0.0, "y": 0.0, "width": 100.0, "height": 50.0,
                "text": "à voir", "colour": None, "z": 0, "parent_id": None, "anchor_element_id": None}
        current = (await client.get(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}")).json()["revision"]
        await client.put(
            f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/layout",
            json={"revision": current, "nodes": [box(a)], "objects": [note], "edges": []},
        )

        refused = await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/unfreeze", json={"discard_annotations": False})
        thawed = await client.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/unfreeze", json={"discard_annotations": True})

        assert (refused.status_code, refused.json()["error"]) == (409, "has_annotations")
        assert (thawed.status_code, thawed.json()["generation"]) == (200, "live")
        assert thawed.json()["objects"] == []

        freeze = await secured.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/freeze", json={"nodes": []}, headers=reader)
        thaw = await secured.post(f"/diagrams/{view['id']}/unfreeze", json={"discard_annotations": True}, headers=reader)

        assert opened.status_code == 200
        assert (generate.status_code, freeze.status_code, thaw.status_code) == (403, 403, 403)
```

`backend/src/ea/api/schemas.py` : imports `from typing import Self`, `from pydantic import model_validator` (si absent), `from ea.domain.generated_views import Generation, PackageSource, QuerySource` et `from ea.domain.generated_views import SourceDiff` ; `from ea.domain.generated_views import ViewSource as PlacedSource`. Ajouter à la section diagrammes :

```python
class ViewSource(_Input):
    """Where a generated view reads its members: a saved query, or a package.

    Exactly one of the two ids. Both fields are required (nullable) so the
    model is one schema for the request and for the answer.
    """

    query_id: UUID | None
    package_id: UUID | None
    recursive: bool

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _exactly_one(self) -> Self:
        if (self.query_id is None) == (self.package_id is None):
            msg = "name a saved query or a package, not both and not neither"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if self.query_id is not None and self.recursive:
            msg = "`recursive` applies to a package"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        return self

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, source: PlacedSource) -> ViewSource:
        if isinstance(source, QuerySource):
            return cls(query_id=source.query_id, package_id=None, recursive=False)
        return cls(query_id=None, package_id=source.package_id, recursive=source.recursive)

    def placed(self) -> PlacedSource:
        if self.query_id is not None:
            return QuerySource(self.query_id)
        assert self.package_id is not None  # noqa: S101 — the validator guarantees one of the two
        return PackageSource(self.package_id, self.recursive)


class DiagramGenerate(_Input):
    name: Name
    source: ViewSource


class DiagramFreeze(_Input):
    """The layout the SPA computed for a live view, stored when it is frozen."""

    nodes: Annotated[list[DiagramNode], Field(max_length=MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES)]


class DiagramUnfreeze(_Input):
    discard_annotations: bool = False


class SourceDiffRead(BaseModel):
    added: list[ElementRead]
    removed: list[UUID]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, diff: SourceDiff) -> SourceDiffRead:
        return cls(added=[ElementRead.of(e) for e in diff.added], removed=list(diff.removed))
```
`DiagramSummaryRead` : `generation: Generation | None` (+ `of`: `generation=diagram.generation`). `DiagramRead` : `generation: Generation | None`, `source: ViewSource | None` (+ `of` : `generation=diagram.generation`, `source=ViewSource.of(diagram.source) if diagram.source else None`).

```python
@router.post(
    "/diagrams/generate",
    response_model=DiagramRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=GENERATE_REJECTED,
)
async def generate_diagram(body: DiagramGenerate, diagrams: Diagrams) -> DiagramRead:
    """A new **live** view: everything a saved query or a package yields, at each opening.

    It stores no box. To edit it, freeze it; to see it frozen from the start,
    freeze it right after (the layout is the SPA's, the server computes none).
    """
    return DiagramRead.of(await diagrams.generate(name=body.name, source=body.source.placed()))


@router.get(
    "/diagrams/{diagram_id}/source-diff", response_model=SourceDiffRead, responses=GENERATE_REJECTED
)
async def read_source_diff(diagram_id: UUID, diagrams: Diagrams) -> SourceDiffRead:
    """What a frozen view would gain and lose if it followed its source again. Writes nothing."""
    return SourceDiffRead.of(await diagrams.source_diff(diagram_id))


@router.post("/diagrams/{diagram_id}/freeze", response_model=DiagramRead, responses=GENERATE_REJECTED)
async def freeze_diagram(diagram_id: UUID, body: DiagramFreeze, diagrams: Diagrams) -> DiagramRead:
    """Store the layout and make the live view an ordinary editable diagram."""
    await diagrams.freeze(diagram_id, [node.placed() for node in body.nodes])
    return DiagramRead.of(await diagrams.open(diagram_id))


@router.post("/diagrams/{diagram_id}/unfreeze", response_model=DiagramRead, responses=GENERATE_REJECTED)
async def unfreeze_diagram(
    diagram_id: UUID, body: DiagramUnfreeze, diagrams: Diagrams
) -> DiagramRead:
    """Back to a live view. Refused (409) while notes, groups or routes would be lost,
    unless `discard_annotations` is true."""
    await diagrams.unfreeze(diagram_id, discard_annotations=body.discard_annotations)
    return DiagramRead.of(await diagrams.open(diagram_id))
```

```python
    @server.tool(annotations=ADDS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def create_generated_diagram(
        name: Name,
        query_id: Annotated[UUID | None, Field(description="A saved query, as `list_saved_queries` reports it.")] = None,
        package_id: Annotated[UUID | None, Field(description="A package, as `list_packages` reports it.")] = None,
        recursive: Annotated[bool, Field(description="With a package: include its sub-packages.")] = False,
    ) -> DiagramRead:
        """Draw a perimeter: a live view of everything a saved query or a package holds.

        Prefer this to placing boxes one by one. The view follows the catalogue
        by itself — an element created tomorrow that answers the query appears
        — and the application arranges it by ArchiMate layer. Name exactly one
        of `query_id` and `package_id`; a source that yields more than 500
        elements is refused with its count, so narrow the filter.
        """
        if (query_id is None) == (package_id is None):
            msg = "name a saved query or a package, not both and not neither"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        source = (
            QuerySource(query_id)
            if query_id is not None
            else PackageSource(package_id, recursive)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        )
        return DiagramRead.of(await get_diagrams().generate(name=name, source=source))
```
et un paragraphe d'`INSTRUCTIONS` : « To draw a perimeter, generate a view from a saved query or a package (`create_generated_diagram`) rather than placing boxes: it follows the catalogue by itself. »

### Task 34: Auto-layout par couches ArchiMate (`autoLayout.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/src/lib/autoLayout.ts`
- Create: `frontend/tests/autoLayout.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `BOX_WIDTH`, `BOX_HEIGHT` (`lib/graphLayout.ts`), `Point`, `Rect` (`lib/diagramGeometry.ts`), `components['schemas']['Layer']`.
- Produces (TypeScript) :
  - `MAX_ROW_WIDTH = 1400`, `GAP_X = 40`, `GAP_Y = 90`, `ORIGIN = { x: 24, y: 24 }`
  - `ROW_OF: Record<Layer, number>` — table de **présentation** de l'ordre visuel des couches (motivation et stratégie en haut, métier, application, technologie avec le physique, implémentation/migration en bas)

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

`frontend/tests/autoLayout.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import {
  GAP_X,
  GAP_Y,
  MAX_ROW_WIDTH,
  ORIGIN,
  ROW_OF,
  layoutByLayers,
  placeAdded,
  type LayoutInput,
} from '../src/lib/autoLayout'
import { BOX_HEIGHT, BOX_WIDTH } from '../src/lib/graphLayout'

type El = LayoutInput['elements'][number]
const el = (id: string, layer: El['layer'], name = id): El => ({ id, name, layer })
const link = (source_id: string, target_id: string) => ({ source_id, target_id })

describe('layoutByLayers', () => {
  it('stacks the layers top to bottom in the order of ArchiMate, physical with technology', () => {
    const positions = layoutByLayers({
      elements: [
        el('t', 'technology'),
        el('b', 'business'),
        el('a', 'application'),
        el('m', 'motivation'),
        el('p', 'physical'),
        el('i', 'implementation_migration'),
      ],
      relationships: [],
    })

    const y = (id: string) => positions.get(id)!.y
    expect(y('m')).toBeLessThan(y('b'))
    expect(y('b')).toBeLessThan(y('a'))
    expect(y('a')).toBeLessThan(y('t'))
    expect(y('p')).toBe(y('t'))
    expect(y('t')).toBeLessThan(y('i'))
  })

  it('leaves no empty band between two layers that are present', () => {
    const positions = layoutByLayers({ elements: [el('b', 'business'), el('t', 'technology')], relationships: [] })

    expect(positions.get('t')!.y - positions.get('b')!.y).toBe(BOX_HEIGHT + GAP_Y)
  })

  it('gives the same answer whatever the order of the input', () => {
    const elements = ['d', 'a', 'c', 'b', 'e'].map((id, i) => el(id, i % 2 ? 'business' : 'application', `nom ${id}`))
    const relationships = [link('a', 'b'), link('c', 'd'), link('e', 'a')]

    const first = layoutByLayers({ elements, relationships })
    const second = layoutByLayers({ elements: [...elements].reverse(), relationships: [...relationships].reverse() })

    expect([...first.entries()].sort()).toEqual([...second.entries()].sort())
  })

  it('orders a row by the barycentre of its neighbours, so links cross less', () => {
    // Business a, b (alphabetical). Application x is wired to b and y to a:
    // alphabetically x < y would cross; the barycentre swaps them.
    const positions = layoutByLayers({
      elements: [el('a', 'business'), el('b', 'business'), el('x', 'application'), el('y', 'application')],
      relationships: [link('a', 'y'), link('b', 'x')],
    })

    expect(positions.get('y')!.x).toBeLessThan(positions.get('x')!.x)
  })

  it('breaks ties by name, then by id', () => {
    const positions = layoutByLayers({
      elements: [el('2', 'business', 'Même nom'), el('1', 'business', 'Même nom'), el('0', 'business', 'Autre')],
      relationships: [],
    })

    const order = [...positions.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1].x - b[1].x).map(([id]) => id)
    expect(order).toEqual(['0', '1', '2'])
  })

  it('wraps a row wider than the limit into sub-rows, never past the limit', () => {
    const elements = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => el(`e${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}`, 'application'))

    const positions = layoutByLayers({ elements, relationships: [] })

    const rows = new Set([...positions.values()].map((p) => p.y))
    expect(rows.size).toBe(3)
    const right = Math.max(...[...positions.values()].map((p) => p.x + BOX_WIDTH))
    expect(right).toBeLessThanOrEqual(ORIGIN.x + MAX_ROW_WIDTH)
  })

  it('never yields a negative coordinate and ignores a link to an element that is not there', () => {
    const positions = layoutByLayers({ elements: [el('a', 'business')], relationships: [link('a', 'ghost')] })

    const only = positions.get('a')!
    expect(only).toEqual({ x: ORIGIN.x, y: ORIGIN.y })
    expect(only.x).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0)
  })

  it('lays out nothing as nothing', () => {
    expect(layoutByLayers({ elements: [], relationships: [] }).size).toBe(0)
  })

  it('keeps the visual order as a presentation table over every layer of the API', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(ROW_OF).sort()).toEqual(
      ['application', 'business', 'implementation_migration', 'motivation', 'other', 'physical', 'strategy', 'technology'],
    )
    expect(GAP_X).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })
})

describe('placeAdded', () => {
  it('places only the added elements, below everything that is already there', () => {
    const occupied = [{ x: 24, y: 24, width: BOX_WIDTH, height: 300 }]

    const positions = placeAdded(
      { elements: [el('new1', 'application'), el('new2', 'application')], relationships: [] },
      occupied,
    )

    expect([...positions.keys()].sort()).toEqual(['new1', 'new2'])
    for (const point of positions.values()) {
      expect(point.y).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(24 + 300 + GAP_Y)
    }
  })

  it('starts at the origin when nothing is there yet', () => {
    const positions = placeAdded({ elements: [el('a', 'business')], relationships: [] }, [])

    expect(positions.get('a')).toEqual({ x: ORIGIN.x, y: ORIGIN.y })
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implémenter**

`frontend/src/lib/autoLayout.ts` :

```ts
// Where the boxes of a generated view go: ArchiMate's own reading, in layers.
//
// A hand-drawn diagram is placed by a person; a view generated from a query is
// placed by this, and must come out the same every time the same elements are
// there — otherwise a live view would shuffle at each opening. So: rows by
// layer, boxes ordered inside a row by the barycentre of their neighbours (two
// sweeps, down then up), ties broken by name then id, rows folded when too
// wide. No randomness, no iteration to convergence.
//
// Which layer an element is in comes from the API (`ElementRead.layer`); only
// the *visual order* of the layers is written here, as a presentation table
// like `LAYER_LABELS` — it is not a rule of the metamodel. This module shares
// with the rings of the neighbourhood nothing but the size of a box.
import type { components } from '../api/schema'
import type { Point, Rect } from './diagramGeometry'
import { BOX_HEIGHT, BOX_WIDTH } from './graphLayout'

type Layer = components['schemas']['Layer']

/** A row is folded into sub-rows past this width, in canvas units. */
export const MAX_ROW_WIDTH = 1400
export const GAP_X = 40
export const GAP_Y = 90
export const ORIGIN: Point = { x: 24, y: 24 }

/** Top to bottom: motivation and strategy, business, application, technology (physical with it), migration. */
export const ROW_OF: Record<Layer, number> = {
  motivation: 0,
  strategy: 0,
  business: 1,
  application: 2,
  technology: 3,
  physical: 3,
  implementation_migration: 4,
  other: 5,
}

export type LayoutInput = {
  elements: { id: string; name: string; layer: Layer }[]
  relationships: { source_id: string; target_id: string }[]
}

type Item = LayoutInput['elements'][number]

const byName = (a: Item, b: Item) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id)

export function layoutByLayers(
  input: LayoutInput,
  options: { rowOf?: Record<Layer, number> } = {},
): Map<string, Point> {
  const rowOf = options.rowOf ?? ROW_OF
  const rows = new Map<number, Item[]>()
  for (const item of [...input.elements].sort(byName)) {
    const key = rowOf[item.layer]
    rows.set(key, [...(rows.get(key) ?? []), item])
  }
  const keys = [...rows.keys()].sort((a, b) => a - b)

  const known = new Set(input.elements.map((item) => item.id))
  const neighbours = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
  for (const { source_id, target_id } of input.relationships) {
    if (known.has(source_id) && known.has(target_id)) {
      neighbours.set(source_id, (neighbours.get(source_id) ?? new Set()).add(target_id))
      neighbours.set(target_id, (neighbours.get(target_id) ?? new Set()).add(source_id))
    }
  }

  const index = new Map<string, number>()
  const reindex = (row: Item[]) => row.forEach((item, position) => index.set(item.id, position))
  keys.forEach((key) => reindex(rows.get(key)!))

  // Order one row by the mean position of the neighbours it has in `reference`.
  function sweep(key: number, reference: number): void {
    const row = rows.get(key)!
    const above = new Set((rows.get(reference) ?? []).map((item) => item.id))
    const centre = (item: Item): number => {
      const positions = [...(neighbours.get(item.id) ?? [])]
        .filter((other) => above.has(other))
        .map((other) => index.get(other)!)
      return positions.length > 0
        ? positions.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p, 0) / positions.length
        : index.get(item.id)!
    }
    const scored = new Map(row.map((item) => [item.id, centre(item)]))
    row.sort((a, b) => scored.get(a.id)! - scored.get(b.id)! || byName(a, b))
    reindex(row)
  }
  for (let k = 1; k < keys.length; k += 1) {
    sweep(keys[k], keys[k - 1])
  }
  for (let k = keys.length - 2; k >= 0; k -= 1) {
    sweep(keys[k], keys[k + 1])
  }

  const perLine = Math.max(1, Math.floor((MAX_ROW_WIDTH + GAP_X) / (BOX_WIDTH + GAP_X)))
  const positions = new Map<string, Point>()
  let line = 0
  for (const key of keys) {
    const row = rows.get(key)!
    for (let start = 0; start < row.length; start += perLine) {
      row.slice(start, start + perLine).forEach((item, column) => {
        positions.set(item.id, {
          x: ORIGIN.x + column * (BOX_WIDTH + GAP_X),
          y: ORIGIN.y + line * (BOX_HEIGHT + GAP_Y),
        })
      })
      line += 1
    }
  }
  return positions
}

/**
 * Lay out only what a refresh adds, below everything already on the diagram,
 * so no box that is there — and may have been moved by hand — is touched.
 */
export function placeAdded(added: LayoutInput, occupied: Rect[]): Map<string, Point> {
  const bottom = occupied.length > 0 ? Math.max(...occupied.map((box) => box.y + box.height)) : null
  const shift = bottom === null ? 0 : bottom + GAP_Y - ORIGIN.y
  const fresh = layoutByLayers(added)
  return new Map([...fresh].map(([id, point]) => [id, { x: point.x, y: point.y + shift }]))
}
```
Note : le test « leaves no empty band » attend `BOX_HEIGHT + GAP_Y` entre deux lignes présentes ✓ (`line` ne s'incrémente que pour les rangées non vides). Le test de repli : 20 éléments, 8 colonnes par ligne (`floor(1440 / 172) = 8`) → 3 lignes (8, 8, 4) ✓ ; l'extrémité droite maximale `24 + 7 × 172 + 132 = 1360 ≤ 24 + 1400` ✓.

- [ ] **Step 1: Écrire les tests qui échouent**

`frontend/tests/useDiagramGenerated.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { SAVE_PAUSE_MS, useDiagram } from '../src/features/diagrams/useDiagram'
import { anElement, aRelationship, stubApi, type Route } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => {
  vi.useRealTimers()
  vi.unstubAllGlobals()
})

const ID = 'dddddddd-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
const QUERY = 'qqqqqqqq-0000-4000-8000-000000000000'
const A = anElement({ id: 'aaaaaaaa-1111-4111-8111-111111111111', name: 'Facturation', layer: 'application' })
const B = anElement({
  id: 'bbbbbbbb-2222-4222-8222-222222222222',
  name: 'Commandes',
  element_type: 'business_process',
  layer: 'business',
})
const C = anElement({ id: 'cccccccc-3333-4333-8333-333333333333', name: 'Paiement', layer: 'application' })
const LINK = aRelationship({ source_id: B.id, target_id: A.id })
const SOURCE = { query_id: QUERY, package_id: null, recursive: false }

const base = {
  id: ID,
  name: 'Périmètre',
  description: '',
  created_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
  updated_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
  revision: 0,
  objects: [],
  edges: [],
  source: SOURCE,
}
const node = (element_id: string, x: number, y: number) => ({
  element_id, x, y, width: 132, height: 46, parent_object_id: null, z: 0,
})
const live = (): Route => ({
  path: `/diagrams/${ID}`,
  body: { ...base, generation: 'live', nodes: [], elements: [A, B], relationships: [LINK] },
})
const frozen = (): Route => ({
  path: `/diagrams/${ID}`,
  body: { ...base, generation: 'frozen', revision: 1, nodes: [node(A.id, 24, 24), node(B.id, 300, 24)], elements: [A, B], relationships: [LINK] },
})

async function pause(): Promise<void> {
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(SAVE_PAUSE_MS)
  await vi.waitFor(() => undefined)
}

describe('useDiagram — a live view', () => {
  it('lays itself out by layer and saves nothing, ever', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([live(), { method: 'PUT', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/layout`, status: 204 }])
    const editor = useDiagram()

    await editor.open(ID)
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['setTimeout', 'clearTimeout'] })
    editor.persist()
    await pause()

    expect(editor.live.value).toBe(true)
    const y = (id: string) => editor.boxes.value.find((box) => box.element.id === id)!.y
    expect(y(B.id)).toBeLessThan(y(A.id)) // business above application
    expect(calls.some((call) => call.method === 'PUT')).toBe(false)
  })

  it('freezes with the layout it computed and then behaves as an editable diagram', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      live(),
      { method: 'POST', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/freeze`, body: frozen().body },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)

    await editor.freeze()

    const sent = calls.find((call) => call.method === 'POST')?.body as { nodes: { element_id: string }[] }
    expect(sent.nodes.map((n) => n.element_id).sort()).toEqual([A.id, B.id])
    expect(editor.generation.value).toBe('frozen')
    expect(editor.live.value).toBe(false)
    expect(editor.revision.value).toBe(1)
  })
})

describe('useDiagram — a frozen view', () => {
  it('shows the gap with its source and writes nothing until it is applied', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      frozen(),
      { path: `/diagrams/${ID}/source-diff`, body: { added: [C], removed: [B.id] } },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)

    await editor.refresh()

    expect(editor.diff.value?.added.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([C.id])
    expect(calls.some((call) => call.method === 'PUT')).toBe(false)
    expect(editor.boxes.value).toHaveLength(2)
  })

  it('places the added below the survivors without moving them, and removes the gone only when asked', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      frozen(),
      { path: `/diagrams/${ID}/source-diff`, body: { added: [C], removed: [B.id] } },
      { method: 'PUT', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/layout`, status: 204 },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)
    await editor.refresh()

    await editor.applyDiff({ removeGone: false })

    const put = calls.find((call) => call.method === 'PUT')?.body as { nodes: { element_id: string; x: number; y: number }[] }
    const byId = new Map(put.nodes.map((n) => [n.element_id, n]))
    expect(byId.get(A.id)).toMatchObject({ x: 24, y: 24 })
    expect(byId.get(B.id)).toMatchObject({ x: 300, y: 24 })
    expect(byId.get(C.id)!.y).toBeGreaterThan(24 + 46)
    expect(editor.diff.value).toBeNull()
  })

  it('removes the elements that left the source when the user confirmed it', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      frozen(),
      { path: `/diagrams/${ID}/source-diff`, body: { added: [], removed: [B.id] } },
      { method: 'PUT', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/layout`, status: 204 },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)
    await editor.refresh()

    await editor.applyDiff({ removeGone: true })

    const put = calls.find((call) => call.method === 'PUT')?.body as { nodes: { element_id: string }[] }
    expect(put.nodes.map((n) => n.element_id)).toEqual([A.id])
  })

  it('thaws, surfacing the refusal when notes would be lost', async () => {
    stubApi([
      frozen(),
      { method: 'POST', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/unfreeze`, status: 409, body: { error: 'has_annotations', detail: 'would delete notes' } },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)

    await expect(editor.unfreeze()).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'has_annotations' })
    expect(editor.generation.value).toBe('frozen')
  })

  it('thaws on request and goes back to a live layout', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      frozen(),
      { method: 'POST', path: `/diagrams/${ID}/unfreeze`, body: live().body },
    ])
    const editor = useDiagram()
    await editor.open(ID)

    await editor.unfreeze(true)

    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ discard_annotations: true })
    expect(editor.live.value).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

`frontend/tests/NewViewForm.spec.ts` :

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import NewViewForm from '../src/features/diagrams/NewViewForm.vue'

const QUERIES = [{ id: 'q1', name: 'Applications critiques' }]
const PACKAGES = [{ id: 'p1', path: 'SI / Paiement' }]

function draw() {
  return render(NewViewForm, { props: { queries: QUERIES, packages: PACKAGES } })
}

describe('NewViewForm', () => {
  it('creates a live view of a saved query by default', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'Périmètre')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Requête enregistrée'), 'q1')

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().create).toEqual([
      [{ name: 'Périmètre', source: { query_id: 'q1', package_id: null, recursive: false }, frozen: false }],
    ])
  })

  it('reads a package, with or without its sub-packages, and can be frozen from the start', async () => {
    const rendered = draw()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'Paiement')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Un paquet'))
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Paquet'), 'p1')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Avec les sous-paquets'))
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Figée'))

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().create).toEqual([
      [{ name: 'Paiement', source: { query_id: null, package_id: 'p1', recursive: true }, frozen: true }],
    ])
  })

  it('does not submit without a name and a source', async () => {
    draw()

    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' })).toBeDisabled()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'X')
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' })).toBeDisabled()
  })
})
```

4. Ajouter :

```ts
  // --- Generated views (docs/adr: vues générées) -------------------------

  /** Store the layout computed here and make the live view an editable diagram. */
  async function freeze(): Promise<void> {
    const current = diagram.value
    if (!current || !live.value) {
      return
    }
    const answer = unwrap(
      await api.POST('/diagrams/{diagram_id}/freeze', {
        params: { path: { diagram_id: current.id } },
        body: { nodes: nodes.value.map((node) => ({ ...node })) },
      }),
    )
    applyAnswer(answer)
  }

  /**
   * Back to a live view. A view that carries notes, groups or routes is refused
   * (`has_annotations`): the error is thrown so the screen can ask, then call
   * again with `discard`.
   */
  async function unfreeze(discard = false): Promise<void> {
    const current = diagram.value
    if (!current) {
      return
    }
    applyAnswer(
      unwrap(
        await api.POST('/diagrams/{diagram_id}/unfreeze', {
          params: { path: { diagram_id: current.id } },
          body: { discard_annotations: discard },
        }),
      ),
    )
  }

  /** Read how the source moved since the freeze. Writes nothing. */
  async function refresh(): Promise<void> {
    const current = diagram.value
    if (current) {
      diff.value = unwrap(
        await api.GET('/diagrams/{diagram_id}/source-diff', {
          params: { path: { diagram_id: current.id } },
        }),
      )
    }
  }

  /** Follow the source: the added are placed below what is there, the gone leave if asked. */
  async function applyDiff(options: { removeGone: boolean }): Promise<void> {
    const gap = diff.value
    const current = diagram.value
    if (!gap || !current) {
      return
    }
    const gone = new Set(options.removeGone ? gap.removed : [])
    const survivors = nodes.value.filter((node) => !gone.has(node.element_id))
    const fresh = gap.added.filter((element) => !survivors.some((node) => node.element_id === element.id))
    const positions = placeAdded(
      {
        elements: fresh.map((e) => ({ id: e.id, name: e.name, layer: e.layer })),
        relationships: relationships.value,
      },
      survivors,
    )
    nodes.value = [
      ...survivors,
      ...fresh.map((e) => ({
        element_id: e.id,
        ...(positions.get(e.id) ?? { x: 24, y: 24 }),
        ...DEFAULT_BOX,
        parent_object_id: null,
        z: 0,
      })),
    ]
    for (const element of fresh) {
      if (!byId.value.has(element.id)) {
        elements.value.push(element)
      }
    }
    prune()
    diff.value = null
    persist()
    await flush()
    await open(current.id) // the links between the new boxes and the old ones come back with the read
  }
```

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// "Nouvelle vue": a diagram that is everything a saved query or a package holds.
// It decides nothing — it names the source and whether to freeze at once.
import { computed, ref } from 'vue'

import type { components } from '../../api/schema'

type ViewSource = components['schemas']['ViewSource']

defineProps<{
  queries: { id: string; name: string }[]
  packages: { id: string; path: string }[]
}>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ create: [payload: { name: string; source: ViewSource; frozen: boolean }] }>()

const name = ref('')
const kind = ref<'query' | 'package'>('query')
const queryId = ref('')
const packageId = ref('')
const recursive = ref(false)
const frozen = ref(false)

const ready = computed(
  () => name.value.trim() !== '' && (kind.value === 'query' ? queryId.value !== '' : packageId.value !== ''),
)

function submit(): void {
  const source: ViewSource =
    kind.value === 'query'
      ? { query_id: queryId.value, package_id: null, recursive: false }
      : { query_id: null, package_id: packageId.value, recursive: recursive.value }
  emit('create', { name: name.value.trim(), source, frozen: frozen.value })
}
</script>
```

```ts
async function createView(payload: { name: string; source: ViewSource; frozen: boolean }): Promise<void> {
  viewFailure.value = ''
  try {
    const created = unwrap(await api.POST('/diagrams/generate', { body: { name: payload.name, source: payload.source } }))
    if (payload.frozen) {
      await editor.open(created.id)
      await editor.freeze()
    }
    creatingView.value = false
    await router.push({ query: { diagram: created.id } })
  } catch (caught) {
    viewFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}

async function thaw(discard: boolean): Promise<void> {
  viewFailure.value = ''
  try {
    await editor.unfreeze(discard)
    confirmingThaw.value = false
  } catch (caught) {
    if (caught instanceof ApiError && caught.code === 'has_annotations') {
      confirmingThaw.value = true
    } else {
      viewFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
    }
  }
}

async function follow(): Promise<void> {
  viewFailure.value = ''
  try {
    await editor.applyDiff({ removeGone: removeGone.value })
  } catch (caught) {
    viewFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}
```

````markdown
---
titre: Vues générées — un diagramme qui est « tout ce qui répond à cette requête », vivant ou figé
date: 2026-10-10
statut: Accepté
affects: backend/src/ea/domain/generated_views.py, backend/src/ea/domain/diagrams.py, backend/migrations/versions/0011_generated_views.py, backend/src/ea/repositories/diagram_store.py, backend/src/ea/services/diagrams.py, backend/src/ea/services/view_sources.py, backend/src/ea/api/diagrams.py, backend/src/ea/mcp/server.py, frontend/src/lib/autoLayout.ts, frontend/src/features/diagrams/
---

# NN. Vues générées : vivante ou figée

Date : 2026-10-10
Statut : Accepté

## Contexte

Un architecte veut souvent un périmètre — « toutes les applications critiques », « le paquet Paiement et ses
sous-paquets » — sans le dessiner boîte par boîte. Le sous-projet « référentiel »
(paquets, requêtes enregistrées, `ElementQuery`) sait déjà dire quels éléments répondent. Reste la
question du diagramme : est-il un instantané, ou suit-il la requête ? Un instantané périme ; un suivi
interdit de ranger à la main. Et le serveur n'a, par construction, aucune coordonnée calculée
([`0035`](0035-taille-des-boites-de-diagramme.md) : la géométrie est du dessin).

## Décision

**Une vue générée est vivante par défaut — aucune boîte stockée, le contenu recalculé à chaque
ouverture, la mise en page calculée par le SPA — et se fige à la demande en un diagramme ordinaire qui
garde la référence à sa source.**

| Point | Choix |
|---|---|
| Source | `diagrams.source_query_id` ou `source_package_id` (+ `source_recursive`), clés étrangères `RESTRICT` : supprimer une requête ou un paquet lu par une vue est un 409 qui nomme les diagrammes (`names_using_source`) |
| Contraintes | au plus une source ; `generation` est non nul **si et seulement si** une source existe ; `generation` ∈ `live`, `frozen` |
| Vivante | `GET /diagrams/{id}` renvoie `nodes = []` et le graphe de la source ; lecture seule (`PUT /layout` → 409 `diagram_is_live`) ; mise en page de `lib/autoLayout.ts`, pure et déterministe, donc stable tant que le contenu l'est |
| Figer | `POST …/freeze` reçoit la mise en page que le SPA vient de calculer, la stocke en une transaction, passe à `frozen`, garde la source. Le diagramme s'édite alors comme un autre (notes, groupes, coudes, révision) |
| Rafraîchir | `GET …/source-diff` calcule `{added, removed}` sans rien écrire ; le SPA place les ajoutés sous les survivants (`placeAdded`, aucun survivant ne bouge), retire les disparus **seulement si l'utilisateur le demande**, puis enregistre par le `PUT` habituel |
| Dégeler | `POST …/unfreeze` supprime les boîtes et revient à vivante ; refusé (409 `has_annotations`) s'il existe notes, groupes ou tracés, sauf `discard_annotations=true` après confirmation explicite |
| Plafond | 500 éléments (`MAX_DIAGRAM_NODES`) : au-delà, 422 `generated_view_too_large` avec le décompte réel — on réduit le filtre |
| Filtre | Celui du sous-projet 1 (`ElementQuery`), jamais recopié. Un seul adaptateur (`services/view_sources.py`) connaît ses noms ; le reste dépend du port `ViewSourceResolver` |
| Auto-layout | Rangées par couche (motivation et stratégie, métier, application, technologie avec le physique, migration), tri par barycentre sur deux balayages, ex æquo par nom puis identifiant, rangées repliées à 1 400 unités |
| Agents | `create_generated_diagram(query_id | package_id)` : « pour dessiner un périmètre, génère une vue plutôt que de poser des boîtes » |
| « Figée d'emblée » | `generate` puis `freeze` : le serveur ne calcule aucune coordonnée, donc ne peut pas créer une vue figée de lui-même |

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Toujours un instantané | Simple, éditable | Périme au premier élément créé ; l'utilisateur ne sait pas que c'est périmé | Écarté |
| Toujours vivante | Toujours juste | Interdit de ranger, d'annoter, de grouper | Écarté |
| Boîtes stockées et resynchronisées en tâche de fond | Éditable et à jour | Une resynchronisation écrit à l'insu de l'utilisateur et déplace ce qu'il a rangé | Écarté |
| Calculer la mise en page côté serveur | Une vue figée d'emblée | La géométrie n'est pas un fait du serveur ; un second moteur de mise en page à tester | Écarté |
| `ON DELETE SET NULL` sur la source | Supprimer sans friction | La vue changerait de sens en silence, ou se viderait | Écarté |
| Auto-layout par force | Joli sur un graphe quelconque | Non déterministe ; une vue vivante se brasserait à chaque ouverture | Écarté |

## Conséquences

- **Une requête qui change fait changer une vue vivante sous les yeux de qui l'ouvre** : c'est le sens du mot ;
  une vue figée, elle, ne bouge jamais seule — « Rafraîchir » montre l'écart.
- **Le plafond est une garde de lisibilité, pas de performance** : une source de plus de 500 éléments se
  découpe en plusieurs vues.
- **Dépend du sous-projet 1** : la migration `0011` référence `saved_queries` et `packages` ; un déploiement qui
  n'en a pas répond 503 `generated_views_unavailable` aux routes de génération (le reste des diagrammes n'est pas touché).
- **`PUT /layout` d'une vue vivante est un 409** : un onglet resté ouvert sur une vue qu'un collègue vient de
  dégeler ou de figer doit se recharger.
- Le décompte « 40 outils MCP » de `CLAUDE.md` est tenu à jour avec les lots.

## Références

- ADR liés : [0031](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md), [0035](0035-taille-des-boites-de-diagramme.md),
  l'ADR des objets de vue (concurrence et notes d'une vue figée) et l'ADR du référentiel (sous-projet 1)
- Code concerné : `backend/src/ea/domain/generated_views.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/diagrams.py`,
  `backend/src/ea/services/view_sources.py`, `frontend/src/lib/autoLayout.ts`
````

# Lot 5 — Modèles de diagrammes

> Dépend du lot 2 (un modèle contient notes et groupes). Les stéréotypes de slot s'appuient sur le lot 3 s'il est livré ; sans lui, un slot qui exige un stéréotype ne peut être lié à aucun élément existant (il peut être créé vide ou omis).

### Task 37: Domaine — corps de modèle, validation, « depuis un diagramme », plan d'instanciation

- Modify: `backend/src/ea/domain/errors.py` (`TemplateNotFoundError`, `DuplicateTemplateError`)
- Create: `backend/tests/unit/test_diagram_templates.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `permitted_relationships`, `ElementType`, `RelationshipType` (`ea.domain.archimate`), `DiagramScene`, `ObjectKind`, `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH`, `MAX_OBJECT_TEXT` (Task 9), `Element`, `Relationship`.
- Produces (Python) :

```python
"""Diagram templates: a typed skeleton with no element in it — pure rules."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramEdge, DiagramObject, DiagramScene, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.diagram_templates import (
    Binding,
    body_to_json,
    parse_body,
    plan_instantiation,
    template_from_scene,
)
from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode
from ea.domain.model import Element, Relationship

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def raw(**overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    body: dict[str, Any] = {
        "version": 1,
        "slots": [
            {"key": "app", "element_type": "application_component", "label": "Application",
             "x": 10, "y": 10, "width": 132, "height": 46, "stereotype": None, "parent_key": "zone"},
            {"key": "host", "element_type": "node", "label": "Hôte",
             "x": 10, "y": 200, "width": 132, "height": 46, "stereotype": None, "parent_key": None},
        ],
        "groups": [{"key": "zone", "text": "Zone", "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 300, "height": 120, "colour": None, "parent_key": None}],
        "notes": [{"key": "why", "text": "Toujours un hôte dédié", "x": 320, "y": 10, "width": 180, "height": 60,
                   "colour": "#fff4b0", "parent_key": None, "anchor_key": "host"}],
        "links": [{"source_key": "host", "target_key": "app", "relationship_type": "realization"}],
    }
    return {**body, **overrides}


def an_element(element_type: ElementType, name: str = "X") -> Element:
    return Element.create(element_type=element_type, name=name, now=NOW)


class TestTheBody:
    def test_a_well_formed_body_round_trips(self) -> None:
        body = parse_body(raw())

        assert [s.key for s in body.slots] == ["app", "host"]
        assert body_to_json(body) == raw()

    def test_it_holds_no_element_only_typed_slots(self) -> None:
        document = body_to_json(parse_body(raw()))

        assert "element_id" not in str(document)

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("change", "message"),
        [
            ({"version": 2}, "version"),
            ({"slots": [{"key": "a", "element_type": "spaceship", "label": "x", "x": 0, "y": 0,
                         "width": 100, "height": 40, "stereotype": None, "parent_key": None}]}, "spaceship"),
        ],
    )
    def test_an_unknown_version_or_type_is_refused(self, change: dict[str, Any], message: str) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=message):
            parse_body(raw(**change))

    def test_a_link_the_metamodel_does_not_allow_between_the_two_types_is_refused(self) -> None:
        links = [{"source_key": "host", "target_key": "app", "relationship_type": "specialization"}]

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="not allowed"):
            parse_body(raw(links=links))

    def test_a_link_to_a_key_that_is_not_a_slot_is_refused(self) -> None:
        links = [{"source_key": "host", "target_key": "zone", "relationship_type": "association"}]

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="slot"):
            parse_body(raw(links=links))

    def test_keys_are_unique_across_slots_groups_and_notes(self) -> None:
        notes = [{"key": "app", "text": "x", "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 50,
                  "colour": None, "parent_key": None, "anchor_key": None}]

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="twice"):
            parse_body(raw(notes=notes))

    def test_a_parent_must_be_a_group_without_a_loop_and_within_the_depth(self) -> None:
        slots = raw()["slots"]
        slots[0]["parent_key"] = "host"
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="group"):
            parse_body(raw(slots=slots))

        loop = [
            {"key": "g1", "text": "1", "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100, "colour": None, "parent_key": "g2"},
            {"key": "g2", "text": "2", "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100, "colour": None, "parent_key": "g1"},
        ]
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="loop"):
            parse_body(raw(groups=loop, slots=[], notes=[], links=[]))

        deep = [
            {"key": f"g{i}", "text": str(i), "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100, "colour": None,
             "parent_key": f"g{i - 1}" if i else None}
            for i in range(6)
        ]
        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="deep"):
            parse_body(raw(groups=deep, slots=[], notes=[], links=[]))

    def test_a_note_is_anchored_on_a_slot_or_nothing(self) -> None:
        notes = raw()["notes"]
        notes[0]["anchor_key"] = "zone"

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="anchored"):
            parse_body(raw(notes=notes))

    def test_an_empty_group_is_valid(self) -> None:
        assert parse_body(raw(slots=[], notes=[], links=[])).groups[0].key == "zone"


class TestFromADiagram:
    def test_each_element_becomes_a_slot_of_the_same_type_and_the_rest_is_kept_by_kind(self) -> None:
        app, host = an_element(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, "Facturation"), an_element(ElementType.NODE, "srv-01")
        link = Relationship.between(RelationshipType.REALIZATION, host, app, now=NOW)
        group = DiagramObject(uuid4(), ObjectKind.GROUP, 0, 0, 300, 120, text="Zone")
        note = DiagramObject(uuid4(), ObjectKind.NOTE, 320, 10, 180, 60, text="Voir", anchor_element_id=host.id)
        scene = DiagramScene(
            nodes=(DiagramNode(app.id, 10, 10, parent_object_id=group.id), DiagramNode(host.id, 10, 200)),
            objects=(group, note),
            edges=(DiagramEdge(link.id, ()),),
        )

        body = template_from_scene(scene, {app.id: app, host.id: host}, [link], {app.id: "database"})

        assert sorted((s.element_type, s.stereotype) for s in body.slots) == sorted(
            [(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, "database"), (ElementType.NODE, None)]
        )
        slot_of = {s.label: s for s in body.slots}
        assert slot_of["Facturation"].parent_key == body.groups[0].key
        assert body.notes[0].anchor_key == slot_of["srv-01"].key
        assert [(link.relationship_type) for link in body.links] == [RelationshipType.REALIZATION]
        assert str(app.id) not in str(body_to_json(body))  # no element, only types

    def test_a_link_whose_end_is_not_on_the_diagram_is_not_kept(self) -> None:
        app, host = an_element(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT), an_element(ElementType.NODE)
        link = Relationship.between(RelationshipType.REALIZATION, host, app, now=NOW)

        body = template_from_scene(DiagramScene(nodes=(DiagramNode(app.id, 0, 0),)), {app.id: app}, [link], {})

        assert body.links == ()


class TestPlanningAnInstantiation:
    def setup_method(self) -> None:
        self.body = parse_body(raw())

    def test_slots_are_bound_created_or_left_out(self) -> None:
        app = an_element(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT)

        plan = plan_instantiation(
            self.body, [Binding("app", element_id=app.id)], {app.id: app.element_type}, {}
        )
        creating = plan_instantiation(self.body, [Binding("host", create_name="srv-02")], {}, {})

        assert [(s.key, i) for s, i in plan.bound] == [("app", app.id)]
        assert plan.omitted == ("host",)
        assert [(s.key, n) for s, n in creating.to_create] == [("host", "srv-02")]

    def test_an_element_of_another_type_is_refused_naming_both(self) -> None:
        node = an_element(ElementType.NODE)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="application_component.*node"):
            plan_instantiation(self.body, [Binding("app", element_id=node.id)], {node.id: node.element_type}, {})

    def test_a_stereotype_the_slot_requires_must_be_carried(self) -> None:
        slots = raw()["slots"]
        slots[0]["stereotype"] = "database"
        body = parse_body(raw(slots=slots))
        app = an_element(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="database"):
            plan_instantiation(body, [Binding("app", element_id=app.id)], {app.id: app.element_type}, {app.id: frozenset()})
        plan_instantiation(body, [Binding("app", element_id=app.id)], {app.id: app.element_type}, {app.id: frozenset({"database"})})

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        "bindings",
        [
            [Binding("ghost", create_name="x")],
            [Binding("app", create_name="a"), Binding("app", create_name="b")],
            [Binding("app", element_id=uuid4(), create_name="both")],
            [Binding("app", create_name="   ")],
        ],
    )
    def test_malformed_bindings_are_refused(self, bindings: list[Binding]) -> None:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):  # noqa: PT011 — each message is checked by its own test above
            plan_instantiation(self.body, bindings, {}, {})

    def test_one_element_cannot_fill_two_slots(self) -> None:
        body = parse_body(raw(slots=[
            {**raw()["slots"][0], "key": "a1", "parent_key": None},
            {**raw()["slots"][0], "key": "a2", "parent_key": None},
        ], links=[], notes=[], groups=[]))
        app = an_element(ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="twice"):
            plan_instantiation(
                body, [Binding("a1", element_id=app.id), Binding("a2", element_id=app.id)], {app.id: app.element_type}, {}
            )
```

```python
class DuplicateTemplateError(DomainError):

```
`backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_templates.py` :

```python
"""Diagram templates: recurring schemas as a typed skeleton, with no element in it.

A template says "an application component inside a zone, realised by a node,
with this note beside it" — slots of an ArchiMate type, groups, notes, links
between slots by relationship type. It holds **no element**: instantiating it
binds each slot to an existing element of the right type, or to one to create,
or leaves it out (docs/adr: modèles de diagrammes). The body is a versioned
document validated here, against the metamodel the domain already holds — no
list of types or relationships is restated.

Nothing here imports a framework or a driver, per `CLAUDE.md`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Final
from uuid import UUID

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType, permitted_relationships
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import MAX_GROUP_DEPTH, MAX_OBJECT_TEXT, DiagramScene, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.model import Element, Relationship

TEMPLATE_VERSION: Final = 1
MAX_TEMPLATE_NAME: Final = 200
MAX_SLOTS: Final = 100
MAX_TEMPLATE_OBJECTS: Final = 100
MAX_TEMPLATE_LINKS: Final = 300
_KEY = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,39}$")
_HEX_COLOUR = re.compile(r"^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Slot:
    """Where an element of `element_type` goes. Its `label` says what it is for."""

    key: str
    element_type: ElementType
    label: str
    x: float
    y: float
    width: float
    height: float
    stereotype: str | None = None
    parent_key: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TemplateGroup:
    key: str
    text: str
    x: float
    y: float
    width: float
    height: float
    colour: str | None = None
    parent_key: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TemplateNote:
    key: str
    text: str
    x: float
    y: float
    width: float
    height: float
    colour: str | None = None
    parent_key: str | None = None
    anchor_key: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TemplateLink:
    source_key: str
    target_key: str
    relationship_type: RelationshipType


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TemplateBody:
    version: int
    slots: tuple[Slot, ...] = ()
    groups: tuple[TemplateGroup, ...] = ()
    notes: tuple[TemplateNote, ...] = ()
    links: tuple[TemplateLink, ...] = ()


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DiagramTemplate:
    id: UUID
    name: str
    description: str
    body: TemplateBody
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime


def clean_template_name(name: str) -> str:
    cleaned = name.strip()
    if not cleaned or len(cleaned) > MAX_TEMPLATE_NAME:
        msg = f"a template name is between 1 and {MAX_TEMPLATE_NAME} characters"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    return cleaned


def _groups_above(start: str | None, groups: Mapping[str, TemplateGroup], owner: str | None) -> int:
    depth, seen, current = 0, set[str](), start
    while current is not None:
        if current in seen or current == owner:
            msg = "groups cannot contain one another in a loop"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        seen.add(current)
        parent = groups.get(current)
        if parent is None:
            msg = f"{current!r} is not a group of this template"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        depth += 1
        current = parent.parent_key
    return depth


def check_body(body: TemplateBody) -> None:
    """The rules of a body that need no store: keys, nesting, anchors, and the metamodel."""
    if body.version != TEMPLATE_VERSION:
        msg = f"the template version must be {TEMPLATE_VERSION}"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    if len(body.slots) > MAX_SLOTS or len(body.groups) + len(body.notes) > MAX_TEMPLATE_OBJECTS:
        msg = "a template has too many slots or objects"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    if len(body.links) > MAX_TEMPLATE_LINKS:
        msg = f"a template has at most {MAX_TEMPLATE_LINKS} links"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    keys = [*(s.key for s in body.slots), *(g.key for g in body.groups), *(n.key for n in body.notes)]
    if len(set(keys)) != len(keys):
        msg = "a key is used twice in the template"
        raise ValueError(msg)
    for key in keys:
        if not _KEY.match(key):
            msg = f"{key!r} is not a valid key"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    groups = {g.key: g for g in body.groups}
    for slot in body.slots:
        if slot.element_type.is_connector:
            msg = "a slot cannot be a connector"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if _groups_above(slot.parent_key, groups, None) > MAX_GROUP_DEPTH:
            msg = f"groups nest at most {MAX_GROUP_DEPTH} deep"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    for group in body.groups:
        if _groups_above(group.parent_key, groups, group.key) > MAX_GROUP_DEPTH - 1:
            msg = f"groups nest at most {MAX_GROUP_DEPTH} deep"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    slot_by_key = {s.key: s for s in body.slots}
    for item in (*body.groups, *body.notes):
        if len(item.text) > MAX_OBJECT_TEXT:
            msg = f"the text of an object is at most {MAX_OBJECT_TEXT} characters"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if item.colour is not None and not _HEX_COLOUR.match(item.colour):
            msg = "a colour is #rrggbb"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    for note in body.notes:
        if _groups_above(note.parent_key, groups, None) > MAX_GROUP_DEPTH:
            msg = f"groups nest at most {MAX_GROUP_DEPTH} deep"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if note.anchor_key is not None and note.anchor_key not in slot_by_key:
            msg = f"a note is anchored on {note.anchor_key!r}, which is not a slot"
            raise ValueError(msg)
    seen: set[tuple[str, str, RelationshipType]] = set()
    for link in body.links:
        source, target = slot_by_key.get(link.source_key), slot_by_key.get(link.target_key)
        if source is None or target is None:
            msg = f"a link joins {link.source_key!r} and {link.target_key!r}, which must both be slots"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if link.relationship_type not in permitted_relationships(source.element_type, target.element_type):
            msg = (
                f"{link.relationship_type.value} is not allowed between "
                f"{source.element_type.value} and {target.element_type.value}"
            )
            raise ValueError(msg)
        triple = (link.source_key, link.target_key, link.relationship_type)
        if triple in seen:
            msg = "the same link is declared twice"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        seen.add(triple)


def parse_body(raw: Mapping[str, Any]) -> TemplateBody:
    """A body from its JSON form — the same validators for a manual write and an import."""
    try:
        body = TemplateBody(
            version=raw["version"],
            slots=tuple(
                Slot(
                    key=s["key"],
                    element_type=ElementType(s["element_type"]),
                    label=s["label"],
                    x=float(s["x"]),
                    y=float(s["y"]),
                    width=float(s["width"]),
                    height=float(s["height"]),
                    stereotype=s.get("stereotype"),
                    parent_key=s.get("parent_key"),
                )
                for s in raw["slots"]
            ),
            groups=tuple(
                TemplateGroup(
                    key=g["key"], text=g["text"], x=float(g["x"]), y=float(g["y"]),
                    width=float(g["width"]), height=float(g["height"]),
                    colour=g.get("colour"), parent_key=g.get("parent_key"),
                )
                for g in raw["groups"]
            ),
            notes=tuple(
                TemplateNote(
                    key=n["key"], text=n["text"], x=float(n["x"]), y=float(n["y"]),
                    width=float(n["width"]), height=float(n["height"]),
                    colour=n.get("colour"), parent_key=n.get("parent_key"), anchor_key=n.get("anchor_key"),
                )
                for n in raw["notes"]
            ),
            links=tuple(
                TemplateLink(l["source_key"], l["target_key"], RelationshipType(l["relationship_type"]))
                for l in raw["links"]
            ),
        )
    except (KeyError, TypeError) as error:
        msg = f"the template body lacks or misuses {error}"
        raise ValueError(msg) from error
    check_body(body)
    return body


def body_to_json(body: TemplateBody) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "version": body.version,
        "slots": [
            {"key": s.key, "element_type": s.element_type.value, "label": s.label, "x": s.x, "y": s.y,
             "width": s.width, "height": s.height, "stereotype": s.stereotype, "parent_key": s.parent_key}
            for s in body.slots
        ],
        "groups": [
            {"key": g.key, "text": g.text, "x": g.x, "y": g.y, "width": g.width, "height": g.height,
             "colour": g.colour, "parent_key": g.parent_key}
            for g in body.groups
        ],
        "notes": [
            {"key": n.key, "text": n.text, "x": n.x, "y": n.y, "width": n.width, "height": n.height,
             "colour": n.colour, "parent_key": n.parent_key, "anchor_key": n.anchor_key}
            for n in body.notes
        ],
        "links": [
            {"source_key": l.source_key, "target_key": l.target_key, "relationship_type": l.relationship_type.value}
            for l in body.links
        ],
    }


def template_from_scene(
    scene: DiagramScene,
    elements: Mapping[UUID, Element],
    relationships: Sequence[Relationship],
    stereotype_of: Mapping[UUID, str],
) -> TemplateBody:
    """Turn a diagram into a skeleton: each element becomes a slot of its type.

    Notes, groups and the links between the drawn elements are kept, the links
    by relationship type. No element identity survives — only types.
    """
    ordered_nodes = sorted(scene.nodes, key=lambda n: (n.y, n.x, str(n.element_id)))
    ordered_objects = sorted(scene.objects, key=lambda o: (o.z, o.y, o.x, str(o.id)))
    group_keys = {
        o.id: f"g{i + 1}" for i, o in enumerate(o for o in ordered_objects if o.kind is ObjectKind.GROUP)
    }
    slot_keys = {n.element_id: f"s{i + 1}" for i, n in enumerate(ordered_nodes)}
    slots = tuple(
        Slot(
            key=slot_keys[n.element_id],
            element_type=elements[n.element_id].element_type,
            label=elements[n.element_id].name[:100],
            x=n.x, y=n.y, width=n.width, height=n.height,
            stereotype=stereotype_of.get(n.element_id),
            parent_key=group_keys.get(n.parent_object_id) if n.parent_object_id else None,
        )
        for n in ordered_nodes
    )
    groups = tuple(
        TemplateGroup(
            key=group_keys[o.id], text=o.text, x=o.x, y=o.y, width=o.width, height=o.height,
            colour=o.colour, parent_key=group_keys.get(o.parent_id) if o.parent_id else None,
        )
        for o in ordered_objects
        if o.kind is ObjectKind.GROUP
    )
    notes = tuple(
        TemplateNote(
            key=f"n{i + 1}", text=o.text, x=o.x, y=o.y, width=o.width, height=o.height, colour=o.colour,
            parent_key=group_keys.get(o.parent_id) if o.parent_id else None,
            anchor_key=slot_keys.get(o.anchor_element_id) if o.anchor_element_id else None,
        )
        for i, o in enumerate(o for o in ordered_objects if o.kind is ObjectKind.NOTE)
    )
    links = tuple(
        TemplateLink(slot_keys[r.source_id], slot_keys[r.target_id], r.relationship_type)
        for r in relationships
        if r.source_id in slot_keys and r.target_id in slot_keys
    )
    body = TemplateBody(TEMPLATE_VERSION, slots, groups, notes, links)
    check_body(body)
    return body


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Binding:
    """What fills one slot: an existing element, an element to create, or nothing."""

    slot_key: str
    element_id: UUID | None = None
    create_name: str | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InstantiationPlan:
    bound: tuple[tuple[Slot, UUID], ...]
    to_create: tuple[tuple[Slot, str], ...]
    omitted: tuple[str, ...]


def plan_instantiation(
    body: TemplateBody,
    bindings: Sequence[Binding],
    element_types: Mapping[UUID, ElementType],
    element_stereotypes: Mapping[UUID, frozenset[str]],
) -> InstantiationPlan:
    """Decide what an instantiation does — and refuse every mistake before anything is written."""
    slots = {s.key: s for s in body.slots}
    chosen: dict[str, Binding] = {}
    for binding in bindings:
        slot = slots.get(binding.slot_key)
        if slot is None:
            msg = f"the template has no slot {binding.slot_key!r}"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if binding.slot_key in chosen:
            msg = f"slot {binding.slot_key!r} is bound twice"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if binding.element_id is not None and binding.create_name is not None:
            msg = f"slot {binding.slot_key!r}: choose an existing element or create one, not both"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if binding.create_name is not None and not binding.create_name.strip():
            msg = f"slot {binding.slot_key!r}: the name of the element to create is blank"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        chosen[binding.slot_key] = binding
    bound: list[tuple[Slot, UUID]] = []
    to_create: list[tuple[Slot, str]] = []
    used: set[UUID] = set()
    for key, slot in slots.items():
        binding = chosen.get(key)
        if binding is None or (binding.element_id is None and binding.create_name is None):
            continue
        if binding.create_name is not None:
            to_create.append((slot, binding.create_name.strip()))
            continue
        element_id = binding.element_id
        assert element_id is not None  # noqa: S101 — the branch above ruled out the other cases
        actual = element_types.get(element_id)
        if actual is not slot.element_type:
            msg = (
                f"slot {key!r} wants a {slot.element_type.value}, "
                f"got {actual.value if actual else 'an unknown element'}"
            )
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if slot.stereotype and slot.stereotype not in element_stereotypes.get(element_id, frozenset()):
            msg = f"slot {key!r} wants an element carrying the stereotype {slot.stereotype!r}"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        if element_id in used:
            msg = "an element cannot fill two slots (it would be placed twice)"
            raise ValueError(msg)
        used.add(element_id)
        bound.append((slot, element_id))
    filled = {s.key for s, _ in bound} | {s.key for s, _ in to_create}
    omitted = tuple(key for key in slots if key not in filled)
    return InstantiationPlan(tuple(bound), tuple(to_create), omitted)
```
Remarques : le test `loop` (`g1 ↔ g2`) lève « loop » depuis `_groups_above` ; le test de profondeur 6 niveaux (`g0 … g5`) : le groupe `g5` a 5 groupes au-dessus, la limite pour un groupe est `MAX_GROUP_DEPTH - 1 = 4` → « deep » ✓ ; `parent_key = "host"` d'un slot (qui n'est pas un groupe) → « 'host' is not a group of this template » contient « group » ✓ ; la liaison `specialization` NODE → APPLICATION_COMPONENT n'est pas permise → message « is not allowed between » ✓ (« not allowed »).

```python
class InMemoryTemplates:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.templates: dict[UUID, DiagramTemplate] = {}

    def _refuse_a_taken_name(self, template: DiagramTemplate) -> None:
        if any(t.name == template.name and t.id != template.id for t in self.templates.values()):
            msg = f"a template is already named {template.name!r}"
            raise DuplicateTemplateError(msg)

    async def list_all(self) -> tuple[DiagramTemplate, ...]:
        return tuple(sorted(self.templates.values(), key=lambda t: t.name))

    async def get(self, template_id: UUID) -> DiagramTemplate | None:
        return self.templates.get(template_id)

    async def add(self, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplate:
        self._refuse_a_taken_name(template)
        self.templates[template.id] = template
        return template

    async def save(self, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplate:
        if template.id not in self.templates:
            raise TemplateNotFoundError(str(template.id))
        self._refuse_a_taken_name(template)
        self.templates[template.id] = template
        return template

    async def delete(self, template_id: UUID) -> bool:
        return self.templates.pop(template_id, None) is not None


@pytest.fixture
def templates() -> InMemoryTemplates:
    return InMemoryTemplates()


@pytest.fixture
def template_service(
    templates: InMemoryTemplates, service: ArchitectureService, diagram_service: DiagramService
) -> TemplateService:
    return TemplateService(templates, service, diagram_service, clock=lambda: FIXED_NOW)
```

```python
"""Templates through the service: relationships are never created silently."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from ea.domain.archimate import ElementType, RelationshipType
from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramObject, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.diagram_templates import Binding
```
