```python
from ea.domain.ports import ElementFilter
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from ea.services.caller import acting_as
from ea.services.diagrams import DiagramService
from ea.services.templates import TemplateService
from tests.conftest import a_reader, lay_out

BODY: dict[str, Any] = {
    "version": 1,
    "slots": [
        {"key": "app", "element_type": "application_component", "label": "Application",
         "x": 10, "y": 10, "width": 132, "height": 46, "stereotype": None, "parent_key": "zone"},
        {"key": "host", "element_type": "node", "label": "Hôte",
         "x": 10, "y": 200, "width": 132, "height": 46, "stereotype": None, "parent_key": None},
    ],
    "groups": [{"key": "zone", "text": "Zone", "x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 300, "height": 120, "colour": None, "parent_key": None}],
    "notes": [{"key": "why", "text": "Un hôte dédié", "x": 320, "y": 10, "width": 180, "height": 60,
               "colour": None, "parent_key": None, "anchor_key": "host"}],
    "links": [{"source_key": "host", "target_key": "app", "relationship_type": "realization"}],
}


async def count(service: ArchitectureService) -> int:
    return len(await service.list_elements(ElementFilter()))


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestInstantiating:
    async def test_it_creates_the_diagram_and_the_elements_asked_for_and_no_relationship_by_default(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="Application + hôte", body=BODY)

        detail = await template_service.instantiate(
            template.id,
            name="Facturation",
            bindings=[Binding("app", create_name="Facturation"), Binding("host", create_name="srv-01")],
        )

        assert {e.name for e in detail.graph.elements} == {"Facturation", "srv-01"}
        assert detail.graph.relationships == ()
        assert await service.list_relationships() == ()
        assert [o.kind for o in detail.objects].count(ObjectKind.GROUP) == 1
        app = next(n for n in detail.nodes if n.parent_object_id is not None)
        assert app.parent_object_id == next(o.id for o in detail.objects if o.kind is ObjectKind.GROUP)
        note = next(o for o in detail.objects if o.kind is ObjectKind.NOTE)
        assert note.anchor_element_id is not None

    async def test_relationships_are_created_only_on_request_through_the_architecture_service(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)

        detail = await template_service.instantiate(
            template.id,
            name="Avec liens",
            bindings=[Binding("app", create_name="A"), Binding("host", create_name="H")],
            create_relationships=True,
        )

        assert [r.relationship_type for r in detail.graph.relationships] == [RelationshipType.REALIZATION]

    async def test_a_relationship_that_already_exists_is_not_created_twice(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)
        app = await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="A")
        host = await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.NODE, name="H")
        await service.connect(relationship_type=RelationshipType.REALIZATION, source_id=host.id, target_id=app.id)

        await template_service.instantiate(
            template.id, name="V",
            bindings=[Binding("app", element_id=app.id), Binding("host", element_id=host.id)],
            create_relationships=True,
        )

        assert len(await service.list_relationships()) == 1

    async def test_an_omitted_slot_leaves_its_note_unanchored_and_its_links_out(
        self, template_service: TemplateService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)

        detail = await template_service.instantiate(
            template.id, name="Sans hôte", bindings=[Binding("app", create_name="A")], create_relationships=True
        )

        assert len(detail.nodes) == 1
        assert detail.graph.relationships == ()
        assert next(o for o in detail.objects if o.kind is ObjectKind.NOTE).anchor_element_id is None

    async def test_a_binding_of_the_wrong_type_is_refused_and_nothing_is_written(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService, diagram_service: DiagramService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)
        node = await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.NODE, name="H")
        before = await count(service)

        with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="application_component"):
            await template_service.instantiate(
                template.id, name="Mauvais", bindings=[Binding("app", element_id=node.id), Binding("host", create_name="X")]
            )

        assert await count(service) == before
        assert await diagram_service.list_diagrams() == ()

    async def test_a_taken_diagram_name_fails_before_any_element_is_created(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService, diagram_service: DiagramService
    ) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)
        await diagram_service.create(name="Pris")
        before = await count(service)

        with pytest.raises(DuplicateDiagramError):
            await template_service.instantiate(template.id, name="Pris", bindings=[Binding("app", create_name="A")])

        assert await count(service) == before

    async def test_a_reader_cannot_instantiate(self, template_service: TemplateService) -> None:
        template = await template_service.create_template(name="T", body=BODY)

        with acting_as(a_reader()):
            assert [t.name for t in await template_service.list_templates()] == ["T"]
            with pytest.raises(NotAuthorisedError):
                await template_service.instantiate(template.id, name="X", bindings=[])


@pytest.mark.asyncio
class TestAsTemplate:
    async def test_a_diagram_becomes_a_skeleton_that_instantiates_to_the_same_shape(
        self, template_service: TemplateService, service: ArchitectureService, diagram_service: DiagramService
    ) -> None:
        app = await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.APPLICATION_COMPONENT, name="Facturation")
        host = await service.create_element(element_type=ElementType.NODE, name="srv-01")
        await service.connect(relationship_type=RelationshipType.REALIZATION, source_id=host.id, target_id=app.id)
        diagram = await diagram_service.create(name="Source")
        note = DiagramObject(uuid4(), ObjectKind.NOTE, 300, 10, 150, 60, text="Voir", anchor_element_id=host.id)
        await lay_out(diagram_service, diagram.id, [DiagramNode(app.id, 10, 10), DiagramNode(host.id, 10, 200)], [note])

        template = await template_service.create_from_diagram(diagram.id, name="Modèle")
        detail = await template_service.instantiate(
            template.id, name="Copie", bindings=[Binding("s1", create_name="A2"), Binding("s2", create_name="H2")], create_relationships=True
        )

        assert sorted(s.element_type.value for s in template.body.slots) == ["application_component", "node"]
        assert len(detail.nodes) == 2 and len(detail.graph.relationships) == 1
        assert [o.text for o in detail.objects] == ["Voir"]
        assert {e.name for e in detail.graph.elements} == {"A2", "H2"}
```

```python
"""Templates against a real PostgreSQL: a body survives as jsonb, a name is unique."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine

from ea.db.postgres import create_session_factory
from ea.domain.diagram_templates import DiagramTemplate, parse_body
from ea.domain.errors import DuplicateTemplateError
from ea.repositories.template_store import PostgresTemplateRepository
from tests.unit.test_template_service import BODY

pytestmark = [pytest.mark.postgres, pytest.mark.asyncio]

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 10, 12, 0, tzinfo=UTC)


def a_template(name: str = "Modèle") -> DiagramTemplate:
    return DiagramTemplate(uuid4(), name, "é", parse_body(BODY), NOW, NOW)


@pytest.fixture
def templates(engine_at_head: AsyncEngine) -> PostgresTemplateRepository:
    return PostgresTemplateRepository(create_session_factory(engine_at_head))


async def test_a_template_survives_the_round_trip(templates: PostgresTemplateRepository) -> None:
    stored = await templates.add(a_template())

    assert await templates.get(stored.id) == stored


async def test_a_name_is_unique_and_the_store_stays_usable(templates: PostgresTemplateRepository) -> None:
    await templates.add(a_template("A"))

    with pytest.raises(DuplicateTemplateError):
        await templates.add(a_template("A"))
    await templates.add(a_template("B"))

    assert [t.name for t in await templates.list_all()] == ["A", "B"]


async def test_save_and_delete(templates: PostgresTemplateRepository) -> None:
    stored = await templates.add(a_template())

    renamed = await templates.save(DiagramTemplate(stored.id, "Autre", "", stored.body, NOW, NOW))

    assert (await templates.get(stored.id)).name == "Autre"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
    assert await templates.delete(renamed.id) is True
    assert await templates.delete(renamed.id) is False
```

```python
"""les modèles de diagrammes

Revision ID: 0012
Revises: 0011
Date: 2026-10-10

Un modèle est un squelette typé — des emplacements, des groupes, des notes, des
liens par type de relation — sans aucun élément (ADR sur les modèles de
diagrammes). Son corps est un `jsonb` versionné qu'on n'interroge jamais à
l'intérieur : il est validé en domaine, relu en entier. Une table neuve, rien à
convertir.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision: str = "0012"
down_revision: str | None = "0011"
branch_labels: str | Sequence[str] | None = None
depends_on: str | Sequence[str] | None = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    op.create_table(
        "diagram_templates",
        sa.Column("id", sa.Uuid(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("name", sa.String(length=200), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("description", sa.Text(), server_default="", nullable=False),
        sa.Column("body", postgresql.JSONB(astext_type=sa.Text()), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
        sa.PrimaryKeyConstraint("id", name=op.f("pk_diagram_templates")),
        sa.UniqueConstraint("name", name=op.f("uq_diagram_templates_name")),
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.drop_table("diagram_templates")
```

```python
"""The table of diagram templates — see docs/adr: modèles de diagrammes."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, String, Text, UniqueConstraint, Uuid
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from ea.db.base import Base


class DiagramTemplateRecord(Base):
    """A typed skeleton. `body` is a versioned document, validated by the domain, never queried inside."""

    __tablename__ = "diagram_templates"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("name"),)

    id: Mapped[UUID] = mapped_column(Uuid, primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(200), nullable=False)
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False, server_default="")
    body: Mapped[dict[str, Any]] = mapped_column(JSONB, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False)
```

(importer `DiagramTemplate` sous `TYPE_CHECKING`.) `backend/src/ea/repositories/template_store.py` :

```python
"""The PostgreSQL implementation of `TemplateRepository` — a body in, a body out."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, cast

from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.engine import CursorResult
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from ea.db.models.diagram_template import DiagramTemplateRecord
from ea.domain.diagram_templates import DiagramTemplate, body_to_json, parse_body
from ea.domain.errors import DuplicateTemplateError, TemplateNotFoundError

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from uuid import UUID

    from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker


def _to_template(row: DiagramTemplateRecord) -> DiagramTemplate:
    return DiagramTemplate(
        id=row.id,
        name=row.name,
        description=row.description,
        body=parse_body(row.body),
        created_at=row.created_at,
        updated_at=row.updated_at,
    )


def _taken(template: DiagramTemplate) -> DuplicateTemplateError:
    return DuplicateTemplateError(f"a template is already named {template.name!r}")


class PostgresTemplateRepository:
    def __init__(self, sessions: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]) -> None:
        self._sessions = sessions

    async def list_all(self) -> tuple[DiagramTemplate, ...]:
        statement = select(DiagramTemplateRecord).order_by(DiagramTemplateRecord.name)
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            return tuple(_to_template(row) for row in await session.scalars(statement))

    async def get(self, template_id: UUID) -> DiagramTemplate | None:
        async with self._sessions() as session:
            row = await session.get(DiagramTemplateRecord, template_id)
            return _to_template(row) if row is not None else None

    async def add(self, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplate:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            session.add(
                DiagramTemplateRecord(
                    id=template.id,
                    name=template.name,
                    description=template.description,
                    body=body_to_json(template.body),
                    created_at=template.created_at,
                    updated_at=template.updated_at,
                )
            )
            try:
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(template) from error
        return template

    async def save(self, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplate:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            row = await session.get(DiagramTemplateRecord, template.id)
            if row is None:
                msg = f"no template with id {template.id}"
                raise TemplateNotFoundError(msg)
            row.name = template.name
            row.description = template.description
            row.body = body_to_json(template.body)
            row.updated_at = template.updated_at
            try:
                await session.flush()
            except IntegrityError as error:
                raise _taken(template) from error
        return template

    async def delete(self, template_id: UUID) -> bool:
        async with self._sessions.begin() as session:
            deleted = await session.execute(
                delete(DiagramTemplateRecord).where(DiagramTemplateRecord.id == template_id)
            )
        return bool(cast("CursorResult[Any]", deleted).rowcount)
```

`backend/src/ea/services/templates.py` :

```python
"""Use cases over diagram templates: save a schema as a skeleton, build a diagram from it.

The rule this layer owns: **an instantiation never goes around the
architecture service**. Elements are created by `create_element` (so the
ArchiMate and IP-addressing rules apply) and relationships by `connect`, and
only when the caller asked for them with `create_relationships` — a template's
links are what the author *drew*, not facts nobody decided. Everything that can
be refused is refused before anything is written.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import replace
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from ea.domain.diagram_objects import DiagramObject, DiagramScene, ObjectKind
from ea.domain.diagram_templates import (
    Binding,
    DiagramTemplate,
    clean_template_name,
    parse_body,
    plan_instantiation,
    template_from_scene,
)
from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramNode
from ea.domain.errors import TemplateNotFoundError
from ea.services.caller import require_caller, require_editor

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from ea.domain.diagrams import DiagramDetail
    from ea.domain.ports import TemplateRepository
    from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
    from ea.services.diagrams import DiagramService

Clock = Callable[[], datetime]

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


def _utc_now() -> datetime:
    return datetime.now(UTC)


class TemplateService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: TemplateRepository,
        architecture: ArchitectureService,
        diagrams: DiagramService,
        *,
        clock: Clock = _utc_now,
    ) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._architecture = architecture
        self._diagrams = diagrams
        self._now = clock

    async def list_templates(self) -> tuple[DiagramTemplate, ...]:
        require_caller()
        return await self._repository.list_all()

    async def get_template(self, template_id: UUID) -> DiagramTemplate:
        require_caller()
        template = await self._repository.get(template_id)
        if template is None:
            msg = f"no template with id {template_id}"
            raise TemplateNotFoundError(msg)
        return template

    async def create_template(
        self, *, name: str, description: str = "", body: Mapping[str, Any]
    ) -> DiagramTemplate:
        require_editor()
        now = self._now()
        stored = await self._repository.add(
            DiagramTemplate(
                id=uuid4(),
                name=clean_template_name(name),
                description=description.strip(),
                body=parse_body(body),
                created_at=now,
                updated_at=now,
            )
        )
        self._audit("template created: %r", stored, "template_created")
        return stored

    async def update_template(
        self,
        template_id: UUID,
        *,
        name: str | None = None,
        description: str | None = None,
        body: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> DiagramTemplate:
        require_editor()
        current = await self.get_template(template_id)
        stored = await self._repository.save(
            replace(
                current,
                name=current.name if name is None else clean_template_name(name),
                description=current.description if description is None else description.strip(),
                body=current.body if body is None else parse_body(body),
                updated_at=self._now(),
            )
        )
        self._audit("template updated: %r", stored, "template_updated")
        return stored

    async def delete_template(self, template_id: UUID) -> None:
        require_editor()
        if not await self._repository.delete(template_id):
            msg = f"no template with id {template_id}"
            raise TemplateNotFoundError(msg)
        logger.info(
            "template deleted",
            extra={"action": "template_deleted", "template_id": str(template_id)},
        )

    async def create_from_diagram(
        self, diagram_id: UUID, *, name: str, description: str = ""
    ) -> DiagramTemplate:
        """Keep the shape of a diagram: each element becomes a slot of its type."""
        require_editor()
        detail = await self._diagrams.open(diagram_id)
        elements = {element.id: element for element in detail.graph.elements}
        scene_nodes = tuple(n for n in detail.nodes if n.element_id in elements)
        body = template_from_scene(
            DiagramScene(nodes=scene_nodes, objects=detail.objects, edges=detail.edges),
            elements,
            detail.graph.relationships,
            {
                element.id: element.stereotypes[0].name
                for element in detail.graph.elements
                if element.stereotypes
            },
        )
        now = self._now()
        stored = await self._repository.add(
            DiagramTemplate(
                id=uuid4(),
                name=clean_template_name(name),
                description=description.strip(),
                body=body,
                created_at=now,
                updated_at=now,
            )
        )
        self._audit("template saved from a diagram: %r", stored, "template_created")
        return stored

    async def instantiate(
        self,
        template_id: UUID,
        *,
        name: str,
        bindings: Sequence[Binding],
        create_relationships: bool = False,
    ) -> DiagramDetail:
        """Build a diagram from a template.

        Order matters. First everything that can be refused is: the bindings
        against the elements' real types (and stereotypes). Then the diagram is
        created — its name is the likeliest collision, and an element created
        before it would be left over. Then the elements, then (only if asked)
        the relationships, then the scene. A failure after the diagram exists
        removes the diagram; elements already created stay, as they are real.
        """
        require_editor()
        template = await self.get_template(template_id)
        body = template.body
        types = {}
        stereotypes: dict[UUID, frozenset[str]] = {}
        for binding in bindings:
            if binding.element_id is not None:
                element = await self._architecture.get_element(binding.element_id)
                types[element.id] = element.element_type
                stereotypes[element.id] = frozenset(ref.name for ref in element.stereotypes)
        plan = plan_instantiation(body, bindings, types, stereotypes)

        diagram = await self._diagrams.create(name=name)
        try:
            ids: dict[str, UUID] = {slot.key: element_id for slot, element_id in plan.bound}
            for slot, element_name in plan.to_create:
                created = await self._architecture.create_element(
                    element_type=slot.element_type, name=element_name
                )
                ids[slot.key] = created.id
            if create_relationships:
                await self._connect(body.links, ids)
            group_ids = {group.key: uuid4() for group in body.groups}
            nodes = [
                DiagramNode(
                    ids[slot.key], slot.x, slot.y, slot.width, slot.height,
                    parent_object_id=group_ids.get(slot.parent_key) if slot.parent_key else None,
                )
                for slot in body.slots
                if slot.key in ids
            ]
            objects = [
                DiagramObject(
                    group_ids[g.key], ObjectKind.GROUP, g.x, g.y, g.width, g.height,
                    text=g.text, colour=g.colour, z=-1,
                    parent_id=group_ids.get(g.parent_key) if g.parent_key else None,
                )
                for g in body.groups
            ] + [
                DiagramObject(
                    uuid4(), ObjectKind.NOTE, n.x, n.y, n.width, n.height,
                    text=n.text, colour=n.colour, z=1,
                    parent_id=group_ids.get(n.parent_key) if n.parent_key else None,
                    anchor_element_id=ids.get(n.anchor_key) if n.anchor_key else None,
                )
                for n in body.notes
            ]
            await self._diagrams.replace_layout(diagram.id, revision=0, nodes=nodes, objects=objects)
        except Exception:
            await self._diagrams.delete(diagram.id)
            raise
        logger.info(
            "diagram built from a template",
            extra={
                "action": "template_instantiated",
                "template_id": str(template_id),
                "diagram_id": str(diagram.id),
                "slots": len(ids),
                "relationships": create_relationships,
            },
        )
        return await self._diagrams.open(diagram.id)

    async def _connect(self, links: Sequence[Any], ids: Mapping[str, UUID]) -> None:
        """Create the template's links between filled slots — never twice the same one."""
        for link in links:
            source, target = ids.get(link.source_key), ids.get(link.target_key)
            if source is None or target is None:
                continue
            existing = await self._architecture.list_relationships(element_id=source, limit=200)
            if any(
                r.source_id == source
                and r.target_id == target
                and r.relationship_type is link.relationship_type
                for r in existing
            ):
                continue
            await self._architecture.connect(
                relationship_type=link.relationship_type, source_id=source, target_id=target
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _audit(message: str, template: DiagramTemplate, action: str) -> None:
        logger.info(message, template.name, extra={"action": action, "template_id": str(template.id)})
```

```python
"""Template endpoints end to end: statuses, typed errors, nothing created silently."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import AsyncIterator
from typing import Any

import httpx
import pytest
import pytest_asyncio

from ea.core.config import Settings
from ea.main import create_app
from ea.services.architecture import ArchitectureService
from tests.conftest import InMemoryDiagrams, InMemoryTemplates, StaticVerifier, a_reader, an_editor
from tests.unit.test_template_service import BODY

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio

EDITOR, READER = "editor-token", "reader-token"


@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def client(
    service: ArchitectureService, diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams, templates: InMemoryTemplates
) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=False),
        architecture_service=service,
        diagrams=diagrams,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        templates=templates,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:

@pytest_asyncio.fixture
async def secured(
    service: ArchitectureService, diagrams: InMemoryDiagrams, templates: InMemoryTemplates, nobody_calling: None
) -> AsyncIterator[httpx.AsyncClient]:
    app = create_app(
        Settings(debug=True, auth_enabled=True),
        architecture_service=service,

        templates=templates,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        verifier=StaticVerifier({EDITOR: an_editor(), READER: a_reader()}),
    )
    async with httpx.AsyncClient(transport=httpx.ASGITransport(app=app), base_url="http://test") as client:
        yield client


async def a_template(client: httpx.AsyncClient, name: str = "Application + hôte", body: dict[str, Any] | None = None):
    return await client.post("/diagram-templates", json={"name": name, "description": "", "body": body or BODY})


class TestTheLibrary:
    async def test_a_template_is_created_listed_read_renamed_and_deleted(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        created = await a_template(client)
        template_id = created.json()["id"]

        listed = (await client.get("/diagram-templates")).json()
        renamed = await client.put(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}", json={"name": "Autre"})
        deleted = await client.delete(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}")

        assert created.status_code == 201
        assert (listed[0]["slot_count"], listed[0]["group_count"], listed[0]["note_count"], listed[0]["link_count"]) == (2, 1, 1, 1)
        assert renamed.json()["name"] == "Autre"
        assert deleted.status_code == 204
        assert (await client.get(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}")).status_code == 404

    async def test_a_taken_name_is_a_409(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        await a_template(client)

        response = await a_template(client)

        assert (response.status_code, response.json()["error"]) == (409, "duplicate")

    async def test_a_link_the_metamodel_forbids_a_bad_colour_or_too_many_slots_is_a_422(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        forbidden = {**BODY, "links": [{"source_key": "host", "target_key": "app", "relationship_type": "specialization"}]}
        coloured = {**BODY, "groups": [{**BODY["groups"][0], "colour": 'red" onload="x'}]}
        slot = BODY["slots"][1]
        crowded = {**BODY, "slots": [{**slot, "key": f"s{i}"} for i in range(101)], "links": [], "notes": []}

        results = [(await a_template(client, f"T{i}", body)).status_code for i, body in enumerate((forbidden, coloured, crowded))]

        assert results == [422, 422, 422]


class TestInstantiating:
    async def test_a_diagram_is_built_with_no_relationship_unless_asked(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        template_id = (await a_template(client)).json()["id"]
        bindings = [{"slot_key": "app", "element_id": None, "create": {"name": "Facturation"}},
                    {"slot_key": "host", "element_id": None, "create": {"name": "srv-01"}}]

        plain = await client.post(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate", json={"name": "Sans liens", "bindings": bindings})
        linked = await client.post(
            f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate",
            json={"name": "Avec liens", "bindings": [
                {**bindings[0], "create": {"name": "A2"}}, {**bindings[1], "create": {"name": "H2"}}],
                "create_relationships": True},
        )

        assert plain.status_code == 201 and plain.json()["relationships"] == []
        assert [r["relationship_type"] for r in linked.json()["relationships"]] == ["realization"]
        assert len(plain.json()["objects"]) == 2  # the group and the note

    async def test_an_element_of_the_wrong_type_is_a_422_and_nothing_is_created(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        template_id = (await a_template(client)).json()["id"]
        node = (await client.post("/elements", json={"element_type": "node", "name": "H"})).json()["id"]

        response = await client.post(
            f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate",
            json={"name": "X", "bindings": [{"slot_key": "app", "element_id": node, "create": None}]},
        )

        assert response.status_code == 422
        assert (await client.get("/diagrams")).json() == []
        assert (await client.get("/elements")).json()["total"] == 1

    async def test_a_diagram_becomes_a_template(self, client: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        app = (await client.post("/elements", json={"element_type": "application_component", "name": "Facturation"})).json()["id"]
        diagram = (await client.post("/diagrams", json={"name": "Source"})).json()["id"]
        node = {"element_id": app, "x": 10.0, "y": 10.0, "width": 132.0, "height": 46.0, "parent_object_id": None, "z": 0}
        await client.put(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/layout", json={"revision": 0, "nodes": [node], "objects": [], "edges": []})

        response = await client.post(f"/diagrams/{diagram}/as-template", json={"name": "Squelette", "description": ""})

        assert response.status_code == 201
        assert [s["element_type"] for s in response.json()["body"]["slots"]] == ["application_component"]
        assert app not in response.text


class TestWhoMay:
    async def test_a_reader_lists_but_cannot_write_or_instantiate(self, secured: httpx.AsyncClient) -> None:
        editor = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {EDITOR}"}
        reader = {"Authorization": f"Bearer {READER}"}
        template_id = (await secured.post("/diagram-templates", json={"name": "T", "description": "", "body": BODY}, headers=editor)).json()["id"]

        listed = await secured.get("/diagram-templates", headers=reader)

        built = await secured.post(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate", json={"name": "X", "bindings": []}, headers=reader)
        deleted = await secured.delete(f"/diagram-templates/{template_id}", headers=reader)

        assert listed.status_code == 200
        assert (created.status_code, built.status_code, deleted.status_code) == (403, 403, 403)
```

`backend/src/ea/api/schemas.py`, section modèles (importer `MAX_SLOTS, MAX_TEMPLATE_LINKS, MAX_TEMPLATE_OBJECTS, Binding, DiagramTemplate, body_to_json` de `ea.domain.diagram_templates`) :

```python
# --- Diagram templates (docs/adr: modèles de diagrammes) --------------------
# A body is a typed skeleton with no element in it. Every field is required
# (nullable where it may be empty): the same models serve the request and the
# answer, so no `-Input`/`-Output` twin.

TemplateKey = Annotated[str, Field(pattern=r"^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,39}$")]


class TemplateSlotSchema(_Input):
    key: TemplateKey
    element_type: ElementType
    label: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)]
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate
    width: BoxSize
    height: BoxSize
    stereotype: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=100)] | None
    parent_key: TemplateKey | None


class TemplateGroupSchema(_Input):
    key: TemplateKey
    text: ObjectText
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate
    width: BoxSize
    height: BoxSize
    colour: Colour | None
    parent_key: TemplateKey | None


class TemplateNoteSchema(_Input):
    key: TemplateKey
    text: ObjectText
    x: Coordinate
    y: Coordinate
    width: BoxSize
    height: BoxSize
    colour: Colour | None
    parent_key: TemplateKey | None
    anchor_key: TemplateKey | None


class TemplateLinkSchema(_Input):
    source_key: TemplateKey
    target_key: TemplateKey
    relationship_type: RelationshipType


class TemplateBodySchema(_Input):
    version: Literal[1]
    slots: Annotated[list[TemplateSlotSchema], Field(max_length=MAX_SLOTS)]
    groups: Annotated[list[TemplateGroupSchema], Field(max_length=MAX_TEMPLATE_OBJECTS)]
    notes: Annotated[list[TemplateNoteSchema], Field(max_length=MAX_TEMPLATE_OBJECTS)]
    links: Annotated[list[TemplateLinkSchema], Field(max_length=MAX_TEMPLATE_LINKS)]

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, template: DiagramTemplate) -> TemplateBodySchema:
        return cls.model_validate(body_to_json(template.body))

    def raw(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return self.model_dump(mode="json")


class DiagramTemplateCreate(_Input):
    name: Name
    description: Description = ""
    body: TemplateBodySchema


class DiagramTemplateUpdate(_Input):
    name: Name | None = None
    description: Description | None = None
    body: TemplateBodySchema | None = None


class DiagramTemplateRead(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    name: str
    description: str
    body: TemplateBodySchema
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplateRead:
        return cls(
            id=template.id,
            name=template.name,
            description=template.description,
            body=TemplateBodySchema.of(template),
            created_at=template.created_at,
            updated_at=template.updated_at,
        )


class DiagramTemplateSummaryRead(BaseModel):
    id: UUID
    name: str
    description: str
    slot_count: int
    group_count: int
    note_count: int
    link_count: int
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, template: DiagramTemplate) -> DiagramTemplateSummaryRead:
        body = template.body
        return cls(
            id=template.id,
            name=template.name,
            description=template.description,
            slot_count=len(body.slots),
            group_count=len(body.groups),
            note_count=len(body.notes),
            link_count=len(body.links),
            created_at=template.created_at,
            updated_at=template.updated_at,
        )


class CreateElementSchema(_Input):
    name: Name


class InstantiateBinding(_Input):
    """What fills one slot: an existing element, an element to create, or neither (the slot is left out)."""

    slot_key: TemplateKey
    element_id: UUID | None
    create: CreateElementSchema | None

    def placed(self) -> Binding:
        return Binding(
            slot_key=self.slot_key,
            element_id=self.element_id,
            create_name=self.create.name if self.create else None,
        )


class InstantiateRequest(_Input):
    name: Name
    bindings: Annotated[list[InstantiateBinding], Field(max_length=MAX_SLOTS)]
    # Off unless asked for: a template's links are what its author drew, not facts nobody decided.
    create_relationships: bool = False


class AsTemplateRequest(_Input):
    name: Name
    description: Description = ""
```
`backend/src/ea/api/errors.py` : `TemplateNotFoundError: (404, "not_found")`, `DuplicateTemplateError: (409, "duplicate")`. `dependencies.py` : `template_service_of`, `get_template_service`, `Templates`.

`backend/src/ea/api/diagram_templates.py` :

```python
"""The endpoints of diagram templates — typed skeletons, never an element in them.

Instantiating builds a diagram through `ArchitectureService`: the ArchiMate and
IP-addressing rules apply to every element and relationship it creates, and a
relationship is created only when `create_relationships` is true.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Response, status

from ea.api.dependencies import Templates
from ea.api.schemas import (
    AsTemplateRequest,
    DiagramRead,
    DiagramTemplateCreate,
    DiagramTemplateRead,
    DiagramTemplateSummaryRead,
    DiagramTemplateUpdate,
    ErrorResponse,
    InstantiateRequest,
)

router = APIRouter(tags=["diagram-templates"])

_ERROR = {"model": ErrorResponse}
REJECTED: dict[int | str, dict[str, type[ErrorResponse]]] = {
    status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND: _ERROR,
    status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT: _ERROR,
    status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_CONTENT: _ERROR,
}


@router.get("/diagram-templates", response_model=list[DiagramTemplateSummaryRead])
async def list_templates(templates: Templates) -> list[DiagramTemplateSummaryRead]:
    return [DiagramTemplateSummaryRead.of(t) for t in await templates.list_templates()]


@router.post(
    "/diagram-templates",
    response_model=DiagramTemplateRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=REJECTED,
)
async def create_template(body: DiagramTemplateCreate, templates: Templates) -> DiagramTemplateRead:
    return DiagramTemplateRead.of(
        await templates.create_template(name=body.name, description=body.description, body=body.body.raw())
    )


@router.get("/diagram-templates/{template_id}", response_model=DiagramTemplateRead, responses=REJECTED)
async def read_template(template_id: UUID, templates: Templates) -> DiagramTemplateRead:
    return DiagramTemplateRead.of(await templates.get_template(template_id))


@router.put("/diagram-templates/{template_id}", response_model=DiagramTemplateRead, responses=REJECTED)
async def update_template(
    template_id: UUID, body: DiagramTemplateUpdate, templates: Templates
) -> DiagramTemplateRead:
    return DiagramTemplateRead.of(
        await templates.update_template(
            template_id,
            name=body.name,
            description=body.description,
            body=body.body.raw() if body.body else None,
        )
    )


@router.delete(
    "/diagram-templates/{template_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT, responses=REJECTED
)
async def delete_template(template_id: UUID, templates: Templates) -> Response:
    await templates.delete_template(template_id)
    return Response(status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)


@router.post(
    "/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate",
    response_model=DiagramRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=REJECTED,
)
async def instantiate_template(
    template_id: UUID, body: InstantiateRequest, templates: Templates
) -> DiagramRead:
    """Build a diagram from a template.

    Each slot is bound to an existing element of its type, to an element to
    create, or left out. Everything refusable is refused before anything is
    written; relationships are created only if `create_relationships` is true.
    """
    detail = await templates.instantiate(
        template_id,
        name=body.name,
        bindings=[binding.placed() for binding in body.bindings],
        create_relationships=body.create_relationships,
    )
    return DiagramRead.of(detail)


@router.post(
    "/diagrams/{diagram_id}/as-template",
    response_model=DiagramTemplateRead,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
    responses=REJECTED,
)
async def save_as_template(
    diagram_id: UUID, body: AsTemplateRequest, templates: Templates
) -> DiagramTemplateRead:
    """Keep the shape of a diagram: each element becomes a slot of the same type."""
    return DiagramTemplateRead.of(
        await templates.create_from_diagram(diagram_id, name=body.name, description=body.description)
    )
```

```python
    @server.tool(annotations=READS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def list_diagram_templates() -> list[DiagramTemplateSummaryRead]:
        """The diagram templates: recurring schemas, each a typed skeleton.

        A template holds no element, only slots of an ArchiMate type (an
        application component, a node…) with the groups and notes around them.
        Read this to find the `template_id` and the slot keys that
        `instantiate_diagram_template` binds.
        """
        return [DiagramTemplateSummaryRead.of(t) for t in await get_templates().list_templates()]

    @server.tool(annotations=ADDS)
    @speaking_plainly
    async def instantiate_diagram_template(
        template_id: Annotated[UUID, Field(description="A template, as `list_diagram_templates` reports it.")],
        name: Name,
        bindings: Annotated[
            list[InstantiateBinding],
            Field(description="One per slot to fill: an existing `element_id` of the slot's type, or `create` with a name. A slot with neither is left out."),
        ],
        create_relationships: Annotated[
            bool, Field(description="Also create the relationships the template draws. Off by default.")
        ] = False,
    ) -> DiagramRead:
        """Build a diagram from a template.

        Elements are created and linked by the same rules as everywhere else,
        so a slot bound to an element of another type is refused, saying which.
        Relationships are created **only** when `create_relationships` is true.
        """
        return DiagramRead.of(
            await get_templates().instantiate(
                template_id,
                name=name,
                bindings=[binding.placed() for binding in bindings],
                create_relationships=create_relationships,
            )
        )
```

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import { useTemplates } from '../src/features/diagrams/useTemplates'
import { stubApi } from './support/api'

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals())

const SUMMARY = {
  id: 't1', name: 'Application + hôte', description: '', slot_count: 2, group_count: 1, note_count: 1, link_count: 1,
  created_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z', updated_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
}

describe('useTemplates', () => {
  it('loads the library', async () => {
    stubApi([{ path: '/diagram-templates', body: [SUMMARY] }])
    const templates = useTemplates()

    await templates.load()

    expect(templates.templates.value.map((t) => t.name)).toEqual(['Application + hôte'])
  })

  it('saves a diagram as a template and reloads the library', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: '/diagrams/d1/as-template', status: 201, body: { ...SUMMARY, body: {} } },
      { path: '/diagram-templates', body: [SUMMARY] },
    ])
    const templates = useTemplates()

    await templates.saveFromDiagram('d1', 'Modèle')

    expect(calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST')?.body).toEqual({ name: 'Modèle', description: '' })
    expect(templates.templates.value).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('instantiates and hands back the diagram, relationships off unless asked', async () => {
    const calls = stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: '/diagram-templates/t1/instantiate', status: 201, body: { id: 'd2' } },
    ])
    const templates = useTemplates()

    const created = await templates.instantiate('t1', {
      name: 'Facturation',
      bindings: [{ slot_key: 'app', element_id: null, create: { name: 'Facturation' } }],
      create_relationships: false,
    })

    expect(created.id).toBe('d2')
    expect((calls[0].body as { create_relationships: boolean }).create_relationships).toBe(false)
  })

  it('throws the refusal of a binding of the wrong type, for the wizard to show', async () => {
    stubApi([
      { method: 'POST', path: '/diagram-templates/t1/instantiate', status: 422,
        body: { error: 'invalid_input', detail: "slot 'app' wants a application_component, got node" } },
    ])
    const templates = useTemplates()

    await expect(templates.instantiate('t1', { name: 'X', bindings: [], create_relationships: false })).rejects.toThrow(
      /wants a application_component/,
    )
  })
})
```

```ts
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/vue'
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest'

import InstantiateWizard from '../src/features/diagrams/InstantiateWizard.vue'
import { anElement } from './support/api'

const TEMPLATE = {
  id: 't1', name: 'Application + hôte', description: '',
  created_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z', updated_at: '2026-10-10T12:00:00Z',
  body: {
    version: 1,
    slots: [
      { key: 'app', element_type: 'application_component', label: 'Application', x: 0, y: 0, width: 132, height: 46, stereotype: 'database', parent_key: null },
      { key: 'host', element_type: 'node', label: 'Hôte', x: 0, y: 100, width: 132, height: 46, stereotype: null, parent_key: null },
    ],
    groups: [], notes: [], links: [{ source_key: 'host', target_key: 'app', relationship_type: 'realization' }],
  },
} as never

function draw(extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const search = vi.fn(async (term: string) => [anElement({ id: 'e1', name: `${term} Facturation` })])
  const rendered = render(InstantiateWizard, {
    props: { template: TEMPLATE, search, typeLabel: (v: string) => v, failure: '', ...extra },
  })
  return { rendered, search }
}

describe('InstantiateWizard', () => {
  it('searches an element per slot, filtered on the slot type, and shows the stereotype required', async () => {
    const { search } = draw()

    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Application — chercher un élément'), 'Fac')

    await waitFor(() => expect(search).toHaveBeenCalledWith('Fac', 'application_component'))
    expect(screen.getByText(/«database»/)).toBeInTheDocument()
  })

  it('binds an existing element, creates another, and leaves a slot out — relationships off by default', async () => {
    const { rendered } = draw()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'Facturation')
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Application — chercher un élément'), 'Fac')
    await fireEvent.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: /Facturation/ }))

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' }))

    expect(rendered.emitted().submit).toEqual([
      [{
        name: 'Facturation',
        bindings: [{ slot_key: 'app', element_id: 'e1', create: null }],
        create_relationships: false,
      }],
    ])
  })

  it('creates an element for a slot when asked, with the name given', async () => {
    const { rendered } = draw()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'V')
    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByLabelText('Hôte — créer l\'élément'))
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Hôte — nom du nouvel élément'), 'srv-01')

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' }))

    expect((rendered.emitted().submit[0] as [{ bindings: unknown[] }])[0].bindings).toEqual([
      { slot_key: 'host', element_id: null, create: { name: 'srv-01' } },
    ])
  })

  it('creates the template relationships only when the box is ticked', async () => {
    const { rendered } = draw()
    const box = screen.getByLabelText('Créer aussi les relations du modèle')
    expect(box).not.toBeChecked()
    await fireEvent.update(screen.getByLabelText('Nom de la vue'), 'V')
    await fireEvent.click(box)

    await fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Créer la vue' }))

    expect((rendered.emitted().submit[0] as [{ create_relationships: boolean }])[0].create_relationships).toBe(true)
  })

  it('shows the refusal it is given', () => {
    draw({ failure: "slot 'app' wants a application_component, got node" })

    expect(screen.getByRole('alert')).toHaveTextContent('wants a application_component')
  })
})
```

`frontend/src/features/diagrams/useTemplates.ts` :

```ts
// The library of diagram templates, and the two ways to use it: save a diagram
// as one, and build a diagram from one. A refusal (a slot bound to an element
// of another type, say) is thrown for the wizard to show.
import { ref } from 'vue'

import type { components } from '../../api/schema'
import { api, unwrap } from '../../lib/api'
import { useLatestRequest } from '../../lib/latest'

export type DiagramTemplateSummaryRead = components['schemas']['DiagramTemplateSummaryRead']
export type DiagramTemplateRead = components['schemas']['DiagramTemplateRead']
export type InstantiateRequest = components['schemas']['InstantiateRequest']
export type DiagramRead = components['schemas']['DiagramRead']

export function useTemplates() {
  const templates = ref<DiagramTemplateSummaryRead[]>([])
  const listing = useLatestRequest()
  const { status, error } = listing

  async function load(): Promise<void> {
    await listing.run(
      async (signal) => unwrap(await api.GET('/diagram-templates', { signal })),
      (answer) => {
        templates.value = answer
      },
    )
  }

  async function read(id: string): Promise<DiagramTemplateRead> {
    return unwrap(
      await api.GET('/diagram-templates/{template_id}', { params: { path: { template_id: id } } }),
    )
  }

  async function remove(id: string): Promise<void> {
    unwrap(
      await api.DELETE('/diagram-templates/{template_id}', { params: { path: { template_id: id } } }),
    )
    await load()
  }

  async function saveFromDiagram(
    diagramId: string,
    name: string,
    description = '',
  ): Promise<DiagramTemplateRead> {
    const created = unwrap(
      await api.POST('/diagrams/{diagram_id}/as-template', {
        params: { path: { diagram_id: diagramId } },
        body: { name, description },
      }),
    )
    await load()
    return created
  }

  async function instantiate(id: string, payload: InstantiateRequest): Promise<DiagramRead> {
    return unwrap(
      await api.POST('/diagram-templates/{template_id}/instantiate', {
        params: { path: { template_id: id } },
        body: payload,
      }),
    )
  }

  return { templates, status, error, load, read, remove, saveFromDiagram, instantiate }
}
```

`frontend/src/features/diagrams/InstantiateWizard.vue` :

```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Fill the slots of a template: for each, pick an element of its type, create
// one, or leave it out. The type filter is the slot's own — the API refuses a
// wrong one anyway. Relationships are off unless the box is ticked.
import { reactive, ref } from 'vue'

import { debounce } from '../../lib/debounce'
import type { ElementRead } from './useDiagram'
import type { DiagramTemplateRead, InstantiateRequest } from './useTemplates'

const props = defineProps<{
  template: DiagramTemplateRead
  search: (term: string, elementType: string) => Promise<ElementRead[]>
  typeLabel: (value: string) => string
  failure: string
}>()
const emit = defineEmits<{ submit: [payload: InstantiateRequest]; cancel: [] }>()

type Choice = { term: string; results: ElementRead[]; picked: ElementRead | null; create: boolean; name: string }
const choices = reactive<Record<string, Choice>>(
  Object.fromEntries(
    props.template.body.slots.map((slot) => [slot.key, { term: '', results: [], picked: null, create: false, name: '' }]),
  ),
)
const viewName = ref('')
const createRelationships = ref(false)

// One debounce per slot would be an array of timers; the typing pause is short
// and the request guarded by the API's own cap, so one shared pause is enough.
const lookup = debounce(async (key: string, term: string, type: string) => {
  choices[key].results = term.trim() ? await props.search(term.trim(), type) : []
}, 250)

function typed(key: string, type: string, term: string): void {
  choices[key].term = term
  choices[key].picked = null
  lookup.call(key, term, type)
}

function pick(key: string, element: ElementRead): void {
  choices[key].picked = element
  choices[key].results = []
  choices[key].term = element.name
}

function submit(): void {
  const bindings = props.template.body.slots.flatMap((slot) => {
    const choice = choices[slot.key]
    if (choice.create && choice.name.trim()) {
      return [{ slot_key: slot.key, element_id: null, create: { name: choice.name.trim() } }]
    }
    return choice.picked ? [{ slot_key: slot.key, element_id: choice.picked.id, create: null }] : []
  })
  emit('submit', { name: viewName.value.trim(), bindings, create_relationships: createRelationships.value })
}
</script>

<template>
  <form class="wizard" aria-label="Instancier un modèle" @submit.prevent="submit">
    <h3>{{ template.name }}</h3>
    <label for="wizard-name">Nom de la vue</label>
    <input id="wizard-name" v-model="viewName" maxlength="200" autocomplete="off" />

    <fieldset v-for="slot in template.body.slots" :key="slot.key" class="slot">
      <legend>{{ slot.label }} — {{ typeLabel(slot.element_type) }}</legend>
      <p v-if="slot.stereotype" class="hint">Doit porter «{{ slot.stereotype }}»</p>
      <label :for="`search-${slot.key}`">{{ slot.label }} — chercher un élément</label>
      <input
        :id="`search-${slot.key}`"
        :value="choices[slot.key].term"
        :disabled="choices[slot.key].create"
        autocomplete="off"
        @input="typed(slot.key, slot.element_type, ($event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)"
      />
      <ul v-if="choices[slot.key].results.length > 0">
        <li v-for="element in choices[slot.key].results" :key="element.id">
          <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="pick(slot.key, element)">{{ element.name }}</button>
        </li>
      </ul>
      <label>
        <input v-model="choices[slot.key].create" type="checkbox" :aria-label="`${slot.label} — créer l'élément`" />
        Créer l'élément
      </label>
      <template v-if="choices[slot.key].create">
        <label :for="`new-${slot.key}`">{{ slot.label }} — nom du nouvel élément</label>
        <input :id="`new-${slot.key}`" v-model="choices[slot.key].name" maxlength="200" />
      </template>
    </fieldset>

    <label>
      <input v-model="createRelationships" type="checkbox" />
      Créer aussi les relations du modèle
    </label>
    <p class="hint">Les relations du modèle ne sont créées dans le catalogue que si cette case est cochée.</p>

    <p v-if="failure" class="banner banner--error" role="alert">{{ failure }}</p>
    <div class="actions">
      <button type="submit" :disabled="!viewName.trim()">Créer la vue</button>
      <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="emit('cancel')">Renoncer</button>
    </div>
  </form>
</template>

<style scoped>
.wizard,
.slot {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0.4rem;
}
.slot {
  border: 1px solid var(--border);
  border-radius: 6px;
  padding: 0.6rem;
}
.actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}
</style>
```
(Le test « Nom de la vue » utilise `getByLabelText('Nom de la vue')` : le `<label for="wizard-name">` a ce texte exact.)

```vue
    <div v-if="canWrite" class="toolbar__group" role="group" aria-label="Modèle">
      <button type="button" @click="emit('save-template')">Enregistrer comme modèle</button>
    </div>
```

`DiagramsSection.vue` : importer `TemplateLibrary`, `InstantiateWizard`, `useTemplates` ; `const templates = useTemplates()`, états `savingTemplate`, `templateName`, `wizardTemplate: ref<DiagramTemplateRead | null>(null)`, `wizardFailure`. Dans `onMounted`, pour tout appelant : `await templates.load()`. Gestionnaires :

```ts
async function saveTemplate(): Promise<void> {
  const current = editor.diagram.value
  if (!current || !templateName.value.trim()) {
    return
  }
  viewFailure.value = ''
  try {
    await editor.flush()
    await templates.saveFromDiagram(current.id, templateName.value.trim())
    savingTemplate.value = false
    templateName.value = ''
  } catch (caught) {
    viewFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}

async function useTemplate(id: string): Promise<void> {
  wizardFailure.value = ''
  try {
    wizardTemplate.value = await templates.read(id)
  } catch (caught) {
    listFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}

async function searchByType(term: string, elementType: string): Promise<ElementRead[]> {
  const page = unwrap(await api.GET('/elements', { params: { query: { search: term, element_type: [elementType as never], limit: 10 } } }))
  return page.items
}

async function instantiate(payload: InstantiateRequest): Promise<void> {
  const template = wizardTemplate.value
  if (!template) {
    return
  }
  wizardFailure.value = ''
  try {
    const created = await templates.instantiate(template.id, payload)
    wizardTemplate.value = null
    await router.push({ query: { diagram: created.id } })
  } catch (caught) {
    wizardFailure.value = messageOf(caught)
  }
}
```
(`editor.flush` doit être exposé par `useDiagram` : l'ajouter au retour s'il ne l'est pas — la fonction existe.) Template : barre d'outils `@save-template="savingTemplate = true"` ; formulaire inline

```vue
          <button type="submit" :disabled="!templateName.trim()">Enregistrer le modèle</button>
          <button type="button" class="secondary" @click="savingTemplate = false">Renoncer</button>
        </form>
```

````markdown
# NN. Modèles de diagrammes

Date : 2026-10-10
Statut : Accepté

## Contexte

Les mêmes schémas reviennent : une application, son hôte, sa base de données, la zone qui les entoure,
la note qui rappelle la règle. Les redessiner coûte un quart d'heure et produit autant de variantes.
Un modèle doit garder la **forme** — des emplacements d'un type ArchiMate, des groupes, des notes, des
liens — sans garder les **faits** : les éléments d'un diagramme appartiennent au catalogue, et un modèle
qui en contiendrait serait un second modèle libre de contredire le premier
([`0031`](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md)).

## Décision

**Un modèle est un squelette typé : un document JSON versionné, validé par le domaine, qui ne contient
aucun élément. L'instancier lie chaque emplacement à un élément existant du bon type, à un élément à
créer, ou le laisse de côté — et ne crée une relation que si l'appelant l'a demandé.**

| Point | Choix |
|---|---|
| Stockage | `diagram_templates(id, name unique, description, body jsonb, …)` (migration `0012`). Le corps n'est jamais interrogé à l'intérieur : validé en domaine, relu en entier |
| Corps | `version: 1` ; `slots` (clé, type d'élément, libellé, géométrie, stéréotype facultatif, groupe parent), `groups`, `notes` (ancrées sur un emplacement), `links` (clé source, clé cible, type de relation) |
| Validation | Types d'éléments et de relations contrôlés par le métamodèle du domaine (`permitted_relationships`), jamais par une liste recopiée ; clés uniques, groupes sans boucle, profondeur ≤ 5, note ancrée sur un emplacement, lien entre emplacements existants et permis pour leurs deux types |
| Depuis un diagramme | `POST /diagrams/{id}/as-template` : chaque élément devient un emplacement du même type (libellé = son nom), notes, groupes et liens sont gardés par type ; l'identité des éléments ne survit pas |
| Instanciation | Liaisons `{slot_key, element_id | create:{name}}` ; tout ce qui peut être refusé l'est avant toute écriture (type d'élément différent de celui du slot, stéréotype exigé non porté, élément lié à deux slots, nom blanc) |
| Relations | **Jamais créées en silence** : `create_relationships` vaut faux par défaut, la case de l'assistant n'est pas cochée ; alors chaque lien passe par `ArchitectureService.connect` (règles ArchiMate) et un lien déjà présent n'est pas dupliqué |
| Éléments | Créés par `ArchitectureService.create_element` : les règles ArchiMate et l'IPAM s'appliquent. Aucun chemin ne contourne le service |
| Ordre | Liaisons validées → diagramme créé (le nom pris est la collision la plus probable) → éléments → relations (si demandé) → scène. Un échec après la création du diagramme le supprime ; les éléments déjà créés restent, ils sont réels |
| Agents | `list_diagram_templates`, `instantiate_diagram_template` (sans relations par défaut) |
| Droits | Lire : tout appelant authentifié ; écrire, instancier : `ea-editor` |

## Alternatives envisagées et écartées

| Option | Pour | Contre | Verdict |
|---|---|---|---|
| Un modèle = un diagramme que l'on copie | Aucun nouveau concept | Copie des éléments ou des identifiants : le « modèle » référence des faits qui bougent | Écarté |
| Un modèle avec des éléments pré-créés | Instanciation immédiate | Un second catalogue ; des éléments fantômes dans les analyses d'impact | Écarté |
| Créer les relations du modèle par défaut | Un diagramme prêt | Écrit des faits que personne n'a décidés, pour des paires dont le catalogue a peut-être déjà la relation | Écarté |
| Valider le corps seulement à l'instanciation | Écriture du modèle plus simple | Un modèle invalide dort dans la bibliothèque jusqu'au jour où on en a besoin | Écarté |
| Tables relationnelles `template_slots`, `template_links` | Requêtes dans le corps | Personne n'interroge l'intérieur d'un modèle ; deux tables de plus pour un document | Écarté |

## Conséquences

- **L'instanciation n'est pas une transaction unique** : elle appelle des cas d'usage qui possèdent chacun la leur.
  Les éléments créés avant un échec tardif restent dans le catalogue ; le diagramme, lui, est retiré.
- **Un modèle dont le corps cite un stéréotype** n'est liable qu'à un élément qui le porte ; sans le lot « profils »,
  un tel emplacement ne peut être que créé vide ou omis.
- **Le métamodèle est figé (3.2)** : un modèle ne peut pas devenir invalide du fait d'une évolution du métamodèle,
  et chaque instanciation revalide de toute façon.
- **Pas de partage entre environnements** au-delà de l'export du JSON d'un modèle lu par `GET`.
- Journal : `action=template_created|template_updated|template_deleted|template_instantiated`, avec des identifiants
  et des comptes, jamais le texte d'une note.

## Références

- ADR liés : [0005](0005-metamodele-archimate-3-2.md), [0031](0031-diagrammes-enregistres.md),
  l'ADR des objets de vue (notes et groupes) et l'ADR des profils (stéréotypes)
- Code concerné : `backend/src/ea/domain/diagram_templates.py`, `backend/src/ea/services/templates.py`,
  `backend/migrations/versions/0012_diagram_templates.py`
````
